44\. The following four should not be undertaken in the cremation rite: avahana, arcana, patralambha and avagahana. Let there be sankalpa for each gift and let there be a pindadana.

## \|\| 4.45 \|\|

*padārthapañcakaṃ na syādrekhā pratyavanejanam \|*  
*dadyādakṣayyamudakaṃ na syādetattrayaṃ punaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The fiveart ides common to every Sacrifice should not be there. The libation of water should be given but not the trio

## \|\| 4.46 \|\|

*svadhāvācanamāśīśca tilakaṃ ca khagottama \|*  
*ghaṭaṃ dadyātsamāṣānnaṃ dadyāllohasya dakṣiṇām \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Svadha-Vacana Asisa and Titaka, О bird. A vessel full of mustard should be given. Iron should also be gifted,

## \|\| 4.47 \|\|

*piṇḍasya cālanaṃ proktaṃ naiva proktamidaṃ trikam \|*  
*pracchādanavisargau ca svastivācanakaṃ tathā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The pinda-calana rite is followed but not the following three, Pracchadana, visarga and Svastivacana.

## \|\| 4.48-49 \|\|

*eṣu ṣaṭsu vidhiḥ proktaḥ śrāddheṣu malineṣu te \|*  
*ṣaḍeva maraṇasthāne dvāri cātvarike tathā \|\| 48 \|\|*

*viśrāme kāṣṭhacayane tathā sañcayane khaga \|*  
*mṛtisthāne śavo nāma bhūmistuṣyati devatā \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. Procedure has been laid down for six sraddhas, at six places; at the place of death, at the door, at the crossroads, at the place of rest, on the wood-pile and at the collection rite. The dead body is called lava at the place of death. The deity Earth is pleased thereby.

## \|\| 4.50 \|\|

*pāntho dvāri bhavettena prītā syādvāstudevatā \|*  
*catvare khecarastena tuṣyedbhṛtādidevatā \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. It is called pantha at the door; the deity Vastu is pleased. It is called Khecara at the crossroads; the deity Bhuta is pleased.

## \|\| 4.51 \|\|

*viśrāme bhūtasaṃjño 'yaṃ tuṣṭastena diśo daśa \|*  
*citāyāṃ sādhaka iti sañcitau preta ucyate \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. It is called bhuta in the resting place. The ten quarters are satisfied thereby. It is called Sadhaka on the pyre and preta at the collection- rite.

## \|\| 4.52 \|\|

*tiladarbhaghṛtedhāṃsi gṛhītvā tu sutādayaḥ \|*  
*gāthāṃ yamasya sūktaṃ vāpyadhīyānā vrajanti hi \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Holding gingelly seeds, Darbha, clarified butter, the sons and other relatives go round the pyre reciting gatha or the sukta of Yama.

## \|\| 4.53 \|\|

*aharaharnīyamāno gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ vṛṣam \|*  
*vaivasvato na tṛpyeta surayā tviva durmatiḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Taking cow, horse, man and bull everyday Yama is not satiated just as a wicked person is not satiated with wine.

## \|\| 4.54 \|\|

*imāṃ gāthamapeteti sūktaṃ vā pathi saṃpaṭhet \|*  
*dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśyaraṇyaṃ vrajeyuḥ sarvabāndhavāḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. They should recite the gatha or the hymn apeta in the way. The relatives accompany the corpse to a forest in the southern direction.

## \|\| 4.55 \|\|

*pathi śrāddhadvayaṃ kuryātpūrvoktavidhinā khaga \|*  
*tataḥ śanairbhūtale vai dakṣiṇāśirasaṃ śavam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. О bird, in the aforesaid manner the two sraddhas should be performed in the way.

## \|\| 4.56 \|\|

*sthāpayitvā citābhūmau pūrvoktaṃ śrāddhamācaret \|*  
*tṛṇakāṣṭhatilājyādi svayaṃ ninyuḥ sutādayaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Then the corpse should be laid lightly on the earth with its head to the south and the aforesaid sraddha should be performed. The sons should fetch dry grass, dry pieces of wood and gingelly seeds.

## \|\| 4.57 \|\|

*śūdrānītaiḥ kṛtaṃ karma sarvaṃ bhavati niṣphalam \|*  
*prācīnāvītinā bhāvyaṃ dakṣiṇābhimukena ca \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. If these are fetched by a kdra each and every act done in favour of the deceased would go futile. The performer of the funeral rite should wear the sacred thread over the right shoulder and should sit, along with the mourners, facing the south.

## \|\| 4.58 \|\|

*vedī tatra prakartavyā yathāśāstramathāṇḍaja \|*  
*pratevastraṃ dvidhā kṛtvārdhena taṃ chādayettataḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. О bird, an altar should be made there as prescribed. The cloth for the corpse should be tom into twofold pieces and the corpse should be covered with the one-half.

## \|\| 4.59 \|\|

*ardhaṃ śmaśānavāsārthaṃ bhūmāveva viniḥ kṣipet \|*  
*tataḥ pūrvoktavidhinā piṇḍaṃ pretakare nyaset \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The other half should be spread over the earth. The pinda should be kept in the hand of the deceased as stated before.

## \|\| 4.60 \|\|

*ājyenābhyañjanaṃ kāryaṃ sarvāṅgeṣu śavasya ca \|*  
*dāhamṛtyorantarāle vidhiḥ piṇḍasya taṃ śṛṇu \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The corpse should be anointed With the clarified butter. Now hear about the pinda- vidhi for the deceased prior to his cremation.

## \|\| 4.61 \|\|

*pūrvoktaiḥ pañcabhiḥ piṇḍaiḥ śavasyāhutiyogyatā \|*  
*anyathā copaghātācca rākṣasādyā bhavanti hi \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. By virtue of the aforesaid five pindas, the departed soul attains fitness for becoming a mane (pitar). Or else, it attains the form of a demon.

## \|\| 4.62 \|\|

*saṃmṛjya copalipyātha ullikhyoddhṛtya vedikām \|*  
*abhyukṣyopasamādhāya vahniṃ tatra vidhānataḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Having cleansed and smeared the ground at the altar, the fire should be lit as prescribed.

## \|\| 4.63-64 \|\|

*puṣpākṣataiśca saṃpūjya devaṃ kravyādasaṃjñakam \|*  
*śrautena tu vidhānena hyāhitāgniṃ dahedvudhaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

*caṇḍālāgniṃ citāgniṃ ca patitāgniṃ parityajet \|*  
*tvaṃ bhūtakṛjjagadyonistvaṃ lokaparipālakaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

63-64. Having worshipped the deity named Kravyad with flowers and grains of rice, the wise man should lighten fire according to the Vedic rites, but avoid Candala, cita or patita fires.

## \|\| 4.65 \|\|

*upasaṃhara tasmāttvamenaṃ svargaṃ nayāmṛtam \|*  
*iti kravyādamabhyarcya śarīrāhutimācaret \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. "You are the creator of creatures, the birthplace 'of the world, the protector of the people. Please therefore, consume the corpse and carry the soul to the heaven"-having thus worshipped the deity kravyada — the meat-devouring firegod, he should set fire to the corpse.

## \|\| 4.66-68 \|\|

*ardhadagdhe tathā dehe dadyādājyāhutiṃ tataḥ \|*  
*asmāttvamadhijāto 'si tvadayaṃ jāyatāṃ punaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*asau svargāya lokāya svāhetyuktvā tu nāmataḥ \|*  
*evamājyāhutiṃ dattvā tilamiśrāṃ samantrakam \|\| 67 \|\|*

*roditavyaṃ tato gāḍhamevaṃ tasya sukhaṃ bhavet \|*  
*dāhasyānantaraṃ tatra kṛtvā sañcayanikriyām \|\| 68 \|\|*

66-68. When the body is half-burnt, a quantity of clarified butter should be poured over it with the mantra: 'ou are born from it, you be born again. Let this be for heaven, Svaha He should recite this mantra announcing the name of the deceased and pour forth butter along with the gingelly seeds while the relatives weep loudly. This gives comfort to the departed soul (still wandering in the air). After cremating the corpse the rite of collection should be done there.

## \|\| 4.69-70 \|\|

*pretapiṇḍaṃ pradadyācca dāhārtiśamanaṃ khaga \|*  
*tataḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛktvā citāprasthānavīkṣakāḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

*kaniṣṭhapūrvāḥ snānārthaṃ gaccheyuḥ sūktajāpakāḥ \|*  
*tato jālasamīpe tu gatvā prakṣālya cāṃśukam \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. О bird, Preta-pinda is given in order to comfort the deceased. Then having circumambulated the pyre and casting a mournful look at it, the mourners should go for bath muttering the hymn, with the youth marching ahead.

## \|\| 4.71 \|\|

*paridhāya punastacca brṛyustaṃ puruṣaṃ prati \|*  
*udakaṃ tu kariṣyāmaḥ sacailaṃ puruṣāstataḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Then having reached water and washed cloth, they should wear the same saying for the deceased-we are taking bath.

## \|\| 4.72-73 \|\|

*kurudhvamityeva vadecchatavarṣāvare mṛte \|*  
*putrādyā vṛddhapūrvāste ekavastrāḥ śikhāṃ vinā \|\| 72 \|\|*

*prācīnāvītinaḥ sarve viśeyurmaunino jalam \|*  
*apanaḥ śośucadaghamanena pitṛdiṅmukhāḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. Then they all, each wearing one cloth only, with shaven heads, wearing the sacred thread over the right shoulder should enter water silently.

## \|\| 4.74-76 \|\|

*jalāvaghaṭṭanaṃ cava na kuryuḥ snānakārakāḥ \|*  
*tatastaṭe samāgatya śikhāṃ baddhvā ṛjūn kuśān \|\| 74 \|\|*

*dakṣiṇāgrahastayostu kṛtvātha satilaṃ jalam \|*  
*ādāyāñjalinā yāmyāṃ duḥ khī paitṛkatīrthataḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

*ekavāraṃ trivāraṃ vā daśavāramathāpi vā \|*  
*bhūmāvaśmani vā sarve kṣipeyurvāgyatāḥ khaga \|\| 76 \|\|*

74-76. The bathers should not agitate Water. Then coming out of the water to the bank, tying their Shikha (knot of hair in the centre of the head) they should take in their right hand kusas and water with the gingelly seeds and offer the same in the southern direction, pouring it from the paitrka-tirtha over the earth silently once, thrice or ten times.

## \|\| 4.77 \|\|

*tṛpyantu tṛpyatāṃ vāpi tarpayāmyupatiṣṭhatām \|*  
*pretaitadamukagotretyukteṣvevaṃ samuccaret \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. Be gratified, be gratified with this pinda О preta of such and such gotra. Let this water reach you; reciting this formula he should let the water go down.

## \|\| 4.78 \|\|

*jalāñjalau kṛte paścādvidheyaṃ dantadhāvanam \|*  
*tyajanti gotriṇaḥ sarve dināni nava kāśyapa \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. After giving the water offerings he should cleanse his teeth, О bird. The wateroffering shall be done regularly for nine days by all his kith and kin.

## \|\| 4.79 \|\|

*tata uttīryodakādvai vastrāṇi paridhāya ca \|*  
*snānavastraṃ sakṛtpīḍya viśeyuḥ śucibhūtale \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Then coming out of water, they wear the same clothes as before, while they wrinse the bathing dress which they spread over the clean earth (for drying).

## \|\| 4.80 \|\|

*aśrupātaṃ na kurvīta dattvā dāhajalāñjalim \|*  
*śleṣmāśru bāndhavairmuktaṃ preto bhuṅkte yato 'vaśaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. They should abstain from shedding tears while giving the water-offerings after cremation. But if they shed tears and vomit cough, the departed spirit consumes the same helplessly.

## \|\| 4.81 \|\|

*ato na roditavyaṃ hi kriyāḥ kāryāḥ svaśaktitaḥ \|*  
*tatasteṣūpaviṣṭeṣu purāṇajñaḥ sukṛtsvakaḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. Hence, they should neither weep nor cough. The entire ritual should be performed according to the means,

## \|\| 4.82-85 \|\|

*śokāpanodaṃ kurvīta saṃsārānityatāṃ bruvan \|*  
*mānuṣye kadalīstanbhe asāre sāramārgaṇam \|\| 82 \|\|*

*karoti yaḥ sa saṃmūḍho jalabudvadrasannibhe \|*  
*pañcadhā saṃbhṛtaḥ kāyo yadi pañcatvamāgataḥ \|\| 83 \|\|*

*karmabhiḥ svaśarīrotthaistatra kā paridevanā \|*  
*gantrī vasumatī nāśamudadhirdaivatāni ca \|\| 84 \|\|*

*phenaprakhyaḥ kathaṃ nāśaṃ martyaloko na yāsyati \|*  
*evaṃ saṃśrāvayettatra mṛduśādvalasaṃsthitān \|\| 85 \|\|*

82-85. And when they have sat quietly, a learned person well versed in the Purana should alleviate their sorrow discoursing on the temporality of time and the unsubstantial nature of the universe. He should tell them about the hollow-ness of life and if anybody searches substance inside the human body resembling the trunk of a banana plant he is a perfect fool; for it is like the water bubble. The body is constituted of five elements and if it goes back to the elements by virtue of bodily actions what is there to be lamented for? The earth, ocean and even deities are bound to be destroyed. The same fate awaits the universewhich has arisen like a bubble. How it can escape destruction? Thus, he should speak to them about the transient nature of life, while they ail sit on the soft grass in the courtyard of the cremation ground.

## \|\| 4.86-87 \|\|

*te 'yi saṃśrutya gaccheyurgṛhaṃ bālapuraḥ sarāḥ \|*  
*vidaśya rnibapatrāṇi niyatā dvāri veśmanaḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

*ācamya vahnisalilaṃ gomayaṃ gaurasarṣapān \|*  
*dūrvāpravālaṃ vṛṣabhamanyadapyatha maṅgalam \|\| 87 \|\|*

86-87. Having heard this, the mourners should return home with the youngsters walking in front. At the door of their house they should chew the margosa leaves, wrinse their mouths with water and touching cowdurig, gingelly seeds, Durva grass, coral, bull or any other auspicious thing and keeping their feet lightly on the stone should enter the house.

## \|\| 4.88-90 \|\|

*praviśeyuḥ samālabhya kṛtvāśmani padaṃ śanaiḥ \|*  
*śrautena tu vidhānena āhitāgniṃ dehadvadhaḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

*ūnadvivarṣaṃ nikhanenna kuryādudakaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*yoṣitpativratā yā syādbhartāraṃ yānugacchati \|\| 89 \|\|*

*prayoga pūrvaṃ bhartāraṃ namaskṛtyāruheccitim \|*  
*citibhraṣṭā tu yā mohātsā prājāpatyamācaret \|\| 90 \|\|*

88-90. Ahitagni should kindle the sacred fire according to the Vedic rites. He should not dig up the earth for less than two years. The wateroffering should be made (on return to the house).

A woman who has been chaste and faithful to her husband should mount on the pyre after bowing to her (deceased) husband before the funeral rites start. One who gets away from the pyre due to fainting should observe the vow named Prajapatya.

## \|\| 4.91 \|\|

*tisraḥ koṭyordhakoṭī ya yāni lomāni mānuṣe \|*  
*tāvatkālaṃ vasetsvarge bhartāraṃ yānugacchati \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. One who ascends the pyre and follows up her husband stays in heaven for a period equal to the number of hair on the body, three and a halfcrore.

## \|\| 4.92 \|\|

*vyālagrāhī yathā vyālaṃ bilāduddharate balāt \|*  
*tadvaduddhṛtya sā nārī tenaiva saha modate \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. Just as the snake-charmer takes out the snake from the hole so also she takes out her husband from hell and enjoys with him in paradise.

## \|\| 4.93 \|\|

*tatra sā bhartṛparamā stūyamānāpsarogaṇaiḥ \|*  
*krīḍate patinā sārdhaṃ yāvadindrāścaturdaśa \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. She who ascends the pyre goes to heaven. She is praised by the celestial nymphs and enjoys with her husband so long as the fourteen Indras rule in heaven successively.

## \|\| 4.94 \|\|

*brahmaghno vā kṛghno vā mittrighno vā bhavetpatiḥ \|*  
*punātyavidhavā nārī tamādāya mṛtā tu yā \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. Even if the man has killed a Brahmana or a friend or any other person of noble conduct he is purified of sins by his wife who ascends his pyre.

## \|\| 4.95 \|\|

*mṛte bhartari yā nārī samāroheddhutāśanam \|*  
*sārandhatīsamācārā svargaloke mahīyate \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. A woman who enters fire after the death of her husband prospers in the heaven like Anmdhati.

## \|\| 4.96 \|\|

*yāvaccāgnau mṛte patyau strī nātmānaṃ pradāhayet \|*  
*tāvanna mucyate sā hi strīśarīrātkathañcana \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Until and unless the woman bums herself after her husband's death she is never released from the bond other sex.

## \|\| 4.97 \|\|

*mātṛkaṃ paitṛkaṃ caiva yatra caiva pradīyate \|*  
*kulatrayaṃ punātyeṣā bhartāraṃ yānugacchati \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. A woman who follows her husband purifies the three families on her mother's side, the three families on her father's side and the three families on her husband's side.

## \|\| 4.98-100 \|\|

*ārtārte mudite hṛṣṭā proṣite malinā kṛśā \|*  
*mṛte mriyeta yā patyau sā strī jñeyā pativratā \|\| 98 \|\|*

*pṛthak citāṃ samāruhya na priyā gantumarhati \|*  
*kṣattriyādyāḥ savarṇāśca āroheyurapīha tāḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

*cāṇḍālīmavadhiṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇītaḥ samo vidhiḥ \|*  
*agarbhiṇīnāṃ sarvāsāmabālatākme(kā)nāmapi \|\| 100 \|\|*

98-100. That woman is chaste who is sad when her husband is sad, who is glad when her husband is glad and who pines when he is out of station and dies when he is dead. Common rites are enjoined for all women right from Brahman! to Candall except for those who are pregnant or who have young children.

## \|\| 4.101 \|\|

*dahanasya vidhiḥ proktaḥ sāmānyena mayā khaga \|*  
*viśeṣamapi tasyāsya kañcitkiṃ śrotumicchati \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. О bird, I have told you in general about the method of cremating the corpse. Do you want to hear something more with reference to it.

## \|\| 4.102 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*proṣite tu mṛte svāminyasthnināśamupeyuṣi \|*  
*kathaṃ dāhaḥ prakartavyastanme vada jagatpate \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. Garuda said: — If a man dies out of station and his bones too are destroyed, then how should the rite of cremation be performed. Tell me, О lord of the world!

## \|\| 4.103 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*asthīni cenna labhyante proṣitasya narasya ca \|*  
*teṣāñca hi gatisthānaṃ vidhānaṃ kathayāmyaham \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. Krsna said: — If the bones of a person who died abroad are not available, I shall tell you about the procedure of rites of his death.

## \|\| 4.104-109 \|\|

*śṛṇu tārkṣya paraṃ gopyaṃ patyurdurmaraṇeṣu yat \|*  
*laṅghanairye mṛtā jīvāṃ daṃṣṭribhiścābhighātitāḥ \|\| 104 \|\|*

*kaṇṭhagrahe vilagnānāṃ kṣīṇānāṃ tuṇḍaghātinām \|*  
*viṣāgnivṛṣaviprebhyo viṣūcyā cātmaghātakāḥ \|\| 105 \|\|*

*patanodbandhanajalairmṛtānāṃ śṛṇu saṃsthitim \|*  
*sarpavyāghraiḥ śṛṅgibhiśca upasargopalodakaiḥ \|\| 106 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇaiḥ śvāpadaiścaiva patanairvṛkṣavaidyutaiḥ \|*  
*nakhairlohairgireḥ pātairbhittipātairbhṛgostathā \|\| 107 \|\|*

*kaṭvāyāmantarikṣe ca cauracāṇḍālatastathā \|*  
*udakyāśunakīśūdrarajakādivibhūṣitāḥ \|\| 108 \|\|*

*ūrdhvocachiṣṭādharocchiṣṭobhayocchiṣṭāstu ye mṛtāḥ \|*  
*śastraghātairmṛtā ye cāsyaśvaspṛṣṭāstathaiva ca \|\| 109 \|\|*

104-109. О Garuda hear. I shall now tell you the great secret about the rite of death of those who die through serpent, tiger, toothed, headbreaking homed animals, disease, stone, water, Brahmana, dog, nails, iron; those who die of hunger, poison, fire and cholera; those who are killed by a bull, a thief, a candala, a woman in her menses, SunakI, Siidra, washerman; those who commit suicide or die by fall from a tree, hill, wall or precipice; those who die in water, on cot, in firmament, in bondage.

## \|\| 4.110-112 \|\|

*tattu durmaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ yacca jātaṃ vidhaiṃ vinā \|*  
*tena pāpena narakān bhuktvā pretatvabhāginaḥ \|\| 110 \|\|*

*na teṣāṃ kārayeddāhaṃ sūtakaṃ nodakakriyām \|*  
*na vidhānaṃ mṛtādyañca na kuryā daurdhvadaihikam \|\| 111 \|\|*

*na piṇḍadānaṃ kartavyaṃ pramādāccetkaroti hi \|*  
*nopatiṣṭhati tatsarvamantarikṣe vinaśyati \|\| 112 \|\|*

110-112. All these deaths are known as bad deaths where norite of Aurdhvadehika or pinda is performed. If the same is done by mistake it is all destroyed in the air.

## \|\| 4.113 \|\|

*atastasya sutaiḥ pauttraiḥ sapiṇḍaiḥ śubhamicchubhiḥ \|*  
*nārāyaṇabaliḥ kāryo lokagarhābhiyā khaga \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. But desiring welfare of the deceased and out of fear of the people's reproach his sons or grandsons or sapindas should perform Narayana Bali, О bird.

## \|\| 4.114 \|\|

*tathā teṣāṃ bhavecchaucaṃ nānyathetyabravīdyamaḥ \|*  
*kṛte nārāyaṇabalāvaurdhvadehikayogyatā \|\| 114 \|\|*

114\. By that the relatives of the deceased are purified. This is stated by Yama. There are no other means. When Narayana Bali is performed they become fit for aurdhvadehika.

## \|\| 4.115-116 \|\|

*tasya suddhikaraṃ karma tadbhavenna tadanyathā \|*  
*nārāyaṇabaliṃ samyak tīrthe sarvaṃ prakpayet \|\| 115 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇāgre kārayedbiprairyena pūto bhavennaraḥ \|*  
*pūrvantu tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ vipraiḥ paurāṇavaidikaiḥ \|\| 116 \|\|*

115-116. Except Narayana Bali, there is no rite for their purification. The Narayana Bali should be performed in some holy place, through the Brahmanas, preferably.in the temple of lord Krsna for the sake of purification. At first, water-offering should be mace by the Brahmanas well versed-in the Veda and the Parana.

## \|\| 4.117 \|\|

*sarvauṣadhyakṣatairmiśrairviṣṇumuddiśya tarpayet \|*  
*kāryaṃ puruṣasūktena mantrairvā vaiṣṇavairapi \|\| 117 \|\|*

117\. This should be done for lord Visnu with herbs and aksatas mingled with water by reciting the Purusa-sukta or the mantras of Visnu, facing south and remembering Visnu as well as the deceased.

## \|\| 4.118 \|\|

*dakṣiṇābhimukho bhūtvā pretaṃ viṣṇumiti smaran \|*  
*anādinidhano devaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ \|\| 118 \|\|*

118\. Visnu should be thought of as beginningless fearing conch, discus and mace, who is immortal, who has eyes as lovely as a lotus and who renders moksa to the preta.

## \|\| 4.119 \|\|

*akṣayaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ pretamokṣaprado bhava \|*  
*tarpaṇasyāvasāne syādvītarāgo vimatsaraḥ \|\| 119 \|\|*

119\. At the end of water-offering he should remain calm, desireless and free from envy.

## \|\| 4.120 \|\|

*jitendriyamanā bhūtvā śuciṣmāndharmatatparaḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā tatra prakurvīta śrāddhānyekādaśaiva tu \|\| 120 \|\|*

120\. Having controlled his sense-organs and mind and being full of devotion he should devoutly perform the eleven sraddhas.

## \|\| 4.121 \|\|

*sarvakarmavidhāne ekaikāgre samāhitaḥ \|*  
*toyavrīhiyavāndadyādgodhūmāṃśca priyaṅgavaḥ \|\| 121 \|\|*

121\. Performing all rites with concentrated mind he should give water, rice, barley, wheat and long pepper.

## \|\| 4.122 \|\|

*haviṣyānnaṃ śubhaṃ mudrāṃ chatroṣṇīṣe ca dāpayet \|*  
*dāpayetsarvasaṃsyāni kṣīraṃ kṣaudrasamānvitam \|\| 122 \|\|*

122\. He should give ablation, coins, umbrella, turban, milk with honey.

## \|\| 4.123 \|\|

*vastropānahasaṃyuktaṃ dadyādaṣṭavidhaṃ padam \|*  
*dpayetsarvapāpebhyo na kuryātpaṅktivañcanam \|\| 123 \|\|*

123\. Eight types of pada should also be given along with clothes and shoes. The should be given essentially for all sins without break.

## \|\| 4.124 \|\|

*bhūmau sthiteṣu piṇḍeṣu gandhapuṣpākṣatānvitam \|*  
*dātavyaṃ sarvaṃviprebhyo vedaśāstravidhānataḥ \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. The pindas should be placed on the earth together with incense, flowers and aksata and be gifted to Brahmanas as prescribed for the ritual,

## \|\| 4.125 \|\|

*śaṅkhe khaḍge 'tha vā tāmre tarpaṇañca pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*dhyānadhāraṇasaṃyukto jānubhyāmavanīṃ gataḥ \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. Water-offering should, be made by conch, sword,, or copper, by each separately.

## \|\| 4.126 \|\|

*ṛcā vai dāpayedarghamarghoddiṣṭaṃ pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca yamaḥ pretaśca pañcamaḥ \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. He should meditate while kneeling on the earth on Brahma, Visnu, Rudra, Yama and the deceased who is the-fifth.

## \|\| 4.127 \|\|

*pṛthakkumbhe tataḥ sthāpyāḥ pañcaratnasamanvitāḥ \|*  
*vastrayajñopavītāni pṛthaṅmudgāḥ padāni ca \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. Then in separate pitchers he should keep five jewels, cloth, sacred thread smf Mudga.

## \|\| 4.128 \|\|

*pañca śrāddhāni kurvīta devatānāṃ yathāvidhi \|*  
*jaladhārāṃ tataḥ kuryātpiṇḍepiṇḍe pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. Five sraddhas should be done for deities as prescribed in the ritual. Then on each pinda, separately, water should be poured,

## \|\| 4.129 \|\|

*śaṅkhe vā tāmrapātre vā alābhe mṛnmaye pi vā \|*  
*tilodakaṃ samādāya sarvoṣadhimasanvitam \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. In conch or in a vessel of copper and if that is not available, in an earthen pitcher he should keep water full of gingelly seeds and containing specified herbs.

## \|\| 4.130 \|\|

*tāmrapātraṃ tilaiḥ pūrṇaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ sadakṣiṇam \|*  
*dadyādbrāhmaṇamukhyāya padadānaṃ tathaica \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. The copper-vessel with gold and daksina should be given to a learned brahmana. For Yama iron with gingelly seeds should be given together with daksina.

## \|\| 4.131-132 \|\|

*yamoddeśetilāṃllauhaṃ tato dadyācca dakṣiṇām \|*  
*evaṃ viṣṇubaliṃ dattvā yathāśaktyā vidhānataḥ \|\| 131 \|\|*

*samuddharati tatkṣipraṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇa nāgadaṃśānmṛto yastu viśeṣastantu me śṛṇu \|\| 132 \|\|*

131-132. Thus by giving Visnu-bali'according to the procedure and his means he can secure the release of the deceased very quickly. There is no doubt in this. If a person dies of snake-bite, there is a specific rite to be performed for him.

## \|\| 4.133 \|\|

*suvarṇabhāraniṣpannaṃ nāgaṃ kṛtvā tathaiva gām \|*  
*viprāya dattvā vidhivatpiturānṛṇyamāpnuyāt \|\| 133 \|\|*

133\. A serpent made of gold and a milch cow.should be given to a Brahmana in the prescribed way. Thereby he becomes free from the debt which he owes to the manes.

## \|\| 4.134 \|\|

*evaṃ sarpabaliṃ dattvā sarpadoṣādvimucyate \|*  
*paścātputtalakaṃ kāryaṃ sarvoṣadhisamanvitam \|\| 134 \|\|*

134\. By giving snake-ablation thus, he becomes free from Sarpadosa.

## \|\| 4.135 \|\|

*palāśasya ca vṛntānāṃ vibhāgaṃ śṛṇu kāśyapa \|*  
*kṛṣṇājinaṃ samāstīrya kuśaiśca puruṣākṛtim \|\| 135 \|\|*

135\. Then he should make an effigy of the deceased out of Osadhis and the stalks of palasa. О bird, I shall now tell you about the details. Black-deer-skin should be spread and the effigy should be made of grass.

## \|\| 4.136 \|\|

*śatatrayeṇa ṣaṣṭyā ca vṛntaiḥ prokto 'sthisañcayaḥ \|*  
*vinyasya tāni vṛntāni aṅgeṣveṣu pṛthak pṛthak \|\| 136 \|\|*

136\. Three hundred and sixty stalks should be spread over his limbs separately.

## \|\| 4.137 \|\|

*catvāriṃśacchirobhāge grīvāyāṃ daśa vinyaset \|*  
*viṃśatyuraḥ sthale dadyādviṃśatiñjaṭhare tathā \|\| 137 \|\|*

137\. Forty represent the bones on the head, ten on neck, twenty on chest, twenty on stomach.

## \|\| 4.138 \|\|

*bāhudvaye śataṃ dadyātkaṭideśe ca viṃśatim \|*  
*ūrudvaye śatañcāpi triṃśajjaṅghādvaye nyaset \|\| 138 \|\|*

138\. A hundred on both the hands, twenty on waist, a hundred on both thighs, three hundred on both shanks.

## \|\| 4.139 \|\|

*dadyāccatuṣṭayaṃ śiśne ṣaḍ dadyādvṛṣāṇadvaye \|*  
*daśa pādāṅgulībhāge evamasthīni vinyaset \|\| 139 \|\|*

139\. Four on the generating organ, six on both scro-tums, ten on the toes.

## \|\| 4.140 \|\|

*nārikelaṃ śiraḥ sthāneṃ tumbaṃ dadyācca tāluke \|*  
*pañcaratnaṃ mukhe dadyājjihvāyāṃ kadalīphalam \|\| 140 \|\|*

140\. Coconut on the head, tumba for palate, five jewels for mouth and plantain for tongue.

## \|\| 4.141 \|\|

*antreṣu nālikaṃ tadyādvālukāṅghrāṇe eva ca \|*  
*vasāyāṃ mṛttikāṃ dadyāddharitālamanaḥ śilāḥ \|\| 141 \|\|*

141\. Nalika for intestine, valuka for nose, earth for marrow, so for all haritala and manah-sila.

## \|\| 4.142 \|\|

*pāradaṃ retasaḥ sthāne purīṣe pittalaṃ tathā \|*  
*manaḥ śilā tathā gātre tilapakvantu sandhiṣu \|\| 142 \|\|*

142\. Mercury for semen, brass for the waist, Manah-sila for all limbs, tilapakva for the joints.

## \|\| 4.143 \|\|

*yavapiṣṭaṃ yathā māṃse madhu śoṇitameva ca \|*  
*keśeṣu ca jaṭājūṭaṃ tvacāyāñca mṛgatvacam \|\| 143 \|\|*

143\. Yava-pista for meat, honey for blood, jata jiisa for hair and deer-skin for skin.

## \|\| 4.144-146 \|\|

*karṇayostālapatrañca stanayoścaiva guñjikāḥ \|*  
*nāsāyāṃ śatapatrañca kamalaṃ nābhimaṇḍale \|\| 144 \|\|*

*vṛntākaṃ vṛṣaṇadvandve liṅge syādgṛñjanaṃ śubham \|*  
*ghṛtaṃ nābhyāṃ pradeyaṃ syātkau pīne ca trapusmṛtam \|\| 145 \|\|*

*mauktikaṃ stanayormūrdhni kuṅkumeva vilepanam \|*  
*karpūrāgurudhūpaiśca śubhairmālyaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ \|\| 146 \|\|*

144-146. Talapatra for ears, Gufijas for breasts, satapatra for nose, lotus for navel, Brnjala for scrotums, Grnjana for penis, clarified butter for the navel and trapu for kaupma, pearls (mauktika) for the breasts, saffron, camphor, aguru, incense for the forehead and scented garlands for the neck.

## \|\| 4.147-148 \|\|

*paridhānaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃhṛdaye caiva vinyaset \|*  
*ṛddhivṛddhī bhujau dvau ca cakṣurbhyāñca kapardakam \|\| 147 \|\|*

*danteṣu dāḍimībījānyaṅgulīṣu ca campakam \|*  
*sindūraṃ netrakoṇe ca tāmbūlādyupahārakam \|\| 148 \|\|*

147-148. Thus the garment and the silk-thread on the chest, rddhi-vrddhi for the arms, Kapardikas for eyes, seeds of pomegranate for teeth, campaka for fingers, sindhura for eye-corners and Tambula for the gift.

## \|\| 4.149 \|\|

*sarvauṣadiyutaṃ pretaṃ kṛtvā pūjāṃ yathoditām \|*  
*sāgnike cāpi vidhinā yajñapātraṃ nyasyetkramāt \|\| 149 \|\|*

149\. Thus the effigy of the deceased should be made of Osadhis. Worship should be performed as prescribed, before the sacrificial fire with the sacrificial vessels if the deceased person kept fire in the house.

## \|\| 4.150 \|\|

*striyaḥ punantu ma śira imaṃ me varuṇena ca \|*  
*pretasya pāvanaṃ kṛtvā śālagrāmaśilodakaiḥ \|\| 150 \|\|*

150\. Reciting the mantra Sriyah punantu me sirah or imam me Varuna he should sprinkle water made pure by contact with the Shalagrama gild. He should gift a gentle milch cow to a Brahmana for the propitiation of god Visnu.

## \|\| 4.151 \|\|

*viṣṇumuddiśya dātavyā suśīlā gauḥ payasvinī \|*  
*tilā lauhaṃ hiraṇyañca karpāsaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā \|\| 151 \|\|*

151\. Gingelly seeds, iron, gold, cotton, salt, seven grains, earth, — all these are called purificatory. Then a vessel full of gingelly seeds should be given as also the gift of pada.

## \|\| 4.153 \|\|

*kartavyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ śrāddhaṃ pretamuktyarthamātmanaḥ \|*  
*pretamokṣaṃ tataḥ kuryāddhṛdi viṣṇuṃ prakalpyaca \|\| 153 \|\|*

153\. A Vaisnava Shraddha should be performed for the release of the deceased from pretahood. Thus thinking about Visnu in the heart one can get the deceased released immediately from Pretahood.

## \|\| 4.154 \|\|

*evaṃ puttalakaṃ kṛtvā dāhayedvidhipūrvakam \|*  
*tacchruddhaye tu saṃskartā putrādirniṣkṛtiṃ caret \|\| 154 \|\|*

154\. Having made the effigy of the deceased in this way, he should bum the same in the prescribed way. As for the purification of the deceased he should observe atonement.

## \|\| 4.155 \|\|

*trīnkṛcchrānṣaḍdvādaśa ca tathā pañcadaśāpi ca \|*  
*prāyaścittanimittānusāreṇa vipravatsmṛtaḥ \|\| 155 \|\|*

155\. Three krcchras should be performed, or six, or twelve, or fifteen, for the sake of atonement as prescribed for a Brahmana.

## \|\| 4.156 \|\|

*aśaktau gohiraṇyādi pratyāmnāyaṃ caredapi \|*  
*ātmano 'nadhikāritve śuddhimevaṃ caredvundhaḥ \|\| 156 \|\|*

156\. If one cannot do so, one should donate a milch cow or gold. If he cannot do that, he should perform a sort of purification.

## \|\| 4.157 \|\|

*aśuddhena tu yaddattamuddiśyāśuddhimeva ca \|*  
*nopatiṣṭhati tatsarvamantarikṣe vinaśyati \|\| 157 \|\|*

157\. That which is performed without purification, does not stay. It is all destroyed in the air.

## \|\| 4.158-161 \|\|

*śuddhiṃ sampādya kartavyaṃ dahanādyaurdhvadehikam \|*  
*akṛtvā niṣkṛtiṃ yastu kurute dahanādikam \|\| 158 \|\|*

*matipūrvamamatyā ca kramāttaniṣkṛtiṃ śṛṇu \|*  
*kṛtvāgnimudakaṃ snānaṃ sparśanaṃ vahanaṃ kathām \|\| 159 \|\|*

*rajjucchedāśrupātañca taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati \|*  
*eṣāmanyatamaṃ pretaṃ yo vahettu dehata vā \|\| 160 \|\|*

*kaṭodakakriyāṃ kṛtvā kṛcchra sāntapanaṃ caret \|*  
*nimitte laghuni svalpaṃ mahanmahati kalpayet \|\| 161 \|\|*

158-161. After purification the rite of aurdhvadehika should be performed for the deceased.

If a person performs obsequies without doing atonement consciously or unconsciously in favour of the deceased, he incurs sin. I shall now tell you how he can eliminate the sins of omission and commission. If one has set fire to the corpse or taken bath in water, touched Or carried the corpse, cut the ropes binding the corpse, or shed tears after cremation or delivered funeral ovation, he is purified by taptakrcchra. Anyone of those who carry the corpse or cremate the dead may do katodaka- Kriya and perform krcchra santapana. A little should be done if the cause be small and much should be done if the cause be big.

## \|\| 4.162 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kṛcchrasya taptakṛcchrasya tathā sāntapanasya ca \|*  
*lakṣaṇaṃ brūhi me svāmiṃstrayāṇāmapi suvrata \|\| 162 \|\|*

162\. Garuda said: — О lord observing good vows, please explain to me the details of krcchra, Tapta-krcchra and Santapana.

## \|\| 4.163-164 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*tryahaṃ prātastryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryahamadyādayācitam \|*  
*upavāsastryahañcaiva eṣa kṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ \|\| 163 \|\|*

*taptakṣīraghṛtāmbūnāmekaikaṃ pratyahaṃ pibet \|*  
*ekarātropavāsaśca taptakṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ \|\| 164 \|\|*

163-164. Shri Krsna said: — О bird, hear. If he eats for three days in the morning and for three days in the evening and observes fast for three days, the Vrata is called Krcchra. If he drinks hot milk, clarified butter and once water in a day and observes fast for one night, the Vrata is called Taptakrcchra.

## \|\| 4.165 \|\|

*gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam \|*  
*jagdhvā pare 'hnyupavasetkṛcchraṃ sāntapanañcaran \|\| 165 \|\|*

165\. For one day, each one should take cow's urine, cow-dung, milk, curd, clarified butter and water purified by the holy grass and observe fast the next day, the Vrata is called' Krcchra santapana.

## \|\| 4.166 \|\|

*mayā te 'yaṃ samākhyāto durmṛtasya vidhiḥ khaga \|*  
*tadā mṛtaṃ vijānīyāddīpanirvāṇamāgataḥ \|\| 166 \|\|*

166\. О bird, thus I have told you the rites to be performed for durmrta. He is known mrta if they perform for him the rite of dipa-

## \|\| 4.167 \|\|

*agnidāhaṃ tataḥ kuryātsūtakañca dinatrayam \|*  
*daśāhaṃ gartapiṇāḍañca kartavyaṃ pretapūrvakam \|\| 167 \|\|*

167\. Then they should perform cremation and observe impurity for three days. Then for ten days the rite of garta-pinda should be performed for the deceased.

## \|\| 4.168 \|\|

*evaṃ vidhiṃ tataḥ kuryāttataḥ pretaśca muktibhāk \|*  
*mṛtabhrāntyā pratikṛteḥ kṛte dāhe sa vai yadi \|\| 168 \|\|*

168\. All these rites should be performed for the person who died abroad. However, if the person concerned returns home, after his effigy has been burnt thinking him to he dead.

## \|\| 4.169 \|\|

*āyāti tena kartavyaṃ majjanaṃ ghṛkuṇḍake \|*  
*jātakarmādisaṃskārāḥ kartavyāḥ punareva tu \|\| 169 \|\|*

169\. Then the person thought to be dead should take bath in the ghrtakunda and go through all the rites from Jatakaraman onward.

## \|\| 4.170-171 \|\|

*ūḍhāmeva svakāṃ bhāryāmudvahedvidhivatpumān \|*  
*varṣe pañcadaśe pakṣin dvādaśe vā gate sati \|\| 170 \|\|*

*ajñātasya proṣitasya kṛtvā pratikṛtiṃ dahet \|*  
*rajasvalāsūtikayorviśeṣaṃ maraṇe śṛṇu \|\| 171 \|\|*

170-171. He should marry his wife again, О bird. If one remains out of station for fifteen or twelve years and his whereabouts remain unknown, his effigy should be burnt. Now hear specially about the death of rajasvala and sutika.

## \|\| 4.172 \|\|

*sūtikāyāṃ mṛtāyāntu evaṃ kurvanti yājñikāḥ \|*  
*kumbhe salilamādāya pañcagavyaṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 172 \|\|*

172\. When a sutika is dead, the performers of obsequy do thus: Water is taken in the pitcher. So also pancagavya.

## \|\| 4.173 \|\|

*puṇyābhirabhimantryāpo vācā śuddhiṃ labhettataḥ \|*  
*śatasūrpodakenādau snāpayitvā yathāvidhi \|\| 173 \|\|*

173\. Then water is purified by reciting punyaha mantras. Then the bathing is done by the water cooled by hundred winnowing fans.

## \|\| 4.174 \|\|

*tenaiva snāpayitvā tu dāhaṃ kuryātsvageśvara \|*  
*pañcabhiḥ snāpayitvā tu gavyaiḥ pretāṃ rajasvalām \|\| 174 \|\|*

174\. When the bathing is done they should start the rite of cremation, О lord of birds. The deceased rajasvala is bathed by pancagavya.

## \|\| 4.175 \|\|

*vastrāntarākṛtiṃ kṛtvā dāhayedvidhipūrvakam \|*  
*mṛtasya pañcake dāhavidhiṃ vacmi śṛṇuṣva me \|\| 175 \|\|*

175\. Her worn clothes are changed for the fresh ones and the cremation is done as prescribed. Now hear, I shall tell you about the rites to be performed for those who die during the pancaka.

## \|\| 4.176-177 \|\|

*ādau kṛtvā dhaniṣṭhārdhametannakṣatrapañcakam \|*  
*revatyantaṃ sadā dūṣyamaśubhaṃ sarvadā bhavet \|\| 176 \|\|*

*dāhastatra na kartavyo viṣādaḥ sarvajantuṣu \|*  
*na jalaṃ dīyate teṣu aśubhaṃ sarvadā bhavet \|\| 177 \|\|*

176-177. The five constellations from Dhanistha to Revatl are always inauspicious. Cremation should not be done on these days. It is tortuous to all creatures. Water-offering should also be avoided, for it is inauspicious on these days.

## \|\| 4.178 \|\|

*pañcakānantaraṃ sarvaṃ kāryaṃ kartavyamanyathā \|*  
*puttrāṇāṃ gotriṇāṃ tasya santāpo 'pyupajāyate \|\| 178 \|\|*

178\. All rites should be performed after the Pancaka is over. If done otherwise, sons or close relatives are affected thereby.

## \|\| 4.179 \|\|

*gṛhe hānirbhavatyeva ṛkṣeṣveṣu mṛtasya ca \|*  
*atha vā ṛkṣamadye ca dāhastu vidhipūrvakaḥ \|\| 179 \|\|*

179\. If one dies during, these constellations and his cremation is performed during this period his family suffers tremendously.

## \|\| 4.180 \|\|

*kriyate mānuṣāṇāntu sa vā āhutipūrvakaḥ \|*  
*viprairvidhirataḥ kāryo mantraistu vidhipūrvakam \|\| 180 \|\|*

180\. Cremation is harmless during the period if the following injunctions are observed strictly. Brahmanas should perform sacrifice along with ahuti mantras.

## \|\| 4.181-182 \|\|

*śavasthānasamīpe tu kṣeptavyāḥ puttalāstataḥ \|*  
*darbhakḷptāstu catvāra ṛkṣamantrābhimantritāḥ \|\| 181 \|\|*

*tato dāhaḥ prakartavyastaiśca puttalakaiḥ saha \|*  
*sūtakānte tadā puttraiḥ kāryaṃ śāntikapauṣṭikam \|\| 182 \|\|*

181-182. Close to the place of corpse, four effigies made of grass should be raised and then thrown into the pyre while the Brahmanas recite the mantras of constellations. Then after completing the days ef impurity, the rite santikapaustika should be performed by the sons of the deceased.

## \|\| 4.183 \|\|

*pañcakeṣu mṛto yo 'sau na satiṃ labhate naraḥ \|*  
*tilān gāñca suvarṇañca tamuddiśya ghṛtaṃ dadet \|\| 183 \|\|*

183\. For the deceased to obtain heaven, cow, gold, butter and gingelly seeds should be gifted.

## \|\| 4.184 \|\|

*viprāṇāṃ dāpayeddānaṃ sarvavighnavināśanam \|*  
*bhojanopānahaucchattraṃ hemamudrā ca vāsasī \|\| 184 \|\|*

184\. To the Brahmanas should be given gifts to quell all obstacles. Food, shoes, umbrella, gold-coin and clothes should be given as gifts to get release from sin.

## \|\| 4.185 \|\|

*dakṣiṇā dīyate vipre pātakasya pramocanaḥ \|*  
*mayāte 'yaṃ samākhyāto vidhiḥ pañcaharaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*saṃyaminyāṃ yathāyānaṃ yathāvarṣaṃ mṛtakriyā \|\| 185 \|\|*

185\. Thus I have told you all about the rites for the deceased, how the dead goes to Yama's abode and what are the post-death rites over a year for him.

## Chapter 5

## \|\| 5.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ dagdhvā naraṃ pretaṃ snātvā kṛtvā tilodakam \|*  
*agrataḥ strījano gacchedvrajeyuḥ pṛṣṭhato narāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said: — Thus having burnt the corpse, having taken bath and offered sesame-water, they should return home, with woman- folk proceeding ahead.

## \|\| 5.2 \|\|

*prāśayennimbapatrāṇi rudanto nāmapūrvakam \|*  
*vidhātavyaṃ cācamanaṃ pāṣāṇopari saṃsthite \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In the way, they should consume leaves of margosa tree and rinse their mouths while sitting on stone.

## \|\| 5.3 \|\|

*te praviśya gṛhaṃ sarve sutādyāśca sapiṇḍakāḥ \|*  
*bhaveyurdaśarātraṃ vai yata āśaucakaṃ khaga \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Then having entered the house, the sons and relatives of the deceased should observe asauca for ten nights.

## \|\| 5.4-5 \|\|

*krītalabdhāśanāḥ sarve svapeyuste pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*akṣāralavaṇānnāḥ syurnimajjeyuśca te tryaham \|\| 4 \|\|*

*amāṃsabhojanāścādhaḥ śayīranbrahmacāriṇaḥ \|*  
*parasparaṃ na saṃspṛṣṭā dānādhyayanavarjitāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Having brought food from the market, they should take meals. During the days of impurity, they should not take saline and pungent food. They should take bath thrice a day and abstain from taking meat. The couples should observe celibacy and sleep on the ground separately without touching each other. They should abstain from study as well charity.

## \|\| 5.6-7 \|\|

*malināścādhomukhāśca dīnā bhogavivarjitāḥ \|*  
*aṅgasaṃvāhanaṃ keśamārjanaṃ varjayanti te \|\| 6 \|\|*

*mṛnmaye patraje vāpi bhuñjīraṃste ca bhājane \|*  
*uvāsantu te kuryurekāhamatha vā tryaham \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. They should wear dirty clothes, move with downward faces and avoid all pleasures. They should leave shampooing of body, hair, etc. They should eat in earthen pots or in those made of dry leaves. They should observe fast on the cremation day or for more number of days during impurity.

## \|\| 5.8 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*āśaucina iti proktamāśaucasya ca vai prabho \|*  
*lakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ kiyatkālaṃ bhāvyaṃ vā tadyutairnaraiḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Garuda said: — Sir, you have just stated that they should observe asauca. Now please tell us what is this asauca? What is its period?

## \|\| 5.9 \|\|

*śṛkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*apanodyantvidaṃ kālādibhirāśu niṣedhakṛt \|*  
*piṇḍādhyayanadānādeḥ puṅgato 'tiśayo hi tat \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. 10$ri Krsna said:-The ten-day-period of asauca is prescribed for the relatives of the deceased on the father's side. This commences from the hour of birth, also with a view to obtain purity.

## \|\| 5.11 \|\|

*janmanyekodakānāntutrirātrācchuddhiriṣyate \|*  
*śāvasya śeṣācchudhyanti tryahādudakadāyinaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Since the time of birth, purity can be had after three nights. After death, purity is attained after three days.

## \|\| 5.12 \|\|

*ādantajananatsadya ā caulānnaiśikī smṛtā \|*  
*trirātramā vratādeśāddaśarātramataḥ param \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After teething immediately, after caula overnight, after Vratadesa for three nights, and for ten nights in other cases.

## \|\| 5.13 \|\|

*āśaucaṃ te samākhyātaṃ saṃkṣepātprakṛtaṃ bruve \|*  
*jalaṃ tridivamākāśe sthāpyaṃ kṣīrañca mṛnmaye \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus I have told you about asauca. Now I shall tell you in brief how purity is achievable. Water in the open and milk in an earthen pot should be kept for three days.

## \|\| 5.14 \|\|

*atra snāhi pibātreti mantreṇānena kāśyapa \|*  
*kāṣṭhatraye guṇairbaddhe prītyai rātrau catuṣpathe \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. 'Bathe here, drink here', this mantra should be recited, О Kasyapa. The sticks bound with a string should be placed on the crossroads in the night in order to propitiate the dead person.

## \|\| 5.15 \|\|

*prathame 'hni tṛtīye vā saptame navame tathā \|*  
*asthisaṃcayanaṃ kāryaṃ dine tadgotrajaiḥ saha \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Bones should be gathered on the first, third, seventh or the ninth day by the relatives.

## \|\| 5.16 \|\|

*tadūrdhvamaṅgasaṃsparśaḥ sapiṇḍānāṃ vidhīyate \|*  
*yogyāḥ sarvakriyāṇāṃ ca samānasalilāstathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The relatives should touch the body of one another as prescribed, perform actions as far as they can and share in the common water- offerings.

## \|\| 5.17 \|\|

*pretapiṇḍaṃ bahirdadyāddarbhamātravivarjitam \|*  
*prāgudīcyāṃ caruṃ kṛtvā snātvā prayatamānasaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Preta-pinda (an offering to the mane) should be given outside, leaving aside grass. Caru should be given in the north-eastern direction, after bath and with calm mind.

## \|\| 5.18 \|\|

*bhūmāvasaṃskṛtānāṃ ca saṃskṛtānāṃ kuśeṣu ca \|*  
*navabhirdivasaiḥ piṇḍānnava dadyātsamāhitaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. For the non-purified on the ground, for the purified on the kusa grass; he should offer nine pindas in nine days i.e. one pinda on each day.

## \|\| 5.19 \|\|

*daśamaṃ piṇḍamutsṛjya rātriśeṣe śucirbhavet \|*  
*asagotraḥ sagotro vā yadi strī yati vā pumān \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After giving the tenth pinda, one becomes pure after the night expires. One may be asagotra (indirect relation), sagotra (direct relation), man or woman.

## \|\| 5.20 \|\|

*prathame 'hani yo dadyātsa daśāhaṃ samāpayet \|*  
*śālinā saktubhirvāpi śākairvāpyatha nirvapet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. That which is given on the fiist day should be continued for ten days. It may be rice, saktu or saka.

## \|\| 5.21 \|\|

*prathame 'hani yaddravyaṃ tadeva syāddaśāhikam \|*  
*yāvadāśaucamekaikasyāñjalerdānamucyate \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. So far as there is asauca, a gift of one anjali should be made, i.e. so much of gift as joined hands can contain.

## \|\| 5.22 \|\|

*yadvā yasmindine dānaṃ tasmiṃstaddinasaṃkhyayā \|*  
*daśāhe 'ñjalayaḥ pakṣinpañcāśadantime \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Or else, the number should be equal to the number of days on which the gift is given, О bird, and the number on the last day should be fifty-five.

## \|\| 5.23 \|\|

*dvivṛddhyā vā bhavetpakṣinnañjalīnāṃ śataṃ punaḥ \|*  
*yadāhi tryahamāśaucaṃ tadā vāñjalayo daśa \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Or these doubled shall be hundred anjahs. When there is asauca for three days anjalis shall be ten.

## \|\| 5.24 \|\|

*trayo 'ñjalaya evaṃ tu prathame 'hanivai tadā \|*  
*catvārastu dvitīye 'hni tṛtīye syustrayastathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. These shall be three on the first day, four on the second day and three on the third day.

## \|\| 5.25 \|\|

*śatāñjali yadā pakṣinnādye triṃśattadāhani \|*  
*catvāriṃśaddvitīye 'hni triṃśadahni tṛtīyake \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. If one hundred anjalis are given, then thirty should be given on the first day, forty on the second day and thirty on the third day.

## \|\| 5.26 \|\|

*evaṃ jalasyāñjalayo vibhājyāḥ pakṣayordvayoḥ \|*  
*sarveṣu pitṛkāryeṣu putro mukyo 'dhikāravān \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О bird, thus the water-offering is divided. For performing the rites, of the manes, the son. is the proper person.

## \|\| 5.27 \|\|

*piṇḍaprasekastūṣṇīñca puṣpadhūpādikaṃ tathā \|*  
*daśame 'hani samprāpte snānaṃ grāmādvahiścaret \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Pinda-praseka shall be done in silence and with flowers, incense, etc. On the tenth day, bath should be taken outside the village.

## \|\| 5.28-29 \|\|

*tatra tyājyāni vāsāṃsi keśaśmaśrunakhāni ca \|*  
*vipraḥ śudhyatyapaḥ spṛṣṭvā kṣattriyo vāhanaṃ tathā \|\| 28 \|\|*

*vaiśyaḥ pratodaṃ raśmīnvā śūdro yaṣṭiṃ kṛtakriyaḥ \|*  
*mṛtādalpavayobhiśca sapiṇḍaiḥ parivāpanam \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Then they should leave their old clothes, hair, beard and nails. Brahmana is purified by, touching water, Ksatriya by touching Vehicle, Vaisya by touching a goad (or a whip) or reins, and sudra by touching stick after performing the rites. The relatives, younger in age, should undergo shave.

## \|\| 5.30 \|\|

*kāryantu ṣoḍaśī ṣaḍbhiḥ piṇḍairdaśabhiraiva ca \|*  
*prathamā malinā hyetairādaśāhaṃ mṛterbhavet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. SodasI should be performed by offering sixteen pindas.

## \|\| 5.31 \|\|

*dināni daśa yānṣiṇḍānkurvantyatra sutādayaḥ \|*  
*pratyahaṃ te vibhajyante caturbhāgaiḥ khagottama \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О bird, the pindas given for ten daysone pinda on each day regularly, by sons, etc. are divided, each one into four.

## \|\| 5.32 \|\|

*bhāgadvayena dehaḥ syāttṛtīyena yamānugāḥ \|*  
*tṛpyanti hi caturthena svayamapyupajīvati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Two parts for the body, the third one for Yama's servants and by the fourth he lives.

## \|\| 5.33 \|\|

*ahorātraistu navabhirdeho niṣpattimāpnuyāt \|*  
*śirastvādyena piṇḍena pretasya kriyate tathā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. During nine days and nights, the body is formed. By offering the first pinda, the head of the Preta is formed.

## \|\| 5.34 \|\|

*dvitīyena tu karṇākṣināsikaṃ tu samāsataḥ \|*  
*galāṃsabhujavakṣaśca tṛtīyena tathā kramāt \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. By the second, ears, eyes and nose. By the third, neck, shoulder, arms and chest.

## \|\| 5.35 \|\|

*caturthena ca piṇḍena nābhiliṅgagudaṃ tathā \|*  
*jānujaṅghaṃ tathā pādau pañcamena tu sarvadā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. By the fourth pinda, navel and private parts. By the fifth thighs, calves.

## \|\| 5.36 \|\|

*sarvamarmāṇi ṣaṣṭhena saptamena tu nāḍayaḥ \|*  
*dantalomānyaṣṭamena vīryantu navamena ca \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. By the sixth all vital organs, by the seventh nerves, by the eighth teeth and hair, by the ninth semen.

## \|\| 5.37 \|\|

*daśamena tu pūrṇatvaṃ tṛptatā kṣudviparyayaḥ \|*  
*madhyamāṃ ṣoḍaśīṃ vacmi vainateya śṛṇuṣva me \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. By the tenth fulness is achieved, hunger is satisfied. Now, I shall tell you, О Garuda, about Madhya-sodasl.

## \|\| 5.38 \|\|

*viṣṇavādiviṣṇuparyantānyekādaśa tathā khaga \|*  
*śrāddhāni pañca devānāmityeṣāṃ madhyaṣīḍaśī \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The eleven beginning with Visnu and ending with Visnu. Then five sraddhas for the deities. This is called madhya-sodagi, О bird.

## \|\| 5.39 \|\|

*nimittaṃ durmatiṃ kṛtvā yadi nārāyaṇo baliḥ \|*  
*ekādaśāhe kartavyo vṛṣotsargo 'pi tatra vai \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. If Narayana-bali is to be performed for the atonement of bad death, it should be done on the eleventh day. Vrsogsarga rite is also prescribed for such a deceased.

## \|\| 5.40 \|\|

*ekādaśāhe pretasya yasyātsṛjyeta no vṛṣaḥ \|*  
*pretatvaṃ susthiraṃ tasya dattaiḥ śrāddhaśatairapi \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. If the bull is not released on the eleventh day in favour of the deceased he remains ghost eternally, even if hundred sraddhas are performed for his sake.

## \|\| 5.41 \|\|

*akṛtvā yadvṛṣotsargaṃ kṛtaṃ vai piṇḍapātanam \|*  
*niṣphalaṃ sakalaṃ vidyātpramītāya na tadbhavet \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. If the rite of pinda-palana is done without Vrsotasarga then all rites will go in vain.

## \|\| 5.42 \|\|

*vṛṣotsargādṛte nānyatkiñcidasti mahītale \|*  
*putraḥ patnyatha dauhitraḥ pitā vā duhitātha vā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. No rite is superior to Vrsotsarga. It is prescribed for the deceased and performed by his son, wife, grandson, father or daughter.

## \|\| 5.43 \|\|

*mṛtādanantaraṃ tasya dhruvaṃ kāryo vṛṣotsavaḥ \|*  
*caturvatsatarīyukto yasyotsṛjyeta vā vṛṣaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Vrsotsarga is performed only after death. If the bull is released along with four she-calves duly decorated the deceased does not turn into ghost.

## \|\| 5.44 \|\|

*alaṅkṛto vidhānena pretatvaṃ tasya no bhavet \|*  
*ekādaśe 'hni samprāpte vṛṣālābho bhavedyadi \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. If the actual bull is not available on the eleventh day, a bull of darbhas and pista should be made.

## \|\| 5.45-46 \|\|

*darbhaiḥ piṣṭaistu sampādya taṃ vṛṣaṃ mocayedvudhaḥ \|*  
*vṛṣotsarjanavelāyāṃ vṛṣābhāva (lābha) kathañcana \|\| 45 \|\|*

*mṛttikābhistu darbhairvā vṛṣaṃ kṛtvā vimocayet \|*  
*yadiṣṭaṃ jīvatastasya dadyādekādaśe 'hani \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. If that too is not available, a bull made of mud and grass should be released- If an alive bull is desired to be released, it should be done on the eleventh day.

## \|\| 5.47 \|\|

*mṛtamuddiśya dātavyaṃ śayyādhenvādikaṃ tathā \|*  
*viprānbahūn bhojayīta pretasya kṣudviśāntaye \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. They should give bed, cow, etc. to the Brahmanas in favour of the deceased. Brahmanas should be fed to satisfy the hunger of the departed soul.

## \|\| 5.48 \|\|

*tṛtīyāṃ ṣoḍaśīṃ vacmi vainateya śṛṇuṣva tām \|*  
*dvādaśa pratimāsyāni ādyaṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ tathā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Now, О Vainateya, I shall tell you about the third SodasL Twelve for the twelve months and the half yearly one, and sapindikarana. These are called third SodsI,

## \|\| 5.49 \|\|

*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ caiva tṛtīyā ṣoḍaśī matā \|*  
*dvādaśāhe tripakṣe ca ṣaṇmāse māsike 'bdike \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. On the twelfth day for the three fortnights, half-yearly, monthly and yearly,

## \|\| 5.50 \|\|

*tṛtīyāṃ ṣoḍaśīmenāṃ vadanti matabhedataḥ \|*  
*yasyaitā ni na dattāni pretaśrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. This too is called third sodas! according to another opinion. If these sixteen preta- are not given.

## \|\| 5.51 \|\|

*piśācatvaṃ sthiraṃ tasya dattaiḥ śrāddhaśatairapi \|*  
*ekādaśe dvādaśe vā dine ādyaṃ prakīrtitam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Then his ghosthood becomes permanent, even if hundred sraddhas are performed for his benefit.

## \|\| 5.52-53 \|\|

*māsādau pratimāsañca śuddhaṃ mṛtatithau khaga \|*  
*ekenāhnā tribhirvāpi hīneṣu vinatāsuta \|\| 52 \|\|*

*māsaṣaṇmāsavarṣeṣu tripakṣeṣu bhavanti hi \|*  
*śrāddhānyathasyātsāpiṇḍyaṃ pūrṇe varṣe tadardhake \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. Sraddhas are performed on the elevent or twelfth day, at the beginning of the month, every month, on the date of death, one day or three days, О son of Vinata, every month, every six months, yearly, or on three fortnights. The rite of sapinda is performed after a year or six months.

## \|\| 5.54 \|\|

*tripakṣe 'bhyudaye vāpi dvādaśāhe 'tha vā nṛṇām \|*  
*ānantyātkuladharmāṇāṃ puṃsāñcaivāyuṣaḥ kṣayāt \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Or after three fortnights, at the rise of a planet or within twelve days, for, the family traditions are many and human life is short.

## \|\| 5.55 \|\|

*asthiratvāccharīrasya dvādaśāhe praśasyate \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇeṣvevaṃ vidhiṃ pakṣīndra me śṛṇu \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Human life is transitory. It is better therefore to perform the rite of sapindl within twelve days. О bird, now, I shall tell you about the method of spindlkarana.

## \|\| 5.56 \|\|

*ekoddiṣṭavidhānena kāryaṃ tadapi kāśyapa \|*  
*tilagandhodakairyuktaṃ kuryātpātracatuṣṭayam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О Kasyapa, it should be done by the process of ekoddista. Four vessels should be made- ready along with sesame, incense and water.

## \|\| 5.57 \|\|

*pātraṃ pretasya tatraikaṃ pitryaṃ pātratrayaṃ tathā \|*  
*secayetpitṛpātreṣu pretapātraṃ khaga triṣu \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Out of these one is the vessel.for the preta; the other three are for the manes. О bird, the performer should sprinkle the three Pitrpatras with the water from the pretapatra.

## \|\| 5.58 \|\|

*caturo nirvapetpiṇḍānpūrvanteṣu samāpayet \|*  
*tataḥ prabhṛti vai pretaḥ pitṛsāmānyamaśnute \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Then four pindas should be offered and libations be made. Thereafter, the preta becomes one with the manes.

## \|\| 5.59 \|\|

*tataḥ pitṛtvamāpanne tasminprete khageśvara \|*  
*śrāddhadharmairaśeṣaistu tatpūrvānarcayetpitṝn \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Then, О bird, when the preta has become one with the manes, the manes should be worshipped along with him.

## \|\| 5.60 \|\|

*ekacityārohaṇe ca ekāhni maraṇe tathā \|*  
*sāpiṇḍyantu striyā nāsti mṛte bhartuḥ striyo bhavet \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. If the woman ascends the pyre of her husband on the same day, then no separate sapindya is prescribed for her.

## \|\| 5.61 \|\|

*pākaikyamatha kālaikyaṃ kartraikyañca bhavetkhaga \|*  
*śrāddhādau saha dāhe ca patipatnyorna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. If husband and wife are cremated together, there is one рака, one kala and one kartr О bird, you need not entertain any doubt in this.

## \|\| 5.62 \|\|

*bharturmṛtatitheranyatithau citimathāruhet \|*  
*tāṃmṛtāhani tu samprāpte pṛthak piṇḍena yojayet \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. If she ascends the pyre on a different date, she should be offered a separate pinda on the separate date of death.

## \|\| 5.63 \|\|

*pratyabdañca yugapattu samāpayet \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Every year, nine sraddhas should be performed together.

## \|\| 5.64 \|\|

*yasya saṃvatsarādarvāk sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ bhavet \|*  
*māsikañcodakumbhañca deyaṃ tasyāpi vatsaram \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. If the sapindlkarana is done before a year has passed, then a monthly offering of a water jar should be made for one year.

## \|\| 5.65 \|\|

*navaśrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍatvaṃ śrāddhānyapi ca ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*ekenaiva tu kāryāṇi saṃvibhaktadhaneṣvapi \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Nine sraddhas, sapindlkarana and sixteen sraddha should be done by the eldest son, even though the inheritor? have divided property.

## \|\| 5.66 \|\|

*pitāmahībhiḥ sāpiṇḍyaṃ tathā mātāmahaiḥ saha \|*  
*uktaṃ bhartrāpi sāpiṇḍyaṃ striyā veṣayabhedataḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Women's sapindya may be done with the paternal grandmother, or maternal grandmother or with husband, according to the tradition prevailing in different regions.

## \|\| 5.67 \|\|

*navaśrāddhasya te kālaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu kāśyapa \|*  
*maraṇāhni mṛtisthāne śrāddhaṃ pakṣinprakalpayet \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Now, I shall tell you about the time of nine sraddhas. О Kasyapa bird. The first sraddha should be performed on. the day of death near the corpse.

## \|\| 5.68 \|\|

*dvitīyañca tato mārge viśrāmo yatra kāritaḥ \|*  
*tataḥ sañcayanasthāne tṛtīyaṃ śrāddhamucyate \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The second on the same date on the way to cemetery where the mourners take rest; the third on the day at the place when or where they collect the bones.

## \|\| 5.69 \|\|

*pañcame saptame tadvadaṣṭame navame tathā \|*  
*daśamaikādaśe caiva nava śrāddhāni vai khaga \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The rest of the sraddhas on the fifth, seventh, eighth ninth, tenth and eleventh day. These are nine sraddhas, О bird.

## \|\| 5.70 \|\|

*śrāddhāni nava caitāni tṛtīyā ṣoḍaśī smṛtā \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭavidhānena kāryāṇi manujaistathā \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The third one is called sodas! wherein the procedure is the same as in ekoddista.

## \|\| 5.71 \|\|

*prathame 'hni tṛtīye vā pañcame saptame tathā \|*  
*navamaikādaśe caiva navaśrāddhaṃ prakīrtitam \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. The six sraddhas are performed on the first, third, fifth, seventh, ninth and eleventh day.

## \|\| 5.72 \|\|

*ucyante ṣaḍimānīha nava syurapi yāgetaḥ \|*  
*uktāni te mayā tāni ṛṣīṇāṃ matabhedataḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. These six are told but they may be nine by addition. I have stated six according to different traditions of the sages.

## \|\| 5.73 \|\|

*rūḍhipakṣo mamābhīṣṭo yogaḥ kaiścidiheṣyate \|*  
*ādye dvitīye dātavyastathaivaikaṃ pavitrakam \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. I have preferred tradition but practice is not altogether ruled out. On each of the first and second days a sacred thread should be gifted.

## \|\| 5.74 \|\|

*pretāya piṇḍo dātavyo bhuktavatsu dvijātiṣu \|*  
*praśrastatrābhiraṇyeti yajamānadvijanmanā \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. So when the brahmanas are feasting, pinda should be offered to the preta. Then the host and the brahmana should indulge in question-answer dialogue.

## \|\| 5.75 \|\|

*akṣayyamamukasyeti vaktavyaṃ viratau tathā \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭaṃ me nibodha cetthamāvatsaraṃ smṛtam \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. When the same is completed then "such and such (N. of the preta) should become perennial" is to be uttered. The ekoddista is perennial, please note.

## \|\| 5.76 \|\|

*sapiṇḍīkaraṇādūrdhvaṃ yāni śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭavidhānena caredvā pārvaṇādṛte \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The sixteen sraddhas after sapindikarana should be ekoddista except those performed on festival days.

## \|\| 5.77 \|\|

*pratyabdaṃ yo yathā kuryāttathā kuryātsa tānyapi \|*  
*ekādaśe dvādaśe 'hni preto bhuṅkte dinadvayam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. He who performs them annually should perform them in that manner. The preta eats twice in a day on the eleventh and the twelfth day.

## \|\| 5.78 \|\|

*yoṣitaḥ puruṣasyāpi piṇḍaṃ preteti nirvapet \|*  
*sāpiṇḍye tu kṛte tasya pretaśabdo nivartate \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. To each-husband and wife-а pinda should be offered. Once sapsndya is completed, the pretahood ends.

## \|\| 5.79 \|\|

*dīpadānaṃ prakartavyamāvarṣantu gṛhādbahiḥ \|*  
*annaṃ dīpo jalaṃ vastramanyadvādīyate ca yat \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. For one year, a lamp should be kept burning at the door of the house. Food, lamp, water, cloth, or whatever else is given, the same gives satisfaction to the preta.

## \|\| 5.80 \|\|

*tṛptidaṃ pretaśabdena sapiṇḍīkaraṇāvadhi \|*  
*abdakṛtyaṃ mayoktante samāsādvinatāsuta \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. In brief, I have told you the period of sapindikarana as one year.

## \|\| 5.81-82 \|\|

*vaivasvatagṛhe yānaṃ yathā tattu nibodhameḥ \|*  
*trayodaśe 'hni śravaṇākarmaṇonantarantu saḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

*tvaggṛhītāhivattārkṣya gṛhīto yamakiṅkaraiḥ \|*  
*tasminmārge vrajatyeko gṛhīta iva markaṭaḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

81-82. Now, I shall tell you how people go to Yama's abode. On (the thirteenth day, after sravana-krama, he is captured by Yama's attendents, just as you capture the serpents by neck, О Garuda. He is led in the way like the caught-up monkey.

## \|\| 5.83 \|\|

*vāyvagrasārivadrūpaṃ dehamanyatprapadyate \|*  
*tatpiṇḍajaṃ pātanārthamanyattu pitṛsambhavam \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. The body attains a form like air. born out of pinda it assumes a shape different from the one born from the womb of the mother.

## \|\| 5.84-89 \|\|

*tatpramāṇavayo 'vasthāsaṃsthānāṃ pragbhavo yathā \|*  
*ṣaḍaśīti sahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ pramāṇataḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

*adhvāntarāliko jñeyo yamamānuṣalokayoḥ \|*  
*sādhikārdhakrośayutaṃ yojanānāṃ śatadvayam \|\| 85 \|\|*

*catvāriṃ śattathā sapta pratyahaṃ yāti tatra saḥ \|*  
*aṣṭācatvāriṃśatā ca traiṃśatā divasairiti \|\| 86 \|\|*

*vaivasvatapuraṃ yāti kṛṣyamāṇo yamānugaiḥ \|*  
*evaṃ krameṇa yātabye mārge pāparataistu yat \|\| 87 \|\|*

*jāyate saprapañcaṃ tacchṛṇu tvamaruṇānuja \|*  
*trayodaśadine dattaḥ pāśairbaddhvātidāruṇaiḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

*yamasyāṅkuśahasto vai bhṛkuṭīkuṭilānanaḥ \|*  
*daṇḍaprahārasambhrāntaḥ kṛṣyate dakṣiṇāṃ diśam \|\| 89 \|\|*

84-89. According to age, state, etc it differs. Eightysix thousand yojanas long is the distance between the mortal's and Yama's worlds. Two hundred and a half yojanas and forty-seven krosas he covers daily. So in fortyeight and thirty days he reaches Yama's city, being dragged by Yama's servants. Thus when the way is being covered by the sinner, what happens then, you hear, О Garuda. For thirteen days, he is tied with fierce nooses, by Yama's servants with goad in hand, with looks frowning and the hand engaged in striking with sticks. Thus, he is dragged towards the south.

## \|\| 5.90 \|\|

*kuśakaṇṭakavalmīkaśaṅkupāṣāṇakarkaśe \|*  
*tathā pradīptajvalane kvacicchvabhraśatotkaṭe \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. There, on the fierce way, full of thorny grass, thorns, anthills, sharp stones, which contains burning fires as well as hundreds of charms.

## \|\| 5.91 \|\|

*pradīptādityatapte ca dahyamānaḥ sadaṃśake \|*  
*kṛṣyate yamadūtaiśca śivāvannādabhīṣaṇaiḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Which is heated by a burning sun, is infested by dogs or flies, which echoes with the howling sounds of jackals, he is dragged by Yama's messengers.

## \|\| 5.92 \|\|

*prayātiḥ dāruṇe mārge pāpakarmā yamālaye \|*  
*kalevare dahyamāne mahāntaṃ kṣayamṛcchati \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. The sinner perishes when his body is burnt in Yama's abode.

## \|\| 5.93 \|\|

*bhakṣyamāṇe tathaivāṅge bhidyamāne ca dāruṇam \|*  
*chidyamāne cirataraṃ janturduḥ khamavāpnute \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. Thus as his body is eaten, torn asunder, pierced, he suffers immensely.

## \|\| 5.94 \|\|

*svena karmavi pākena dehāntaragato 'pi san \|*  
*purāṇi ṣoḍaśāmuṣmanmārge tāni ca me śṛṇu \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. On account of his own deeds, though he is dead. There are sixteen cities in this way. Now hear about them.

## \|\| 5.95 \|\|

*yāmyaṃ sauripuraṃ nagedvabhavanaṃ gandharvaśailāgamau krauñcaṃ krūrapuraṃ vicitrabhavanaṃ bahvāpadaṃ duḥ khadam \|*  
*nānākrandapuraṃ sutaptabhavanaṃ raudraṃ payovarṣaṇaṃ śītāḍhyaṃ bahubhītiṣoḍaśapurāṇyetānyadṛṣṭani te \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. Yamya, Sauripura, Nagendrabhavana, Gandharva, sailagama Kraunca, Krurapura, Vicitra-bhavana, Bahvapada, Duhkhada, Nana- krandapura, Sutaptabhavana, Raudra, Payo- varsana, ^Itadhya, and Bahubhiti these are the sixteen cities not seen by you.

## \|\| 5.96 \|\|

*tatra yāmya puraṃ gacchanputraputreti ca bruvan \|*  
*hāheti krandate nityaṃ svakṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ smaran \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Then he goes to Yamya saying, 'O son, О son' he cries: painfully remembering his own misdeeds.

## \|\| 5.97 \|\|

*aṣṭādaśodine tārkṣya tatpura prāpnuyādasau \|*  
*puṣpabhadrā nadī yatra nyagrodhaḥ priyadarśanaḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. He reaches that city on the eighteenth day, О Tarkhya. There is Puspabhadra river and a good-looking tree.

## \|\| 5.98 \|\|

*viśrāmecchāṃ karotyatra kārayanti na te bhaṭāḥ \|*  
*kṣitau dattaṃ sutaistasya snehādvā kṛpayā tathā \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. He wants to take rest there but Yama's servants do-not bid him do so.

## \|\| 5.99 \|\|

*māsikaṃ piṇḍamaśnāti tataḥ sauripuraṃ vrajet \|*  
*vrajannevaṃ pralapate mudgarāhatipīḍitaḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Here, on the earth, whatever is given by his sons every month, rice-ball, etc. due to affection or kindness he eats the same and then he goes to Saurlpura. As he goes he cries being beaten with clubs.

## \|\| 5.100 \|\|

*jalāśayo naiva kṛto mayā tadā manuṣyatṛptyai paśupakṣitṛptaya \|*  
*gotṛptihetorna ca gocaraḥ kṛtaḥ śarīra he nistara yattvayā kṛtam \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. "I did not construct a tank for satiating thirsty people, beasts and birds. I did not make a pasture for satisfying cattle. Now, О body, suffer for whatever you have done".

## \|\| 5.101 \|\|

*tatra nāmnā tu rājāsau jaṅgamaḥ kāmarūpadhṛk \|*  
*bhayāt taddarśanājjātādbhuṅkte piṇḍaṃ sa śaṅkitaḥ \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. King Jangama rules in that city who can assume any shape at will. Out of fear of that king's frowns he eats the rice-ball.

## \|\| 5.102 \|\|

*tripakṣe jalasaṃyuktaṃ kṣitau dattaṃ tato vrajet \|*  
*vrajannevaṃ pralapate khaḍgāghātaprapīḍitaḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. Whatever is offered to him in the three fortnights together with the oblation of water, he eats and drinks. He is then struck with clubs.

## \|\| 5.103 \|\|

*na nityadānaṃ na gavāhnikaṃ kṛtaṃ pustaṃ ca dattaṃ na hi vedaśāstrayoḥ \|*  
*purāṇadṛṣṭo na hi sevito 'dhvā śarīra he nistara yattvayā kṛtam \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. Neither daily gifts were given nor pastures made, nor the sacred books such as the Vedas and Dharama-sastras offered in charity nor Puranas heard. Now, О body, you suffer for your acts of omission.

## \|\| 5.104 \|\|

*nagendranagaraṃ gatvā bhuktvā cānnaṃ tathāvidham \|*  
*māsi dvitīye yaddattaṃ bāndhavaistu tato vrajet \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. Having reached Nagendra nagar and having eaten the rice-balls offerd by the relatives in the second month, he moves further.

## \|\| 5.105 \|\|

*vrajannevaṃ pralapate kṛpāṇatsarutāḍitaḥ \|*  
*parādhānamabhūtsarvaṃmama mūrkhaśiromaṇeḥ \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. As he goes onward he laments being struck with the sword. "A fool that I am, I have lost everything to others.

## \|\| 5.106 \|\|

*mahatā puṇyayogena mānuṣyaṃ labdhavānaham \|*  
*tṛtīye māsi samprāpte gandharvanagare śubham \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. I obtained manhood by virtue of accumulated merit". Then he reaches Gandha- madana in the third month.

## \|\| 5.107 \|\|

*tṛtīyamāsikaṃ piṇḍaṃ tatra bhuktvā brajatyasau \|*  
*vrajannevaṃ vilapate tadagreṇāhataḥ pathi \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. There he eats the rice-ball. Then he goes further. As he goes further he laments being beaten by Yama's emissary

## \|\| 5.108 \|\|

*mayā na dattaṃ na hutaṃ hutāśane tapo na taptaṃ himaśailagahvare \|*  
*na sevitaṃ gāṅgamaho mahājalaṃ śarīra he nistara yattvayā kṛtam \|\| 108 \|\|*

108\. I neither gave gifts nor poured oblations into fire nor undertook penance in the caves of the Himalayas nor bathed into Ganga's water. Hence, О body, suffer for whatever you have done.

## \|\| 5.109 \|\|

*turye śailāgamaṃ māsi prāpnuyāttatra varṣaṇam \|*  
*tasyopari bhavetpakṣinpāṣāṇānāṃ nirantaram \|\| 109 \|\|*

109\. He reaches sailagama in the fourth month. There atones rain upon him continuously, О bird.

## \|\| 5.110 \|\|

*caturthamāsikaṃ śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā tatra prasarpati \|*  
*sa patanneva vilapanpāṣāṇādyatipīḍitaḥ \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. Having eaten the fourth monthly oblation he moves. As he moves, he is struck again with the stones by the messengers.

## \|\| 5.111 \|\|

*na jñānamārgo na ca yogamārgo na karmamārgo na ca bhaktimārgaḥ \|*  
*na sādhusaṅgātkimapi śrutaṃ mayā śarīra he nistara yattvayā kṛtam \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. I followed neither Jnanamarga nor Yoga- marga nor Karma-marga nor Bhaktimarga. Nor did I remain in the company of saints. О body, now suffer for whatever you have done."

## \|\| 5.112 \|\|

*tataḥ krūrapura māsi pañcame yāti kāśyapa \|*  
*bhuvi dattaṃ piṇḍajalaṃ bhuktvā krūrapuraṃ vrajet \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. Then m the fifth month, he goes to Krurapura, О Kasyapa. Having eaten the riceball and drunk water by his relatives he goes to Krurapura.

## \|\| 5.113 \|\|

*vrajannevaṃ vilapate paṭṭiśaiḥ pātitaḥ pathi \|*  
*hā mātarhāpitarbhrātaḥ sutā hā hā mama striyaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. As he goes, he laments, being struck with spears. "O mother, О father, О brother, О sons, О my wives.

## \|\| 5.114 \|\|

*yuṣmābhirnopadiṣṭo 'hamavasthāṃ prāpta īdṛśīm \|*  
*evaṃ lālapyamānaṃ te yamadūtā vadantihi \|\| 114 \|\|*

114\. I was not advised by you. I have reached this state/' As he laments thus Yama's messengers say to him.

## \|\| 5.115 \|\|

*kva mātā kva pitā mūḍha kva jāyā kva sutaḥ suhṛt \|*  
*svakarmopārjite bhuṅkṣvaṃ mūrkha yātāściraṃ pathi \|\| 115 \|\|*

115\. "Where is the mother? Where the father? О fool, Where is the wife? Where the sons? Where the friends? О fool. Now suffer the results of your evil activities. All sinners go by this ancient path".

## \|\| 5.116 \|\|

*jānāsi śambalamalaṃ balamadhvagānāṃ no 'śambalaḥ prayatate paralokagatyai \|*  
*gantavyamasti tava niścitameva tena mārgeṇa yena na bhavet krayavikrayo 'pi \|\| 116 \|\|*

116\. As you know, the provisions are the only strength of the traveller. So none without provision can venture to travel over this path. For the path is such where no selling and purchasing system exists.

## \|\| 5.117 \|\|

*ūnaṣāṇmāsike krauñce bhuktvā piṇḍantu sodakam \|*  
*ghaṭīmātrantu viśramya vicitranagaraṃ vrajet \|\| 117 \|\|*

117\. In less than six months he reaches Kraunca and having eaten the rice-ball and drunk water he rests there for a while and then leaves for Vicitra nagara.

## \|\| 5.118 \|\|

*vrajannevaṃ vilapate śūlāgreṇa vidāritaḥ \|\| 118 \|\|*

118\. As he goes, he laments being struck off with spearheads by Yama's emissaries.

## \|\| 5.119 \|\|

*kutra yāmi na hi gāmi jīvitaṃ hā mṛtasya maraṇaṃ punarna vai \|*  
*itthameva vilapan prayātyasau yātanārhadhṛtavigrahaḥ pati \|\| 119 \|\|*

119\. "Where do I go, О my life, a dead man cannot revive again. Thus lamenting he goes in the way, his body being captured by tortures".

## \|\| 5.120 \|\|

*vicitranagare tatra vicitro nāma pārithivaḥ \|*  
*tatra ṣaṇmāsapiṇḍena tṛptaḥ san vrajate puraḥ \|\| 120 \|\|*

120\. Vicitranagara is ruled by king Vicitra. Being satiated with the six monthly rice-ball he moves further.

## \|\| 5.121 \|\|

*vrajannevaṃ vilapate prāsāgreṇa prapīḍitaḥ \|\| 121 \|\|*

121\. As he goes, he laments being struck with the club.

## \|\| 5.122 \|\|

*mātā bhrātā pitā putraḥ ko 'pi me vartate na vā \|*  
*yo māmuddharate pāpaṃ patantaṃ duḥ khasāgare \|\| 122 \|\|*

122\. "Do my brother, son, parents or any one exist who may take out a sinner like me falling in the ocean of distress.

## \|\| 5.123 \|\|

*vrajatastatra mārge tu tatra vaitaraṇī śubhā \|*  
*śatayojanavistīrṇā pūyaśoṇitasaṃkulā \|\| 123 \|\|*

123\. As he goes, there in the way is Vaitarani hundred yojanas long, full of pus and blood.

## \|\| 5.124 \|\|

*āyāti tatra dṛśyante nāvikā dhīvarādayaḥ \|*  
*te vadanti pradattā gauryadi vaitaraṇī tvayā \|*  
*nāvamenāṃ samāroha sukenottara vai nadīm \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. There are seen boatmen, fishermen. They say, if he has gifted Vaitarani cow, he can get into the boat and cross the river.

## \|\| 5.125 \|\|

*tatra yena pradattā gauḥ sa sukhenaiva tāṃ taret \|*  
*adāyī tatra ghṛṣyeta karagrāhantu nāvikaiḥ \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. But if he has not gifted the cow, he is brushed aside by the boatmen.

## \|\| 5.126 \|\|

*ukhaiḥ kākairbakolūkaistīkṣṇatuṇḍairvitudyate \|*  
*manujānāṃ hitaṃ dānamante vaitaraṇī khaga \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. He is bit by the birds with sharp beaks, like crows, cranes, ants, etc., О bird, the gift of cow at the time of death serves for the relief of the deceased.

## \|\| 5.127 \|\|

*dattā pāpaṃ dahet sarvaṃ mama lokantu sā nayet \|*  
*maptame māsi samprāpte puraṃ bahvāpadaṃ mṛtaḥ \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. The gift of cow destroys all sins and affords all facilities. The deceased goes to Bhavapada in the seventh month.

## \|\| 5.128 \|\|

*vrajettu sodakaṃ bhuktvā piṇḍaṃ vai saptamāsikam \|*  
*vrajannevaṃ vilapate parighāhatipīḍitaḥ \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. After partaking the seventh-monthly offering made by the relatives he reaches Bahva- pada, where hetaments being struck with the iron club.

## \|\| 5.129 \|\|

*na dattaṃ na hutaṃ taptaṃ na snātaṃ na kṛtaṃ hitam \|*  
*yādṛśaṃ caritaṃ karma mūḍhātman bhuṅkṣva tādṛśam \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. Neither you offered gifts nor gave oblations nor performed penance nor undertook bath nor rendered a good act. So О foolish creature, now suffer for whatever act you have done;

## \|\| 5.130 \|\|

*māsyaṣṭame duḥ khade tu pare bhuktvātha sodakam \|*  
*piṇḍaṃ prayātsayau tārkṣya nānākrandapuraṃ tataḥ \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. In the eighth month, after eating the riceball and drunk water, he goes to Nanakrandapura, О Tarkhya.

## \|\| 5.131-132 \|\|

*prayāṇe ca pravadate musalāghātapīḍitaḥ \|*  
*kva jāyācaṭulaiścāṭupaṭubhirvacanairmama \|\| 131 \|\|*

*bhojanaṃ bhallabhallībhirmusalaiśca kva māraṇam \|*  
*navame māsi dattaṃ vai nānākrandapure tataḥ \|\| 132 \|\|*

131-132. Being struck with pebbles there, he says: "Where are those sweet dishes offered with sweet tit-tat by my wife, and where this beating with, the pestles." In the ninth month, he eats his rice-ball in Nanakrandapura.

## \|\| 5.133 \|\|

*piṇḍamaśrāti karuṇaṃ nānākrandān karotyapi \|*  
*daśame māsi dattaṃ vai sutaptabhavanaṃ tataḥ \|\| 133 \|\|*

133\. Then he laments in many ways. In the tenth month he reaches Sutaptabhavana.

## \|\| 5.134-135 \|\|

*sarannevaṃ vilapate halāhatihataḥ pathi \|*  
*kva sūnupeśalakaraiḥ pādasaṃvāhanaṃ mama \|\| 134 \|\|*

*kva dūtavajrapratimakairmatpadakarṣaṇam \|*  
*daśame māsi piṇḍādi tatra bhuktvā prasarpati \|\| 135 \|\|*

134-135. Thus moving, he laments in the way being struck with ploughs. 'Where the massaging of my feet by my sons and where the dragging of my legs by the thunderhke hands of messengers". He then eats the rice- given in the tenth month together with the offerings of wateroblation.

## \|\| 5.136 \|\|

*māse caikādaśe pūrṇe puraṃ raudraṃ sa gacchati \|*  
*gacchanneva vilapate yathā pṛṣṭhe prapīḍitaḥ \|\| 136 \|\|*

136\. At the end of the eleventh month he goes to, Raudrapura. As he goes to Raudrapura he laments being struck in the back.

## \|\| 5.137 \|\|

*kvāhaṃ satūlīśayane parivartan kṣaṇe kṣaṇe \|*  
*bhaṭahastabhraṣṭayaṣṭikṛṣṭapṛṣṭhaḥ kva vā punaḥ \|\| 137 \|\|*

137\. "Where the cotton-bed I used to roll in and where this rigid place where I am beaten by the messengers".

## \|\| 5.138 \|\|

*kṣitau dattañca piṇḍādi bhuktvā tatra tato vrajet \|*  
*payovarṣaṇamityetannāmakaṃ puramaṇḍaja \|\| 138 \|\|*

138\. There he eats the rice-ball etc. given by his relatives and then he goes to the city called Payo-Varsana.

## \|\| 5.139 \|\|

*vrajannevaṃ vilapate kuṭhārairmūrdhni tāḍitaḥ \|*  
*kva bhṛtyakomalakarairgandhatailāvasecanam \|\| 139 \|\|*

139\. While going he laments, as he is struck at the head. "Where the servants who with soft hands used to massage oil in my head.

## \|\| 5.140 \|\|

*kva kīnāśānugaiḥ krodhātkuṭhāraiḥ śirasi vyathā \|*  
*ūnābdikañca yacchrāddhaṃ tatra bhuṅkte suduḥ khitaḥ \|\| 140 \|\|*

140\. Where my head being struck by Yama's servants". At the end of the year he eats the annual sraddha offered by his relatives.

## \|\| 5.141 \|\|

*saṃpūrṇe tu tato varṣe śītāḍhyaṃ nagaraṃ vrajet \|*  
*gacchannevaṃ churikayā cchinnajihvastu roditi \|\| 141 \|\|*

141\. Then at the end of the year he goes to srotriya. He weeps while his tongue is cut out with the knife.

## \|\| 5.142 \|\|

*priyālāpaiḥ kva ca sasamadhuratvasya varṇanam \|*  
*uktamātre 'sipatrādijihvācchedaḥ kva caiva hi \|\| 142 \|\|*

142\. Where that sweet feeling of rasa and where this cutting of my tongue with the knife."

## \|\| 5.143 \|\|

*vārṣikaṃ piṇḍadānādi bhuktvā tatra prasarpati \|*  
*bahubhītikaraṃ tattat piṇḍajaṃ devamāsthitaḥ \|\| 143 \|\|*

143\. Having partaken of the annual sraddha there, he goes to Bahubhuti. He has assumed by this time a body composed of the essence of his merit accruing from the gift of rice.

## \|\| 5.144 \|\|

*prakāśayati pāppānamātmānañca vinindati \|*  
*yoṣidapyevametasmin mārge vai paridevati \|\| 144 \|\|*

144\. The sinner abuses his own self. He also abuses his wife and thus laments in the way.

## \|\| 5.145 \|\|

*tato yāmyaṃ nātidūre nagaraṃ sa hi gacchati \|*  
*catvāriṃśadyojanāni caturyuktānivistṛtam \|\| 145 \|\|*

145\. Then he reaches the city of Yama nearby. It is forty yojanas in measurement.

## \|\| 5.146 \|\|

*trayodaśa pratīhārāḥ śravaṇā nāma tatra vai \|*  
*śravaṇākarmatastuṣyantyanyathā krodhamāpnuyuḥ \|\| 146 \|\|*

146\. There are fourteen door-keepers called Sravanas. They are pleased with the Sravana karma performed by his relatives or else they get angry.

## \|\| 5.147-149 \|\|

*tatastatrāśu raktākṣaṃ bhinnāñjanacayopamam \|*  
*mṛtyukālāntakādīnāṃ madhye paśyati vai yamam \|\| 147 \|\|*

*daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ bhṛkuṭīdāruṇākṛtim \|*  
*virūpairbhoṣaṇairvaktrairvṛtaṃ vyādhiśataiḥ prabhum \|\| 148 \|\|*

*daṇḍāsaktamahābāhuṃ pāśahastaṃ subhairavam \|*  
*tannirdiṣṭāṃ tato janturgatiṃ yāti śubhāśubhām \|\| 149 \|\|*

147-149. There very soon among Death, Time, etc. he sees Yama with red eyes, looking fierce and dark like a heap of collyrium, with fierce jaws and frowning fiercely, chosen as their lord by many ugly, fierce-faced hundreds of diseases, possessing an iron-rod in his hand and also a noose. The creature goes either to good or to bad state as directed by him.

## \|\| 5.150 \|\|

*pāpī pāpāṃ gatiṃ yāti yathā te kathitaṃ purā \|*  
*chatropānahadātāro ye ca veśmapradāyakāḥ \|\| 150 \|\|*

150\. A sinner goes-to bad state, as I have told you before.

## \|\| 5.151 \|\|

*ye tu puṇyakṛtastatra te paśyanti yamaṃ tadā \|*  
*saumyākṛtiṃ kuṇḍalinaṃ maulimantaṃ dhṛtaśriyam \|\| 151 \|\|*

151\. Those who give umbrella, shoes and shelter sec Yama as gentle-faced with earrings and a shining crest.

## \|\| 5.152 \|\|

*ekādaśe dvādaśe hi ṣaṇmāse ābdike tathā \|*  
*viprān bahūn bhojayet tatra yanmahatī kṣudhā \|\| 152 \|\|*

152\. In the Shraddha of the eleventh or twelfth month many brahmanas should be feasted because then the deceased is very hungry.

## \|\| 5.153 \|\|

*jīvan putrakalatrādipradiṣṭamitaraiḥ khaga \|*  
*yo na sādhayati svārthamevaṃ paścāddhikhidyate \|\| 153 \|\|*

153\. He who does not perform the needful while alive being instructed by sons, wife and others suffers afterwards.

## \|\| 5.154 \|\|

*etat te sarvamākhyātaṃ saṃyaminyāṃ yathāgati \|*  
*proktamāvarṣakṛtyaṃ te kimanyacchrotumicchasi \|\| 154 \|\|*

154\. Thus I have told you how one goes to Yama's abode; what shall be done in his favour for one year by his relatives. Do you wish to ask anything more.

## Chapter 6

## \|\| 6.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*api sādhanayuktasya tīrthadānaratasya ca \|*  
*akṛte tu vṛṣotsarge paralokagatirna hi \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — Even if he has the means and is fond of pilgrimage and offering gifts he cannot reach the splendid world if he does not perform a Vrsotsarga.

## \|\| 6.2-3 \|\|

*tasmāt kṛṣṇa vṛṣotsargaḥ kartavya iti me śrutam \|*  
*kiṃ phalaṃ vṛṣayajñasya purā kena kṛto hare \|\| 2 \|\|*

*anaḍvān kīdṛśaḥ proktaḥ kasmin kāle viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*ko vidhistasya nirdiṣṭaḥ sarvaṃ me kṛpayā vada \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Hence, Vrsotsarga shall be performed, О Krsna, this I have heard. Now tell me what type of bull shall be chosen. At what time shall the bull be released. What is the method? Please tell me everything.

## \|\| 6.4 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*itihāsaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ pravakṣyāmi khageśvara \|*  
*brahmaputreṇa yat proktaṃ rājānaṃ vīravāhanam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Sri Krsna said: — О lord of birds, I shall tell you the past history which was told to king VIravahana by Narada, the son of Brahma.

## \|\| 6.5 \|\|

*virādhanagare rājā vīravāhananāmakaḥ \|*  
*dharmātmā satyasandhaśca vadānyo vipratuṣṭikṛt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. A king, Viravahana by name, reigned in the Viradha city. He was pious, truthful, generous as well as heroic. He used to satiate swimming in the ocean, the Brahmanas with sumptuous meals.

## \|\| 6.6 \|\|

*sa kadācidvanaṃ vīro mahātmākheṭakaṃ gataḥ \|*  
*kiñcit praṣṭumanāstārkṣya vasiṣṭhasyāśramaṃ yayau \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. With a mind to satisfy bis curiosity he went to Vasistha's hermitage.

## \|\| 6.7 \|\|

*namaskṛtya muniṃ tatra kṛtāsanaparigrahaḥ \|*  
*paśrayāvanato rājā papraccha ṛṣisaṃsadi \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He saw the sage surrounded by the munis, bowed to him putting his problems before him.

## \|\| 6.8-9 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*mune mayā kṛto dharmo yathāśakti prayatnataḥ \|*  
*yamasya śāsanaṃ śrutvā bibhemi nitarāṃ hṛdi \|\| 8 \|\|*

*yamañca yamadūtāṃśca nirayān ghoradarśanān \|*  
*na paśyāmi mahābhāga tathā vada dayānidhe \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. О sage, I have performed religious duties to the best of my capacity. I am terribly afraid in my heart, О ocean of mercy. Now tell me the way that I may not see Yama and his fierce-looking messengers.

## \|\| 6.10 \|\|

*vasiṣṭha uvāca \|*  
*dharmā bahuvidhā rājan varṇyante śāstrakovidaiḥ \|*  
*sūkṣmatvānna vijānanti karmamārgavimohitāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Vasistha said: — О king, the sages who are well versed in scriptures have prescribed many religious rites. But due to their subtlety people attached to Karma-yoga do not know them.

## \|\| 6.11 \|\|

*dānaṃ tīrthaṃ tapo yajñāḥ saṃnyāsaḥ paitṛko mahaḥ \|*
