*na ca mitraṃ hitastādṛgyaḥ kuryādaurdhvadaihikam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. I had no progeny, no friends, no kins and no friends who could perform my funeral rites.

## \|\| 9.47-49 \|\|

*pretatvaṃ susthiraṃ tena mama jātaṃ nṛpottama \|*  
*ekādaśaṃ tripakṣañca ṣāṇmāsikamathābdikam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*pratimāsyāni cānyā ni hyevaṃ śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*yasyaitāni na dīyante pretaśrāddhāni bhūpate \|\| 48 \|\|*

*pretatvaṃ susthiraṃ tasyaḥ dattaiḥ śrāddhaśatairapi \|*  
*evaṃ jñātvā mahārāja pretatvāduddharasva mām \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. For that reason, my ghosthood became perpetual. Now hear, О king, there are sixteen principal Sraddhas — the eleventh day, monthly, half-quarterly, six monthly, etc. The dead who do not receive these Sraddhas turn into gliosts perpetually even though hundreds of other Sraddhas are performed for them. Knowing this, О king, you perform Sraddhas and release me from ghosthood.

## \|\| 9.50 \|\|

*varṇānāñcaiva sarveṣāṃ rājā bandhurihocyate \|*  
*tanmāṃ tāraya rājendra maṇiratnaṃ dadāmi te \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. For, a king is the relative of all castes. Therefore, I request you to release me from ghosthood. I give you the best of jewels in my custody.

## \|\| 9.51 \|\|

*yathā mama śubhāvāptirbhavennṛpavarottama \|*  
*tathā kāryaṃ mahīpāla dayāṃ kṛtvā mayi prabho \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Taking pity on me, О king, you act in such a way that I may be released from ghosthood.

## \|\| 9.52-53 \|\|

*sapiṇḍairvā sagotrairvā niṣṭurairna kṛto hime \|*  
*vṛṣotsargastato duṣṭaṃ pretatvaṃ prāptavānaham \|\| 52 \|\|*

*kṣuttṛṣāviṣṭadehaśca bhakṣyaṃ pānaṃ na cāpnuyām \|*  
*ato vikṛtireṣā vai kṛśātvādiramāṃsakā \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. (Moreover, I may sell you why I became a ghost). My kins being disinterested in me did not perform Vrsotsarga in my favour. Hence, I was transformed into ghost. I feel constantly hungry and thirsty, for want of food and drink. Hence, this deformity and leanness without food.

## \|\| 9.54 \|\|

*kṣuttṛḍjanyaṃ mahāduḥ khamanubhavāmi punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*akalyāṇaṃ hi pretatvaṃ vṛṣotsargaṃ vinā kṛtam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. I suffer pain due to hunger and thirst, for this ghost-hood is malevolent.

## \|\| 9.55 \|\|

*tasmādrājandayāsindho prārthayāmi tavāgrataḥ \|*  
*rājovāca \|*  
*vartate matkule preta iti jñeyaṃ kathaṃ naraiḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. I, therefore, pray you, О king, for; you are the ocean, of pity.

## \|\| 9.56 \|\|

*tanmamācakṣva hi preta pretatvānmucyate katham \|*  
*preta uvāca \|*  
*liṅgena pīḍayā preto 'numātavyo naraiḥ sadā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Tell me, О ghost, how one is released from; ghosthood. The people can infer about the present of a ghost at home by signs and tormentations.

## \|\| 9.57 \|\|

*vakṣyāmi pīḍāstā rājanyā vai pretakṛtā bhuvi \|*  
*ṛtuḥ syādaphalaḥ strīṇāṃ yadā vaṃśo na vardhate \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. I shall now tell you about tormentations given by the ghost to the people on earth. When the menses of the women go in vain and the family does not multiply.

## \|\| 9.58-62 \|\|

*miyante cālpavayasaḥ sā pīḍā petasambhavā \|*  
*akasmādvṛttiharaṇamapratiṣṭhā janeṣu vai \|\| 58 \|\|*

*akasmādgahadāhaḥ syātsā pīḍā pretasambhavā \|*  
*svagehe kalaho nityaṃ syācca mithyābhiśaṃsanapa \|\| 59 \|\|*

*gajayakṣmādisambhūtiḥ sā pīḍā pretasambhavā \|*  
*api svayaṃ dhanaṃ muktaṃ prayatnādanave pathi \|\| 60 \|\|*

*naiva labhyeta naśyetaḥ sā pīḍā pretasaṃmavā \|*  
*suvṛṣṭau kṛṣināśaḥ syādvāṇijyādvṛttināśanam \|\| 61 \|\|*

*kalatraṃ patikūlaṃ syātsā pīḍā pretasambhavā \|*  
*evantu pīḍayā rājanpretajñānaṃ bhavennṛṇām \|\| 62 \|\|*

58-62. When men die young it is tormentation by ghost. Sudden loss in profession and insult among the people, sudden setting of house on fire, permanent quarrelling at home, false praise, suffering from consumption and foul diseases are due to tormentation by ghost. When the money invested in the customary way bears no fruit but is destroyed, it is due to tormentation by ghost. When crops are ruined even after proper rains, when commerce is unsuccessful, when wife creates tension, it is due to tormentation by ghosts. By these tormentations, О king, people can know about the presence of ghost at home.

## \|\| 9.63 \|\|

*vṛṣotsargo yadi bhavetpretatvānmucyate tadā \|*  
*tasmānnapa tvamapyevaṃ vṛṣotsargaṃ kuru prabho \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. If Vrsotsarga is done properly, one is released from ghosthood. Hence, О king, I pray you to perform Vrsotsarga in my favour.

## \|\| 9.64 \|\|

*māmuddiśya nṛpe 'pyatrādhikāro 'tyanukampayā \|*  
*rājaputro hataḥ kaścinmayaivāptastato mayā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. I authorize you to perform this rite for me. Once, I killed a certain prince and as a result thereof I became ghost.

## \|\| 9.65 \|\|

*kuruṣva tvaṃ gṛhītvā me taddhanena vṛṣotsavam \|*  
*kārtikyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vā'śvayuṅmadhye 'thavā nṛpa \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. О king please take this precious'gem froi i me and with the money received through it, perform Vrsotsarga for me on the fullmoon day in the month of Karttika or Asvayuj or on the day conjunct with Revatl. For perfoming Vrsotsarga you invite the learned brahmanas and set up fire as prescribed in the Shastras.

## \|\| 9.66-67 \|\|

*revatīyuktadivase kṛṣīṣṭhā me vṛṣotsavam \|*  
*puṇyānviprānsamāhūya vahniṃ sthāpya vidhānataḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*mantrairhemastathā kāryaḥ ṣaḍbhirnṛpa vidhānataḥ \|*  
*bahūnviprān bhojayethāstadratnāptadhanena vai \|*  
*evaṃ kṛte mahīpāla mama muktirbhaviṣyati \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. Then perform sacrifice, reciting the prescribed mantras. You shall feast the Brahmanas, spending the money obtained by the sale of this jewel. When this is done, I shall be released from ghostliood, О king.

## \|\| 9.68 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*tatheti pratijagrāha maṇiṃ rājā tataḥ khaga \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Shri Krsna says: — The king took the jewel, saying, 'be it so'.

## \|\| 9.69-71 \|\|

*kriyādhikārastasyaiva yo dhanagrāhako bhavet \|*  
*kurvatostu tayorvārtāmevaṃ pretamahīkṣitoḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

*jhaṇatkārastu ghaṇṭānāṃ bherīṇāṃ bhāṅkṛtistathā \|*  
*jātastadā rājasenā caturaṅgā samāpatat \|\| 70 \|\|*

*tasyāmāgatamātrāyāṃ pretaścādṛśyatāṃ gataḥ \|*  
*tasmādvanādviniḥ sṛtya rājāpi puramāgamat \|\| 71 \|\|*

69-71. He had no alternative but to perform the rite, for he had taken the gem from the ghost. When the ghost and the king were talking together, there was heard a resounding noise of bells and trumpets. At the indication that the king's caturanga army was close by, the ghost disappeared. The king too left the forest for the capital.

## \|\| 9.72 \|\|

*sa kārtikyāṃ pūrṇimāyāṃ pretamuddiśya saṃvyadhāt \|*  
*vṛṣotsargaṃ vidhānena tanmaṇyāptadhanena ca \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Then on the Karttiki Purnima with the money realized from the sale of the jewel, the king performed Vrsotsarga for the ghost.

## \|\| 9.73 \|\|

*preto 'pyayaṃ sapadilabdhasuvarṇadehaḥ karmānta āgata iti praṇanāma bhūpam \|*  
*deva tvadīyamahimāyamiti stuvan sa yāto divaṃ garuḍa bhūpatinā kṛtajñaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. The ghost obtained a new body as splendid as gold, at the end of the rite..He approached the king in the new splendid body and eulogized him. Attributing his release from ghosthood to the pious deed of the king he was grateful to him for the obsequies he had made in his favour.

## \|\| 9.74 \|\|

*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yathā bhūpatināpi saḥ \|*  
*uddhṛtaḥ pretabhāvādvai kimanyacchrotumicchasi \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Thus, I have told you how the ghost was released. What else do you desire to hear?

## Chapter 10

## \|\| 10.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇa jāte ābdike ca svakarmabhiḥ \|*  
*devatvaṃ vā manuṣyatvaṃ pakṣitvaṃ vāpnuyurnarāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — After sapindlkarana has been done and the annual rites performed, the deceased may obtain godhood, manhood or birdhood as the case may be.

## \|\| 10.2 \|\|

*teṣāṃ vibhinnāhārāṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ vai tṛptidaṃ katham \|*  
*yadapyanyairdvijairbhuktaṃ hūyate yadi vānale \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. But they eat different types of food. How can sraddha satiate them? The sraddha food is eaten by the Brahmanas or given as oblation into the fire.

## \|\| 10.3 \|\|

*śubhāśubhātmakaiḥ pretastaddattaṃ bhujyate katham \|*  
*śrāddhasyāvaśyakatvantu āmāvāsyādiṣu śrutam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The pinda is given by the good as well as the bad relative. How can the ghost discriminate? Why should the sraddha be performed on a particular day?

## \|\| 10.4 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*pretānāṃ śṛṇu pakṣīndra yathā śrāddhantu tṛptidam \|*  
*devo yadapi jāto 'yaṃ manuṣyaḥ karmayogataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О bird, hear, Sraddha is gratifying to ghosts. There is no restriction on a person if he becomes a deity or a man or an animal according to the actions of his previous life.

## \|\| 10.5-7 \|\|

*tasyānnamamṛtaṃ bhūtvā devatve 'pyanuyāti ca \|*  
*gāndhavye bhogarūpeṇa paśutve ca tṛṇaṃ bhavet \|\| 5 \|\|*

*śrāddhaṃ hi vāyurūpeṇa nāgatve 'pyanugacchati \|*  
*phalaṃ bhavati pakṣitve rākṣaseṣu tathāmiṣam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*dānavatve tathā māṃsaṃ pretatverudhiraṃ tathā \|*  
*manuṣyatve 'nnapānādi bālye bhogaraso bhavet \|\| 7 \|\|*

5-7. If he becomes a deity, the sraddha food turns into nectar; an article of enjoyment if he becomes a gandharva; a grass if he becomes an animal; an air if he becomes a naga; fruit in case of a bird; meat in case of a demon; blood in case of a ghost; grain in case of a man and an article of enjoyment in case of a child.

## \|\| 10.8 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ kavyāni dattāni havyāni ca janairiha \|*  
*gacchanti pitṛlokaṃ vā prāpakaḥ ko 'tra gadyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. How kavya and havya given by men reach pitrloka and devaloka and who takes them there?

## \|\| 10.9 \|\|

*mṛtānāmapi jantūnāṃ śrāddhamāpyāyanaṃ yadi \|*  
*nirvāṇasya pradīpasya tailaṃ saṃvardhayecchikhām \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. How can a sraddha gratify the dead? Can oil in a blown-out lamp light the wick?

## \|\| 10.10 \|\|

*mṛtāśca puruṣāḥ svāmin svakarmajanitāṃ gatim \|*  
*gāhantaḥ ke kathaṃ svasya sutasya śreya āpnuyuḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Tell me, О Lord! How does the deceased enjoy the fruits of the deeds such as rites of obsequy performed by his son.

## \|\| 10.11 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*śruteḥ pratyakṣatastārkṣya prāmāṇyaṃ balavattaram \|*  
*śrutyā tuḥ bodhitārthasya pīyūṣatvādirūpatā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The validity of tradition, О lord, is more powerful than the validity of direct perception. The sruti declares that the food served to the Brahmanas in the Sraddha turns into nectar, etc. for the departed souls.

## \|\| 10.12 \|\|

*nāmagotraṃ pitṝṇāṃ vai prāpakaṃ havyakavyayoḥ \|*  
*śrāddhasya mantrāstadvattu prāpakāścaiva bhaktitaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. By their names and gotras the manes receive the offerings made by the relatives. The mantras carry the same when thev are recited with devotion and faith.

## \|\| 10.13 \|\|

*acetanāni caitāni prāpayanti kathantviti \|*  
*suparṇa nāvagantavyaṃ prāpakaṃ vacmi te 'param \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. How the articles reach the manes should not be doubted, О bird. I tell you how the articles of gift can reach the manes.

## \|\| 10.14-15 \|\|

*agniṣvāttādayasteṣā mādhipatye vyavasthitāḥ \|*  
*kāle nyāyāgataṃ pātre vidhinā pratipāditam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*annaṃ nayanti tatraite janturyatrāvatiṣṭhate \|*  
*nāma gotrañca mantrāśca dattamannaṃ nayanti te \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. The Agnisvatta pitrs (the manes who are cremated in fire) remain in charge of the departed. When food is offered to the departed souls in the appropriate manner, at the appropriate time and place, as prescribed in the Shastras, they carry the same to the manes in their path.

## \|\| 10.16 \|\|

*api yoniśataṃ prāptāṃstāṃstṛptirupatiṣṭhati \|*  
*teṣāṃ lokāntarasthānāṃ vividhairnāmagotrakaiḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Even though they assume different species arid reach different worlds, with different names and gotras, they receive the offering made in their favour by their kins.

## \|\| 10.17 \|\|

*apasavyaṃ kṣitau darbhe dattāḥ piṇḍāstrayastu vai \|*  
*yānti tāṃstarpayantyevaṃ pretasthānasthitānpitṝn \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The three pindas given on Darbha or spread over the earth, by the nearest relative of the deceased, with his sacred thread wome over the left shoulder, gratify the manes even in their ghosthood.

## \|\| 10.18-19 \|\|

*aprāptayātanāsthānāḥ śreṣṭhā ye bhuvi pañcadhā \|*  
*nānārūpāstu jātā ye tiryagyonyādijātiṣu \|\| 18 \|\|*

*yadāhārā bhavantyete pitaro yatra yoniṣu \|*  
*tāsutāsu tadāhāraḥ śrāddhā avatiṣṭhati \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Those who have been pious on the earth in all ways do not go to the torture-place. These as well as others who have assumed multifarious forms and those who are born in low species obtain, whatever food they eat in those species.

## \|\| 10.20 \|\|

*yathā goṣu pranaṣṭāsu vatso vindati mātaram \|*  
*tathānnaṃ nayate vipra janturyatrāvatiṣṭhate \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Just as when the cow is lost in the herd of cattle, the calf searches for her and ultimately finds her, so the sraddha food searches the deceased person, though he is set on journey (or has, assumed a different form).

## \|\| 10.21-22 \|\|

*pitaraḥ śrāddhamoktāro viśverdevaiḥ sadā saha \|*  
*ete śrāddhaṃ sadā bhuktvā pitṝnsantarpayantyataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*vasurudrāditisutāḥ pitaraḥ śrāddhadevatāḥ \|*  
*prīṇayāte manuṣyāṇāṃ pitṝñśrāddheṣu tarpitāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. The manes consume the Sraddha rictuals in the company of Vaisvedevas, Vasus, Rudras, Adityas, ancestors, Sraddha devas. When gratified they gratify the deceased person.

## \|\| 10.23 \|\|

*ātmānaṃ gurviṇī garbhamapi prīṇāti vai yathā \|*  
*dohadena tathā devāḥ śrāddhaiḥ svāṃśca pitṝnnṛṇām \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Just as a pregnant woman gratifies herself and her foetus by satiating her desire, so also the deities gratify themselves and the manes by partaking the sraddhas.

## \|\| 10.24 \|\|

*hṛṣyanti pitaraḥ śrutvā śrāddhakālamupasthitam \|*  
*anyonyaṃ manasā dhyātvā sampatanti manojavam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. They feel elated at the approach of sraddha time.. Thus thinking in their mind they reach the sraddha-place With the spread of imagination.

## \|\| 10.25 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaiḥ saha cāśranti pitaro hyantarikṣagāḥ \|*  
*vāyubhūtāśca tiṣṭhanti bhuktvā yānti parāṃ gatim \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Staying in the horizen they eat with the brahmanas. In their aerial form they eat with pleasure.

## \|\| 10.26 \|\|

*nimantritāstu ye viprāḥ śrāddhapūrvadineḥ khaga \|*  
*praviśya pitarasteṣu bhuktvā yānti svamālayam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Whosoever be the Brahmana invited for the feast, they enter his body, eat and return to their abode.

## \|\| 10.27-30 \|\|

*śrāddhakartrā tu yadyekaḥ śrāddhe vipro nimantritaḥ \|*  
*udarasthaḥ pitā tasya vāmapārśve pitāmahaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*prapitāmaho dakṣiṇataḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ piṇḍabhakṣakaḥ \|*  
*śrāddhakāle yamaḥ pretānpitṝṃścāpi yamālayāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

*visarjayati mānuṣye nirayasthāṃśca kāśyapa \|*  
*kṣudhārtāḥ kīrtayantaśca duṣkṛtañca svayaṅkṛtam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*kāṅkṣanti putrapautrebhyaḥ pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyutam \|*  
*tasmāttāṃstatra vidhinā tarpayetpāyasena tu \|\| 30 \|\|*

27-30. If the performer of Sraddha (such as the son of the deceased) has invited a single Brahmana for sraddha, the father stays in his stomach, the grandfather on his left side, the great-grandfather on the right and the consumer of pinda at the back. During the period of sraddha Yama releases even the ghosts and the manes staying in hell who being hungry run to the world of mortals to receive the offering made by their relatives. They repent for their misdeeds while they desire to be served with the milk-rice mixed with honey by their sons and grandsons. Therefore, the descendent should gratify them with the milk-rice.

## \|\| 10.31 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*svāminkenāpi te dṛṣṭā āgatāḥ pitaro dvija \|*  
*lokādamuṣmādāgatya bhuñjanto bhuvi mānada \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О Lord, now tell me, has any one seen the manes descending from the other world and partaking sraddha on this earth?

## \|\| 10.32 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*garutmañchṛṇu vakṣyāmi yathā dṛṣṭāstu sītayā \|*  
*pitaro vipradehe vai śvaśurādyāstrayaḥ kvacit \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Hear, О Garuda, I shall tell you how once Sits saw in the body of a Brahmana the manes, the father-in-law, grand-father-in-law and the great-grand-father-in-law.

## \|\| 10.33-34 \|\|

*gṛhītvā piturājñāṃ vai rāmo vanamupāgamat \|*  
*tataḥ puṣkarayātrārthaṃ rāmo 'yātsītayā saha \|\| 33 \|\|*

*tīrthaṃ cāpi samāgatya śrāddhaṃ prārabdha vāṃstu saḥ \|*  
*phalaṃ pakvantu jānakyā siddhaṃ rāme niveditam \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. At the behest of his father, Rama went to the forest. When he reached Puskara, the holy centre, together with his consort Slta he performed Sraddha with the ripe fruits Sita collected from the trees.

## \|\| 10.35-36 \|\|

*snātapriyoktavākyāttu susnātā namapālayat \|*  
*nabhomadhyagate sūrye kāle kutupa āgate \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ayātā ṛḥṣayaḥ sarve ye rāmeṇa nimantritāḥ \|*  
*tānmunīnāgatāndṛṣṭvā vaidehī janakātmakā \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. When the sun reached the middle of the sky. the sages who had been invited by Rama preented themselves. When Sits saw the sages she was extremely delighted.

## \|\| 10.37 \|\|

*rāmājñayānnamādāya pariveṣṭumupāgatā \|*  
*apāsarpattato dūre vipramadhye tu saṃsthitā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. By the instructions of Rama she served them with food. Then, all of a sudden, she got away from among the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 10.38-40 \|\|

*gulmairācchādya cātmānaṃ nigūḍhaṃ sā sthitā tadā \|*  
*ekānte tu tadā sītāṃ jñātvā rāghavanandanaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*vimṛśya suciraṃ kālamidaṃ kimiti satvaram \|*  
*kiñcitkvacidgatā sādhvī trapāyāḥ kāraṇena hi \|\| 39 \|\|*

*kiṃ vā na bhojayanviprānsī tāmanveṣayāmyaham \|*  
*vimṛśannevamevaṃ sa svayaṃ viprānabhojayat \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. Covering herself behind the bushes she hid herself. "Then having learnt that Sita had gone all alone, Rama was anxious and lost in thought. He wondered why she had gone away so soon without feasting the Brahmanas. He thought to himself. May be she felt shy. I shall search for her. Thinking this way or that he himself feasted the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 10.41 \|\|

*gateṣu dvijamukhyeṣu priyāṃ rāmo 'bravīdidam \|*  
*kathaṃ talāsu līnā tvaṃ munīndṛṣṭvā samāgatān \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. When the Brahmanas went away Sita returned. Then Rama spoke to her: "Why did you leave when the sages came here to the forest. Let me know the reason of your abrupt departure.

## \|\| 10.42 \|\|

*tatsarvaṃ mama tanvaṅgi kāraṇaṃ vada mā ciram \|*  
*evamuktā tadā bhartrā sītā sādhomukhī sthitā \|*  
*muñcantī cāśrusaṃghātaṃ rāghavaṃ vākyamabravīt \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Then addressed by the lord, Sita stood with her face cast dawn.. With tears flowing down from her eyes she spoke thus to her lord.

## \|\| 10.43-44 \|\|

*sītovāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tvaṃ nātha yaddṛṣṭamāścaryamiha yādṛśam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*pitā tava mayā dṛṣṭo brāhmaṇāgre tu rāghavaḥ \|*  
*sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktodvau cānyau ca tathāvidhau \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. О lord, hear, I saw a wonder here. I saw your father in front of the Brahmanas, dressed in royal costume. I saw two elderly folk doning the similar garb.

## \|\| 10.45-46 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tvatpitarañcāhamapakrāntā tavāntikāt \|*  
*valkalājinasaṃvītā kathaṃ rājñaḥ puraḥ prabho \|\| 45 \|\|*

*bhavāmi ripuvīraghna satyametaduhāhṛtam \|*  
*svahastena kathaṃ deyaṃ rājñe vā bhojanaṃ mayā \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. On seeing your father I moved away from his presence. Attired in bark and hide, how could I serve him with food?

## \|\| 10.47 \|\|

*dāsānāmapi ye dāsā nopabhuñjanti karhicit \|*  
*tṛṇapātre kathaṃ tasmai annaṃ dātuṃ hi śakruyām \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. How could I offer him food in a vessel of grass in which even slaves would not eat?

## \|\| 10.48 \|\|

*yāhaṃ rājñā purā dṛṣṭā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā \|*  
*sā svedamaladigdhāṅgī kathaṃ yāsyāmi bhūpatim \|*  
*apakṛṣṭāsmi tenāhaṃ trapayā raghunandana \|*  
*śrībhāgavānuvāca \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. How could I, full of perspiration and dirt, go before him knowing that he had never seen me before in that miserable state. I felt shy and moved away from his presence, О Rama.

## \|\| 10.49-51 \|\|

*iti śrutvā priyāvākyaṃ rāmo vismitamānasaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*āścaryamiti tajjñātvā tadā svasthānamāgamat \|*  
*sītayā pitaro dṛṣṭā yathā tatte niveditam \|\| 50 \|\|*

*aparaṃ śrāddhamāhātmyaṃ kiñcicchṛṇu samāsataḥ \|*  
*amāvasyādine prāpte gṛhadvāre samāsthitāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

49-51. On hearing these words of his beloved, Rama was surprised at heart, thinking that it was a miracle never witnessed before. Thus I have told you how Sita saw the manes. Now, here in short is another account of Sraddha. On the day of Amavasya, the airy manes stand at the threshold of. their decendants in order to receive sraddha.

## \|\| 10.52-53 \|\|

*vāyubhūtāḥ pravāñchanti śrāddhaṃ pitṛgaṇā nṛṇām \|*  
*yāvadastamayaṃ bhānoḥ kṣutpipāsāsamākulāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

*tataścāstaṃ gate sūrye nirāśā duḥ khasaṃyutāḥ \|*  
*niḥ śvasantaściraṃ yānti garhayantastu vaṃśajam \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. They remain there till Sun-set. When they do not receive sraddha they fall in despair and out of suffering heave long sighs and go back cursing the descendents.

## \|\| 10.54-55 \|\|

*tasmācchrāddhaṃ prayatnena amāyāṃ kartumarhati \|*  
*yadi śrāddhaṃ prakurvanti putrādyāstasya bāndhavāḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

*uddhṛtā ye gayāśrāddhe brahmalokañca taiḥ saha \|*  
*bhajante kṣutpipāsā vā na teṣāṃ jāyate kvacit \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. Therefore, one should carefully perform sraddha on the amavasya day. If sons or relatives perform sraddha at Gaya, they uplift their ancestors from lower regions and themselves enjoy Brahmaloka together with the manes. They never suffer from thirst and hunger.

## \|\| 10.56-58 \|\|

*tasmācchrāddhaṃ prayatnena samyakkuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*tasmācchrāddhaṃ caredbhaktyā śākairapi yathāvidhi \|\| 56 \|\|*

*kurvīta samaye śrāddhaṃ kule kaścinna sīdati \|*  
*āyuḥ putrānyaśaḥ svargaṃ kīrtiṃ puṣṭiṃ balaṃ śriyam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*paśūnsāraivyaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ prāpnuyātpitṛpūjanāt \|*  
*devakāryādapi sadā pitṛkāryaṃ viśiṣyate \|\| 58 \|\|*

56-58. Therefore, the wise should perform sraddha imperatively. If no other article of food is available even vegetable if offered with devotion would suffice. If sraddha is done in time the family would never perish. Age, sons, fame, heaven, glory, health, strength, splendour, cattle, happiness, wealth and grain — all these can be procured by worshipping the manes. The riles performed in favour of the manes are more beneficial than the rites performed for the deities.

## \|\| 10.59-60 \|\|

*devatābhyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ hi pūrvamāpyāyanaṃ śubham \|*  
*ye yajanti pitṝndevānbrāhmaṇāṃśca hutāśanam \|\| 59 \|\|*

*sarvabhūtāntarātmānaṃ māmeva hi yajanti te \|*  
*smārtena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā svavibhavocitam \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. Gratification of the manes in proportion to that -of the deities is more beneficial. Those who worship manes, deities; Brahmanas and file, in fact, worship me who am the soul of all beings. By performing sraddha according to the mean and method, 'the' man pleases the universe as far above as Brahmaloka.

## \|\| 10.61-65 \|\|

*ābrāhmastambaparyantaṃ jagatprīṇāti mānavaḥ \|*  
*annaprakiraṇaṃ yattu manuṣyaiḥ kriyate bhuvi \|\| 61 \|\|*

*tena tuptimupāyānti ye piśācatvamāgatāḥ \|*  
*yaccāmbuḥ snānavastrebhyo bhūmau patati khecara \|\| 62 \|\|*

*tena ye tarutāṃ prāptāsteṣāṃ tṛptiḥ prajāyate \|*  
*yāni gandhāmbūni caiva patanti dharaṇītale \|\| 63 \|\|*

*tena cāpyāyanaṃ teṣāṃ ye devatvamupāgatāḥ \|*  
*ye cāpi svakulādbāhyāḥ kriyāyogyā hyasaṃskṛtāḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

*vipannāste tu vikirasaṃmārjanajalāśinaḥ \|*  
*bhuktvā cācamanaṃ yacca jalaṃ yaccāhni sevitam \|\| 65 \|\|*

61-65. Whatever food is spread over the earth by the performer, the same gratifies them, though they have become ghosts. Whatever water falls on the ground dripping from the bathing clothes of the performer the same gratifies them, though they may have become trees. Whatever scents fall on the ground the same gratifies them though they may have obtained deity-hood. Those who are outside the caste groups, unfit for rites or averse to rites, consume water scattered after washing.

## \|\| 10.66 \|\|

*brāhmaṇānāṃ tathaivānyattena tṛptiṃ prayānti vai \|*  
*piśācatvamanuprāptāḥ kṛmikīṭatvameva ye \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Whatever water is revised in the day by the Brahmanas and others, the same gratifies those who have become pisaca, worms and germs.

## \|\| 10.67 \|\|

*uddhṛteṣvannapiṇḍeṣu bhuvi ye cānnakāṅkṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*tairevāpyāyanaṃ teṣāṃ ye manuṣyatvamāgatāḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Whatever pindas are left on the earth the same gratify those who have become men.

## \|\| 10.68-69 \|\|

*evaṃ vai kriyamāṇānāṃ teṣāṃ caiva dvijanmanām \|*  
*kaścijjalānnavikṣepaḥ śucirucchiṣṭa eva vā \|\| 68 \|\|*

*tenānnena kule teṣāṃ ye vai jātyantaraṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*bhavatyāpyāyanaṃ teṣāṃ samyak śrāddhe kṛte sati \|\| 69 \|\|*

68-69. Thus when all this is being done by the twice-born, whatever water and food is left, good or bad, the same gratifies those who have opted for another caste, in case the sraddha is done properly.

## \|\| 10.70 \|\|

*anyāyopārjitairdravyairyacchrāddhaṃ kriyate naraiḥ \|*  
*takṛpyanti tena caṇḍālāḥ pukkasādyupayoniṣu \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. When men perform sraddha with illgotten wealth, the same gratifies candalas and others of low species.

## \|\| 10.71 \|\|

*evaṃ saṃprāpyate pakṣin yaddattamiha bāndhavaiḥ \|*  
*śrāddhaṃ kurvadbhirannāmvuśākaistṛptirhi jāyate \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Thus, О bird, whatever is given to the manes, by their relatives while doing sraddha, they receive the same and remain gratified with food, water and vegetable.

## \|\| 10.72-73 \|\|

*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yanmāṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi \|*  
*sadyo dehāntaraprāptirvilaṃbenāvanītale \|\| 72 \|\|*

*pṛṣṭavānasi tatte 'haṃ pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ \|*  
*sadyo vilambatañcaivobhayathāpi kalevaram \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. Thus, I have told you all what you had asked me for. Now that you have queried me whether the soul obtains body immediately or after some time I shall tell you about the same in short. Body is obtained both immediately and late

## \|\| 10.74 \|\|

*yato hi martyaḥ prāpnoti tadviśeṣañca me śṛṇu \|*  
*adhūmakajyotirivāṅguṣṭhamātraḥ pumāṃstataḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Now the soul gets a new body, hear from me. The soul without body is like a Same without fire. It is about a thumb in size.

## \|\| 10.75-77 \|\|

*dehamekaṃ sadya eva vāyavīyaṃ prapadyate \|*  
*yathā nṛṇajalaukā hi paścatpādaṃ tadoddharet \|\| 75 \|\|*

*sthitiragryasya pādasya yadā jātā dṛḍhā bhavet \|*  
*evaṃ dehī pūrvadehaṃ samutsṛjati taṃ yadā \|\| 76 \|\|*

*bhogārthamagre syāddeho vāyavīya upasthitaḥ \|*  
*viṣayagrāhakaṃ yadvanmriyamāṇasya cendriyam \|\| 77 \|\|*

75-77. After leaving the earthly body, the soul obtains an airy body. Like a caterpillar who lifts up the back feet only when the position of the front feet becomes firm the soul leaves the previous body only when the airy body is available to enjoy.

## \|\| 10.78 \|\|

*nirvyāpāraṃ tacca dehe vāyunaiva sa gacchati \|*  
*śarīraṃ yadavāpnoti taccāpyutkvamyati svayam \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. In that actionless body he moves in air only. Whatever body he has obtained, the same he himself leaves.

## \|\| 10.79 \|\|

*gṛhītvā svaṃ viniryāti jīvo garbha īvāśayāt \|*  
*utkrāmantaṃ sthitaṃ vāpi bhuñjānaṃ vā guṇānvitam \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. He leaves the body just as the child leaves the womb. He may come out, stay and enjoy.

## \|\| 10.80 \|\|

*vimūḍhā nānupaśyanti paśyanti jñānacakṣuṣaḥ \|*  
*ātivāhikamityevaṃ vāyavīyaṃ vadanti hi \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. The fools do not see him, only those who possess the vision of knowledge can see him in that state.

## \|\| 10.81 \|\|

*evaṃ tu yātudhānānāṃ tameva ca vadanti hi \|*  
*suparṇa īdṛśo deho nṛṇāṃ bhavati piṇḍajaḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. О bird, this type of body the deceased can have out of the pinda (rice-ball) offered to him.

## \|\| 10.82 \|\|

*putrādibhiḥ kṛtāścetsyuḥ piṇḍā daśa daśāhikāḥ \|*  
*piṇḍajena tu dehena vāyujaścaikatāṃ vrajet \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. Whatever pinda the sons or kins give him during the ten days, the same unites the Vayuja body with the pindaja body.

## \|\| 10.83 \|\|

*piṇḍajo yadi naiva syādvāyujor'hati yātanām \|*  
*dehino 'sminyathā dehe kaumāraṃ yauvanaṃ jarā \|*  
*tathā dehāntaraprāptiḥ pakṣīndretyavadhāraya \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. If the pindaja body be not there, the Vayuja body suffers. Just as in his body a man has childhood, youth and old age, so also he obtains another body, this you already know, О king of birds.

## \|\| 10.84 \|\|

*vāsāṃsi jīrṇāni yathā vihāya navāni gṛhṇāti naroparāṇi \|*  
*tathā śarīrāṇi vihāya jīrṇānyanyāni saṃyāti navāni dehī \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Just as men wear new clothes casting off old ones, so also the soul obtains another body leaving the old one.

## \|\| 10.85 \|\|

*nainaṃ chindanti śastrāṇi nainaṃ dahati pāvakaḥ \|*  
*na cainaṃ kledayantyāpo na śoṣayati mārutaḥ \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Neither the weapons cut it nor the fire burns it nor the water wets it nor the wind dries it.

## \|\| 10.86 \|\|

*vāyavīyāṃ tanuṃ yāti sadya ityuktameva te \|*  
*prāptirvilaṃbato yasya taṃ dehaṃ khalu me śṛṇu \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Just now, I have told you that it obtains the airy body immediately. Now, hear about the body it obtains belatedly.

## \|\| 10.87 \|\|

*kvacidvilaṃbato dehaṃ piṇḍajaṃ sa samāpnuyāt \|*  
*atho gato yāmyalokaṃ svīyakarmānusārataḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. After sometime, the Jfva when he reaches Yamaloka. obtains the pindaja body.

## \|\| 10.88-89 \|\|

*citraguptasyavākyena nirayāṇi bhunakti saḥ \|*  
*yātanāḥsamavāpyātha paśupakṣyādikīṃ tanum \|\| 88 \|\|*

*yā gṛhṇāti naraḥ sā syānmohena mamatāspadam \|*  
*śubhāśubhaṃ karmaphalaṃ bhuktvā mucyate mānavaḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

88-89. As directed by Citragupta, he suffers in hell. Having suffered tortures there, he is born in the low species as an animal, a bird, etc. There too, very full of attachment, he suffers from the result of good and bad actions, О king of birds.

## \|\| 10.90 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*tīrtvā duḥ khabhavāmbhodhiṃ bhavantaṃ kathamāpnuyāt \|*  
*bahupātakayukto 'pi tadvadasva dayānidhe \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. Garuda said: — О Treasure of pity, please tell me how even though full of sins, a Jiva may obtain you, after crossing the occean of worldly existence.

## \|\| 10.91 \|\|

*bhūyo duḥ khasya saṃsargo narasya na bhavedyathā \|*  
*brūhi śuśrūṣamāṇasya pṛcchato me ramāpate \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Please also tell me, О lord, how a man can avoid suffering?

## \|\| 10.92 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*svesve karmaṇyabhirataḥ saṃsiddhiṃ labhate naraḥ \|*  
*svakarmanirataḥ siddhiṃ yathā vindati tacchṛṇu \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. Man obtains success being busy in the performance of assigned acts. Now, hear how he can get success while he is engaged in doing acts.

## \|\| 10.93-96 \|\|

*karmavibhraṣṭakāluṣyo vāsudevānucintayā \|*  
*buddhyā viśuddhayā yukto dhṛtyātmānaṃ niyamya ca \|\| 93 \|\|*

*śabdādīnviṣayāṃstyaktvā rāgadveṣau vyudasya ca \|*  
*viraktasevī labdhvāśī yatavākkāyamānasaḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

*dhyānayogaparo nityaṃ vairagyaṃ samupāśritaḥ \|*  
*ahaṅkāraṃ balaṃ darpaṃ kāmaṃ krodhaṃ parigraham \|\| 95 \|\|*

*vimucya nirmamaḥ śānto brahmabhūyāya kalpate \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ nṛṇāṃ kṛtyaṃ nāsti kaśyapanandana \|\| 96 \|\|*

93-96. By taking shelter in Vasudeva, possessing pure intellect, controlling the self with fortitude, discarding the objects of enjoyment and leaving attachment and envy, serving without expecting reward, controlling speech, body and mind, practising meditation and Yoga, taking recourse to detachment, leaving pride, force, desire, anger, thus calm and serene he attains brahma. After this, he has to do nothing, О son of Kasyapa.

## Chapter 11

## \|\| 11.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*mānuṣatvaṃ labhetkasmānmṛtyumāpnoti tatkatham \|*  
*mriyate kaḥ suraśreṣṭha dehamāśritya kutracit \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. How does a person obtain manhood and how does he attain death? О best of deities, when he dies where does his body go?

## \|\| 11.2 \|\|

*indriyāṇi kuto yānti hyaspṛśyaḥ sa kathaṃ bhavet \|*  
*kva karmāṇi kṛtānīha kathaṃ bhuṅkte prasarpati \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Where do the organs of sense repair? How does the body become untouchable? How and where does he suffer the fruits of his actions?

## \|\| 11.3 \|\|

*prasādaṃ kuru me mohaṃ chettumarhasyaśeṣataḥ \|*  
*kāśyapo 'haṃ suraśreṣṭha vinatāgarbha saṃbhavaḥ \|*  
*yamalokaṃ kathaṃ yānti viṣṇulokaṃ ca mānavāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Be pleased, for you alone can remove my ignorance. О best of deities, I am Garuda, son of Kasyapa from Vinata. Please tell me how do men go to Yamaloka or Visnuloka.

## \|\| 11.4-5 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*parasya yoṣitaṃ hṛtvā brahmasvamapahṛtya ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

*araṇye nirjane deśe jāyate brahmarākṣasaḥ \|*  
*hīnajātau prajāyeta ratnānāmapahārakaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. By kidnapping another man's wife and stealing the property of a Brahmana, one becomes brahmaraksasa in wilderness. The thief of jewels is born in a low caste.

## \|\| 11.6 \|\|

*yaṃyaṃ kāmamabhidhyāyetsa talliṅgo 'bhijāyate \|*  
*nainaṃ chindanti śastrāṇi nainaṃ dahati pāvakaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Whatsoever one desires one is born, having the symbol of the same. The weapons do not cut his soul nor fire can bum it.

## \|\| 11.7-8 \|\|

*na cainaṃ kledayantyāpo na śoṣayati mārutaḥ \|*  
*vāk cakṣurnāsikā karṇau gudaṃ mūtrasya sañcaraḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*aṇḍajādikajantūnāṃ chidrāṇyetāni sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*ānābhermūrdhaparyantamūrdhvacchidrāṇi cāṣṭa vai \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. Nor water wets it nor wind dries it. Mouth, eyes, nose, ears, anus and penisthese are the holes in all animals born of egg, etc. There are eight upper, holes from navel up to the head.

## \|\| 11.9-10 \|\|

*santaḥ sukṛtino martyā ūrdhvacchidreṇa yānti vai \|*  
*mṛtāhe vārṣikaṃ yāvadyathoktavidhinā khaga \|\| 9 \|\|*

*kuryātsarvāṇi karmāṇi nirdhano 'pi hi mānavaḥ \|*  
*dehe yatra vasejjantustatra bhuṅkte śubhāśubham \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. If men have done good deeds, they depart from the upper holes, О bird. Whatever rites are prescribed for the deceased from the day of his death to the end of the year (of his death) shall be performed even by the poor. As for the place of enjoyment in whatever body the jiva has entered he reaps the fruits of his actions therein, no matter whether the actions be good or bad.

## \|\| 11.11 \|\|

*manovākkāyajāndoṣāṃstathāṃ bhuṅkte khageśvara \|*  
*mṛtaḥ sa sukhamāpnoti māyāpāśairna badhyate \|*  
*pāśabaddho naro yastu vikarmanirato bhavet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. In this way, О king of birds, a person suffers from the faults of body, mind and speech. After his death, he obtains happiness when he has suffered the results of his actions. He is released from the noose of Maya and he is not likely to be born again to indulge in evil acts.

## Chapter 12

## \|\| 12.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ te kathitastārkṣya jīvitasya vinarṇayaḥ \|*  
*mānuṣāṇāṃ hitārthāya pretatvavinivṛttaye \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О Garuda, I have thus explained to you the various proclivities of life for the benefit of men and to ward off the stalemate in which the dead may find themselves.

## \|\| 12.2 \|\|

*caturaśītilakṣāṇi caturbhedāśca jantavaḥ \|*  
*aṇḍajāḥ svedajāścaiva udbhijjāśca jārāyujāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. There are eight million four hundred thousand species of creatures divided into four main classes. They are (1) andajas (bom of egg), (2) svedajas (sweat-bom like bugs, etc. (3) udbhijjas (bom of seeds), (4) Jarayujas' (the viviparous.)

## \|\| 12.3-4 \|\|

*ekaviṃśatilakṣāṇi aṇḍajāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*svedajāśca tathā proktā udbhijjāśca krameṇa tu \|\| 3 \|\|*

*jarāyujāstathā proktā manuṣyādyāstathā pare \|*  
*sarveṣāmeva jantūnāṃ mānuṣatvaṃ hi durlabham \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. The andajas are of two million one hundred thousand varieties. Similarly, the Svedajas, Udbhijjas and the Jarayujas in the last of which are included human beings are also enumerated. It is very difficult for the lower animals to get manhood.

## \|\| 12.5 \|\|

*pañcendriyanidhānatvaṃ mahāpuṇyairavāpyate \|*  
*brāhmaṇāḥ kṣattriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrāstatparajātayaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The receptacle of the set of five senseorgans can be acquired through great merits. Mankind is divided into four varnas — Ksatriyas, Vaisyas, sudras and also Antyajas (the lowest class).

## \|\| 12.6 \|\|

*rajakaścarmakāraśca naṭo buruḍa eva ca \|*  
*kaivartamedabhillāśca saptaite hyantyajāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The Antyajas are divided into seven groups viz., Washermen, Cobblers, Actors, Vamdas, Kaivartas, Bhedas and Bhillas.

## \|\| 12.7 \|\|

*mlecchatumbavibhedena jātibhedāstvanekaśaḥ \|*  
*jantūnāmeva sarveṣāṃ jātibhedāḥ sahasraśaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Adding the MIecchas and Tumbas there are thirteen groups of people. The species of lower strata of animals are thousands.

## \|\| 12.8 \|\|

*jantūnāmeva sarveṣāṃ bhedāścaiva sahasraśaḥ \|*  
*āhāro maithunaṃ nidrā bhayaṃ krodhastathaiva ca \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Taking food, indulging in sexual inter course, sleeping, fearing and becoming angry- these are found in all living beings. Here the distinction is impossible.

## \|\| 12.9-10 \|\|

*sarveṣā meva jantūnāṃ viveko durlabhaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*ekapādādirūpeṇa dehabhedāstvanekaśaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇasāro mṛgo yatra dharmadaśaḥ sa ucyate \|*  
*brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvāstatra tiṣṭhanti sarvaśaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Bodies are of ten varieties in view of the divisions such as single-footed, etc. Where the deer Krsnasara is found in plenty that region is called Dharmadesa (the land of virtue). О Bird, the deities Brahma and others, the sages and the Pitrs, virtue, truthfulness and learning are always present in that land of virtue.

## \|\| 12.11 \|\|

*bhūtānāṃ prāṇinaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ prāṇināṃ matijīvinaḥ \|*  
*matimatsu narāḥ śreṣṭhā nareṣu brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Among living beings, the animals are the best; among the animals, the intelligent are the best; among the intelligent, men are the best and among men, the brahmins are the best.

## \|\| 12.12 \|\|

*mānuṣyaṃ yaḥ samāsādya svargamekṣaikasādhakam \|*  
*tayorna sādhayedekaṃ tenātmā vañcito dhruvam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Among the brahmins the scholars, among the scholars those who cultivate Vedic studies, among them those who act according to the injunction and among them the Brahmavadins are the best.

## \|\| 12.13 \|\|

*icchati śatī sahasraṃ sahasrī lakṣamīhate kartum \|*  
*lakṣādhipatī rājyaṃ rājāpi sakalāṃ dharāṃ labdhum \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. One is guilty of self-deception who does not strive to gain either heaven or salvation after being born as a man whereby he could gain either.

## \|\| 12.14 \|\|

*cakradharo 'pi suratvaṃ surabhāve sakalasurapatirbhavitum \|*  
*surapatirūrdhvagatitvaṃ tathāpi nanivartate tṛṣṇā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. A man who has hundred (silver pieces) craves for a thousand; a man who has a thousand, yearns for a lac; a person who has a lac, wishes to rule over a kingdom; a man who rules over a kingdom pines away to become an emperor.

## \|\| 12.15 \|\|

*tṛṣṇayā cābhibhūtastu narakaṃ pratipadyate \|*  
*tṛṣṇāmuktāstu ye kecitsvargavāsaṃ labhanti te \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. An emperor wishes to become a Deva (god) and on getting godhood he wishes to be the lord of gods. The lord of gods wishes to go still further and still his thirst for power does not recede.

## \|\| 12.16 \|\|

*ātmādhīnaḥ pumāṃlloke sukhī bhavati niścitam \|*  
*śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaśca tadguṇāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. A person afflicted by covetous thirst falls into hell. Those who are freed of undue thirst secure a residence in heaven.

## \|\| 12.17 \|\|

*tathā ca viṣayādhīno duḥ khī bhavati niścitam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. A man depending upon his own self is sure to be happy. The qualities of sound, touch, colour, taste and smell make one dependent on the objects of sense and hence one is sure to be unhappy.

## \|\| 12.18 \|\|

*kuraṅgamatāṅgapataṅgabhṛṅgamīnā hatāḥ pañcabhireva pañca \|*  
*ekaḥ pramādī sa kathaṃ na hanyate yaḥ sevate pañcabhireva pañca \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The deer, the elephant, the moth, the honeybee and the fish-these five are destroyed due to addiction to their five senseorgans.

## \|\| 12.19 \|\|

*pitṛmātṛmayo bālye yauvane dayitāmayaḥ \|*  
*putrapautramayaścānte mūḍho nātmamayaḥ kvacit \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. In infancy one is extremely obsessed with one's parents; in youth, one is obsessed with one's wife ; later in life one becomes obsessed with one's sons and grandsons. Never is one obsessed with the Atman.

## \|\| 12.20 \|\|

*lohadārumayaiḥ pāśaiḥ pumānbaddho vimucyate \|*  
*putradāramayaiḥ pāśairnaiva baddho vimucyate \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. It is easy for one bound with iron fetters to wooden pegs to get oneself released. But one bound with the nooses of children and wives is never released.

## \|\| 12.21 \|\|

*ekaḥ karoti pāpāni phalaṃ bhuṅkte mahājanaḥ \|*  
*bhoktāro viprayujyante kartā doṣeṇa lipyate \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. It is impossible to escape death whether one is a fool or a scholar, a child or an old man, young (or old and infirm), extremely happy or excessively dejected. He comes and goes. (He dies and is born again.)

## \|\| 12.22 \|\|

*ko 'pi mṛtyuṃ na jayati bālo vṛddho yuvāpi vā \|*  
*sukhaduḥ khādiko vāpi punarāyāti yāti ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Man is born alone; man dies alone; he enjoys his merits by himself; he reaps the bitter fruits of his sins by himself.

## \|\| 12.23-25 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ paśyatāmeva mṛtaḥ sarvaṃ parityajet \|*  
*ekaḥ prajāyate jantureka eva pralīyate \|\| 23 \|\|*

*eko 'pi bhuṅkte sukṛtameka eva ca duṣkṛtam \|*  
*mṛtaṃ śarīramutsṛjya kāṣṭhaloṣṭasamaṅkṣitau \|\| 24 \|\|*

*bāndhavā vimukhā yānti dharmastamanugacchati \|*  
*gṛheṣvarthā nivartante śmaśānānmitrabāndhavāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

23-25. Even as every one is watching, one leaves off everything and dies.. Casting off the dead body along with logs; of wood or clods of earth the kinsmen turn away (from the cremation or burial ground) but the dead man's virtue or evil definitely follows him. His riches recede from him in the house itself and his kinsmen turn away at the cremation grounds with friends.

## \|\| 12.26-27 \|\|

*śarīraṃ vahnirādatte sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vrajet \|*  
*śarīraṃ vahninā dagdhaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ saha sthitam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*śubhaṃ vā yadi vā pāpaṃ bhuṅkte sarvatra mānavaḥ \|*  
*yadanastamite sūrye na dattaṃ dhanamarthinām \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The fire consumes the body but the merit and demerit accompany him. The body is burnt by fire but the actions perpetrated by him keep his company.

## \|\| 12.28-30 \|\|

*na jāne tasya tadvittaṃ prātaḥ kasya bhaviṣyati \|*  
*rāraṭīti dhanaṃ tasya ko me bhartā bhaviṣyati \|\| 28 \|\|*

*na dattaṃ dvijamukhyebhyaḥ paropakṛtaye tathā \|*  
*pūrvajanmakṛtātpuṇyādyallabdhaṃ bahu cālpakam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*tadīdṛśaṃ parijñāya dharmārthe dīyate dhanam \|*  
*dhanena dhāryate dharmaḥ śraddhāpūtena cetasā \|\| 30 \|\|*

28-30. Auspicious or otherwise, a man has to experience the fruits of his action. If before sunset wealth is not distributed among the suppliants, I do not know to whom it will go in the morning that follows. If some wealth is not handed over to Brahmins and friends or spent in holy rites or pilgrimages, the wealth begins to cry 'who shall be my lord.?' Whether plentiful or scanty whatever wealth one has, is due to one's previous merits. Realizing this, one has to spend it away in virtuous rites. It is by wealth that virtue is sustained if the mind is sanctified by faith.

## \|\| 12.31-33 \|\|

*śraddhāvirahito dharmo nehāmutra ca tatphalam \|*  
*dharmācca jāyate hyartho dharmātkāmo 'pi jāyate \|\| 31 \|\|*

*dharma evāpavargāya tasmāddharmaṃ samācaret \|*  
*śraddhayā sādhyate dharmo bahubhirnārtharāśibhiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*akiñcanā hi munayaḥ śraddhāvanto divaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*aśraddhayā hutaṃ dattaṃ tapastaptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat \|*  
*asadityucyate pakṣinpretya ceha na tatphalam \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. A sacred rite devoid of faith is neither fruitful here nor there. It does not nourish. Virtue is the cause of wealth, love and salvation. Hence, one shall be virtuous. It is by faith that virtue is sustained and not by heaps of riches. Indigent sages endowed with faith have attained heaven. Homas, gifts, penances and actions without faith are base, О bird, and they are not found fruitful, here or in the life after death.

## Chapter 13

## \|\| 13.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*karmaṇā kena deveśa pretatvaṃ naiva jāyate \|*  
*pṛthivyāṃ sarvajantūnāṃ tad brūhi parameśvara \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О Lord of gods, please tell me by means of what rite can one prevent a stalemate after death. (What are the rites that men should do in the world to forestall the state of being a ghost for ever?)

## \|\| 13.2 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepātkṣayāhādaurdhvadaihikam \|*  
*kvahastenaiva kartavyaṃ mokṣakāmaistu mānavaiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Please listen, I shall explain briefly the obsequious rites. If one desires to attain salvation, one has to perform them oneself.

## \|\| 13.3-4 \|\|

*strīṇāmapi viśeṣeṇa pañcavarṣādhike śiśau \|*  
*vṛṣotsargādikaṃ karma pretatvavinivṛttaye \|*  
*vṛṣotsargādṛte nānyatkiñcidasti mahītale \|\| 3 \|\|*

*jīvanvāpi mṛto vāpi vṛṣotsargaṃ karoti yaḥ \|*  
*pretatvaṃ na bhavettasya vinā dānamakhavrataiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. Even in regard to women, and children above the age of five, this is specially insisted on. The rites of Vrsotsarga (gifting away a bull calf) are for forestalling the continuance of ghosthood. There is no other rite in the world except Vrsotsarga (for that purpose). While during his life or after death if Vrsotsarga is performed he will never continue for long as a ghost. There is no necessity for other gifts or sacrifices.

## \|\| 13.5-6 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kasminkāle vṛṣotsargaṃ jīvanvāpi mṛto 'pi vā \|*  
*kuryātsuravaraśreṣṭha brūhi me madhusūdana \|\| 5 \|\|*

*kiṃ phalaṃ tu bhavedante kṛtaiḥ śrāddhaistu ṣoḍaśaiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Garuda said: — If Vrsotsarga is to the performed during life or after death, at what time has it to be performed, О foremost among gods, Madhusudana, please tell me. If the sixteen types of Sraddha are performed what are then effects on the dead man?

## \|\| 13.7 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*akṛtvā tu vṛṣotsargaṃ kurute piṇḍapātanam \|*  
*nopatiṣṭhati tacchreyo dātuḥ pretasya niṣphalam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. If Pindas are offered without performing Vrsotsarga no benefit accrues. Every thing given unto the dead is fruitless.

## \|\| 13.8 \|\|

*ekādaśāhe pretasya yasya notsṛjyate vṛṣaḥ \|*  
*pretatvaṃ susthiraṃ tasya dattaiḥ śrāddhaśatairapi \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. If Vrsotsarga is not carried out on the eleventh day after death, the ghosthood of the dead becomes eternal even if hundreds of sraddhas are performed in his favour.

## \|\| 13.9-10 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*sarpāddhi prāptamṛtyūnāmagnidāhādi na kriyā \|*  
*jalena śṛṅgiṇā vāpi śastrādyairmriyate yadi \|\| 9 \|\|*

*asanmṛtyumṛtānāṃ ca kathaṃ śuddhirbhavatprabho \|*  
*etanme saṃśayaṃ deva cchettumarhasyaśeṣataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Garuda said: — If a person dies of snakebite, no funeral rite, such as cremation takes place. Similarly, if he dies by drowning or being struck by a homed animal or through weapons or an accident for how many days do the relatives remain impure? О lord, please remove my doubts on this point.

## \|\| 13.11 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*ṣaṇmāsairbrāhmaṇaḥ śudhyedyugme sārdhe tu bāhujaḥ \|*  
*sārdhamāsena vaiśyastu śūdro māsena śudhyati \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. If he is a Brahmin, impurity lasts for six months; if a Ksatriya, two months and a half; if a Vaisya, one month and a half, and if a sudra it lasts for a month.

## \|\| 13.12 \|\|

*dattvā dānānyaśeṣāṇi sutīrthe mriyate yadi \|*  
*brahmacārī śucirbhūtvā na sa yātīha durgatim \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If a person has gifted some precious articles of different sorts to a worthy receiver and has lived a celibate life when alive, he does not go to a wretched state (even if he dies an unnatural death).

## \|\| 13.13 \|\|

*vṛṣotsargādikaṃ kṛtvā yatidharmaṃ samācaret \|*  
*yatitve mṛtyumāpnoti sa gacchedbrahmaśākhvatam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. If a person has performed the rite of Vrsotsarga and has lived a celibate life, he goes to Brahmaloka, even if he dies an unnatural death.

## \|\| 13.14 \|\|

*vikarma kurute yastu śiṣṭācāravivarjitaḥ \|*  
*vṛṣotsargādikaṃ kṛtvā na gacchedyamaśāsanam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. If a person has lived an unrighteous life devoid of good conduct, but has performed Vrsotsarga, he does not go to the world of Yama after death.

## \|\| 13.15 \|\|

*puttro vā sodaro vāpi pautro bandhujanastathā \|*  
*gotriṇaścārthabhāgī ca mṛte kuryād vṛṣotsavam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. When a person dies, his son, brother, grandson, relative, caste-man or the successor should perform Vrsotsarga.

## \|\| 13.16 \|\|

*putrābhāve tu patnī syāddauhittro duhītāpi vā \|*  
*puttreṣu vidyamāneṣu vṛṣaṃ nānyena kārayet \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. If there be no son, the wife can perform Vrsotsarga; if no wife, the daughter's son or the daughter herself can do the same. But, in case, he has sons, none else should perform Vrsotsarga.

## \|\| 13.17 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*puttrā yasya na vidyante narā nāryaḥ sureśvara \|*  
*etanme saṃśayaṃ deva cchetumarhasyaśeṣataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. If one has no kinsmen such as sons, mother, relatives, wife or husband, how are the obsequial rites to be performed? By what rite are men and women to attain salvation warding off miseries. О lord, please dispel this doubt of mine entirely.

## \|\| 13.18 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*aputtrasya gatirnāsti svargo naiva ca naiva ca \|*  
*tasmātkenāpyupāyena puttrasya jananaṃ caret \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. There is no salvation for a man without a son. He can never hope to attain heaven., Hence, somehow a man must strive to get a son.

## \|\| 13.19 \|\|

*yāni kāni ca dānāni svayaṃ dattāni mānavaiḥ \|*  
*tānitāni ca sarvāṇi tūpatiṣṭhanti cāgrataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Whatever gifts are made by one during the life time, become beneficial later.

## \|\| 13.20 \|\|

*vyañjanāni vicitrāṇi bhakṣyabhojyāni yāni ca \|*  
*svahastena pradattāni dehānte cākṣayaṃ phalam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Different sorts of delicacies and foodstuff given by one's hand stand in good stead after death.

## \|\| 13.21 \|\|

*gobhūhiraṇyavāsāṃsi bhojanāni pādani ca \|*  
*yatrayatra vasejjantustatratatropatiṣṭhati \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Cows, plots of land, gold, clothes, foodstuffs, good posts (positions), etc., offered to the needy succour a man wher ever he is.

## \|\| 13.22 \|\|

*yāvatsvasthaṃ śarīraṃ hi tāvaddharmaṃ samācaret \|*  
*asvasthaḥ preritaścānyairnākiñcitkartumarhati \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. One shall perform sacred rites as long When a person dies, his son, brother, as the body is hale and hearty. Falling sick, one may not feel enthusiastic to do anything even if prompted by others.

## \|\| 13.23 \|\|

*jīvato 'pi mṛtasyeha na bhūtaṃ caurdhvadaihikam \|*  
*vāyubhūtaḥ kṣudhāviṣṭo bhramate ca divāniśam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Until the rites of obsequies are performed the dead man, ever hungry, roams about day and night in the form of airy matter.

## \|\| 13.24 \|\|

*kṛmiḥ kīṭaḥ pataṅgo vā jāyate mriyate punaḥ \|*  
*asadgarbhe bhavetso 'pi jātaḥ sadyo vinaśyati \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He is likely to be born again, a worm or an insect or a locust or in the womb of an evil- minded base woman. He may die immediately after rebirth.

## \|\| 13.25 \|\|

*yāvatsvasthamidaṃ śarīramarujaṃ yāvajjarā dūrato yāvaccendriyaśaktirapratihatā yāvatkṣayo nāyuṣaḥ \|*  
*ātmaśreyasi tāvadeva viduṣā kāryaḥ prayatno mahānsandīpte bhavane tu kūpakhananaṃ pratyudyamaḥ kīdṛśaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. As long as the body is healthy, free from sickness, as long as old age has not set in, as long as the power of sense-organs is not impaired, as long as there is no risk to life, an intelligent man should endeavour for the welfare of the soul. It is foolishness to begin to dig a well when the house has caught fire.

## Chapter 14

## \|\| 14.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*ārtena mriyamāṇena yaddattaṃ tatphalaṃ vada \|*  
*svasthāvasthena dattena vidhihīnena vā vibho \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О lord, please tell me what is the effect if one offers things oneself or through others, if one offers things when one is hale and hearty or when one is sick; or if things are offered when one is unconscious or without reference to the injunctions in the sacred texts?

## \|\| 14.2-3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*ekā gauḥ svasthacittasya hyāturasya ca gośatam \|*  
*sahasraṃ mriyamāṇasya dattaṃ vittavivarjitam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*mṛtasyaiva punarlakṣaṃ vidhipūtaṃ ca tatsamam \|*  
*tīrthapātrasamāyogādekā gaurlakṣapuṇyadā \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. One cow, offered when one's mind is at rest and peaceful, is on a par with hundred co\\ys offered when it is not, or a thousand cows given at the time of his death when it is doubtful whether he is conscious of it at all, or a hundred thousand cows after his death. If it is given without reference to the injunctions in the sacred texts it is fruitless. Even a single cow offered to a deserving person at a holy centre is on a par with a gift of a hundred thousand cows elsewhere in yielding merit.

## \|\| 14.4-5 \|\|

*pātre datte khagaśreṣṭha ahanyahani vardhate \|*  
*dāturdānamapāpāya jñānināṃ ca pratigrahaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*viṣaśītāpaho mantravahniḥ kiṃ doṣabhājanam \|*  
*dātavyaṃ pratyahaṃ pātre nimitteṣu viśeṣataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. О foremost among birds, anything given to a deserving person nourishes day by day. A gift wipes off the sin of the giver and the acceptance of the same by a person of good wisdom does not affect him. The mantra quells person and the fire chillness. Are they affected by the same?

## \|\| 14.6 \|\|

*nāpātre viduṣā kiñcidātmanaḥ śreya icchatā \|*  
*apātre jātu gaurdattā dātāraṃ narakaṃ nayet \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Every day something must be given to deserving persons, particularly so on special occasions. If one wishes for one's own weal, one shall be sane enough not to give anything to the undeserving.

## \|\| 14.7 \|\|

*kulaikaviṃśatiyutaṃ grahītāraṃ ca pātayet \|*  
*dehāntaraṃ pariprāpya svahastena kṛtaṃ ca yat \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. A cow gifted to an undeserving causes the downfall of the giver into hell. The receiver too falls into hell along with the members of his family for twenty one generations. If on being born again he performs some meritorious deeds, (he effect there-ofis also wiped off.

## \|\| 14.8 \|\|

*dhanaṃ bhūmigataṃ yadvatsvahastena niveśitam \|*  
*tadvatphalamavāpnoti hyahaṃ vacmi khageśvara \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. О lord of birds, I affirm that just as seeds and other valuable things invested or sown in the ground offer fruitful results so also things offered by one's own hands yield fruitful results.

## \|\| 14.9 \|\|

*aputro 'pi viśeṣeṇa kriyāṃ caivāndhvadauhikīm \|*  
*prakuryānmokṣakāmaśca nirdhanaśca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Particularly, a man having no son shall perform the funeral rites in his life-time and the same is true with those who crave for salvation and even indigent persons.

## \|\| 14.10 \|\|

*svalpenāpi hi vittena svayaṃ hastena yatkṛtam \|*  
*akṣayaṃ yāti tatsarvaṃ yathājyaṃ ca hutāśane \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Any sacred rite performed by ourselves even with very little wealth accords undying results, just like drops of clarified butter offered into the fire with sacred mantras.

## \|\| 14.11 \|\|

*ekā caikasya dātavyā śayyā kanyā payasvinī \|*  
*sā vikrītā vā dahatyāsaptamaṃ kulam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. A gift of a single bed, a virgin, and a cow. if sold or shared bums the members of the family till the seventh generation.

## \|\| 14.12 \|\|

*tasmātsarvaṃ prakurvīta cañcale jīvite sati \|*  
*gṛhītadānapātheyaḥ sukhaṃ yāti mahādhvani \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Therefore, life being unsteady it is incumbent on everyone to do every thing beforehand. With the food for the journey in the form of gifts offered to the deserving, one is able-to trawl with comfort in the Great Highway in the journey after death.

## \|\| 14.13 \|\|

*anyathā kliśyate jantuḥ pātheyarahitaḥ pathi \|*  
*evaṃ jñātvā khagaśreṣṭha vṛṣayajñaṃ samācaret \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Otherwise, the wretch undergoes great' hardships without the food to subsist pn, on the way. О foremost among birds, after realising this, one shall perform Vrsayajna.

## \|\| 14.14 \|\|

*akṛtvā mriyate yastu aputro naiva muktibhāk \|*  
*aputro 'pi hi yaḥ kuryātsukhaṃ yāti mahāpathe \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Even a man having sons, dying without the performance of these rites, does not attain salvation. A man without sons by doing these rites beforehand shall have a happy journey on the Great Highway.

## \|\| 14.15 \|\|

*agnihotrādhibhiryajñairdānaiśca vividhairapi \|*  
*na tāṃ gatimavāpnoti vṛṣotsargeṇa yā gatiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The goal that is reached by Vrsotsarga cannot be attained by the performance of sacrifices like Agnihotra, etc. or by various kinds of gifts.

## \|\| 14.16 \|\|

*yajñānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ vṛṣayajñastathottamaḥ \|*  
*tasmātsarvaprayatnena vṛṣayajñaṃ samācaret \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Among all sacrifices the Vrsayajna is the best. Hence, it must be performed with redoubled efforts.

## \|\| 14.17 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kathayasva prasādena kṣayāhaṃ caurdhvadaihikam \|*  
*kasminkāle tithau kasyāṃ vidhinā kena tadbhavet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Garuda said: — Please tell me the details of Vrsayajna when, on what Tttht, by what rites shall it be performed?

## \|\| 14.18 \|\|

*kṛtvā kiṃ phalamāpnoti etanme vada sāmpratam \|*  
*tvatprasādena govinda mukte bhavati mānavaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. What is The fruit that he reaps after performing the same? Now please tell me this.

## \|\| 14.19-21 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*kārtikādiṣu māseṣu yāmyāyatagate ravau \|*  
*śuklapakṣe tathā pakṣindvādaśyāditithau śubhe \|\| 19 \|\|*

*śubhe lagne muhūrte vā śucau deśe samāhitaḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇaṃ tu samāhūya vidhijñaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*japahomaistathā dānaiḥ kuryāddahesya śodhanam \|*  
*puṇye 'bhijitsunakṣatre grahāndevānsamarcayet \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. Sri Krsna said: — When the sun is in uttarayana (northern Transit i.e. proceeding away from the tropic of Capricorn) in the months of Karttika, etc., on the twelfth day of either the bright or dark-lunar fortnight, on an auspicious hour, in, a clean place, he shall perform the rites of Japa and Нота after inviting a pious-brahmin conversant with the rites.

## \|\| 14.22 \|\|

*homaṃ kuryādyathāśakti mantraiśca vividhairapi \|*  
*grahāṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ kuryātpūrvaṃ caiva khageśvara \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. On a holy day in conjunction with an auspicious-star he shall worship the planets and deities. He shall perform Нота according to his capacity with various auspicious mantras.

## \|\| 14.23 \|\|

*mātṝṇāṃ pūjanaṃ kāryaṃ vasordhārāṃ ca pātayet \|*  
*vahniṃ saṃsthāpya tatraiva pūrṇaṃ homaṃ tu kārayet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Hе shall fix the planets mystically and worship,. О lord of birds. He shall then perform the worship of the Mothers and Vasudhara (offerings of gold or other coins in a. stream).

## \|\| 14.24 \|\|

*śālagrāmaṃ ca saṃsthāpya vaiṣṇavaṃ śrāddhamācaret \|*  
*vṛṣaṃ sampūjya tatraiva vastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. After kindling the fire he shall perform the Purna Нота. Then he shall instal Shalagrama and perform Vaisnava Srdddha.

## \|\| 14.25 \|\|

*catasro vatsataryaśca pūrvaṃ samadhivāsayet \|*  
*pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kuryāddhomānte ca visarjanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The Vrsa (bull calf) must be bedecked with good-clothes and ornaments. Four heifers must be kept in front.

## \|\| 14.26 \|\|

*imaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya uttarābhimukhaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*dharma tvaṃ vṛṣarūpeṇa brahmaṇā nirmitaḥ purā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. At the end of the Нота the householder shall circumambulate the bull and let it loose reciting the following mantra and facing the north.

## \|\| 14.27 \|\|

*tavotsargaprabhāvānmāmuddharasvabhavārṇāvāt \|*  
*abiṣicya śubhairmantraiḥ pāvanairvidhipūrvakam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. "O Bull, thou art Dharma (virtue) created by Brahma in the form of a bull. Please raise one up from the ocean of worldly existence by means of the power of this setting free."

## \|\| 14.28 \|\|

*tanakrīḍantimantreṇa vṛṣotsargaṃ tu kārayet \|*  
*abhiṣiñcettato nīlaṃ rudrakumbho dakena tu \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. With this mantra and along with the holy water from Rudrakumbha (the water pot sanctified by Rudra mantras) poured over its head, the Visarga. rite is performed. The water pot is then placed on Darbha grass.

## \|\| 14.29-30 \|\|

*nābhimūle samāsthāya tadambu mūrdhani nyaset \|*  
*anna (ātma) śrāddhaṃ tataḥ kuryāddadyāddānaṃ dvijottame \|\| 29 \|\|*

*udakecaiva gantavyaṃ jalaṃ tatra pradāpayet \|*  
*yadiṣṭaṃ jīvatastvāsīttacca dadyātsvaśaktitaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. After duly pouring the water with due recital of mantras in accordance with the injunctions in the sacred, texts, he recites the mantra Tena Krida etc and finally releases the bull. He shall then perform Atmasrdddha and feed, a pious brahmin and offer water unto him.

## \|\| 14.31 \|\|

*nyūnaṃ saṃpūrṇatāṃ yāti vṛṣotsarge kṛte sati \|*  
*sutṛpto dustare mārge mṛto yāti na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. What he — liked most in his life time must be made over as a gift in accordance with one's capacity. Then after death he shall be able to go along the rugged path (to the city of Yama) without any difficulty.

## \|\| 14.32 \|\|

*yamalokaṃ na paśyanti sadā dānaratā narāḥ \|*  
*yāvanna dīyate jantoḥ śrāddhaṃ caikādaśāhikam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. If the Srdddha is not performed on the eleventh day, neither what is offered by himself here nor what is offered by others reaches him in the other world.

## \|\| 14.33 \|\|

*svadattaṃ paradattaṃ vā nehāmutropatiṣṭhati \|*  
*trayodaśā tathā sapta pañca trīṇī krameṇa tu \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He shall then make over Padaddnas thirteen, seven, five or three with great faith.

## \|\| 14.34 \|\|

*padadānāni kurvīta śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ \|*  
*tilapātrāṇi kurvīta sapta pañca yathākramam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Three, five or seven pots of gingelly seeds shall be gifted away and Brahmins must be fed. He shall then offer a cow as a gift.

## \|\| 14.35-36 \|\|

*brāhmaṇān bhojayetpaścādekāṃ gāṃ ca pradāpayet \|*  
*vṛṣaṃ hi śannodevīti vedoktavidhinā tataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*catasṛbhirvatsatarībhiḥ pariṇayanamācaret \|*  
*vāme cakraṃ pradātavyaṃ triśūlaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. If a bull calf is gifted a Cakra shall be placed on its left side, a trident on its right and a garland round its neck.

## \|\| 14.37 \|\|

*mūlyaṃ dadyād vṛṣasyāpi taṃ vṛṣaṃ ca visarjayet \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭavidhānena svāhākāreṇa vuddhimān \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The eleventh day rites are to be performed like the Ekoddista rite and the twelfth day rites are performed with Svdhdkara.

## \|\| 14.38 \|\|

*kuryādekādaśāhaṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ ca yatnataḥ \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇādarvākkuryācchrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Before the Sapindikarana, sixteen Sraddhas have to be performed. After feeding the brahmins Padaddna must be made.

## \|\| 14.39 \|\|

*brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu padadānāni dāpayet \|*  
*kāposopari saṃsthāpya tāmrapātre tathācyutam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. A cloth is spread on the ground and a copper vessel is kept over it. The idol of Acyuta is kept in it and covered with a cloth. This is given along with Arghya and good fruits as gifts.

## \|\| 14.40 \|\|

*vastreṇācchādya tatrasthamarghaṃ dadyācchubhaiḥ phalaiḥ \|*  
*nāvamikṣumayīṃ kṛtvā paṭṭasūtreṇa veṣṭayet \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. A boat is to be made with sugar cane stems and covered with a cloth. Clarified butter is kept in a bell metal vessel. This is the symbolic offering to enable him to cross the Vaitaranl river.

## \|\| 14.41 \|\|

*kāṃsyapātre ghṛtaṃ sthāpya vaitaraṇyā nimittataḥ \|*  
*nāvārohaṇaṃ kuryātpūjayedgaruḍadhvajam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The boat is offered for crossing. Garuda- dhvaja is then worshipped. In accordance with one's own wealth many such gifts can be given.

## \|\| 14.42 \|\|

*ātmavittānusāreṇa tacca dānamanantakam \|*  
*bhavasāgaramagnānāṃ śokatāpārtiduḥ khinām \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Janardana is the god who saves people, who are sunk in the ocean of worldly existence, who are dejected due to its waves of sorrow and distress and who are devoid of the boat of Dharma (virtue).

## \|\| 14.43 \|\|

*dharmaplavavihīnānāṃ tārako hi janārdanaḥ \|*  
*tilā lohaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca kārpāsaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Gingelly seeds, metallic substances, gold, cloth, salt, even grains, plots of land and cows- any of these can be given as Dana. It is holy.

## \|\| 14.44 \|\|

*saptadhānyaṃ kṣitirgāvo hyekaikaṃ pāvanaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*tilapātrāṇi kurvīta śayyādānaṃ ca dāpayet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Vessels full of gingelly seeds or beds can be gifted. According to one's capacity, Daksind can be given to poor people, orphans or noble and pious persons.

## \|\| 14.45 \|\|

*dīnānāthaviśiṣṭebhyo dadyācchaktyā ca dakṣiṇām \|*  
*evaṃ yaḥ kurute tārkṣya putravānapyaputravān \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. О Tarksya he who performs thus, whether he has a son, or no son, obtains great siddhis (achievements) like one who has realised Brahma.

## \|\| 14.46 \|\|

*sa siddhiṃ samavāpnoti yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ \|*  
*nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kuryādyāvajjīvati mānavaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. As long as one lives, one shall perform Nitya and Naimittika. rites. Whatever one does righteously shall yield him good results.

## \|\| 14.47-49 \|\|

*yaḥ kaścitkriyate dharmastatphalaṃ cākṣayaṃ bhavet \|*  
*tīrthayātrāvratādīnāṃ śrāddhaṃ saṃvatsarasya hi \|\| 47 \|\|*

*devatānāṃ gurūṇāṃ ca mātāpitrostathaiva ca \|*  
*puṇyaṃ deyaṃ prayatnena pratyahaṃ vardhate khaga \|\| 48 \|\|*

*asminyajñeḥ hiyaḥ kaścidbhūridānaṃ prayacchati \|*  
*tattasya cākṣayaṃ sarvaṃ vedikāyāṃ yathā kila \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. In the rites concerning pilgrimages to holy centres, in the annual sraddha, etc., and in those of gods, preceptors, mother or father, gifts made thus are holy. He who makes gifts in this yajna, О bird, flourishes every day.

## \|\| 14.50-51 \|\|

*yathā pūjyatamā loke yatayo brahmacāriṇaḥ \|*  
*tathaiva pratipūjyante loke sarve ca nityaśaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*varado 'haṃ sadā tasya caturvaktrastathā haraḥ \|*  
*te yānti paramāṃllokāniti satyaṃ vaco mama \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. It yields undying results as the rites performed in altars. Just as Brahmacarins are worthy of worship those who give gifts are also worshipped. I grant boon unto them. Similarly, the fourfaced god Brahma and diva too grant boons.

## \|\| 14.52 \|\|

*utsṛṣṭo vṛṣabho yatra pibatyapo jalāśaye \|*  
*śṛṅgeṇālikhate vāpi bhūmiṃ nityaṃ praharṣitaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Wherever in the tank, the offered bull drinks water or digs the earth with horns, there enough food and water for the manes exist.

## \|\| 14.53-54 \|\|

*pitṝṇāmannapānaṃ ca prabhūtamupatiṣṭhati \|*  
*paurṇamāsyāmamāyāṃ vā tilapātrāṇi dāpayet \|\| 53 \|\|*

*saṃkrāntīnāṃ sahasrāṇi sūryaparvaśatāni ca \|*  
*dattvā yatphalamāpnoti tadvai nīlavisarjane \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. This is my affirmed truth that they go to great regions. On full moon days or in conjunction with Revati star a blue calf is to be set free. In setting free a blue bull calf, the merit accrued is the same as in performing thousands of Sankranti rites or hundreds of rites of solar eclipse.

## \|\| 14.55 \|\|

*vatsataryaḥ pradātavyā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ padāni ca \|*  
*tilapātrāṇi deyāni śivabhaktadvijeṣu ca \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Heifer must be given to a brahmin. Padas, vessels of gingelly seeds, etc. must be-given to the brahmin devotees of diva.

## \|\| 14.56-57 \|\|

*umāmaheśvaraṃ caikaṃ paridhāpya pridāpayet \|*  
*atasīpuṣpasaṅkāśaṃ pītavāsasamacyutam \|\| 56 \|\|*

*ye namasyanti govindaṃ na teṣāṃ vidyate bhayam \|*  
*pretatvānmokṣamicchanto ye kariṣyanti satkriyām \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. After worshipping Uma and Mahesvara, Acyuta, dark as the Masi flower and robed in yellow garments, must be worshipped. Those who do so need not be afraid of anyone. Those who desire to escape ghosthood shall perform their respective rites.

## \|\| 14.58-59 \|\|

*yāsyanti te parāṃllokāniti satyaṃ vaco mama \|*  
*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ mayā caivordhvadaihikam \|\| 58 \|\|*

*yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*śrutvā mahātmyamatulaṃ garuḍo harṣamāgataḥ \|*  
*mānuṣāṇāṃ hitārthāya punaḥ prapacchakeśavam \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. I have thus mentioned everything connected with the rites of obsequies. On hearing this, one freed from sins goes to Visnu's region. On hearing this glory of performing rites and the merits of gifts expounded by the lord, Garuda was delighted. He bowed his head down and asked the lord again.

## Chapter 15

## \|\| 15.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*bagavanbrūhi me sarvaṃ yamalokasya nirṇayam \|*  
*jantoḥ prayāṇamārabhya māhātmyaṃ vartmavistaram \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — О lord, please give me a decisive description of Yama's region, its dimension, extent and greatness.

## \|\| 15.2 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tārkṣya pravakṣyāmi yamamārgasya nirṇayam \|*  
*prayāṇakāni sarvāṇi nagarāṇi ca ṣoḍaśa \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Bhagawan said: — О Tarksya, listen. I shall describe the region of Yama. All the sixteen worlds are very extensive.

## \|\| 15.3 \|\|

*ṣāḍaśītisahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ pramāṇataḥ \|*  
*yamalokasya cordhvaṃ vai antarā mānuṣasya ca \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The distance between the Earth and Yama's region is more than a million and thirtytwo thousand kilometers.

## \|\| 15.4 \|\|

*kukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vāpi bhuktvā loke yathārjitam \|*  
*karmayogādyadā kaścidvyādhirutpadyate khaga \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О bird, after enjoying the fruits of his merits and demerits in the world, a man falls sick due to the effect of some previous action.

## \|\| 15.5 \|\|

*nimittamātraṃ sarveṣāṃ kṛtakarmānusārataḥ \|*  
*yasya yo vihito mṛtyuḥ sa taṃ dhruvamavāpnuyāt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. This sickness is due to a chance cause. Death occurs to a man in accordance with his actions taking their own definite course.

## \|\| 15.6 \|\|

*karmayogādyadā dehī muñcatyatra nijaṃ vapuḥ \|*  
*tadā bhūmigataṃ kuryādgomayenopalipya ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The individual casts off his body and dies due to the effects of his actions. His dead body must be kept on the ground after purifying it with cowdung.

## \|\| 15.7 \|\|

*tilāndarbhānvikīryātha mukhe svarṇaṃ viniḥ kṣipet \|*  
*tulasīṃsannidhau kṛtvā śālagrāmaśilāṃ tathā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Gingelly seeds and Kusa grass must be scattered. A piece of gold is piaced in the mouth of the dead man. Tulasi leaves and the salagrama rock are placed near it.

## \|\| 15.8 \|\|

*setu (evaṃ) sāmādisūktaistu maraṇaṃ muktidāyakam \|*  
*śalākāsvarṇavikṣapaiḥ prataprāṇigṛheṣuca \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Samasuktas are recited near him. This is conducive to his salvation. Rods or pieces of gold are to be placed for the benefit of the dead man in his vital opertures.

## \|\| 15.9 \|\|

*ekā vaktre tu dātavyā ghrāṇayugme tathā punaḥ \|*  
*akṣṇośca karṇayoścaiva dvedve deye yathākramam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. One piece in the mouth, one in each of the nostrils, eyes and ears are to be placed in this order of enumeration,

## \|\| 15.10 \|\|

*atha liṅge tathā caikā tvekāṃ brahmāṇḍakekṣipet \|*  
*karayugme ca kaṇṭhe ca tulasīṃ ca pradāpayet \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. One piece in the operture of the genitals and one piece in the rectum. Tulasi leaves are placed in both the hands and the neck.

## \|\| 15.11 \|\|

*vastrayugmaṃ ca dātavyaṃ kaṅkumaiścākṣatairyajet \|*  
*puṣpamālāyutaṃ kuryādanyadvāreṇa sannayet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The dead body is to be covered with two clothes. Saffron and raw rice grains are strewn. It is then decorated with flower garlands. The body is to be taken out through the back doorway.

## \|\| 15.12-13 \|\|

*putrastu bāndhavaiḥ sārdhaṃ viprastu puravāsibhiḥ \|*  
*pituḥ pretaṃ svayaṃ putraḥ skandhamāropya bāndhavaiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*gatvā śmaśānadeśe tu prāḍmukhaścottarāmukham \|*  
*adagdhapūrvā yā bhūmiścitāṃ tatraiva kārayet \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. The son with his relatives and the Brahmanas of the town should carry the corpse on the shoulder to the cremation ground. The corpse should be placed on the pyre with the face of the dead to the north, while the son should sit on the ground with his lace turned to the east. The pyre should be made on the ground on which no other dead body has been cremated presently.

## \|\| 15.14-15 \|\|

*śrīkhaṇḍatulasīkāṣṭhasamitpālāśasaṃbhṛtām \|*  
*vikalendriyasaṅghāte caitanye jaḍatāṃ gate \|\| 14 \|\|*

*pracalanti tataḥ prāṇā yāmyairnikaṭavartibhiḥ \|*  
*ekībhūtaṃ jagatpaśyeddaivī dṛṣṭiḥ prajāyate \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. The pyre should be made of woods of sandal, Tulasi, and Palasa. When, the organs lose their functioning power, consciousness is benumbed and the messengers of Yama are near at hand, the breath leaves the body. The departed soul attains divine vision and can see the universe at a glance.

## \|\| 15.16-17 \|\|

*bībhatsaṃ dāruṇaṃ rūpaṃ praṇaiḥ kaṇṭhaṃ samāśritaiḥ \|*  
*phenamudgirate kopi mukhaṃ lālākulaṃ bhavet \|\| 16 \|\|*

*durātmānaśca tāḍyante kiṅkaraiḥ pāśabandhanaiḥ \|*  
*sukhena kṛtinastatra nīyante nākanāyakaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. He observes the dreadful form of Yama even when he is on the verge of death. He sees Yama's servants beating the wicked with the cane. He sees the attendants of Visnu cheering the good.

## \|\| 15.18-19 \|\|

*duḥ khena pāpino yānti yamamārge ca durgame \|*  
*yamaścaturbhujo bhūtvā śaṅkhacakragadādibhṛt \|\| 18 \|\|*

*puṇyakarmaratānsamyak śubhānmitravadācaret \|*  
*āhūya pāpinaḥ sarvānyamo daṇḍena rajjayet \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. The path of Yamaloka is hard to pass. The sinners traverse the path in discomfort. Yama assumes a form of body with four arms, holding conch, discus, bow and mace. He treats the pious with amity and accord and chastises the sinful with the rod of iron.

## \|\| 15.20-22 \|\|

*pralayāmbudanirghoṣastvañjanādrisamaprabhaḥ \|*  
*mahiṣastho durārādhyo vidyuttejaḥ samadyutiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*yojanatrayavistāradeho raudro 'tibhīṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*lohadaṇḍadharo bhīmaḥ pāśapāṇirdurākṛtiḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*vakranetro 'tibhayado darśanaṃ yāti pāpinām \|*  
*aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo hāhā kurvan kalevarāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. He thunders like clouds at the time of dissolution. He looks as dreadful and dark as a mount of collyrium. He is seated on a buffalo. He can be easily propitiated or appeased by the devotee. His body shines as the lustre of lightning. It is as long as the three Yojanas in dimension. He is terrible to look.at, wields,an iron-rod and noose in his hands. His bodily appearance and eyes are dreadful to the sinners. The soul in human body is of the size of a thumb.

## \|\| 15.23-24 \|\|

*tadaiva nīyate dūtairyāmyairvokṣansvakaṃ gṛham \|*  
*nirviceṣṭaṃ śarīraṃ tu prāṇairmuktaṃ jugupsitam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*aspṛśyaṃ jāyate tūrṇaṃ durgandhaṃ sarvaninditam \|*  
*tridhāvasthā hi dehasya kṛmiviḍmasmasaṃjñitā \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. When it is dragged out of the body by the messengers of Yama, it cries painfully looking wistfully at his erstwhile home. The disgustful body without life and breath becomes untouchable suddenly, smells foul and is disliked by all. The body suffers in three ways: Either if is eaten by worms or transformed to feces or reduced to ashes.

## \|\| 15.25-29 \|\|

*ko garvaḥ kriyate tārkṣya kṣaṇavidhvaṃsibhirnaraiḥ \|*  
*dānaṃ vittādṛtā vācaḥ kīrtidharmau tathāyuṣaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*paropakaraṇaṃ kāyādasataḥ sāramuddhṛtam \|*  
*tasyaivaṃ nīyamānasya dūtāḥ santarjayanti hi \|\| 26 \|\|*

*darśayanto bhayaṃ tīvraṃ narakāya punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*śīghraṃ pracala duṣṭātman gato 'sitvaṃ yamālaye \|\| 27 \|\|*

*kuṃbhīpākādinarakāṃstvāṃ neṣyāmaśca mā ciram \|*  
*evaṃ vācastadā śṛṇvanbandhūnāṃ ruditaṃ tathā \|\| 28 \|\|*

*uccairhāheti vilapannīyate yamakiṅkaraiḥ \|*  
*sthāne śrāddhaṃ prakurvīta tathā cekādaśe 'hani \|\| 29 \|\|*

25-29. О Garuda, Body is liable to destruction in an instant. How can a man boast of it?

The purpose of wealth is charity, that of speech is truth, that of life is fame and spirituality, that of body is benevolence. In this way, one can gain something substantial out of the things unsubstantial.

When the departed soul is being carried away, the messengers chastise him in the way. They describe the dreadful fate that awaits him in hell.

They tell him thus: 'O wicked soul, walk quickly. You are being taken to hell. We carry you to KumbHipaka and kindred hells. Make haste and do, not delay."

Thus hearing the words of messengers and the lamentations of his relatives he cries aloud and is carried by Yama's messengers.

## \|\| 15.30-31 \|\|

*mṛsyotkrāntisamayātṣaṭ piṇḍānkramaśo dadet \|*  
*mṛtasthāne tathā dvāre catvare tārkṣya kāraṇāt \|\| 30 \|\|*

*viśrāme kāṣṭhacayane tathā sañcayane ca ṣaṭ \|*  
*śṛṇu tatkāraṇaṃ tārkṣya ṣaṭ piṇḍaparikalpane \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. The son should perform sraddha at the appropriate place. He should offer six pindas, on the day of death in the serial order: (1) at the place where the person breathes his last (2) at the door-way, (3) at the courtyard, (4) at the resting place, (5) by the pyre and (6) at the collection of bones.

О Garuda, I shall now tell you the. purpose why the six pindas are offered to the dead.

## \|\| 15.33 \|\|

*tena bhūmirbhavettuṣṭātadadhiṣṭhātṛdevatā \|*  
*dvāre tu piṇḍaṃ deyaṃ ca pānthamityabhidhāya tu \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The pinda that is offered to the dead at the place of death pleases the household deities. This pinda satiates both-the earth and the presiding deity. The doorway pinda should be given addressing the departed soul as pantha (passenger).

## \|\| 15.34 \|\|

*dattena tena prīṇanti dvārasthā gṛhadevatāḥ \|*  
*catvare khecaro nāma tamuddiśya pradāpayet \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The gift of this pinda pleases the household deities that abide at the door. In the courtyard lives the Khecara. A pinda should be given in his name.

## \|\| 15.35 \|\|

*na copaghātaṃ kurvanti bhūtādyā devayonayaḥ \|*  
*viśrāme bhūtasaṃjño 'yaṃ tena tatra pradāpayet \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. At the resting place, he should offer a pinda to the Bhuta categorized as Deva. This pinda is, therefore, called bhuta. The gift of this pinda to the Bhuta makes him genial to the dead.

## \|\| 15.36 \|\|

*piśācā rākṣasā yakṣā ye cānye diśi vāsinaḥ \|*  
*tasya hotavyadehasyanaivāyogyatvakārakāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Pisacas, Raksasas, Yaksas and the rest preserve the sanctity of the body which is sacrificed in, the fire.

## \|\| 15.37 \|\|

*citāpiṇḍhaprabhṛtitaḥ pretatvamupajāyate \|*  
*citāyāṃ sādhakaṃ nāma vadantyeke khageśvara \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. When the pinda is offered by the side of the pyre, the dead is designated as preta. But, О Garuda, instead of calling him preta, some call him Sadhaka.

## \|\| 15.38 \|\|

*kecittaṃ pretamevāhuryathā kalpavido budhāḥ \|*  
*tadādi tatratatrāpi pretanāmnā pradīyate \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. But, generally, he is called preta. Henceforth, the offerings are made to him in the name of preta.

## \|\| 15.39 \|\|

*ityevaṃ pañcabhiḥ piṇḍaiḥ śavasyāhutiyogyatā \|*  
*anyathā copaghātāya pūrvoktāste bhavanti hi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. By gifting away the five pindas, the body achieves sanctity and fitness to be sacrificed in the fire. Otherwise, the presiding deities, as stated above, cause large-scale destruction.

## \|\| 15.40 \|\|

*utkrāme prathamaṃ piṇḍaṃ tathā cārdhapayepi ca \|*  
*citāyāṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ syāttrayaḥ piṇḍāśca kalpitāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Three pindas are necessary: (1) one at the time of death, (2) another in the half-way from the place of death to the cremation ground and still another by the side of the pyre.

## \|\| 15.41 \|\|

*vidhātā prathame piṇḍe dvitīye garuḍadhvajaḥ \|*  
*tṛtīye yamadūtāśca prayogaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*
