*te sarve pretayonisthā vicaranti marusthale \|\| 13 \|\|*

11-13. those who fall from mountains and die, those who die when walls collapse, those who are denied by women in their menses, those who die in the firmament and those who are forgetful of Visnu, those who continue to associate with persons defiled due to births or death, those who die of dog-biting or meet with death in a foul manner, become ghosts and roam over the earth.

## \|\| 22.14 \|\|

*mātaraṃ bhaginīṃ bhāryāṃ snuṣāṃ duhitaraṃ tathā \|*  
*adṛṣṭadoṣāṃ tyajati sa preto jāyatedhruvam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. One who discards one's mother, sister, wife, daughter or daughter-in-law without seeing any fault in them, obtains ghosthood surely.

## \|\| 22.15 \|\|

*bhrātṛdhrugbrahmahā goghnaḥ surāpo gurutalpagaḥ \|*  
*hemakṣaumaharastārkṣya sa vai pretatvamāpnuyāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. One who deceives his own brother, kills a Brahmana or a cow, drinks liquor, defiles the preceptor's bed, steals gold and silk-garments, becomes a ghost, О bird.

## \|\| 22.16 \|\|

*nyāsāpahartā mitradhruk paradāraratastathā \|*  
*viśvāsaghātī krūrastu sa preto jāyate dhruvam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. One who usurps a deposit, deceives a friend, enjoys other man's wife, kills other's faith, is cruel, definitely becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.17 \|\|

*kulamārgāṃśca santyajya paradharmaratastathā \|*  
*vidyāvṛttavihīnaśca sa preto jāyatedhruvam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. One who discards the family-customs, takes to other customs, is without knowledge and good character, definitely becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.18 \|\|

*atraivodāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam \|*  
*yudhiṣṭhirasya saṃvādaṃ bhīṣmeṇa saha suvrata \|*  
*tadahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yacchrutvā saukhyamāpnuyāt \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. To illustrate this there is an anecdote narrated by Bhisma to Yudhisthira. О you of good rites, I shall narrate the same to you, on hearing which you may feel pleasure.

## \|\| 22.19 \|\|

*yudhiṣṭhira uvāca \|*  
*kena karmavipākena pretatvamupajāyate \|*  
*kena vā mucyate kasmāttanme brūhi pitāmaha \|*  
*yacchrutvā na punarmohamevaṃ yāsyā mi suvrata \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Yudhisthira said: — О grandfather, please tell me what those evil deeds are as a result of which one becomes a ghost and what are the means of redemption from the same on hearing which I shall not be deluded thus further.

## \|\| 22.20 \|\|

*bhīṣma uvāca \|*  
*yenaiva jāyate preto yenaiva sa vimucyate \|*  
*prāpnoti narakaṃ ghoraṃ dustaraṃ daivatairapi \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Bhisma said: — I shall tell you entirely what those causes are whereby one turns a ghost and how he is set free after falling into a dismal hell impassable even to gods.

## \|\| 22.21 \|\|

*satataṃ śravaṇādyasya puṇyaśravaṇakīrtanāt \|*  
*mānavā vipramucyante āpannāḥ pretayoniṣu \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. I shall tell all those things, on hearing which a person is set free from ghosthood.

## \|\| 22.22 \|\|

*śrūyate hi purā vatsa brāhmaṇaḥ śaṃsitavrataḥ \|*  
*nāmnā santaptakaḥ khyāta stapor'the vanamāśritaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. О dear, there was a brahmin of rigorous sacred rites named Santaptaka. For practising penance, he went to a forest.

## \|\| 22.23 \|\|

*svādhyāyayukto homena yo (yā) gayukto dayānvitaḥ \|*  
*yajansa sakalānyajñānyuktyā kālaṃ ca vikṣipan \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He was a man of kind, compassionate nature. He used to perform homas and yogic practices as well as great sacrifices. He used to spend time usefully engaged.

## \|\| 22.24 \|\|

*brahāmacaryasamāyukto yuktastapasi mārdave \|*  
*paralokabhayopetaḥ satyaśaucaiśca nirmalaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He strictly observed celibacy. He observed penances. He was soft-hearted, truthful and pure. He was afraid of the other world.

## \|\| 22.25 \|\|

*yukto 'hi guruvākyena yuktaścātithipūjane \|*  
*ātmayoge sadodyuktaḥ sarvadvandvavivarjitaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He strictly followed the instructions of his preceptor. He was delighted in serving guests. He observed yogic practices. He was free from Duandvas (like happiness and misery, heat and cold and such opposite pairs).

## \|\| 22.26 \|\|

*yogābhyāse sadā yuktaḥ saṃsāravijigīṣayā \|*  
*evaṃvṛttaḥ sadācāro mokṣakāṅkṣī jitendriyaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Practising yoga incessantly to conquer mundane existence, he subjugated the sense organs. Following the path of good conduct he eagerly desired salvation.

## \|\| 22.27 \|\|

*bahūnyabdāni vijane vane tasya gatāni vai \|*  
*tasya buddhistato jātā tīrthānugamanaṃ prati \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. He spent years in the secluded forest. Then the idea of visiting holy centres entered his mind.

## \|\| 22.28 \|\|

*puṇyaistīrthajalaireva śoṣayiṣye kalevaram \|*  
*sa tīrthetvaritaṃ snātvā tapasvī bhāskarodaye \|*  
*kṛtajāpyanamaskāro hyadhvānaṃ pratyapadyata \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He thought within himself "I shall keep immersed my body in the waters of a holy river till I die." Accordingly he hastened to a holy centre where he took bath at sunrise. He performed the rites of Japa and Namaskara (obeisance) and started on journey.

## \|\| 22.29-31 \|\|

*ekasmindivase vipro mārgabhraṣṭo mahātapāḥ \|*  
*dadarśādhvani gacchansa pañca pretān sudāruṇān \|\| 29 \|\|*

*araṇye nirjane deśe saṃkaṭe vṛkṣavarjite \|*  
*pañcaitānvikṛtākārāndṛṣṭvā vai ghoradarśanān \|*  
*īṣatsantrastahṛdayo 'tiṣṭhadunmīlya locane \|\| 30 \|\|*

*avalambya tato dhairyaṃ bhayamutsṛjya dūrataḥ \|*  
*papraccha madhurābhāṣī ke yūyaṃ vikṛtānanāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. One day, this brahmin of great penance lost his way and reached a forest full of thorny shrubs, secluded and devoid of big trees. While he was hurrying up, he saw five terrible ghosts. On seeing these live awful ghosts of deformed features he was terrified and he closed his eyes in sheer fright. Then, he cast off his fear and became bold enough to ask in sweet words "O sires, how is it that you are so deformed?"

## \|\| 22.32 \|\|

*kiñcāśubhaṃ kṛtaṃ karma yena prāptāḥ stha vaikṛtam \|*  
*kathaṃ vā caikataḥ karma prasthitāḥ kutra niścitam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. What was the sin committed by you? Wherefore have you attained this deformity? Where are you procccedmg in company?

## \|\| 22.33 \|\|

*pretarāja uvāca \|*  
*svaiḥ svaistu karmabhiḥ prāptaṃ pretatvaṃ hi dvijottama \|*  
*paradroharatāḥ sarve pāpamṛtyuvaśaṃ gatāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Pretaraja said: — О excellent brahmin, our ghosthood is the outcome of our own misdeeds. We had been engaged in harassing others. Hence we became victims of foul death.

## \|\| 22.34 \|\|

*kṣutpipāsārditā nityaṃ pretatvaṃ samupāgatāḥ \|*  
*hatavākyā hataśrīkā hata saṃjñā vicetasaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. In this state of our ghosthood we are oppressed with hunger and thirst. We are unable to speak. We have lost our mental equilibrium. We have lost consciousness too.

## \|\| 22.35 \|\|

*na jānīmo diśaṃ tāta vidiśaṃ cātiduḥ khitāḥ \|*  
*kva nu gacchāmahe mūḍhāḥ piśācāḥ karmajā vayam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. We are Pisacas born of our own misdeeds. We do not know the difference between one quarter and another. We are exiremely distressed. We do not know where we go.

## \|\| 22.36 \|\|

*na mātā na pitāsmākaṃ pretatvaṃ karmabhiḥ svakaiḥ \|*  
*prāptāḥ sma sahasā jātaduḥ khodvegasamākulam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. We have neither fathers nor mothers. This ghosthood is due to our own misdeeds. We are extremely dejected and sorrowful because the attack is all loo sudden.

## \|\| 22.37 \|\|

*darśanena ca te brahmanmuditāpyāyitā vayam \|*  
*muhūrtantiṣṭha vakṣyāmi vṛttāntaṃ sarvamāditaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О Brahmin, we are delighted on seeing you. We feel refreshed- Please wait a little. I shall narrate everything from the very beginning.

## \|\| 22.38 \|\|

*ahaṃ paryuṣito nāma eṣa sūcīmukhastathā \|*  
*śīghrago rogha (ha) kaścaiva pañcamo lekhakaḥ smatṛtaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. My name is Paryyusita. This ghost is known as Suclmukha. The other one is Sighraga and the others are Rohita and Lekhaka. These are our names and we are ghosts.

## \|\| 22.39 \|\|

*evaṃ nāmnā ca sarve vai saṃprāptāḥ pretatāṃ vayam \|*  
*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*pretānāṃ karmajātānāṃ kathaṃ vai nāmasambhavaḥ \|*  
*kiñcitkāraṇamudiśya yena brūyāḥ svanāmakān \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. How can ghosts, the outcome of evil actions, have names? You may have some purpose in view in having these names. Please tell me.

## \|\| 22.40 \|\|

*pretarāja uvāca \|*  
*mayā svādu sadā bhuktaṃ dattaṃ paryuṣitaṃ dvija \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Pretaraja said: — О excellent brahmin. While I myself took all sweet things 1 left stale things for Brahmins to eat.

## \|\| 22.41 \|\|

*śīghraṃ gacchati vipreṇa yācitaḥ kṣudhitena vai \|*  
*etatkāraṇamuddiśya nāma paryuṣitaṃ mama \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. While I was on earth as a man, I showed the hungry Brahmins the exit door. Hence, my name is Paryyusita.

## \|\| 22.42 \|\|

*śīghraṃ gacchati vipreṇa yācitaḥ kṣudhitena vai \|*  
*etatkāraṇamuddiśya śīghrago 'yaṃ dvijottama \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. O excellent Brahmin, whenever a Brahmin begged him for food, out of hunger, this ghost used to run away, hence, he is called Sighraga.

## \|\| 22.43 \|\|

*sūcitā bahavo 'nena viprā annādhikāṅkṣayā \|*  
*etatkāraṇamuddiśya eṣa sūcimukhaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. This other one irritated many Brahmins with sharp tongue when they came to him for food, hence he is called Suclmukha.

## \|\| 22.44 \|\|

*ekākī miṣṭamaśrāti poṣyavargamṛte sadā \|*  
*brāhmaṇānāmabhāvena rodha (ha) kastena cocyate \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. In his life on earth, this ghost ate sumptuously, in isolation, the food-stuffs offered to gods and manes in the absence of Brahmins. Hence, he is known as Rohaka.

## \|\| 22.45 \|\|

*purāyaṃ maunamāsthāya yācito vilikhedbhuvam \|*  
*tena karmavipākena lekhako nāma cocyate \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Whenever a needy person requested him for something, this ghost pretended to be silent and went on scratching on the ground. As a result of this he is known as Lekhaka.

## \|\| 22.46-47 \|\|

*pretatvaṃ karmabhāvena prāptaṃ nāmāni ca dvija \|*  
*meṣānano lekhako 'yaṃ rodha (ha) kaḥ parvatānanaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*śīghragaḥ puśuvaktraśca sūcakaḥ sūcivaktravān \|*  
*duḥkhitā nitarāṃ svaminpaśya rūpaviparyayam \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. Thus acquiring our ghosthood and names from our misdeeds we have got ourselves deformed too. This Lekhaka is goat-mouthed; Rohaka is mountain-faced; Slghraga is cowfaced; Sucimukha is needle-mouthed; I, Parryusita, am crane-necked.

## \|\| 22.48-49 \|\|

*kṛtvā māyāmayaṃ rūpaṃ vicarāmo mahītale \|*  
*sarve ca vikṛtākārā lamboṣṭhā vikṛtānanāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*bṛhaccharīriṇo raudrā jātāḥ svenaiva karmaṇā \|*  
*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ pretatve kāraṇaṃ mayā \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. Taking this illusory form, we wander over this wide region. We suffer from terrible distress. О Brahmin, you can judge from our deformred faces with protruding lips and twisted shape. Our teeth are long, our bodies huge, our faces crooked, due to our misdeeds. Thus I have told you how we turned ghosts.

## \|\| 22.50 \|\|

*jñānino 'pi vayaṃ sarve jātāḥ sma tava darśanāt \|*  
*yatra te śravaṇe śraddhā tatpṛccha kathayāmi te \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. We have become somewhat wise on seeing you. If you wish to hear more, you can ask us further whatever you like to know.

## \|\| 22.51 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*ye jīvā bhuvi jīvanti sarve 'pyāhāramūlakāḥ \|*  
*yuṣmākamapicāhāraṃ śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Brahmana said:-The creatures on this earth subsist on food, I wish to know precisely what you all eat for. your subsistence.

## \|\| 22.52 \|\|

*pretā ūcuḥ \|*  
*yadi te śravaṇe śraddhā āhārāṇāṃ dvijottama \|*  
*asmākaṃ tu mahībhāga śṛṇutvaṃ susamāhitaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Preta said: — If you are inclined to hear what we eat, О noble sir, listen attentively.

## \|\| 22.53 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kathayantu mahāpretā āhāraṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*ityuktāṃ brāhmaṇenemamūcuḥ pretāḥ pṛthakapṛthak \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Brahmana said: — О king of ghosts, please tell me what you eat. Thus requested the ghosts began to explain their diet respectively.

## \|\| 22.54 \|\|

*pretā ūcuḥ \|*  
*śṛṇu cāhāramasmākaṃ sarvasattbavigarhitam \|*  
*yacchrutvā garhase brahman bhūyobhūyaśca garhitam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Preta said: — О Brahmin, our diet is extremely loathsome, despised by all living beings. On hearing it from us you are sure to hate us. It is so despicable.

## \|\| 22.55 \|\|

*śleṣmamūtrapurīṣotthaṃ śarīrāṇāṃ malaiḥ saha \|*  
*ucchiṣṭaiścaiva cānyaiśca pretānāṃ bhojanaṃ bhavet \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Mucous, secretions, faeces and urine together with other exudations, filth as well as leavings of food constitute our diet.

## \|\| 22.56 \|\|

*gṛhāṇi cāpyaśaucāni prakīrṇopaskarāṇi ca \|*  
*malināni prasūtāni pretā bhuñjanti tatra vai \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. We eat, drink and revel in the house where people do not pay attention to cleanliness and where they scatter litter carelessly. We haunt unclean beings as well.

## \|\| 22.57 \|\|

*nāsti satyaṃ gṛhe yatra na śaucaṃ na ca saṃyamaḥ \|*  
*patitairdasyubhiḥ saṅgaḥ pretā bhuñjanti tatra vai \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. We reside and enjoy in the house where there is no purity and where people do not observe truthfulness and restraint and where outcastes, robbers, etc. join together and take meals.

## \|\| 22.58 \|\|

*balimantravihīnāni homahīnāni yāni ca \|*  
*svādhyāya vratahīnāni pretā bhuñjanti tatra vai \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. We take delight in haunting the house where no mantras are recited, where no oblation is offered, where no horns is performed and where people do not read the Vedas regularly nor perform religious rites.

## \|\| 22.59 \|\|

*na lajjā na ca maryādā yadātra strījito gṛhī \|*  
*guravo yatra pūjyā na pretā bhuñjanti tatra vai \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. We hover round the house where gods are not honoured, where the householder is a vile wretch, without shame and decency and where the poor husband is controlled by his sturdy wife.

## \|\| 22.60 \|\|

*yatra lobhastathā krodho nidrā śoko bhayaṃ madaḥ \|*  
*ālasyaṃ kalaho nityaṃ pretā bhuñjanti tatra vai \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. We enjoy gaiety in the house where covetousness, fury, somnolence, sorrow, fear, haughtiness, lethargy, quarrels and deception reign supreme.

## \|\| 22.61 \|\|

*bhartṛhīnā ca yā nārī paravīryaṃ niṣevate \|*  
*bījaṃ mūtrasamāyurkta pretā bhuñjanti tattu vai \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. We lick up the urine mixed with semen from the vaginal passage of the widow having illicit intercourse with her paramour.

## \|\| 22.62 \|\|

*lajjā me jāyate tāta vadato bhojanaṃ svakam \|*  
*yatstrīrajo yonigataṃ pretā bhuñjanti tattu vai \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Dear friend, I am ashamed to tell you about the food we take. О pious Brahmin, we lick up the menstrual blood from the generative organ of a woman.

## \|\| 22.63 \|\|

*nirviṇṇāḥ pretabhāvena pṛcchāmi tvāṃ dṛḍhavrata \|*  
*yathā na bhavitā pretastanme vada tapodhana \|*  
*nityaṃ mṛtyurvaraṃ jantoḥ pretatvaṃ mā bhavetkvacit \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. О noble Brahmin, prefering penance to riches, and engaged in performing the sacred rites, I ask you, out of frustration. Please tell me the means of warding off ghosthood. It is better to die a hundred times than turn a ghost.

## \|\| 22.64 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*upavāsaparo nityaṃ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe rataḥ \|*  
*vrataiśca vividhaiḥ pūto na preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Brahmana said: — A person who is assiduously engaged in fasts such as Krcchra, Candrayana is never born as a ghost.

## \|\| 22.65 \|\|

*ekādaśyāṃ vrataṃ kurvañjāgareṇa samanvitam \|*  
*aparaiḥ sukṛtaiḥ pūto na preto na preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. He who observes fast, keeps awake at night and is purified by meritorious deeds is never born as a ghost.

## \|\| 22.66 \|\|

*iṣṭvā vai vāśvamedhādīndadyāddānāni yo naraḥ \|*  
*ārāmodyānavāpyādeḥ prapāyāścaiva kārakaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. He who performs Asvamedha and other sacrifices, makes liberal gifts and builds monasteries, parks, drinking water-sheds and cowpens is never born as a ghost.

## \|\| 22.67 \|\|

*kumārīṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tu vivāhayati śaktitaḥ \|*  
*vidyādo 'bhayadaścaiva na preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. He who helps brahmins to give their virgin daughters in marriage, according to his capacity, he who enables students to study, and he who accords shelter and refuge to the needy is never born as a ghost.

## \|\| 22.68 \|\|

*śūdrānnena tu bhuktena jaṭharasthena yo mṛtaḥ \|*  
*durmṛtyunā mṛto yaśca sa preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. If a man takes food offered by a fallen man and dies with that food undigested in his stomach, he is supposed to have courted a foul death and hence, he becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.69 \|\|

*ayājyayājakaścaiva yājyānāṃ ca vivarjakaḥ \|*  
*kārubhiśca rato nityaṃ sa preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. If a priest officiates at the sacrifice of an unworthy person and neglects that of a worthy sacrificer, if a man lives in the company of despicable people he becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.70 \|\|

*kṛtvā madyapasamparkaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam \|*  
*ajñānādbhakṣayanmāṃsaṃ sa preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. He who associates with drunkards or indulges in intercourse with a woman addicted to wine or eats meat unconsciously becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.71 \|\|

*devadravyaṃ ca brahmasvaṃ gurudravyaṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*kanyāṃ dadāti śulkena sa preto jāyate naraḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. He who misappropriates a brahmin's s wealth, or the property of the temple or that, of his preceptor and he who takes money from his son-in-law before giving his daughter in marriage becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.72 \|\|

*mātaraṃ bhaginīṃ bhāryāṃ snuṣāṃ duhitaraṃ tathāḥ \|*  
*adṛṣṭadoṣāstyajati sa preto jādṛ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. He who forsakes his innocent and guiltless mother sister, wife, daughter or daughter-in- law becomes a ghost.

## \|\| 22.73 \|\|

*nyāsāpahartā mitradhrukparadārarataḥ sadā \|*  
*viśvāsaghātī kūṭaśca sa predṛ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. All these are sure to be bore as ghosts a man misappropriating a trust property, a man treacherous to his friend, a man fond of another man's wife, a faithless man and a deceptive wretch.

## \|\| 22.74 \|\|

*bhrātṛdhrugbrahmahā goghnaḥ surāpo gurutalpagaḥ \|*  
*kulamārgaṃ parityajya hyanṛtoktau sadā rataḥ \|*  
*hartā hemnaśca bhūmeśca sa predṛ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. A man hating his brother, a murderer of a Brahmin, a slayer of a cow, a wineaddict, a denier of the preceptor's bed, one who casts off customary rites, or one who is fond of telling lies, a stealer of gold or one who takes possession of plots of land illegally all these are born as ghosts.

## \|\| 22.75 \|\|

*bhīṣma uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ bruvati vai vipre ākāśe dundubhisvanaḥ \|*  
*apatatpuṣpavarṣaṃ ca devarmuktaṃ dvijopari \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Bhisma said: — When the Brahmin spoke thus, the beating of drums was heard in the sky. The gods showered flowers over the Brahmin.

## \|\| 22.76 \|\|

*pañca devavimānāni pretānāmāgatāni vai \|*  
*svargaṃ gatā vimānaiste divyaiḥ saṃpṛcchya taṃ munim \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Five celestial chariots arrived there and took the ghosts away, the ghosts having taken leave of the saintly Brahmin.

## \|\| 22.77 \|\|

*jñānaṃ viprasya sambhāṣātpuṇyasaṃkīrtanena ca \|*  
*pretāḥ pāpavinirmuktāḥ paraṃ padamavāpnuyuḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. The ghosts were relieved of their sins after the pious speech of that Brahmin. They all achieved the highest region (Vaikuntha).

## \|\| 22.78 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*idamākhyānakaṃ śrutvā kampito 'śvatthapatravat \|*  
*mānuṣāṇāṃ hitārthāya garuḍaḥ pṛṣṭavānpunaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. On hearing this anecdote, the lord of birds quaked like the Asvattha tree. He asked the lord again, for the benefit of human beings.

## Chapter 23

## \|\| 23.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kiṅkiṃ kurvanti vai pretāḥ piśācatvevyavasthitāḥ \|*  
*vadanti vā kadācitkiṃ tadvadasva sureśvara \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — What do the ghosts do in their ghosthood? When do they speak sometime? Please tell me, О lord of gods

## \|\| 23.2 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*teṣāṃ svarūpaṃ vakṣyāmi cihnaṃ svapnaṃ yathātatham \|*  
*kṣutpipāsārditāste vai praviśeyuḥ svaveśmani \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Bhagavan said: — I shall tell about their form, signs and dreams. Being oppressed by hunger and thirst they enter their former home.

## \|\| 23.3 \|\|

*pratiṣṭhā vāyudeheṣu śayānāṃstu svavaṃśajān \|*  
*tatra yacchanti liṅgāni darśayanti khageśvara \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Though possessed of airy forms, they give signs to their sleeping descendents, О bird.

## \|\| 23.4 \|\|

*svaputrasvakalatrāṇi svabandhuntatra gacchati \|*  
*hayo gajo vṛṣo martyodṛśyate vikṛtānanaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. They visit the place where their sons, wives and relatives sleep.

## \|\| 23.5 \|\|

*śayānaṃ viparītaṃ tu ātmānaṃ ca viparyayam \|*  
*utthitaḥ paśyati yastu tadvindyātpretanirmitam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. If a person dreams of a horse, an elephant, a bull, or a man with deformed face, if a person awakened from sleep sees himself in the opposite side of the bed, this is all due to the working of a ghost.

## \|\| 23.6 \|\|

*svapne narau hi nigaḍairbadhyate bahudhā yadi \|*  
*annaṃ ca yācate svapne kuveṣaḥ pūrvajo mṛtaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. If a man is fastened with chains in dream, if his dead ancestors demand food in dream,

## \|\| 23.7 \|\|

*svapne yo bhujyamānasya gṛhītvānnaṃ palāyate \|*  
*ātmanastu paro vāpi tṛṣārtastu jalaṃ pibet \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. If one snatches the food from him while he is eating in dream, if thirsty, one drinks water,

## \|\| 23.8 \|\|

*vṛṣabhārohaṇaṃ svapne vṛṣabhaiḥ saha gacchati \|*  
*utpatya gaganaṃ yāti tīrthe yāti kṣudhāturaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. If in dream one rides a bull or moves with bulls or if one springs up in the sky or goes to a holy place hungry,

## \|\| 23.9-10 \|\|

*svavācā vadate yastu govṛṣadvijāvājiṣu \|*  
*liṅge gaje tathā deve bhūte prete niśācare \|\| 9 \|\|*

*svapnamadhye tu pakṣīndra pretaliṅgānyanekadhā \|*  
*svakalatraṃ svabandhuṃ vā svasutaṃ svapatiṃ vibhum \|*  
*vidyamānaṃ mṛtaṃ paśyetpretadoṣeṇa niścitam \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. If one speaks aloud among cows, bulls, Brahmanas horses, elephants, deities, ghosts and demons-this is due to the working of a ghost. Many are the signs of ghosts in dream, О bird. It is due to a ghost if one sees his wife, relative, son or husband as dead.

## \|\| 23.11 \|\|

*yācate yaḥ paraṃ svapne kṣuttṛḍbhyāṃ ca pariplutaḥ \|*  
*tīrthe gatvā dahetpiṇḍānpretadauṣairna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He who begs in dream oppressed by hunger or thirst should give pindas to the manes to ward off coming distress.

## \|\| 23.12 \|\|

*nirgacchedvā gṛhādvāpi svapne putrastathā paśuḥ \|*  
*pitā bhrātā kalatraṃ ca pretadoṣaistu paśyati \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If one sees in dream his son cattle, father, brother, wife, getting out of house, it is due to the working of a ghost.

## \|\| 23.13 \|\|

*cihnānyetāni pakṣīndra prāyaścittaṃ nivedayet \|*  
*kṛtvā snānaṃ gṛhe tīrthe śrīvṛkṣe tarpaṇaṃ jalaiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. These signs, О bird, call for atonement. One should bathe at home or at a holy place, give water oblation to a deity at the root of a fig tree.

## \|\| 23.14 \|\|

*kṛṣṇadhānyāni pūjāṃ ca pradadyādvedapārage \|*  
*homaṃ kuryādyathāśakti sampūrṇaṃ vācayetsudhīḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Or give black corn, perform worship, offer gifts to a Vedic scholar and do homa as far as his means can avow.

## \|\| 23.15 \|\|

*etaddhi śraddhayā yastu pretaliṅganidarśanam \|*  
*paṭhate śṛṇute vāpi pretacihnaṃ vinaśyati \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. If, in faith, one reads or hears this discourse, the ghosts disappear immediately from his vicinity.

## Chapter 24

## \|\| 24.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*nākāle mriyate kaściditi vedānuśāsanam \|*  
*kasmānmṛtyumavāpnoti rājā vā śrotriyopi vā \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — As the Shastras declare none dies a premature death. But, how is it that a Brahmin scholar of the Vedas or a mighty monarch sometimes meet with death Erematurely. Hence, what is stated in the Shastras by Brahma appears to be untrue.

## \|\| 24.2 \|\|

*yaduktaṃ brāhmaṇā pūrvamanṛtaṃ taddhi dṛśyate \|*  
*vedairuktaṃ tu yadvākyaṃ śataṃ jīvati mānuṣe \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. What is stated in the Vedas, viz., a man lives for a hundred years is not actually experienced in the Kali age.

## \|\| 24.3-7 \|\|

*jīvanti mānuṣe loke sarve varṇā dvijātayaḥ \|*  
*antyajāmlecchajāścaiva khaṇḍe bhāratasaṃjñake \|\| 3 \|\|*

*na dṛśyate kalau tacca kasmāddeva samādiśa \|*  
*(ādhānānmṛtyumāpnoti bālo vā sthaviro yubā \|\| 4 \|\|*

*sadhano nirdhano vāpi sukumāraḥ surūpavān \|*  
*avidvāṃścaiva vidvāṃśca brāhmaṇastvitaro janaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*taporato yogaśīlo mahājñānī ca yo naraḥ \|*  
*sarvajñānarataḥ śrīmāndharmātmātulavikramaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*sarvametadaśaṣeṇa jāyate vasudhātale \|*  
*kasmānmṛtyumavāpnoti rājā vā śrotriyo 'pi vā \|\| 7 \|\|*

3-7. People, belonging to different classes, Brahmana, Ksatriya, Vaisya and sudra including Mlecchas who live in Bharata do not live upto hundred years. A person can meet with untimely death, no matter if he is a child, old or young, rich or poor, handsome or ugly, learned or fool, of high or low birth, an ascetic or a Yogi, intelligent, pious and all-knowing Brahmin or a mighty monarch. Please tell me how this is so.

## \|\| 24.8 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*sādhusādhu mahāprājña yastvaṃ bhakto 'si me priyaḥ \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ vacanaṃ guhyaṃ nānādeśavināśanam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The Lord said: — О wise bird, well said. You are my staunch devotee. Please listen to my important words that dispel all sorts of sins.

## \|\| 24.9 \|\|

*vidhātṛvihito mṛtyuḥ śīghramādāya gacchati \|*  
*tato vakṣyāmi pakṣīndra kāśyapeya mahādyute \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О lord of birds, the brilliant son of I Kasyapa, I shag tell you how god of death induced by Brahma takes away the life quickly.

## \|\| 24.10 \|\|

*mānuṣaḥ śatajīvīti purā vedena bhāṣitam \|*  
*vikarmaṇaḥ prabhāveṇa śīghraṃ cāpi vinaśyati \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It is true that a man lives for a himdred years as stated in the Vedas, But due to the influence of his misdeeds he dies a premature death.

## \|\| 24.11-12 \|\|

*vedānabhyasanenaiva kulācāraṃ na sevate \|*  
*ālasyātkarmaṇāṃ tyāgo niṣiddhe 'pyādaraḥ sadā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yatra tatra gṛhe 'śrāti parakṣetraratastathā \|*  
*etairanyairmahādoṣairjāyate cāyuṣaḥ kṣayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. Now, people do not make a regular study of the Vedas; they do not follow the traditional conduct; they commit sins and due to idleness forsake their duties. They take food from any house they visit. They are fond of other men's wives. All these reduce their longevity.

## \|\| 24.13 \|\|

*aśraddadhānamaśuciṃ nāstikaṃ tyaktamaṅgalam \|*  
*paradrohānṛtaraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ yata (ma) mandiram \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Evil deeds take men quickly to Yama's abode, if they do not possess faith, if they are impure, if they do not practice Vedic rites, if they have cast off auspicious things or if they indulge in falsehood or if they practise deception.

## \|\| 24.14-15 \|\|

*arakṣitāraṃ rājānaṃ nityaṃ dharmavivarjitam \|*  
*krūraṃ vyasaninaṃ mūrkhaṃ vedavādabahiṣkṛtam \|*  
*prajāpīḍanakartāraṃ rājānaṃ yamaśāsanam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*prāpayanti vaśaṃ mṛtyostato yāti ca yātanām \|*  
*svakarmāṇi parityajya mukhyavṛttāni yāni ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Evil deeds bring about premature death even of a king if he does not protect his people, if he is devoid of virtue if he is cruel, if he indulges in vice, if he is a fool, if he stays out of Vedic discussion if he harasses his people.

## \|\| 24.16 \|\|

*parakarmarato nityaṃ yamalokaṃ sa gacchati \|*  
*śūdraḥ karotiḥ yatkiñcidvijaśuśrūṣaṇaṃ vinā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. If a man forsakes his duties or resorts to forbidden, activities or those which are enjoined on others he dies quickly.

## \|\| 24.17 \|\|

*uttamādhamamadhye vā yamaloke sa pacyate \|*  
*snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homo svādhyāyo davartāccanam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. If a Shudra does something other than service to the twice-born he is wasted in any of the hells-bad, worse or worst or as the

## \|\| 24.18 \|\|

*yasmindine na sevyante sa vṛthā divaso nṛṇām \|*  
*anityamadhruvaṃ dehamanādhāraṃ rasodbhavam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. If one neglects both, Dana, japa, homa, study of the Vedas or worship of gods on a certain day that is a day wasted in the life of that man.

## \|\| 24.19 \|\|

*annodakamaye dehe guṇānetānvadāmyaham \|*  
*yatprātaḥ saṃskṛtaṃ sāyaṃ nūnamannaṃ vinaśyati \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. I affirm that the attributes of body evolved out of the morsels of food and lymph are not permanent or steady or rooted in firm grounds.

## \|\| 24.20 \|\|

*tadīyarasasampuṣṭakāye kā bata nityatā \|*  
*gataṃ jñātvā tu pakṣīndra vapurardhaṃ svakarmabhiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The food cooked in the morning becomes stale in the evening. How can one expect permanence in the body sustained by such a food?

## \|\| 24.21 \|\|

*naraḥ pāpavināśāya kurvīta paramauṣadham \|*  
*dehaḥ kimannadātuḥ svinniṣekturmātureva vā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. О lord of birds, realising that the body bound with its activities is already lost, people should strive for burning away their sins.

## \|\| 24.22 \|\|

*ubhayorvā prabhorvāpi bālanogneḥ śuno 'pi vā \|*  
*kastatra paramo yajñaḥ kṛmiviḍbhasmasaṃjñake \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Body belongs to one who nourishes it with food or to one who generates, father, mother or both. It is reduced to ashes or devoured by dogs or eaten up by worms. What sanctity can there be in regard to it?

## \|\| 24.23 \|\|

*kartavyaḥ paramo yatnaḥ pātakasya vināśane \|*  
*anekabhavasambhūtaṃ pātakaṃ tu tridhā kṛtam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. A person should make an earnest effort for the destruction of sins. The sin which he commits in various births is threefold.

## \|\| 24.24 \|\|

*yadā prāpnoti mānuṣyaṃ tadā sarvaṃ tapatyapi \|*  
*sarvajanmāni saṃsmṛtya viṣādī kṛtacetanaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. When he takes birth as a human being the sins visit him. When he remembers his past sinful life he becomes sorrowful.

## \|\| 24.25 \|\|

*avekṣya garbhavāsāṃśca karmajā gatayastathā \|*  
*mānuṣodaravāsī cettadā bhavati pātakī \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. When lie resides in human womb after, sojourning in the various beings such as birds, etc., the full velocity of his past actions forces an impact on him. only after residing in the womb does he realise it.

## \|\| 24.26 \|\|

*aṇḍajādiṣu bhūteṣu yatrayatra prasarpati \|*  
*ādhayo vyādhayaḥ kleśā jarārūpaviparyayaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He begins to realize the tortures of life, such as anxiety sickness, distress, old age, deformity and the rest.

## \|\| 24.27 \|\|

*garbhavāsādvinirmuktastvajñānatimirāvṛtaḥ \|*  
*na jānātiḥ khagaśreṣṭha bālabhāvaṃ samāśritaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. But the moment he comes out of the womb, ignorance envelops him. As an infant he realizes nothing.

## \|\| 24.28 \|\|

*yauvane timirāndhaśca yaḥ paśyati sa muktibhāk \|*  
*ādhānānmṛtyumāpnoti bālo vā sthaviro yuvā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In youth too, he does not realize, blinded as he is by sexual urge. But he who realizes it from the very beginning attains salvation.

## \|\| 24.29 \|\|

*sadhano nirdhanaścaiva sukumāraḥ kurūpavān \|*  
*avidvāṃścaiva vidvāṃśca brāhmaṇāstvitaro janaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. From the time of conception itself, death awaits the person whether he be infant or an old man or a youth, whether he be rich or poor handsome or ugly.

## \|\| 24.30 \|\|

*taporato yogaśīlo mahājñānī ca yo naraḥ \|*  
*mahādānarataḥ śrīmāndharmātmātulavikramaḥ \|*  
*vinā mānupadehaṃ tu sukhaṃ duḥ khaṃ na vindati \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Even if one is illiterate or a scholar, a Brahmin or another caste, surely he must die. A man of great wisdom devoted to penance or practice of yoga, of liberal gifts and yirtuous life cannot expect to derive pleasure without a physical body.

## \|\| 24.31 \|\|

*prākṛtaiḥ karmapāśaistu mṛtyumāpnoti mānavaḥ \|*  
*ādhānātpañca varṣāṇi svalpapāpairvipacyate \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. When the actions of previous births ripen, man succumbs to death. From the time of conception to the fifth year even a slight sin may cause death.

## \|\| 24.32-33 \|\|

*pañcavarṣādhiko bhūtvā mahāpāpairvipacyate \|*  
*yoniṃ pūrayate yasmānmṛto 'pyāyāti yāti ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

*mṛto dānaprabhāveṇa jīvanmartyaściraṃ bhuvi \|*  
*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti kṛṣṇavacaḥ śrutvā garuḍo vākyamabravīt \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Suta said: — It is due to major sins that man dies after the fifth year. Usually, he completes the allotted span of life, dies and is born again. It is as a result of the influence of sacred rites and gifts that he is able to complete his life's term.

## \|\| 24.34 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*mṛte bāle kathaṃ kuryātpiṇḍadānādikāḥ kriyāḥ \|*  
*garbheṣu ca vipannānāmācūḍākaraṇācchiśoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Garuda said: — How should the rites of pinch be performed if a person dies in infancy? What should be done when death occurs in the womb itself? What should be done when I the death occurs before the tonsure I ceremony?

## \|\| 24.35 \|\|

*kathaṃ kiṃ kena dātavyaṃ mṛtānte ko vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*garuḍoktamiti śrutvā viṣṇurvākyamathābravīt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. What are the rules of obsequy if death occurs after the tonsure ceremony?

## \|\| 24.36 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*yadi garbho vipadyate stravate vāpi yoṣitaḥ \|*  
*yāvanmāsaṃ sthito garbhastāvaddinamaśaucakam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The Lord said: — If there is still birth or abortion, the impurity is for as many days as the number of months of conception.

## \|\| 24.37 \|\|

*tasya kiñcinna kartavyamātmanaḥ śreya icchatā \|*  
*tato jāte vipanne tu ā cūḍākaraṇācchiśoḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Nothing else need be done, it one desires to maintain confirmity with the rules conducive to the welfare of the soul. If the child dies after birth but before the tonsure ceremony the dead body is merely buried.

## \|\| 24.38 \|\|

*dugdhaṃ bhojyaṃ tāśakti bālānāṃ ca pradīyate \|*  
*ā cūḍātpañcavarṣe tu dehadāho vidhīyate \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Milk should be distributed according to one's capacity among the children in the neighbourhood in order to please them After the tonsure and upto five years, if the child dies, cremation should be performed duly.

## \|\| 24.39 \|\|

*dugdhaṃ tasya pradeyaṃ syādbālānāṃ bhojanaṃ śubham \|*  
*pañcavarṣādhike prete svajātivihitāni ca \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Milk should be distributed to children and they may be fed also. The rites of children upto five, on being dead, should be in accordance with the tradition of the family.

## \|\| 24.40 \|\|

*kuryātkarmāṇi sarvāṇi codakumbhādi pāyasam \|*  
*dātavyaṃ tu khagaśreṣṭha ṛṇasambandhakastu saḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Udakumbha rites should be performed. Milk puddings should be given. О foremost among birds, the child is a link in paying off debt to ancestors.

## \|\| 24.41-43 \|\|

*jātasya hi dhruvo mṛtyurdhruvaṃ janma mṛtasya ca \|*  
*kartavyaṃ pakṣiśārdūla punardehakṣayāya vai \|\| 41 \|\|*

*tasmai yadrocate deyamadattvā nirdhane kule \|*  
*svalpāyurnirdhano bhūtvā ratibhaktivivarjitaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*punarjanmāpnuyānmartyastasmāddeyamṛte śiśoḥ \|*  
*purāṇe gīyate gāthā sarvathā pratibhāti me \|\| 43 \|\|*

41-43. It is but certain that whoever is born shall die and whoever dies shall be born again. When a child dies at a tender age without enjoying the pleasures of life he is sure to be born again. Hence, gifts are given, О foremost among birds. The rites are performed to ward off rebirth. This is what I feel. If nothing is made over as a gift, he wilt be born in an indigent house. Such anecdotes.are many in the Puranas.

## \|\| 24.44-45 \|\|

*miṣṭānnaṃ bhojanaṃ deyaṃ dāne śaktistu durlabhā \|*  
*bhojye bhojanaśaktiśca ratiśaktirvarastriyaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

*vibhave dānaśaktiśca nālpasya tapasaḥ phalam \|*  
*dānādbhogānavāpnoti saukhyaṃ tīrthasya sevanāt \|*  
*subhāṣaṇānmṛto yastu sa vidvāndharmavittamaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. Sumptuous food should also be given. Inclination to give is rarely found in the world. It is the fruit of penance of no mean measure that one gets good food and the power to digest it, good sexual virility and handsome women, good wealth and inclination to give it. By making gifts, one can enjoy later; by resorting to holy centres one can attain pleasure and by speaking ywect words one can become a scholar or a religious saint.

## \|\| 24.46 \|\|

*adattadānācca bhaveddaridro daridrabhāvācca karotipāpam \|*  
*pāpaprabhāvānnarakaṃ prayāti punardaridraḥ punareva pāpī \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. By not making gifts one becomes indigent; due to poverty one commits sins, due to sins one falls into hell. He is born again as indigent; again he commits sins and again he falls into hell.

## Chapter 25

## \|\| 25.1-2 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi puruṣastrī vinirṇayam \|*  
*jīvanvāpi mṛto vāpi pañcavarṣādhiko 'pi vā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*pūrṇe tu pañcame varṣe pumāṃścaiva pratiṣṭhitaḥ \|*  
*sarvaindriyāṇi jānāti rūpārūpaviparyayau \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. The Lord said: — Hereafter, I shall explain precisely certain factors concerning men and women. A child after the age of five is established as such. He is able to know the various sense organs and differentiate colours.

## \|\| 25.3 \|\|

*pūrvakarmavipākena prāṇināṃ vadhabandhanam \|*  
*viprādīnantyajānsarvānpāpaṃ mārayati dhruvam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Death or bondage of living beings is a result of their previous actions. It is the sin that causes death

## \|\| 25.4 \|\|

*garbhe naṣṭe kriyā nāsti dugdhaṃ deyaṃ mṛte śiśau \|*  
*paraṃ ca pāyasaṃ kṣīraṃ dadyādvalavipattitaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. If there is miscarriage, there is no obsequial rite. Milk is distributed when infants die. Due to mishaps to children, if death occurs, water-pots, milk puddings etc. are made over as gifts.

## \|\| 25.5 \|\|

*ekādaśāhaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ vṛṣaṃ vṛṣavidhiṃ vinā \|*  
*mahādānavihīnaṃ ca kumāre kṛtyamādiśet \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The rites in the event of the death of a child are the same as for the older people except that the rites of Vrsotsarga and Mahadana are performed for the older people on the eleventh and twelfth days.

## \|\| 25.6 \|\|

*kumārāṇāṃ caiva bālānāṃ bhojanaṃ vastraveṣṭanam \|*  
*bāle vā taruṇe vṛddhe ghaṭo bhavati vai mṛte \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Infants and older children of the neighbourhood are fed and clothed. Water-pots are gifted whether the deceased is a boy, young man or old.

## \|\| 25.7 \|\|

*bhūmau viniḥ kṣipedbālaṃ dvimāsonaṃ dvivārṣikam \|*  
*tataḥ paraṃ khagaśreṣṭha dehadāho vidhoyate \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Till the second year, the dead body is merely buried. Thereafter, О foremost among birds, the body is cremated

## \|\| 25.8 \|\|

*śiśurā dantajananādbālaḥ syādyāvadāśikham \|*  
*kathyate sarvaśāstreṣu kumāro mauñjibandhanāt \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. A child is called infant till the teeth are cut; till the tonsure ceremony he is called a child; a boy till the Kusa girdle is put. This is in accordance with all sacred texts.

## \|\| 25.9-10 \|\|

*śūdrādīnāṃ kathaṃ kuryātsaṃśayo mauñjivarjanāt \|*  
*garbhācca navamaṃ hitvā śiśurāmāsaṣoḍaśam \|\| 9 \|\|*

*bālaścātha parañjñeya āmāsasaptaviṃśati \|*  
*ā pañca varṣātkaumāraḥ paugaṇḍo navahāṃyanaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. From the time of conception till he is sixteen months old he is called infant; from sixteen months to twentysevcn he is called a boy; from twentyseven months to five years he is called Kumara; from five to nine years he is called Pauganda. He is known as Kisora till the sixteenth year. After the sixteenth year he attains maturity.

## \|\| 25.11 \|\|

*kiśoraḥ ṣoḍaśābdaḥ syāttato yauvanamādiśet \|*  
*mṛto 'pi pañcame varṣe avṛtaḥ savṛto 'pi vā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. A boy's death occurring at the fifth year whether normally or due to accident, all rites as mentioned above, should be carried out including the ten pindas.

## \|\| 25.12 \|\|

*pūrvoktameva kartavyamīhate daśapiṇḍakam \|*  
*svalpakarmaprasaṅgācca svalpādviṣayabandhanāt \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Since the rites are notmany, since contact with the worldly objects is of short duration and since body too is of small stature, the obsequies also arc not elaborate.

## \|\| 25.13 \|\|

*svalṣādvapuṣi vastrācca kriyāṃ svalpāmapīcchati \|*  
*yāvadupacayo janturyāvadviṣayaveṣṭitaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. If a child dies before the fifth year, the articles of diet and daily necessaries to which it is accustomed can be made over as. gifts.

## \|\| 25.14-15 \|\|

*yadyadyasyopajīvyaṃ syāttattaddeyamihecchati \|*  
*brahmabījodbhavāḥ putrā devarṣoṇāṃ ca vallabhāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*yamena yamadūtaiśca śāsyante niścitaṃ khaga \|*  
*bālo vṛddho yubā vāpi ghaṭamicchanti dehinaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Sons evolved out of the semen of Brahmins are favourites of Divine sages. In the same way, whether they are in the age group of children, young men or old men, both Yama and his emissaries too certainly consider. The all- pervasive soul experiences both pleasure and pain.

## \|\| 25.15-16 \|\|

*yamena yamadūtaiśca śāsyante niścitaṃ khaga \|*  
*bālo vṛddho yubā vāpi ghaṭamicchanti dehinaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*sukhaṃ duḥ khaṃ sadā vetti dehī vai sarvagastviha \|*  
*parityajya tadātmānaṃ jīrṇāṃ tvacamivoragaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Forsaking the body like the serpent casting off its slough, the subtle soul of the size of the thumb roams about in its aerial form oppressed by hunger.

## \|\| 25.17 \|\|

*aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo vāyubhṛtaḥ kṣudhānvitaḥ \|*  
*tasmāddeyāni dānāni mṛte bāle suniścitam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Therefore, certainly, gifts should be made. From the birth to the fifth year, uncooked things should be gifted to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 25.18 \|\|

*janmataḥ pañca varṣāṇi bhuṅkte dattamasaṃskṛtam \|*  
*pañcavarṣādhike bāle vipattiryadi jāyate \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. If there is accidental death after the fifth year, Vrsotsarga and other rites are performed but the rite of sapinl-karana is not necessary.

## \|\| 25.19 \|\|

*vṛṣotsargādikaṃ karma sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vinā \|*  
*dvādaśe hani samprāpte kuryācchrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. It is on the eleventh day that sons usually perform the sixteen sraddhas and make the Udakumbha and other similar gifts.

## \|\| 25.20 \|\|

*pāyasena guḍenāpi piṇḍāndadyādyathākramam \|*  
*udakumbhapradānaṃ ca pada (upa) dānāni yāni ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. In the case of all persons beyond the age of five at death Brahmins should be fed and major gifts offered according to capacity. The gift of earthen lamp should also be made.

## \|\| 25.22 \|\|

*kartavyaṃ ca khagaśreṣṭha vratātprāk pretatṛptaye \|*  
*yadā nakriyate sarvaṃ mudgalatvaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. О foremost among birds, ghosts should be propitiated by due performance of rites. If the person does not perform the rite he himself will become a ghost.

## \|\| 25.23-24 \|\|

*vratātprāṅgeva deyaṃ tu tataḥ pitṛgaṇasya ca \|*  
*svāhākāreṇa vai kuryādekoddiṣṭāni ṣoḍaśa \|\| 23 \|\|*

*ṛjudarbhaistilaiḥ śuklaiḥ prācīnāvīti niścitam \|*  
*apasavyaṃ ca kartavyaṃ kṛte yānti parāṃ gatim \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. Therefore, before other rites are accomplished, he should perform ekoddista for the mane for appeasing his hunger, put the rice-ball over darbhas while wearing the sacred thread over the right shoulder. When this is done, the obsequial rites are completed.

## \|\| 25.25 \|\|

*punaścirāyuṣo bhūtvā jāyante svakule dhruvam \|*  
*sarvasaukhyapradaḥ putraḥ pitroḥ prītivivardhanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Thereby a son yields all pleasure to his parents. If dead early he is born again in the family.

## \|\| 25.26-28 \|\|

*ākāśamekaṃ hi yathā candrādityau yathaikataḥ \|*  
*ghaṭādiṣu pṛthak sarvaṃ paśya rūpaṃ ca tatsamam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*ātmā tathaiva sarveṣu puttreṣu vicaretsadā \|*  
*yā yasya prakṛtiḥ pūrvaṃ śukraśoṇitasaṅgame \|\| 27 \|\|*

*sā (sa) tena bhāvayogena puttrāstatkarmakāriṇaḥ \|*  
*pitṛrūpaṃ samādāya kasyacijjāyate sutaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. Just as the same single sky, moon or sun are reflected in different water-pots, so also the Atman is reflected in different sons (and daughters) and roams about.

The mentality of the progenitor at the time of sexual intercourse when the semen and the menstrual blood mingle is reflected in the child born of that union. Hence, we can say that the boy takes after the father.

## \|\| 25.29-30 \|\|

*pitṛtaḥ ko 'pi rūpāḍhyo guṇajño dānatatparaḥ \|*  
*sadṛśaḥ ko 'pi loke 'sminna bhūto na bhaviṣyati \|\| 29 \|\|*

*andhādandho na bhavati mūkānmūko na jāyate \|*  
*badhirādbadhiro naiva vidyāvānviduṣo na hi \|*  
*anurūpā na dṛśyante madīyaṃ vacanaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. It is possible that the child may differ in qualities but not in, features. We have never seen a blind man procreating a blind child, a mute procreating a mute child, a deaf procreating a deaf child or an idiot procreating an idiot.

## \|\| 25.31-33 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*aurasakṣetrajādyāśca puttrā daśavidhāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*saṃgṛhītaḥ suto yastu dāsīputtraśca tena kim \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kāṅkāṃ gatimavāpnoti jāyo mṛtyuvaśaṃ gataḥ \|*  
*bhavenna duhitā yasya na dauhitro na vā sutaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*śrāddhaṃ tasya kathaṃ kāryaṃ vidhinā kena tadbhavet \|*  
*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā tu putrasya mucyate paitṛkādṛṇāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. Garuda said: — There aretenkinds of sons: Aurasa (legitimate) Ksetranja (procreated in one's wife by another man) and others. There is a son Sangrhitr (adopted) or Daslputra (son of a slave girl). How do these sons perform rites for their fathers. What is the goal they attain? If any one has only daughters and no son or grandson, who should perform sraddha rites for him? What are the rules regarding the same?

## \|\| 25.34 \|\|

*pauttrasya darśanājjanturmucyate caḥ ṛṇatrayāt \|*  
*lokānantyaṃ divaḥ prāptiḥ puttrapauttra prapauttrakaiḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

The Lord said: — A man is released from his debt to the manes on seeing his son's face.

34\. A man is released from three types of debts on seeing his grandson. On seeing his son, grandson, and great grandson he attains eternal or celestial worlds.

## \|\| 25.35 \|\|

*anyakṣetrodbhavādyā ye bhuktimātrapradāḥ sutāḥ \|*  
*kurvīta pārvaṇaṃ śrāddhamāraiso vidhivatsutaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The Ksetraja and other sons contribute to his worldly welfare. The Aurasa (legitimate) son performs Parvana duly.

## \|\| 25.36 \|\|

*kurvantyanye sutāḥ śrāddhame koddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam \|*  
*brāhmoḍhājastūnnayati saṃgṛhītastvadho nayet \|*  
*śrāddhaṃ sāṃvatsaraṃ kurvañjāyate narakāya vai \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The other nine types of sofa perform Ekoddista sraddha, not Parvana. The Brahmin son takes one upward but the adopted son causes his down-fall. If the latter were to perform the annual sraddha it will result only in his falling into hell.

## \|\| 25.37 \|\|

*sarvadānāni deyāni hyanna dānādṛte khaga \|*  
*saṃgṛhītaḥ sutaḥ kuryādekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О bird, the Sangrhitr son may very well make gifts excluding cooked food-stuff. He shall perform only Ekoddista and not Parvana.

## \|\| 25.38-41 \|\|

*pratyabdaṃ pitṛmātṛbhyāṃ śrāddhaṃ dattvā na lipyate \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭaṃ parityajya pārvaṇaṃ kurute yadi \|\| 38 \|\|*

*ātmānaṃ ca pitṝṃścaiva sa nayedyamamandiram \|*  
*saṃgṛhītastu yaḥ keciddāsīputtrādayaśca ye \|\| 39 \|\|*

*tīrthe kuryuḥ pitṛśrāddhaṃ dānaṃ (māsaṃ) dadyurdvijanmane \|*  
*saṃgṛhītasuto bhūtvā pākaṃ vā yaḥ prayacchati \|\| 40 \|\|*

*vṛthā śrāddhaṃ vijānīyācchūdrānnena yathā dvijaḥ \|*  
*na prīṇayati tacchrāddhaṃ pitāmahamukhānpitṝn \|*  
*evaṃ jñātvā svagaśreṣṭha hīnajātīnsutāṃstyajet \|\| 41 \|\|*

38-41. If he proceeds, in this manner, every year, there is no harm. If he forsakes Ekoddista and performs Parvana lie makes himself and the manes victims of Yama's wrath. The Sangrhitr, Dasiputra and other sons should perform sraddha with uncooked foodstuff in holy centers. If the Sangrhitr son performs sraddha with cooked rice his sraddha becomes fruitless like the food offered by a sudra. to a Brahmin. The grandfather and the other manes do not accept anything offered by him. О foremost among birds, knowing all these things, one shall avoid procreating sons in lower caste women.

## \|\| 25.42-43 \|\|

*(brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇājjātaścāṇḍālādadhamaḥ smṛtaḥ ) \|*  
*yastu pravrajitājjāto brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrataśca yaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*dvāvetau viddhi cāṇḍālau sagotrādyastu jāyate \|*  
*svaryātivihitānputraḥ samutpādya khageśvara \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. Apravrajita and a Shudra procreating sons even in Brahmin women procreate only' Candelas. О lord of birds, one shall marry legitimately a woman of his own caste and procreate sons.

## \|\| 25.44 \|\|

*taiḥ suvṛttaiḥ sukhaṃ prāpyaṃ kuvṛttairnarakaṃ vrajet \|*  
*hīnajātisamudbhūtaiḥ suvṛttaiḥ sukhamedhate \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. If they are of good nature lie will be happy. If they are of bad nature he will go to hell. Even sons of lower caste people, if goodnatured, cause happiness.

## \|\| 25.45 \|\|

*kalikaluṣavimuktaḥ pūjitaḥ siddhasaṅghairamaracamaramālāvījyamāno 'psarobhiḥ \|*  
*pitṛśatamapi bandhūnputtrapauttraprapauttrānapi narakanimagnānuddharedeka eva \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. A single son, free from the sins of Kali is honoured by the siddhas and fanned by celestial damsels with divine chowries. He will be able to lift hundreds of manes, kinsmen, sons, grandsons and greatgrandsons fallen inter the abysmal depths of Inferno.

## Chapter 26

## \|\| 26.1 \|\|

*śrīgaruḍamāhapurāṇam- 26*  
*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*satyaṃ brūhi suraśreṣṭha kṛpāṃ kṛtvā mayi prabho \|*  
*mṛtānāṃ caiva jantūnāṃ kadā kuryātsapiṇḍanam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — О foremost among deities, take pity on me and tell me truly when the sapinda is performed both for man and woman.

## \|\| 26.2 \|\|

*sapiṇḍatve kuto yānti asapiṇḍe kuto gatiḥ \|*  
*kenaiva sahapiṇḍatvaṃ strīpusorvaktumarhasi \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. How do they attain their goal when sapinda rite is performed? What happens when it is not performed? How can you say that sapinda is performed both for man and woman.

## \|\| 26.3 \|\|

*strīpumāṃśau sahaikatvaṃ pāpnutaḥ kathamuttamam \|*  
*jīvedbhartari nārīṇāṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kutaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. How can both husband and wife derive the benefit of sapinda? It the husband is alive how can one perform sapinda for a woman?

## \|\| 26.4 \|\|

*bhartṛlokaṃ kathaṃ yānti svargalokaṃ sureśvara \|*  
*agnyārohe kathaṃ śrāddhaṃ vṛṣotsargaḥ kathaṃ bhavet \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О lord of deities,how in the heaven does she attain the region of her husband? When the sacred fire is lit how is sraddha performed along with Vrsotsarga on the same day?

## \|\| 26.5 \|\|

*ghaṭadānaṃ kathaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇe kṛte \|*  
*kathayasva prasādena hītāya jagatāṃ prabho \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. О lord, how could the water-jar be gifted, if the sapinda rite is performed already? Please tell me, out of grace, since the welfare of the world is involved.

## \|\| 26.6 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*yathāvatkathayiṣyāmi sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ khaga \|*  
*varṣaṃ yāvatkhagaśreṣṭha yadācarati mānavaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The Lord said: — I shall tell you the truth how sapinda should be performed. О foremost among birds, the dead man\* is on the Great Highway for the period of a year (after death).

## \|\| 26.7 \|\|

*sapiṇḍane tato vṛtte pitṛlokaṃ sa gacchati \|*  
*tasmātputtreṇa kartavyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ pituḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Thereafter, he sojourns in the Pitrloka together with the manes. Hence, the sapinda should be performed by the son to his father.

## \|\| 26.8 \|\|

*saṃvatsare tu sampūrṇe kuryātpiṇḍa praveśanam \|*  
*piṇḍapraveśavidhanā tasya nityaṃ mṛtāhnikam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. When a year is complete, the son should perform pinda-pravesa duly on the anniversary of death.

## \|\| 26.9 \|\|

*niścitaṃ pakṣiśārdūla varṣānte piṇḍamelanam \|*  
*sahapiṇḍe kṛte pretastato yāti parāṃ gatim \|*  
*tannāma samparityajya tataḥ pitṛgaṇo bhavet \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О Excellent bird, when the sapinda is performed on the anniversary of death the dead man attains his cherished goal.

## \|\| 26.10 \|\|

*tripakṣe vāpi ṣaṇmāse melayetprapitāmahaiḥ \|*  
*jñātvā vṛddhivivāhādi svagotravihitāni ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After sapindlkarana, he abandons his identity and becomes one with the manes. The assimilation with grandfather is made within six months from the period the rite is completed.

## \|\| 26.11 \|\|

*vivāhaṃ naiva kurvīta mṛte ca gṛhamedhini \|*  
*bhikṣurbhikṣāṃ na gṛhṇāti yāvatkuryātsapiṇḍanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. No joyous ceremony such as marriage, etc. can be performed if the householder dies and the sapinda has notbeen performed. Even a mendicant will not take alms from that house unless the sapinda is performed duly.

## \|\| 26.12 \|\|

*svagotre 'pyaśucistāvadyāvatpiṇḍaṃ na melayet \|*  
*melanātpretaśabdastu nivarteta khageśvara \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. As long as the pindas are not merged, one remains impure. О lord of birds, the dead man gets a new appellation after this merge.

## \|\| 26.13 \|\|

*ānantyātkuladharmāṇāṃ puṃsāñcaivāyuṣaḥ kṣayāt \|*  
*asthiratvāccharīrasya dvādaśāhaḥ praśasyate \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The obligations of a family are many. Man's life is being wasted every moment. Human body is not permanent. In view of this, the twelfth day after death is thought to be appropriate for the sapinda rite.

## \|\| 26.14-15 \|\|

*niragnikaḥ sāgniko vā dvādaśāhe sapiṇḍayet \|\| 14 \|\|*

*dvādaśāhe tripakṣe vā ṣaṇmāse vatsare 'pi vā \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ proktamṛṣibhistattvadarśibhiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Whether one has maintained the sacrificial fire or not, one should perform the sapinda on the twelfth day. If that is not possible, after three fortnights or after six months or on the anniversary day.

## \|\| 26.16 \|\|

*saputtrasya na kartavyamekoddiṣṭaṃ kadācana \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇādūrdhvaṃ yatrayatra pradīyate \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The sapinda rite is made obligatory by the sages who know the truth.

## \|\| 26.17 \|\|

*tatratatra trayaṃ kāryamanyathā pitṛghātakaḥ \|*  
*tribhiḥ kuryādaśaktaśca pārvaṇaṃ muninoditam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. After sapinda rite ekoddista shall not be performed. If it is performed it shall be done for the three, avoiding Ksaya days.

## \|\| 26.18 \|\|

*taddine taddine kuryātpitāmahamukhānyataḥ \|*  
*ajñānāddinamāsānāṃ tasmātpārvaṇamiṣyate \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Ekoddista, if not performed for the three- father, grandfather, great grandfathertanta- mounts to a slaughter of pitrs. If one is unable to perform the three separate sraddhas, one shall perform all the three on the same day which is not the Shraddha day of the grandfather.

If the day or month of death is not known, parvana and not ekoddista sraddha is recommended.

## \|\| 26.19 \|\|

*anutpannaśarīrasya na dānaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha \|*  
*etaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śrāddhaiḥ preto muktastu jāyate \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. If the body has not yet been evolved no sraddha is enjoined for him together with the other manes. If the sixteen Sraddhas are performed he is released from pretahood, acquires the status of pitrs and rejoices in their company.

## \|\| 26.20 \|\|

*aputtrasya sapiṇḍatvaṃ naiva kuryātstriyo 'pi vā \|*  
*yāvajjīva ca sadbhatryā na kuryātsahapiṇḍatām \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. If there be no son, sapindlkarana is not possible. There is no sapindikarana for a low caste woman either, so long as she lives with her husband well and good, but there is no sapindlkarana for her after her death.

## \|\| 26.21 \|\|

*brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu yā vadhūriha saṃskṛtā \|*  
*bhartṛgotreṇa kartavyāstasyāḥ piṇḍodakakriyāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The woman whose marriage this taken place according to the practice observed in the marriages, such as Brahma, etc, is entitled to riceball and water libation which should be offered by the dynastic title of her husband.

## \|\| 26.22 \|\|

*āsurādivivā heṣu yā vyūḍhā kanyakā bhavet \|*  
*tasyāstu pitṛgotreṇa kuryātpiṇḍodakakriyāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The woman who is married according to the custom followed in the marriages such as asura, etc. is also entitled to pinch and water which should be offered by the dynastic title of her father.

## \|\| 26.23-24 \|\|

*pituḥ puttreṇa kartavyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ sadā \|*  
*puttrābhāve tu patnī syātpatnyabhāve sahodaraḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*bhrātā vā bhrātṛputtro vā sapiṇḍaḥ śiṣya eva vā \|*  
*sapiṇḍanakriyāṃ kṛtvā kuryānnāndīmukhaṃ tataḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. The sapindikarana for the deceased is always performed by the son alone. If the deceased has no son, it should be performed by his wife if no wife, by his elder brother; if no elder brother, by his younger brother, or by his younger brollier's son; if no younger brother or his son, by a close relative or by a disciple. After sapindikaraia, he should perform Nandimukha.

## \|\| 26.25 \|\|

*jyeṣṭhasyaiva kaniṣṭhena bhrātṛputtreṇa bhāryayā \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ putrahīne nare khaga \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О lord, if a dead man is issueless, his younger brother or his wife can perform the sapinda.

## \|\| 26.26 \|\|

*bhrātṝṇāmekajātānāmekaścetputravān bhavet \|*  
*sarvete tena putreṇa putriṇo manurabravīt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. According to Manu, if among many brothers in a family only one has a son that son is common to all brothers.

## \|\| 26.27 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ putrahīnānāṃ patnī kuryātsapiṇḍanam \|*  
*ṛtvijā kārayedvāpi purohitamathāpi vā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The sapinda of persons devoid of sons should be performed by the wife or Aviles or the family priest.

## \|\| 26.28 \|\|

*kṛtacūḍopanītaśca pituḥ śrāddhaṃ samācaret \|*  
*uccārayetsvadhākāraṃ na tu vedākṣarāṇyasau \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. A son whose tonsure ceremony has been held is entitled to perform his father's sraddha but he shall not recite the vedic mantras. He can very well say svadha.

## \|\| 26.29 \|\|

*bhartrādibhistribhiḥ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ striyāḥ \|*  
*pitṛvyabhrātṛputreṇa sodareṇa kanīyasā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. A woman's sapinda can be performed by three persons, beginning with her husband (i.e. husband, son or husband's brother).

## \|\| 26.30 \|\|

*arvāk saṃvatsarātsandhau pūrṇe saṃvatsare 'pi vā \|*  
*ye sapiṇḍīkṛtāḥ pretāsteṣāṃ na syātpṛthak kriyā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Either before a year, or after a year or on the anniversary day, the sapinda rite can be performed. After that, all individual rites are forbidden.

## \|\| 26.31 \|\|

*sapiṇḍane kṛte vatsa pṛthaktvaṃ tu vigarhitam \|*  
*yastu kuryātpṛthak piṇḍaṃ pitṛhā so 'bhijāyate \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. After sapinda, all individual Sraddhas are avoided. He who gives separate pinda virtually kills the Pitrs.

## \|\| 26.32 \|\|

*sapiṇḍīkaraṇe vṛtte pṛthaktvaṃ nopapadyate \|*  
*pṛthak piṇḍe kṛte paścātpunaḥ kuryātsapiṇḍanam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. When sapindikarana is performed a separate graddha for each mane is not enjoined. If a separate sraddha is performed, the sapinda rite should be performed again.

## \|\| 26.33 \|\|

*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ekoddiṣṭaṃ karoti yaḥ \|*  
*ātmānaṃ ca tathā pretaṃ sa nayeddamaśāsanam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. If after performing sapinda any one performs ekoddista, he makes himself and the dead man vicrim of Yama's wrath. Upto a year the rite for redemption from ghosthood should be performed under the specific name and gotra by the householder.

## \|\| 26.34 \|\|

*varṣaṃ yāvaktriyā kāryā nāmagotreṇa dhīmatā \|*  
*ghaṭādi bhojanaṃ nityaṃ padadānāni yāni ca \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇe vṛtte ekasyaiva tu dāpayet \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The water-jar offering, feeding, gifts of lights, etc. afteir the sapinda rite is over, should be made in a single unit, ail

## \|\| 26.35 \|\|

*annaṃ pānīyasahitaṃ saṃkhyāṃ kṛtvābdikasya ca \|*  
*dātavyaṃ brāhmaṇe pakṣiñjalapūrṇaghaṭādikam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. О bird, the Brahmin officiating in the first annual sraddha should be given the cost of water-pots and other articles (if water-pots and other articles are not given) after being fed.w

## \|\| 26.36 \|\|

*piṇḍānte tasya sakalā varṣavṛttiḥ svaśaktitaḥ \|*  
*divyadeho vimānasthaḥ sukhaṃ yāti yamālayam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. After the pinch offering has been made, he should gift as much of food-stuff to a Brahmin as would last fora year. A person for whom all due rites are performed shall obtain a divine body and will proceed in the divine aerial car fully contented, to the realm of Yana.

## \|\| 26.37 \|\|

*jīvamāne ca pitari na hi puttre sapiṇḍatā \|*  
*strīṇāṃ sapiṇḍanaṃ nāsti tathā bhartari jīvati \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. If father is alive, no sapinda rite is performed to the dead son. No sapinda is performed to the woman whose husband is alive.

## \|\| 26.38 \|\|

*hutāśaṃ yā samārūḍhā caturthe 'hni pativratā \|*  
*tasyā bhartṛdine kāryaṃ vṛṣotsargādikaṃ ca yat \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. If the chaste wife ascends the pyre on the fourth day, the Vrsotsarga and other rites for her should be performed on the day when the same are due for her husband.

## \|\| 26.39 \|\|

*puttrikā patigotrā syādadhastātputrajanmanaḥ \|*  
*putrotpatteḥ parastātsā pitṛgotraṃ vrajetpunaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. An adopted daughter shall subscribe to her husband's gotra till the birth of a son. After the birth of her son she reverts to the gotra other father.

## \|\| 26.40 \|\|

*patipatnyoḥ sadaikatvaṃ hutāśaṃ yādhirohati \|*  
*putreṇaiva pṛthak śrāddhaṃ kṣayākhye tasya vāsare \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. If the wife ascends the funeral pyre there is aways the identity of rites for her husband and wife. The separate sraddha (ekoddista) shall be performed by the son on the anniversary of his father's death.

## \|\| 26.41 \|\|

*aputtrau cenmṛtau syātāmekacityāṃ same 'hani \|*  
*pṛthak śrāddhāni kurvīta sāpiḍyaṃ patinā saha \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. If both husband and wife die on one and the same day, without a child and are cremated in the same pyre, the kinsman should perform separate sraddha for each. The sapinda is along witji her husband.

## \|\| 26.42 \|\|

*pṛthakpṛthak ca piṇḍena dampatī patinā saha \|*  
*na lipyate mahādoṣairetatsatyaṃ vaco mama \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. This is my sworn statement that if any one offers separate pindas to the deceased couple — husband and wife-he is not affected by major sins at all.

## \|\| 26.43 \|\|

*ekacityāṃ samārūḍhau dampatī nidhanaṃ gatau \|*  
*ekapākaṃ prakurvīta piṇḍāndadyātpṛthakpṛthak \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. If both husband and wife die and are cremated in the same pyre, food is cooked for pinda at the same time but pindas are offered separately.

## \|\| 26.44 \|\|

*ekādaśe vṛṣotsargaṃ pretaśrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*ghaṭādipadadānāni mahādānāni yāni ca \|*  
*varṣaṃ yāvatpṛthakkuryātpretastṛptiṃ vrajecciram \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. This separate offering is enjoined only for a year. Vrsotsarga, Nava sraddha (the first annual offering on the day of death) and the sixteen separate sraddhas, the pada-dana, major gifts are offered separately for a year. The dead person will be satiated for ever.

## \|\| 26.45 \|\|

*ekagotre mṛtānāṃ tu striyā vā puruṣasya vā \|*  
*sthaṇḍilaṃ caikataḥ kuryāddhomaṃ kuryātpṛthakpṛthak \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. If two persons, men or women, of the same gotra die on the same day, the altar for the ritual is common for both, but the offering of gifts is separate.

## \|\| 26.46 \|\|

*ekādaśe 'hni yacchrāddhaṃ pṛthak piṇḍāśca bhojanam \|*  
*pākairena patistrīṇāmanyeṣāṃ ca vigarhitam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The Sraddha performed on the eleventh day should consist of the same kind of cooked food in the case of husband and wife but not in the case of others.

## \|\| 26.47 \|\|

*ekenaiva tu pākena śrāddhāni kurute sutaḥ \|*  
*ekaṃ tu vikiraṃ kuryātpiṇḍāndadyadbahūnapi \|*  
*tīrthe cāparapakṣe vā candrasūryagrahe 'pi vā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. If sraddhas are performed in holy centres, in the dark fortnight or during the period of solar or lunar eclipse, the same cooked food can be used for many Sraddhas.

## \|\| 26.48 \|\|

*nārī bhartāramāsādya kuṇapaṃ dahate yadā \|*  
*agnirdahati gātrāṇi ātmānaṃ naiva pīḍayet \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. If a woman is burnt along with the corpse of the husband, the fire burns out the body. It cannot burn the soul.

## \|\| 26.49 \|\|

*dahyate dhmāyamānānāṃ dhātūnāṃ hi yathā malam \|*  
*tathā nārī daheddehaṃ hutāśe hyamṛtopame \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Just as the impurities of metals are removed when metals are melted in fire, so the woman shall remove her impurities in the fire that is on a par with nectar.

## \|\| 26.50-51 \|\|

*divyādau divyadehastu śuddho bhavati pūruṣaḥ \|*  
*taptatailena lohena vahninā naiva dahyate \|\| 50 \|\|*

*tathā sā patisaṃyuktā dahyate na kadācana \|*  
*antarātmā mṛte tasminmṛto 'pyekatvamāgataḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. Assuming a divine body in heaven both of them attain purity. Just as the oil in a metallic vessel can be heated but not burnt to ashes by fire, so also cremated along with her husband she cannot be reduced to nothing. Her soul remains immortal though it is merged into the soul other husband.

## \|\| 26.52 \|\|

*bhartṛsaṃgaṃ parityajya anyatra mriyate yadi \|*  
*bhartṛlokaṃ na sā yāti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. If she is separated from her husband and dies elsewhere she cannot attain the region of her husband till the day of final deluge.

## \|\| 26.53 \|\|

*lakṣmīyutānparityajya mātaraṃ pitaraṃ tathā \|*  
*mṛtaṃ patimanuvrajya sā ciraṃ sukhamedhate \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Leaving oil her mother, father and sons she will attain an everlasting happiness by follow - ing her dead husband.

## \|\| 26.54 \|\|

*divyavarṣapramāṇena tisraḥ koṭyor'ddhakoṭayaḥ \|*  
*tāvatkālaṃ vasetsvarge nakṣatraiḥ saha sarvadā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Calculating by the celestial calander she shall stay in heaven for thirty five million years with the stars.

## \|\| 26.55 \|\|

*tadante carate loke kule bhavati bhoginām \|*  
*sā hi labdhamahāprītirbhartrā saha pativratā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. At the end of that period she is born in a noble family, where she will be highly delighted in the company of her husband since she has remained chaste throughout.

## \|\| 26.56-57 \|\|

*evaṃ na kurute nārī dharmoḍhā patisaṃgamam \|*  
*janmajanmani duḥ khārtāduḥ śīlāpriyavādinī \|\| 56 \|\|*

*valgulī gṛhasodhā vā godhā vā dvimukhī bhavet \|*  
*svabhartāraṃ parityajya parapuṃsonuvartinī \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. If a woman who is married duly according to religious rites does not associate herself with her husband she will remain miserable for seven births subsequent to this. She will be evil-conducted and repulsive in speech. The woman of such a despicable character who goes after another man, leaving her own husband shay be born as a lizard or an alligator or a leech.

## \|\| 26.58 \|\|

*tasmātsarvaprayatnena svapatiṃ strī niṣevate \|*  
*manasā karmaṇā vācā mṛtaṃ jīvantameva vā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Hence, a woman shall endeavour to serve her husband in thought, word and action and follow him in life and death.

## \|\| 26.59 \|\|

*jīvamāne mṛte vāpi kilbiṣaṃ kurute tu yā \|*  
*sa ca vaidhavyamāpnoti janmajanmani durbhagā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. A woman who commits sins against her husband alive or dead shall never get a husband in her re-birth. She will be the most unfortunate creature among women.

## \|\| 26.60 \|\|

*yaddevebhyo yatpitṛbhyaḥ śraddhayaiva pradīyate \|*  
*tatphalaṃ bhartṛpūjātaḥ kuryādbhartrarcanaṃ tataḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. By not thinking of any one else and by serving her husband with devoted attention, a woman shall derive half the merit her husband achieves by the worship of God, propitiation of the manes and hospitality to guests.

## \|\| 26.61 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛte khagaśreṣṭha pitṛloke ciraṃ vaset \|*  
*yāvadādityacandrau ca tāvaddevasamā divi \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. By doing her duty like this she will attain the region of her husband after death and shine liked celestial damsel in heaven as long as the sun and the moon shine in the firmament.

## \|\| 26.62 \|\|

*punaścirāyuṣo bhūttvā jāyante vipule kule \|*  
*pativratā yathā nārī bhartṛduḥkhaṃ na vindati \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. If reborn they will be born in a flourishing family and will live together for a long time, a chaste lady will never suffer from the pangs of separation from her husband.

## \|\| 26.63 \|\|

*sarvametaddhi kathitaṃ mayā tava khageśvara \|*  
*viśeṣaṃ kathayiṣyāmi mṛtasyaiva sukhapradam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. О lord of birds, I have told you all. Now, I shall tell you some special things which yield happiness to the dead.

## \|\| 26.64 \|\|

*dvādaśāhe kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ varṣaṃ yāvatsapiṇḍanam \|*  
*punaḥ kuryātsadā nityaṃ ghaṭānnaṃ pratimāsikam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The sapinda rite is performed on the twelfth day or thereafter, in any case within a year. The Udakumbha and feeding rites are performed every month.

## \|\| 26.65 \|\|

*kṛtasya karaṇaṃ nāsti pretakāryādṛte khaga \|*  
*yaḥ karoti naraḥ kaścitkṛt pūrvaṃ vinaśyati \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. A rite already done need not be repeated except the rites for the dead. If any one does it again the previous rite becomes fruitless.'

## \|\| 26.66 \|\|

*mṛtasyaiva punaḥ kuryātpreto 'kṣayyamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*pratimāsaṃ ghaṭā deyā sodanā jalapūritāḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. The rites for the dead, of course, can be repeated. О lord of birds, if any auspicious ceremonies (such as marriage etc) are to be celebrated, the sapinda rite should be completed before that.

## \|\| 26.67 \|\|

*arvākca vṛddheḥ karaṇācca tārkṣya sapiṇḍanaṃ yaḥ kurute hi putraḥ \|*  
*tathāpi māsaṃ pratipiṇḍamekamannaṃ ca kumbhaṃ sajalaṃ ca dadyāt \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. A son who performs sapinda rite according to the rules shall offer monthly pindas as well together with feeding and Udakumbha rites.

## Chapter 27

## \|\| 27.1-2 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ pretā vasantyatra kīdṛgrūpā bhavanti te \|*  
*mahāpretāḥ piśācāśca kaiḥkaiḥ karmaphalairvibho \|*  
*sarveṣāmanukampārthaṃ brūhi me madhusūdana \|\| 1 \|\|*

*pretatvānmucyate yena dānena ca śubhe na ca \|*  
*tanme kathaya deveśa mama cedicchasi priyam \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Tarksya said: — How do the ghosts originate? What are their features? О lord, of what misdeeds are the great ghosts and Madhu demon, for the benefit of every one, please shed light on these vital points. What are the meritorious rites and charities whereby the ghost is redeemed? О lord, if you wish me well, please tell me everything?

## \|\| 27.3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā tārkṣya mānuṣāṇāṃ hitāya vai \|*  
*śṛṇucāvahito bhūtvā yadvacmi pretalakṣaṇam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Krsna said: — О Garuda, you have put a pertinent question. For the benefit of human beings please listen to the narrative of a ghost that I am going to tell.

## \|\| 27.4 \|\|

*guhyadguhyataraṃ hyetannākhyeyaṃ yasya kasyacit \|*  
*bhaktastvaṃ hi mahābāho tena te kathayāmyaham \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. This is the secret of secrets which is not to be divulged to any one and everyone. О mighty one, you are my devotee. I tell you, therefore.

## \|\| 27.5 \|\|

*purā tretāyuge tāta rājāsīdbabhruvāhanaḥ \|*  
*mahodayapure ramye dharmaniṣṭho mahābalaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. О bird, formerly in Tretayuga there was a king Babhruvahana by name, in the prosperous city Mahodaya. He was beautiful and righteous too.

## \|\| 27.6 \|\|

*yajvā dānapatiḥ śrīmānbrahmaṇyaḥ sādhusaṃmataḥ \|*  
*śīlācāraguṇopeto dayādākṣiṇyasaṃyutaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He used to make gifts and perform sacrifices. He was rich, revered by saintly men, devoted to Brahmins, endowed with good manners as well as sympathetic and chivalrous qualifies.

## \|\| 27.7 \|\|

*prajāḥ pālayate nityaṃ putrāniva mahābalaḥ \|*  
*kṣattradharmarato nityaṃ sa daṇḍyāndaṇḍayannṛpaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Powerful that he was he protected his subjects like his own sons. One day, he started on a hunting expedition.

## \|\| 27.8 \|\|

*sa kadācinmahābāhuḥ sasainyomṛgayāṃ gataḥ \|*  
*vanaṃ viveśa gahanaṃ nānāvṛkṣasamanvitam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He entered a thick wild forest overgrown with variegated trees, infested by hundred of tigers and resonant with the chirping sound of different kinds of birds.

## \|\| 27.9 \|\|

*śārdūlaśatasaṃjuṣṭaṃ nānāpakṣinināditam \|*  
*vanamadhye tadā rājā mṛgaṃ dūrādapaśyata \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In the middle of the forest, he saw a deer at a great distance and hit it with an arrow.

## \|\| 27.10-11 \|\|

*tena viddho mṛgo 'tīva bāṇena sudṛḍhena ca \|*  
*bāṇamādāya taṃ tasya sa vane 'darśanaṃ yayau \|\| 10 \|\|*

*kakṣe tacchoṇitastrāvātsa rājānujagāma tam \|*  
*tato mṛgaprasaṅgena vanamanyadviveśa saḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. With the arrow sticking to its body the deer vanished behind the trees. The king followed the blood-stained track. In the another forest.

## \|\| 27.12-13 \|\|

*kṣutkṣāmakaṇṭho nṛpatiḥ śramasantāpamūrchitaḥ \|*  
*jalasthānaṃ samāsādya sāśva evāvagāhata \|\| 12 \|\|*

*pītvā tadu dakaṃ śītaṃ padmagandhādhivāsitam \|*  
*tata uttīrya salilādvimalādbabhruvāhanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. His throat became parched with hunger and thirst. He was utterly exhausted. He saw a pond and plunged into the water together with the horse. He drank the cool water rendered fragrant with the contact of lotuses.

## \|\| 27.14-15 \|\|

*nyagrodhavṛkṣamāsādya śītacchāyaṃ manoharam \|*  
*mahāviṭapinaṃ hṛdyaṃ pakṣisaṅghātanāditam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*vanasya tasya sarvasya ketubhūtamivocchritam \|*  
*taṃ mahātarumāsādya niṣasāda mahīpatiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. Не came out of the lake and sat under the cool shade of a beautiful fig tree resonant with the chirping sound of hovering birds. The tree appeared to be the foremost leader of all the trees there.

## \|\| 27.16-17 \|\|

*atha pretaṃ dadarśāsau kṣuttṛṣṇāvyākulendriyam \|*  
*utkacaṃ malinaṃ kubjaṃ (rūkṣaṃ) nirmāṃsaṃ bhīmadarśanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*snāyubaddhāsthicaraṇaṃ dhāvamānamitastataḥ \|*  
*anyaiśca bahubhiḥ pretaiḥ samantātparivāritam \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. While he rested at the root of that tree, he saw a ghost who was excessively hungry and thirsty.

The ghost was of awfully terrific appearance, dirty, rough and skinny, with dishevelled hair and running here and there. His feet were mere bones and sinews. There were many other ghosts surrounding him.

## \|\| 27.18-19 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā vikṛtaṃ ghoraṃ vismito babhruvāhanaḥ \|*  
*preto 'pi dṛṣṭvā tāṃ ghorāmaṭavīmāgataṃ nṛpam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*tadā hṛṣṭamanā bhūtvā tasyāntikamupāgataḥ \|*  
*abravītsa tadā tārkṣya pretarājo nṛpaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. On seeing this hideous ghost, Babhruvahana was struck with wonder. The ghost too seemed to be delighted on seeing the king who had come to that dreadful, dense forest. He approached the king. О Garuda, the king of ghosts then addressed the king thus.

## \|\| 27.20 \|\|

*pretabhāvo mayā tyaktaḥ prāpto 'smi paramāṃ gatim \|*  
*tvatsaṃyogānmahābāho nāstidhanyataro mama \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Verily, my ghosthood has come to an end. I have attained the great goal. О mighty king, thanks to your contact, none else is happier than I.

## \|\| 27.21 \|\|

*nṛpatiruvāca \|*  
*kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ karālāsyastvaṃ preta iva lakṣyase \|*  
*kathayasva mama prītyā yathaivaṃ cāsi tattvataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The King said: — О black and hideous being, you appear to be a ghost. Please tell me the exact state of facts of your life willingly.

## \|\| 27.22 \|\|

*tathā pṛṣṭaḥ sa vai rājñā provāca sakalaṃ svakam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Thus being asked by the king, the ghost revealed everything.

## \|\| 27.23 \|\|

*preta uvāca \|*  
*kathayāmi nṛpaśreṣṭha sarvamevāditastava \|*  
*pretatve kāraṇaṃ śrutvā dayāṃ kartuṃ mamārhasi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Preta said: — О foremost of kings, I shall tell you everything of my life from the very beginning. On hearing the cause of my ghosthood it behoves you to take pity on me.

## \|\| 27.24 \|\|

*vaidiśaṃ nāma nagaraṃ sarvasampatsamanvitam \|*  
*nānājanapadākīrṇaṃ nānāratnasamākulam \|*  
*nānāpuṇyasamāyuktaṃ nānāvṛkṣasamākulam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Vaidisa is a city endowed with many riches. It abounds in genesis of all variety. It is surrounded by villages and countries of all sorts. People are busy in performing holy deeds. The city abounds in fruit-bearing trees.

## \|\| 27.25 \|\|

*tatrāhaṃ nyavasaṃ bhūyo devārcanarataḥ sadā \|*  
*vaiśyo jātyā sudevo 'haṃ nāmnā viditamastu te \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. It was here that I stayed, engaged in the worship of gods. Please know that I am born of Vaisya parents and my name is Sudeva.

## \|\| 27.26 \|\|

*havyena tarpitā devāḥ kavyena pitarastathā \|*  
*vividhairdānayogaiśca viprāḥ santarpitā mayā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. I propitiated gods by sacrificial offerings and the manes by oblations. The Brahmins were propitiated by me with various kinds of gifts.

## \|\| 27.27 \|\|

*āvāhāśca vivāhāśca mayā vai suniveśitāḥ \|*  
*dīnānāthaviśiṣṭebhyo mayā dattamanekadhā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. They were provided with foodstuffs and means of recreation by me. Both to the virtuous and the indigent, helpless people, I had made many gifts.

## \|\| 27.28 \|\|

*tatsarvaṃ viphalaṃ tāta mama daivādupāgatam \|*  
*yathā me niṣphalaṃ jātaṃ sukṛtaṃ tadvadāmi te \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. But, unfortunately, all those gifts became utterly useless in my case.

## \|\| 27.29 \|\|

*na me 'sti santatistāta na suhṛnna ca bāndhavaḥ \|*  
*na ca mitraṃ hi me tādṛgyaḥ kuryādaurdhvadaihikam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. I have no son, no friend, no kinsman and no acquaintance to perform my obsequial rites.

## \|\| 27.30 \|\|

*pretatvaṃ susthiraṃ tena mama jātaṃ nṛpottama \|*  
*ekādaśaṃ tripakṣaṃ ca ṣāṇmāsikamathābdikam \|*  
*pratimāsyāni cānyāni evaṃ śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. О excellent king, my ghosthood appears to be permanent. The sraddhas, like the eleventh day sraddha the three fortnightly sraddhas, the half-yearly, the anniversary, the monthly ones are sixteen.

## \|\| 27.31 \|\|

*yasyaitāni na dīyante pretaśrāddhāni bhūpate \|*  
*pretatvaṃ susthiraṃ tasya dattaiḥ śrāddhaśatairapi \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Preta-sraddhas not being given, they say, ghosthood becomes permanent even though hundreds of other sraddha are performed.

## \|\| 27.32 \|\|

*evaṃ jñātvā mahārāja pretatvāduddharasva mām \|*  
*varṇānāṃ cāpi sarveṣāṃ rājā bandhurihocyate \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. О king, realizing this, save me from ghosthood.

## \|\| 27.33 \|\|

*tanmāṃ tāraya rājendra maṇiratnaṃ dadāmi te \|*  
*yathā mama śubhāvāptirbhavennṛpavarottama \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. A king is supposed to be one who befriends people of all castes. Hence, О king, save me, I shall give you a precious gem.

## \|\| 27.34 \|\|

*tathā kāryaṃ mahābāho kṛpā yadi mayīṣyate \|*  
*ātmanaśca kuru kṣipraṃ sarvamevaurdhvedaihikam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О foremost among excellent kings, of powerful valour, if you have pity for me, please carry out the rites of obsequies for me so that 1 may attain an auspicious state. It is also advisable that you perform such rites as would ward off your future disaster.

## \|\| 27.35 \|\|

*nṛpatiruvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ pretā bhavantīha kṛtairapyaurdhvadaihikaiḥ \|*  
*piśācāśca bhavantīha karmabhiḥ kaiśca tadvada \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The King said: — How do ghosts originate even after the rites of obsequies are performed for them. By what evil deeds do Pisacas originate, please tell me.

## \|\| 27.36-37 \|\|

*preta uvāca \|*  
*devadravyaṃ ca brahmasvaṃ strībāladhanasañcayam \|*  
*ye haranti nṛpaśreṣṭha pretayoniṃ vrajanti te \|\| 36 \|\|*

*tāpasīṃ ca sagotrāṃ ca agamyāṃ ye bhajanti hi \|*  
*bhavanti te mahāpretā ambujāni haranti ye \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. Preta said: — О excellent king, those who steal or nusappropriate a Brahmin's wealth, temple property, woman's wealth or childrens money are turned into ghosts.

Those who indulge in sexual intercourse with saintly women, or women of their own gotra or forbidden women or those who steal conch shells become major ghosts.

## \|\| 27.38-40 \|\|

*pravālavajrahartāro ye ca vastrāpahārakāḥ \|*  
*tathā hiraṇyahartāraḥ saṃyuge 'sanmukhāgatāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*kṛtaghnā nāstikā raudrāstathā sāhasikā narāḥ \|*  
*pañcayajñavinirmuktā mahādānaratāśca ye \|\| 39 \|\|*

*svāmidrohakarā mitrabrāhmadrohakarāśca ye \|*  
*tīrthapāpakarā rājañjāyante pretayonayaḥ \|*  
*evamādyā mahārāja jāyante pretayonayaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40.
