Those who steal corals and diamonds, those who steal garments, those who steal gold, those who do not face enemies but turn away from battlefield and are killed, those who are ungrateful, those who are atheists,harsh, roguish 'and foolhardy, those who are devoid of five major sacrifices- become ghosts, О great king.

## \|\| 27.41 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*kathaṃ muktā bhavantīha pretatvāttvaṃ ca te 'pi ca \|*  
*kathaṃ cāpi mayā kāryamaurdhvadaihikamātmanaḥ \|*  
*vidhinā kena tatkāryaṃ sarvametadvadasva me \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The King said: How are ghosts freed from that state. Please tell me. How am 1 to perform rites to prevent my own future disaster? What are the rules regarding the performance of those rites. Please tell me all.

## \|\| 27.42-45 \|\|

*preta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu rājendra saṃkṣepādvidhiṃ nārāyaṇātmakam \|*  
*sacchāstraśravaṇaṃ viṣṇoḥ pūjā sajjanasaṃgatiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*pretayonivināśāya bhavantīti mayā śrutam \|*  
*ato vakṣyāmi te viṣṇupūjāṃ pretatvanāśinīm \|\| 43 \|\|*

*suvarṇadvayamāhṛtya mūrtiṃ bhūpa prakalpayet \|*  
*nārāyaṇasya devasya sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām \|\| 44 \|\|*

*pītavastrayugācchannāṃ candanāgurucarcitām \|*  
*snāpayedvividhaistoyairadhivāsya yajettataḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

42-45. Preta said: — О lord of kings, please listen to a brief description of the Narayana rite. Hearing of sacred sastras, worship of Visnu, society of the good destroy ghosthood. I, therefore, tell you about the worship of Visnu.

Take two gold pieces and with the same make an idol of god Narayana. The idol should be decorated with ornaments. Two yellow pieces of cloth shall be used to clothe it. Smear the same with sandal paste and sprinkle Aguru over it It should be bathed in holy water collected from various centres and fumigated with fragrant incense.

## \|\| 27.46-47 \|\|

*pūrve tu śrīdharaṃ devaṃ dakṣiṇe madhusūdanam \|*  
*paścime vānamaṃ devamuttare ca gadādharam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*madhye pitāmahaṃ pūjya tathā devaṃ maheśvaram \|*  
*pūjayecca vidhānena gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pṛthak \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. Lord Sriddhara should be worshipped in the east; Madhusudana in the south, Vamadeva in the west and Gadadhara in the North. Brahma and Siva should be worshipped in the centre.

## \|\| 27.48 \|\|

*tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya agnau santarpya devatāḥ \|*  
*ghṛtena dadhnā kṣīreṇa viśvāndevāṃstathā nṛpa \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Then, О king, the deities should be propitiated after circumambulation and offerings of ghl, curd and milk into the fire.

## \|\| 27.49-50 \|\|

*tataḥ snāto vinītātmā yajamānaḥ samāhitaḥ \|*  
*nārāyaṇāgre vidhivatsvakriyāmaurdhvadaihikīm \|\| 49 \|\|*

*ārabheta vinītātmā krodhalobhavivarjitaḥ \|*  
*śrāddhāni kuryātsarvāṇi vṛṣasyotsarjanaṃ tathā \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. Then the devotee should take bath and humbly perform japas with concentration. He must then start the Aurdhvadehika rites, humbly, without anger and covetousness. He should perform all sraddhas and Vrsotsarga.

## \|\| 27.51-52 \|\|

*trayodaśānāṃ viprāṇāṃ vastracchatrāṇyupānahau \|*  
*aṅgulīyakamuktāni bhājanāsanabhojanaiḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

*sānnāśca sodakā deyā ghaṭāḥ pretahitāya vai \|*  
*śayyādānamatho dattvā ghaṭaṃ pretasya nirvapet \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. То thirteen Brahmins he should gift umbrellas, sandals, rings, gems, vessels, seats and foodstuffs. Water-jars filled with water should be given for the benefit of ghosts. Then giving a bed as gift the utensils should be offered to the ghost.

## \|\| 27.53 \|\|

*nārāyaṇeti sannāma saṃpuṭasthaṃ samarcayet \|*  
*evaṃ kṛtvātha vidhivacchubhāśubhaphalaṃ labhet \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Then the Samputa rite should be performed with one's own name, repeating Narayana. If any one does this duly he shall secure welfare.

## \|\| 27.54 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*kathaṃ pretaghaṭaṃ kuryāddadyātkena vidhānataḥ \|*  
*brūhi sarvānukampārthaṃ ghaṭaṃ pretavimuktidam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The King said: — О Ghost, how should the utensil be made and how should the same be donated. Kindly state.

## \|\| 27.55 \|\|

*preta uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mahārāja kathayāmi nibodha te \|*  
*pretatvaṃ na bhavedyena dānena sudṛḍhena ca \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Preta said: — You have asked well. I shall tell you about the gift by offering which one shall not get ghosthood.

## \|\| 27.56 \|\|

*dānaṃ pretaghaṭaṃ nāma sarvāśubhavināśanam \|*  
*durlabhaṃ sarvalokānāṃ durgatikṣayakārakam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. This gift is called pretaghata which removes all evils. It is rare in the world and it destroys the evil state.

## \|\| 27.57 \|\|

*santaptahāṭakamayaṃ tu ghaṭaṃ vidhāya brahmośakeśavayutaṃ saha lokapālaiḥ \|*  
*kṣīrājyapūrṇavivaraṃ praṇipatya bhaktyā viprāya dehi tava dānaśataiḥ kimanyaiḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Get a jar of heated gold manufactured by the smith. Fill it with milk or butter. With full devotion to Brahma, Visnu, Shiva and the guardians of quarters, give the same to a Brahmana. What avails hundreds of other gifts as compared to thm.

## \|\| 27.58-59 \|\|

*brahmā madhye tathā viṣṇuḥ śaṅkaraḥ śaṅkaro 'vyayaḥ \|*  
*prācyādiṣu ca tatkaṇṭhe lokapālānkrameṇa tu \|\| 58 \|\|*

*sampūjya vidhivadrājandhūpaiḥ kusumacandanaiḥ \|*  
*tato dugdhājyasahitaṃ ghaṭaṃ deyaṃ hiraṇmayam \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. Brahma, Visnu in the middle, pacifying Sankara in the east and the guardians of the quarters in their serial order should be worshipped, О king, with incense, flowers and sandal. The golden vessel should be gifted together with milk and ghl.

## \|\| 27.60 \|\|

*sarvadānādhikañcaitanmahāpātakanāśanam \|*  
*kartavyaṃ śraddhayā rājanpretatvavinivṛttaye \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. This gift is the best of all. It removes even the major sins. It should be made in good faith, О king, to ward off ghosthood.

## \|\| 27.61 \|\|

*śrībhāgavānuvāca \|*  
*evaṃ saṃjalṣatastasya pretena niyatātmanaḥ \|*  
*senājagāmānupadaṃ hastyaśvarathasaṃkulā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The Lord said: — Even as the ghost was saying this, О son of Vinata, the royal army consisting of elephants, horses and chariots arrived there.

## \|\| 27.62-64 \|\|

*tato bale samāyāte dattvā rājñe mahāmaṇim \|*  
*namaskṛtya punaḥ prārthya preto 'darśanamīyivān \|\| 62 \|\|*

*tasmādvanādviniṣkramya rājāpi svapuraṃ yayau \|*  
*svapuraṃ sa samāsādya sarvaṃ tatpretabhāṣitam \|\| 63 \|\|*

*cakāra vidhivatpakṣinnaurdhvadehādikaṃ vidhim \|*  
*tasya puṇyapradānena preto mukto divaṃ yayau \|\| 64 \|\|*

62-64. When the army arrived, the ghost disappeared but not before it had given a ruby to the monarch. Thereupon, the king left the for, est for his capital. After reaching the city he performed the aurdhvadehika rites duly in accordance with the statement of the ghost.

## \|\| 27.65 \|\|

*śrāddhena paradattena gataḥ preto 'pi sadgatim \|*  
*kiṃ punaḥ putradattena pitā yātīti cātbhutam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. With due sraddha performed by the king, the ghost attained a nice state. Then, narrative will not obtain ghosthood, even though he may have committed many sins.

## \|\| 27.66 \|\|

*itihāsamimaṃ puṇyaṃ śṛṇoti śrāvayecca yaḥ \|*  
*na tau pretatvamāyātaḥ pāpācārayutāvapi \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Whosoever hears or tells this holy narrative will not obtain ghosthood, even though he may have committed may sins. That wonder is there if the father achieves

## Chapter 28

## \|\| 28.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*sarvepāmanukampārthaṃ brūhi me madhusūdana \|*  
*pretatvānmucyate yena dānena sukṛtena vā \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Garuda said: — О lord, for the benefit of everyone, please tell me, how, whether by making gifts or performing holy rites, can the ghosts be pleased.

## \|\| 28.2 \|\|

*śṛkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu dānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvāśu bhavināśanam \|*  
*santaptahāṭakamayaṃ ghaṭakaṃ vidhāya brahmeśakeśavayutaṃ saha lokapālaiḥ \|*  
*kṣīrājyapūrṇaviviraṃ praṇipatya bhaktyā viprāya dehi tava dānaśataiḥ kimanyaiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The Lord said: — О bird, please listen. I shall tell you about the gift which dispels all inauspicious obstacles.

Make a jar of molten gold. Fill it up with ghee or milk. Worship Brahma, Siva, Visnu and the guardians of the quarters in the serial order. Then, offer the jar to a worthy Brahmin. Of what avail are the hundreds of other gifts made by you?

## \|\| 28.3 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*kimetkathitaṃ deva vistareṇa vadasva me \|*  
*āmuṣmikīṃ krīyāṃ deva utkrāntisamayādanu \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Garuda said: — О lord, what is said by you may kindly be explained in detail. Please tell me, О lord, what are the rites they should perform for the dead since the moment he breathes his last.

## \|\| 28.4 \|\|

*saṃsāre sādhu me nātha brūhi kṛtyaṃ janārdana \|*  
*yathā kāryā naraiḥ samyak kriyā caivaurdhvadaihikī \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. What are the rites to be performed in his favour which can debar him from turning into a ghost?

## \|\| 28.5-6 \|\|

*kathaṃ pretā mahākāyā raudrarūpā bhayānakāḥ \|*  
*kambhavanti suraśreṣṭha karmabhiḥ kaiḥ śubhāśubhaiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*piśācāḥ sambhavantīha kasyedaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam \|*  
*tanme kathaya deveśa ahamicchāmi veditum \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. О best of gods, do also tell me what activities, good or bad, lead one to become a dreadful or fierce-looking preta or pisaca. Tell me, О lord of gods, I wish to know all this.

## \|\| 28.7 \|\|

*bhūmyāṃ prakṣipyate kasmātpañcaratnaṃ kuto mukhe \|*  
*adhastācca tilā darbhāḥ pādau yāmyāṃ vyavasthitāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Why are the five gems put unto the mouth of the dead? Why are gingelly seeds strewn on the ground? Why are the Darbha blades scattered beneath?

## \|\| 28.8 \|\|

*kimarthaṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhūmau gomayenopalipyate \|*  
*kimarthaṃ smaryate viṣṇuḥ viṣṇusūktañca paṭhyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Why are the feet kept southward? Why is the ground besmeared with cow-dung? Why is Visnu meditated upon and his hymn recited?

## \|\| 28.9 \|\|

*kimarthaṃ putraputrāśca tasya tiṣṭhanti cāgrataḥ \|*  
*kimarthaṃ dīpadānañca kimarthaṃ viṣṇupūjanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Why do the sons and grandsons walk ahead? Why do they offer the gift of a lamp? Why do they adore Visnu?

## \|\| 28.10 \|\|

*kimarthamāturo dānaṃ dadāti dvijapuṅgave \|*  
*bandhūnmitrāṇyamitrāṃśca kṣamāpayati tatkatham \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Why do they, when ill, make a gift to a Brahmin? Why do kinsmen, friends and enemies forgive them faults?

## \|\| 28.11 \|\|

*tilā lohaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca kārpāsaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*saptadhānyaṃ kṣitirgāvo dīyate kena he tunā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Why do they gift gingelly seeds, iron, gold, cotton, salt, seven types of grains, land or cow?

## \|\| 28.12 \|\|

*kathaṃ ca mriyate janturmṛtasya ca kuto gatiḥ \|*  
*ativāhaśarīraṃ ca kathaṃ viśramate tadā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. How does a man die? What is his course after death. How does he take shelter in the body being carried (to the cremation ground)? I request you, О lord, to explain all this for the benefit of the world.

## \|\| 28.13 \|\|

*śaṃva skandhe vahetputro vahnidātā ca pautrakaḥ \|*  
*kimarthaṃ deva deveśa ājyenābhyañjanaṃ kutaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Why does the son carry the corpse on his shoulders and the grandson light the fire? Why is the anointment done with the butter?

## \|\| 28.14 \|\|

*yamasūktaṃ kimarthaṃ ca udīcīṃ diśamāharet \|*  
*pānīyamekavastreṇa sūryabimbanirīkṣaṇam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Why is the Yama Sukta recited? Why is the water carried to the North? Why is the sun looked through a cloth?

## \|\| 28.15 \|\|

*yavasarṣapadūrvāścapāṣāṇe nimbacarvaṇam \|*  
*vastraṃ naraśca nārī ca vidadhyādadharottaram \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Why are barley, mustard-seed, durva, stone and leaves of morgosa enjoined in the rite? Why do men and women wear a lower and an upper garment?

## \|\| 28.16 \|\|

*annādyaṃ gṛhamāgatya bhoktavyaṃ gotribhiḥ saha \|*  
*navakāni ca piṇḍāni kimarthaṃ vitaretsutaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Why should the meals be taken along with other persons on returning home? Why do the sons give nine pindas?

## \|\| 28.17 \|\|

*kimarthaṃ catvare dugdhaṃ pātre pakve ca mṛnmaye \|*  
*kāṣṭhatrayaṃ guṇe baddhvā kṛtvā rātrau catuṣpathe \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Why is the milk in the eastern pot placed on the cross-road? Why are the three wooden sticks tied and put on the cross-road in the night?

## \|\| 28.18 \|\|

*niśāyāṃ dīyate dīpo yāvadabdaṃ dinedine \|*  
*dāhodakaṃ kimarthaṃ ca saṃvādaḥ svajanaiḥ saha \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Why is an earthen lamp put every night for one year? Why should the water be offered in the name of the dead by the relatives and other people?

## \|\| 28.19 \|\|

*bhagavannativāhasya navapiṇḍaistu kiṃ bhavet \|*  
*kathaṃ devapitṛbhyaśca vāhasyāvāhanaṃ katham \|*  
*idaṃ ca kriyate deva kasmātpiṇḍaṃ pradāpayet \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Why should the nine pindas be offered? How is the gift to be given to the gods and manes. Why is the call made for the bearers of the corpse? О lord, why is the rite of pinda repeated?

## \|\| 28.20 \|\|

*kiṃ tatpradīyate tasya piṇḍadānādanantaram \|*  
*asthisañcayanaṃ caiva śayyādānaṃ kimarthakam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. What should be given next after the gift of pinda is made? Why are the bones collected? Why is a bed given to a Brahmin in favour of the deceased?

## \|\| 28.21 \|\|

*dvitīye 'hni kutaḥ snānaṃ caturthe sāgnike dvije \|*  
*daśame kiṃ malasnānaṃ kāryaṃ sarvajanaiḥ saha \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Why is a bath prescribed for the householder on the second day? Why on the fourth day? Why on the tenth day along with the mourners?

## \|\| 28.22 \|\|

*kasmāttailodvartanaṃ ca skandhavāhān gṛhaṃ nayet \|*  
*taiḥ samudvartanaṃ cāpi dadyuḥ sthalajalāśraye \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Why should they anoint their bodies with oil. Why should the corpse-bearers be taken home on return from the cremation ground? Why should the corpse-bearers anoint their bodies with oil on the bank of a stream or a stagnant reservoir?

## \|\| 28.23 \|\|

*deśame 'hani yaḥ piṇḍastaṃ dadyādāmiṣeṇa tu \|*  
*piṇḍaṃ caikādaśe kasmād vṛṣotsargaḥ kathaṃ bhavet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The pinda given on the tenth day is accompanied by meat, why? Why is the pinda on the eleventh day given after releasing the bull?

## \|\| 28.24 \|\|

*śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśaitāni abdaṃ yāvatkuto vada \|*  
*annādicodakenaiva ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Why the sixteen sraddhas? Why are the food, water etc. given everyday for three hundred and sixty days of the year?

## \|\| 28.25 \|\|

*dinedine ca dātavyaṃ ghaṭānnaṃ pretatṛptaye \|*  
*prāpte kāle ca mriyate anityo mānavaḥ prabho \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Why is the food put in a vessellfor satiating the dead? О lord, does the mortal man die at the appointed time?

## \|\| 28.26-27 \|\|

*chidraṃ tu naiva paśyāmi kuto jīvaḥ sa nirgataḥ \|*  
*kuto gacchanti bhūtāni pṛthivyāpo manastathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*tejo vadasva me nātha vāyurākāśameva ca \|*  
*vāyavaścaiva pañcaite kathaṃ gacchanti cāptaye \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. I do not see an outlet, then from where does the soul go away? From where do the elements earth, water, fire, air and sky pass away? tell me О lord!

## \|\| 28.28 \|\|

*lobhamohādayaḥ pañca śarīre caiva taskarāḥ \|*  
*tṛṣṇā kāmo 'pyahaṅkāraḥ kuto yānti janārdana \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. From where do the five thieves of the body-greed, affection, desire, love and pride go away, О lord!

## \|\| 28.29 \|\|

*puṇyaṃ vāpyatha vāpuṇyaṃ yatkiñcitsukṛtaṃ tathā \|*  
*naṣṭe dehe kuto yānti dānāni vividhāni ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Whatever little action has been done, whether good or bad, whatever gifts, have been tendered which procure merit, where do these go away after the body has decayed?

## \|\| 28.30 \|\|

*sapiṇḍanaṃ kimarthaṃ ca pūrṇe saṃvatsare 'pi vā \|*  
*pretasya melanaṃ sārdhaṃ kaiḥ samaṃ tatra ko vidhiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Why is the rite of sapindana done within the year of death? With whom is the union of the deceased sought for and how?

## \|\| 28.31 \|\|

*ye dagdhā ye tvadagdhāśca patitā ye narābhuvi \|*  
*yāni cānyāni bhūtāni teṣāmante bhavecca kim \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. What does in the end happen to the people who pass away burnt or unbumt or who have fallen off their caste or who have committed sins or behaved badly or changed faith or committed suicide or murdered a Brahmin or turned thieves or deceivers?

## \|\| 28.32 \|\|

*pāpino ye durācārā mudgalatvaṃ ca ye gatāḥ \|*  
*ātmaghātī brahmahā ca steyī viśvāsaghātakaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. What happens to that Shudra who drinks the milk of a brown cow, reads scriptures, wears the sacred thread or possesses a Brahmin wife? О lord of the world, I am terribly afraid of that sinner. Please answer my queries, for the welfare of the world.

## Chapter 29

## \|\| 29.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā bhadra mānuṣāṇāṃ hitāya vai \|*  
*śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā sarvamevaurdhvadaihikam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shri Krsna said: — О noble being, you have put pertinent questions to me. Listen to my explanation of all obsequial rites for the benefit of the people.

## \|\| 29.2 \|\|

*kamyagvibhedarahitaṃ śrutismṛtisamuddhṛtam \|*  
*yanna dṛṣṭaṃ suraiḥ sendrairyogibhiryogacintakaiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. I shall explain the same on the authorities of the Vedas and Smrti texts. This has not been revealed even to gods and yogins who are devoted to meditation on the self.

## \|\| 29.3 \|\|

*guhyādguhyataraṃ vatsa nākhyātaṃ kasyacitkvacit \|*  
*bhaktastvaṃ hi mahābhāga sarvaṃ te kathayāmyaham \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Dear, it is a guarded secret, not disclosed to anyone hitherto. Since you are my devotee I shall keep nothing hidden from you.

## \|\| 29.4 \|\|

*aputrasya gatirnāsti svargo naiva ca naiva ca \|*  
*yena kenāpyupāyena kāryaṃ janma sutasya ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. There is no salvation for a man without a son. He can never attain heaven without a son. He must obtain a son, somehow.

## \|\| 29.5 \|\|

*tārayennarakātputro yadi mokṣo na vidyate \|*  
*dāhaḥ putreṇa kartavyo deyaḥ pautreṇa pāvakaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Even if ultimate salvation is not possible, a son will necessarily save him from hell.,The cremation rites should be performed by the son. A grandson can lit the funeral pyre.

## \|\| 29.6 \|\|

*tilairdarbhaiśca bhūmyāṃ vai kuṭī dhātumatī bhavet \|*  
*pañcaratnāni vaktre tu yena jīvaḥ prarohati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. By strewing gingelly seeds and Darbha grass on the ground the attention of the mind is diverted to Vaikuntha. If the five gems area put into the mouth, life nourishes (in the next birth).

## \|\| 29.7 \|\|

*lipyāttu gomayairbhūmiṃ tilāndarbhāṃśca niḥ kṣipet \|*  
*tasyāmevāturo muktaḥ sarvaṃ dahati pātakam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The ground should be smeared with, cow- dung. Gingelly seeds and Darbha grass should be strewn. The sick man put thereon will be able to burn off his sins.

## \|\| 29.8 \|\|

*darbhamūlīnayetsvargaṃ saṃsthitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*darbhāṃstatra hi ye bhūmyāṃ tilayuktāna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The Darbha bed helps the sick man ascend the heaven. In the midst of the pack of Darbha grass gingelly seeds should be strewn.

## \|\| 29.9 \|\|

*sarvatra vasudhā pūtā yatra lepo na vidyate \|*  
*yatra lepaḥ sthitastatra punarlepena śudhyati \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. If the ground is not smeared with cowdung, there is no purity. If smeared already, it should be re-smeared. Then alone it becomes pure and holy.

## \|\| 29.10 \|\|

*yātudhānāḥ piśācāśca rākṣasāḥ krūrakarmiṇaḥ \|*  
*alipte āturaṃ muktaṃ viśantyete na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Evil spirits, demons, ghosts and terrible giants of low strata attack the sick man lying on the ground which is not smeared with cowdung by the people,

## \|\| 29.11 \|\|

*nityahomaṃ tathā śrāddhaṃ viprāṇāṃ pādaśodhanam \|*  
*maṇḍalena vinā bhūmyāṃ kurvantyetacca niṣphalam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. If the daily homa, srdddha or washing the feet of the holy Brahmin guests are performed on the bare ground without the mystic diagram drawn they are no better than left undone.

## \|\| 29.12 \|\|

*āturo mucyate naiva maṇḍalena vinā bhuvi \|*  
*brahmā rudraśca viṣṇuśca śrīrhutāśana eva ca \|*  
*maṇḍale copatiṣṭhantastasmātkurvīta maṇḍalam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Without the mystic diagram it is not possible to secure the recovery of the sick. The gods Brahma, Visnu, Rudra, the goddess Shri and the sacrificial fire preside over the mystic diagram. Hence, the mystic diagram should be drawn invariably.

## \|\| 29.13-14 \|\|

*anyathā mriyate yastu bālo vṛddho yuvāpi vā \|*  
*yonyantaraṃ sa vai gacchetkrīḍate vāyunā saha \|\| 13 \|\|*

*miśritaṃ lohatāmraṃ tu tathaiva janma jāyate \|*  
*tasmaivaṃ vāyubhūtasya na śrāddhaṃ nodaka kriyā \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. Otherwise, the dead man whether child, youth, or old cannot transmigrate. He will be playing about with the wind. In the aerial form, the dead has no sraddha rite nor water libation.

## \|\| 29.15 \|\|

*mama svedasamudbhūtāstilāstārkṣya pavitrakāḥ \|*  
*asurā dānavā daityāstṛpyanti tiladānataḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Gingelly seeds originate from my sweat, О Garuda and hence are holy. Asuras, Danavas and Daityas flee from the place where gingelly seeds are kept.

## \|\| 29.16 \|\|

*tilāḥ śvetāstilāḥ kṛṣṇāstilā gomūtrasannibhāḥ \|*  
*te me dahantu pāṣāni śarīreṇa kṛtāni ca \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Gingelly seeds, white, black or brown destroy sins committed by the body.

## \|\| 29.17 \|\|

*eka eva tiladroṇo hemadroṇatilaiḥ samaḥ \|*  
*tarpaṇe dānahome ca datto bhavati cākṣayaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. One gingelly seed offered in the holy rite is on a par with the gift of a drona measure of golden gingelly seeds. Gingelly seeds offered in tarpana and homa have an everlasting benefit.

## \|\| 29.18-19 \|\|

*darbhā mallomasambhūtāstilāḥ svedasamudbhavāḥ \|*  
*tṛptāḥ syurdevatā dānaiḥ śrāddhena pitarastathā \|*  
*prayogavidhinā brahmā viśvañcāpyupajīvanāt \|\| 18 \|\|*

*savyayajñopavītena brahmādyāstṛptimānpuyuḥ \|*  
*apasavyena tṛpyanti pitaro dividevatāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Darbha grass is born of my hair and the gingelly seeds originate from my sweat. Not otherwise. The holy sacred thread is an essential iterrYin all religious rites. The whole universe rests on it. Brahma and other deities are propitiated when the sacred thread is worn in the normal way. When it is worn over the right shoulder and under the left, arm, the manes are propitiated.

## \|\| 29.20 \|\|

*apasavyādito brahmā darbhamadhye tu keśavaḥ \|*  
*darbhāgre śaṅkaraṃ vidyāttrayo devāḥ kuśe sthitāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Brahma is stationed at the root of Darbha grass. Kesava is stationed in the middle. Know that Sankara is stationed at the tip of the Darbha grass. Thus, the three deities are stationed in the darbha grass.

## \|\| 29.21 \|\|

*viprā mantrāḥ kuśā vahnistulasī ca khageśvara \|*  
*naite nirmālyatāṃ yānti kriyamāṇāḥ punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Brahmins, mantras, kusa grass, fire and TulasI plant, О lord of birds, never become impure, though utilized frequently.

## \|\| 29.22 \|\|

*kuśāḥ piṇḍeṣu nirmālyāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ pretabhojane \|*  
*mantrāḥ śūdreṣu patitāścitāyāśca hutāśanaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Kusas become impure, when used with pindas. Brahmins become impure when they partake of preta food. Mantras become futile when practised by Shudras. fire in the funeral pyre is not so holy.

## \|\| 29.23 \|\|

*tulasī brāhmaṇā gāvo viṣṇurekādaśī khaga \|*  
*pañca pravahaṇānyeva bhavābdhau majjatāṃ satām \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. О bird, there are five types of boats to succour saintly men who тару otherwise get drowned in the ocean of worldly existence- TulasI, Brahmins, Cows, Visnu and the EkadasI day.

## \|\| 29.24 \|\|

*viṣṇurekādaśī gītā tulasīvipradhenavaḥ \|*  
*apāre durgasaṃkāre ṣaṭpadī muktidāyinī \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. In the worthless but impassable ocean of worldly existence there are six holy things yielding salvation — Visnu EkadasI, Ganga, TulasI, Brahmins and Cows.

## \|\| 29.25 \|\|

*tilāḥ pavitrāstrividhā darbhāśca tulasīdālam \|*  
*nivārayanti caitāni durgatiṃ yāntamāturam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Gingelly seeds are holy and unparalleled. Similarly, darbhas and TulasI too. The three ward off mishaps.

## \|\| 29.26 \|\|

*hastābhyāmuddhṛtairdarbhaistoyena prokṣayedbhuvam \|*  
*mṛtyukāle kṣipeddarbhānāturasya karadvaye \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Holding the Darbha grass in the hand, the performer of the rites shall sprinkle water over the ground. At the time of death, the darbha grass should be strewn near the bed of the dying person.

## \|\| 29.27 \|\|

*darbheṣu kṣipyate yo 'sau dabhastu pariveṣṭitaḥ \|*  
*viṣṇulokaṃ sa vai yāti mantrahīno 'pi mānavaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The dead body should be enveloped with darbha grass and placed over the bed made of the same grass. Then it is sure that the dead man will attain Visnu's region, even without the recital of mantras.

## \|\| 29.28 \|\|

*darbhamūlīgato bhūmau darbhapāṇistu yo mṛtaḥ \|*  
*prāyaścittaviśuddho 'sau saṃsārepārasāgare \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The man lying on the death-bed of Kusa grass on the ground is as pure as a man who has performed expiatory rites in the world.

## \|\| 29.29 \|\|

*gomayenopalipte tu darbhasyāstaraṇe sthitaḥ \|*  
*tatra dattena dānena sarvaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohati \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. If the ground is smeared with the Cowdung and the death bed is made of Kusa grass, whatever charity is given therefrom dispels all sins.

## \|\| 29.30 \|\|

*lavaṇaṃ tadrasaṃ divyaṃ sarvakāmapradaṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*yasmādannarasāḥ sarve notkaṭā lavaṇaṃ vinā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Salt is on a par with everything divine. It yields everything the person wishes for himself. No dish tastes sweet without salt.

## \|\| 29.31 \|\|

*pitṝṇāṃ ca priyaṃ bhavyaṃ tasmātsvargapradaṃ bhavet \|*  
*viṣṇudehasamudbhūto yato 'yaṃ lavaṇo rasaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Hence, salt is favourite with the manes. The gift of salt leads them to heaven. It is said that salt is originated from Visnu's body.

## \|\| 29.32-33 \|\|

*viśeṣāllavaṇaṃ dānaṃ tena śaṃsanti yoginaḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇa kṣattriyaviśāṃ strīṇāṃ śūdrajanasya ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

*āturāṇāṃ yadā prāṇāḥ prayānti vasudhātale \|*  
*lavaṇaṃ tu tadā deyaṃ dbārasyoddhāṭanaṃ divaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Hence, yogins praise a gift of salt. Whenever a Brahmin, a Ksatriya, a Vaisya or a sudra is on.deathbed, salt should be given as a gift. It opens the doorway to heaven.

## Chapter 30

## \|\| 30.1-2 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tārkṣya paraṃ guhyaṃ dānānāṃ dānamuttamam \|*  
*paramaṃ sarvadānānāṃ paraṃ gopyaṃ divaukasām \|\| 1 \|\|*

*deyamekaṃ mahādānaṃ kārpāsaṃ cottamottamam \|*  
*yena dattena prīyante bhūrbhuvaḥ svariti kramāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. The Lord said: — Listen О Garuda, I shall explain to you the best of all gifts which when rendered with the mantra bhu, bhuvah; svah delight the sages and gods-Shiva, Indra, etc.

## \|\| 30.3-4 \|\|

*brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvāḥ kāryācca prītimāpnuyuḥ \|*  
*deyametanmahādānaṃ pretoddharaṇahetave \|\| 3 \|\|*

*ciraṃ vasedrudraloke tato rājā bhavediha \|*  
*rūpavānsubhago vāgmī śrīmānatula vikramaḥ \|*  
*yamalokaṃ vinirjitya svargaṃ tākṣya sa gacchati \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. This great gift should be given for redeeming the ghosts. He who offers it will stay in Rudraloka for a long time and then be born as a king. He will be endowed with comely features, good fortune, fluency in speech, all riches and unparalleled valour. Leaving off Yama's region he will go to heaven.

## \|\| 30.5 \|\|

*gāṃ tilāṃśca kṣitaṃ hema yo dadāti dvijanmane \|*  
*tasya janmārjirta pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādevanaśyati \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He who, makes a gift of gingelly seeds, cow,plot of land or gold to a worthy Brahmin will have his sins, accruing in different births, instantaneously quelled.

## \|\| 30.6 \|\|

*tilā gāvo mahādānaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam \|*  
*taddvayaṃ dīyate vipre nānyavarṇe kadācana \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Gingelly seeds and cows constitute the major gifts and they quell all major sins. The two should be given only to a Brahmin and never to a member of any other caste.

## \|\| 30.7 \|\|

*kalpitaṃ dīyate dānaṃ tilā gāvaścamedinī \|*  
*anyeṣu naiva varṇeṣu poṣyavarge kadācana \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Gingelly seeds, cow, plot of land are given to Brahmins after the rite of sankalpa. They are not given to the people of other castes. Sometimes, they are given to dependents also.

## \|\| 30.8 \|\|

*poṣyavarge tathā strīṣu dānaṃ deyamakalpitam \|*  
*āture voparāge ca dvayaṃ dānaṃ viśiṣyate \|*  
*āture dīyate dānaṃ tatkāle copatiṣṭhati \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Whenever a gift is made to dependents or to women, it is done without the rite of sankalpa. Whenever a person is in death-bed or when there is an eclipse, all kinds of gifts should be made.

## \|\| 30.9 \|\|

*jīvatastu punardattamupatiṣṭhatyasaṃskṛtam \|*  
*satyaṃsatyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ yaddattaṃ vikalendriye \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. When someone is in the death bed it is better to make the gifts before the life is extinct. A gift made when one is alive stands in good stead, unimpeded.

## \|\| 30.10 \|\|

*yaccānu modate putrastacca dānamanantakam \|*  
*ato dadyāt sa putro vā yāvajjīvatsasau ciram \|*  
*ativāhastathā preto bhogāṃśca labhate yataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It is truth, the exact truth that the gifts made when the sense-organs are operative and, when the same are approved by his son yield endless benefits.

## \|\| 30.11 \|\|

*asvasthā turakāle tu dehapāte kṣitisthite \|*  
*dehe tathātivāhasya parataḥ prīṇanaṃ bhavet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. During the period of sickness, when the body is lying on the ground and during transport he will have no trouble.

## \|\| 30.12 \|\|

*paṅgāvandhe ca kāṇe ca hyardhonmīlitalocane \|*  
*tileṣu darbhānsaṃstīrya dānamuktaṃ tadakṣayam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. When the person is in the deathbed with eyes half-closed, the gift of gingelly seeds alongwith kusa grass to the lame, the blind and the one-eyed yields an imperishable fruit.

## \|\| 30.13 \|\|

*tilā lauhaṃ hiraṇyañca kārpāsaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*saptadhānyaṃ kṣitirgāva ekaikaṃ pāvanaṃ smṛtam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Gingelly seeds, iron, gold, cotton, salt, seven grains, plot of land and cow-each constitutes a holy gift.

## \|\| 30.14 \|\|

*lohadānādyamastuṣyeddharma rājastilārpaṇāt \|*  
*lavaṇe dīyamāne tu na bhayaṃ vidyate yamāt \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The gift of iron and gingelly seeds pleases Yama. The gift of salt wards off fear from Yama.

## \|\| 30.15 \|\|

*karpāsasya tu dānena na bhūtebhyo bhayaṃ bhavet \|*  
*tārayanti naraṃ gāvastrividhāścaiva pātakāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The gift of cotton wards off danger from the ghost. Cows gifted away enable the giver to cross all difficulties and save him from the threefold affliction.

## \|\| 30.16 \|\|

*hemadānātsukhaṃ svarge bhūmidānānnṛpo bhavet \|*  
*hemabhūmipradānācca na pīḍā narake bhavet \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The gift of gold yields happiness in heaven. By gifting a plot of land one is born as a king. If one gifts gold and plot of land one shall never experience any torture or distress in hell.

## \|\| 30.17 \|\|

*sarve 'pi yamadūtāśca yamarūpā vibhīṣaṇāḥ \|*  
*sarve te varadā yānti saptadhānyena prīṇitāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The emissaries of Yama who are as terrific in appearance as Yama himself bestow boons if gifts of seven grains are made and they are satiated.

## \|\| 30.18 \|\|

*viṣṇoḥ smaraṇamātreṇa prāpyate paramā gatiḥ \|*  
*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ martyairyā gatirāpyate \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. By remembering Visnu, one attains the highest goal. Thus, I have told you all 'about the goal attainable by the mortals.

## \|\| 30.19 \|\|

*tasmāt putraṃ praśaṃsanti dadāti piturājñayā \|*  
*bhūmiṣṭhaṃ pitaraṃ dṛṣṭvā hyardhonmīlitalocanam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. If the son on seeing his father lying on the ground with the. eyes half-closed makes gifts with the consent of his father, he is honoured by the gods.

## \|\| 30.20 \|\|

*tasmin kāle suto yastu sarvadānāni dāpayet \|*  
*gayāśrāddhādviśiṣyeta sa putraḥ kulanandanaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. At the time when the father is in the deathbed, the gifts, made by the son exceed the merit one earns by performing a sraddha at Gaya. The son prospers and thereby pleases his family.

## \|\| 30.21 \|\|

*svasthānāccalitaścāsau vikalasya pitustadā \|*  
*putrairyatnena kartavyā pitaraṃ tārayanti te \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The father being dead, the sons should perform the obsequial rites devotedly. Thus, they can make their father cross the ocean of distress.

## \|\| 30.22 \|\|

*kiṃ dattairbahubhirdānaiḥ piturantyeṣṭimācaret \|*  
*aśvamedho mahāyajñaḥ kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. What avail the abundance of charitable deeds. Enough that he performs the funeral rites of his father. Even the horse sacrifice does not merit a sixteenth part of the merit accruing from all these.

## \|\| 30.23 \|\|

*dharmātmā sa nu putro vaidevairapi supūjyate \|*  
*dāpayedyastu dānāni hyāturaṃ pitaraṃ bhuvi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The son who makes gifts in favour of his father who is lying ill in death-bed is honoured even by the gods.

## \|\| 30.24 \|\|

*lohadānañca dātavyaṃ bhūmiyuktena pāṇinā \|*  
*yamaṃ bhīmañca nāpnoti na gacchet tasya veśmani \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The father should make gifts of iron with his hands fixed in the ground. The giver of gifts never finds Yarna terrific. He will not go to his abode even.

## \|\| 30.25 \|\|

*kuṭhāro musalo daṇḍaḥ khaḍgaśca cchurikā tathā \|*  
*etāni yamahasteṣu dṛśyāni pāpakarmiṇām \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Dagger, iron club, baton, sword and lancet are the weapons in the hands of Yama to curb sinners.

## \|\| 30.26 \|\|

*tasmāllohasya dānantu brāhmaṇāyāturo dadet \|*  
*yamāyudhānāṃ santuṣṭyai dānametadudāhṛtam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Hence, gifts of weapons shall be made. For, this gift is for the propriation of Yama's weapons.

## \|\| 30.27 \|\|

*garbhasthāḥ śiśavo ye ca yuvānaḥ sthavirāstathā \|*  
*ebhirdānaviśeṣaistu nirdaheyuḥ svapātakam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. A child in the womb, infant in the arms, young men or old men can burn off their sins by means of these gifts.

## \|\| 30.28 \|\|

*churiṇaḥ śyāmaśabalau ṣaṇḍāmarkā udumbarāḥ \|*  
*śabalā śyāmadūtā ye lohadānena prīṇitāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The different emissaries of YamaChurinas, pandas, Markas, Udumbaras are delighted by gifts of iron.

## \|\| 30.29 \|\|

*putrāḥ pautrāstathā bandhuḥ sagotrāḥ suhahṛdastathā \|*  
*dadate nāture dānaṃ brahmaghnaistu samā hi te \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Sons, grandsons, kinsmen, clansmen, friends, wives, etc. of those who do not make such gifts are no better than the slayers of Brahmins.

## \|\| 30.30 \|\|

*pañcatve bhūmiyuktasya śṛṇu tasya ca yā gatiḥ \|*  
*ativāhaḥ punaḥ pretovarṣordhvaṃ sukṛtaṃ labhet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Listen to the course of a man dying on the earth. The first stage is that of Ativaha (the subtle body in transit); then that of ghost; then at the end of a year that of a pitr.

## \|\| 30.31 \|\|

*agnitrayaṃ trayo lokāstrayo vedāstrayo 'marāḥ \|*  
*kālatrayaṃ trisandhyaṃ ca trayo varṇāstriśaktayaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. There arc three fires, three worlds, three Vedas, three deities, three times, three junctures, three Varnas and three powers.

## \|\| 30.32-33 \|\|

*pādādūrdhvaṃ kaṭiṃ yāvat tāvadbrahyādhitiṣṭhati \|*  
*grīvāṃ yāvaddharirnābheḥ śarīre manujasya ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

*mastake tiṣṭhatīśāno vyaktāvyakto maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*ekamūrtestrayo bhāgā brahmā viṣṇuheśvarāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Brahma presides over the body of a man from the foot to the hips. Visnu presides over the body from the umbilicus to the neck. The manifest and the unmanifest deity Shiva in the form of Rudra is stationed on the head. The deities Brahma, Visnu and Shiva are only the different aspects of the same godhead.

## \|\| 30.34 \|\|

*ahaṃ prāṇaḥ śarīrastho bhūtagrāmacatuṣṭaye \|*  
*dharmādharme matiṃ dadyāt sukhaduḥkhe kṛtākṛte \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. I am the vital air stationed inside the body of living beings. Considering the inclination of the living being as actuated by the actions of his previous birth, I mould the mind and divert it to virtue or evil, joy or sorrow, or things done or undone.

## \|\| 30.35 \|\|

*jantorvuddhiṃ samāsthāya pūrvamarmādhivāsitām \|*  
*ahameva tathā jīvānprerayāmi ca karmasu \|*  
*svargaṃ ca narakaṃ mokṣaṃ prayānti prāṇino dhruvam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. It is I who induce their activities. By actions life attains heaven, hell or salvation.

## \|\| 30.36 \|\|

*svargasthaṃ narakasthaṃ vā śrāddhe vāpyāyanaṃ bhavet \|*  
*tasmācchrāddhāni kurvīta trividhāni vicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The prosperity of man, consigned to heaven or hell, is possible through Shraddhas. Hence, people shall assiduously perform different kinds of sraddhas.

## \|\| 30.37 \|\|

*matsyaṃ karmaṃ ca vārāhaṃ nārasiṃhañca vāmanam \|*  
*rāmaṃ rāmaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ ca buddhaṃ caiva sakalkinam \|*  
*etāni daśa nāmāni smartavyāni sadā budhaiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The names of the ten incarnations of the lord, viz. Matsya (fish), Kurma (tortoise), Varaha (boar), Narasirhha, Shrirama, Parsu- rama, Krsna, Balarama, Buddha and Kalki shall be remembered always.

## \|\| 30.38 \|\|

*svargaṃ jīvāḥ sukhaṃ yānti cyutāḥ svargācca mānavāḥ \|*  
*labdhvā sukhaṃ ca vittaṃ ca dayādākṣiṇyasaṃyutāḥ \|*  
*putrapautrairdhanairāḍhyā jīveyuḥ śaradāṃ śatam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He will be posted in heaven. After enjoying the pleasures therein when he is born again on the Earth, he will enjoy riches. He will be endowed with the qualities of mercy and chivalry. He will have sons and grandsons: He will live for a hundred years.

## \|\| 30.39 \|\|

*āture ca dadeddānaṃ viṣṇupūjāñca kārayet \|*  
*aṣṭākṣaraṃ tathā mantraṃ japedvā dvādaśākṣaram \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He should offer gifts to the needy and adore Visnu with the requisite Nyasas or repeat the mantras of eight syllables or twelve syllables.

## \|\| 30.40 \|\|

*pūjayecchuklapuṣpaiśca naivedyairghṛtapācitaiḥ \|*  
*tathā gandhaiśca dhūpaiśca śrutismṛtimanūditaiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. White flowers should be offered in worship together with food offerings cooked in ghee. Frequent incenses and scents should be offered and hymns from the Vedas, Smrtis such as of Manu should be recited.

## \|\| 30.41 \|\|

*viṣṇurmātā pitā viṣṇurviṣṇuḥ svajanabāndhavāḥ \|*  
*yatra viṣṇuṃ na paśyāmi tena vāsena kiṃ mama \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Visnu is mother, Visnu is father. Visnu is kinsman. If 1 do not see Visnu nothing is of any consequence to me.

## \|\| 30.42 \|\|

*jale viṣṇuḥ sthale viṣṇurviṣṇuḥ parvatamastake \|*  
*jvālāmālākule viṣṇuḥ sarvaṃ viṣṇumayaṃ jagat \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Vismi is on earth. Visnu is in water. Visnu is on the mountain. Visnu is in fiery flames. Everything identifiable in the universe is with Visnu.

## \|\| 30.43 \|\|

*vayamāpo vayaṃ pṛthvī vayaṃ darbhā vayaṃ tilāḥ \|*  
*vayaṃ gāvo vayaṃ rājā vayaṃ vāyurvayaṃ prajāḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. I am the waters. I am the Earth. I am the darbha grass: I art the gingeVy seeds. I am the cows. I am the subjects.

## \|\| 30.44 \|\|

*vayaṃ hema vayaṃ dhānyaṃ vayaṃ madhu vayaṃ ghṛtam \|*  
*vayaṃ viprā vayaṃ devā vayaṃ śambhuśca bhūrbhuvaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. I am gold. I am grains. 1 am honey. 1 am ghee. I am Brahmins. I am deities. I am Shiva. I am mantras: bhuh, bhuvah, svah.

## \|\| 30.45 \|\|

*ahaṃ dātā ahaṃ grāhī ahaṃ yajvā ahaṃ kratuḥ \|*  
*ahaṃ hartā ahaṃ dharmo ahaṃ pṛthvī hyahaṃ jalam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. I am the giver. I am the taker. I am the sacrificer. I am the sacrifice. I am the remover. I am virtue, earth and water.

## \|\| 30.46 \|\|

*dharmādharme matiṃ dadyāṃ karmabhistu śubhāśubhaiḥ \|*  
*yat karma kriyate kvāpi pūrvajanmārjitaṃ khaga \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О bird, I divert a man's mind to virtue or evil through auspicious and inauspicious activities. Whatever man does is actuated by the actions of his previous birth.

## \|\| 30.47 \|\|

*dharme matimahaṃ dadyāmadharme 'pyahameva ca \|*  
*yātanāṃ kurute so 'pi dharme muktiṃ dadāmyaham \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. It is I who induce thoughts of virtue. It is Yama who induces thoughts of evil. It is I who induce thoughts of ascetics towards virtue. It is I who accord salvation.

## \|\| 30.48 \|\|

*manujānāṃ hitā tārkṣya ante vaitaraṇī smṛtā \|*  
*tayāvamatya pāpaughaṃ viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. О Garuda, the river VaitaranI is beneficent to men of virtue. After dispelling all sins the virtuous man goes to Visnu's region.

## \|\| 30.49-51 \|\|

*bālatve yacca kaumāre yacca pariṇatau ca yat \|*  
*sarvāvamthākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yacca janmāntareṣvapi \|\| 49 \|\|*

*yanniśāyāṃ tathā prātaryanmadhyāhnāparāhnayoḥ \|*  
*sandhyayoryat kṛtaṃ karma karmaṇā manasā girā \|\| 50 \|\|*

*dattvā varāṃ sakṛdapi kapilāṃ sarvakāmikām \|*  
*uddharedantakāle sa ātmānaṃ pāpasañcayāt \|\| 51 \|\|*

49-51. If one makes a gift of a brown cow which grants all wishes, one is freed from ail sins-die sins committed in infancy, childhood or youth, in previous births, during nights or mornings, middays or afternoons, at dusk or dawn whether it is by means of action, thought or speech.

## \|\| 30.52-53 \|\|

*gāvo mamāgrataḥ santu pṛṣṭhataḥ pārśvatastathā \|*  
*gāvo me hṛdaye santu gavāṃ madhye vasāmyaham \|\| 52 \|\|*

*yā lakṣmīḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ yā ca deve vyavasthitā \|*  
*dhenurūpeṇa sā devī mama pāpaṃ vyapohatu \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. May cows remain in front of me, may cows remain behind me, may cows remain ever in my heart. I reside in the midst of cows May the splendour of ail living beings, the splendour present in deities remove my sins. May Laksmi assume the form of a cams and dispel my sins.

## Chapter 31

## \|\| 31.1 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*ye narāḥ pāpasaṃyuktāste gacchanti yamālayam \|*  
*nṛṇāṃ matsākṣikaṃ dattamanantaphaladaṃ bhavet \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. The Lord said: — Men tainted by sins go to Yama's abode. A gift made in my name accords endless benefits.

## \|\| 31.2 \|\|

*yāvadrajaḥ pramāṇābdaṃsvarge tiṣṭhati bhūmidaḥ \|*  
*aśvārūḍhāśca te yānti dadate ye hyupānahau \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. A person who gifts plots of land stays in heaven for as many years as the number of steps the land measures. Those who make gifts of sandals go seated on horse-back.

## \|\| 31.3 \|\|

*ātape śramayogena na dahyante ca kutracit \|*  
*chattradānena vai pretā vicaranti sukhaṃ pathi \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. By making a gift of umbrella one will traverse after death, without difficulty, the paths where other men are excessively oppressed by the heat of the sun.

## \|\| 31.4-5 \|\|

*yamuddiśya dadātyannaṃ tena cāpyāyito bhavet \|\| 4 \|\|*

*andhakāre mahāghore amūrte lakṣyavarjite \|*  
*uddyotenaiva te yānti dīpadānena mānavāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. It food is given in gift with the dead man in view he will be nourished by the same. Men who make gifts of lamps can traverse those regions infested by terrible darkness, without the aim or regular form, which shall for him become illuminated brightly.

## \|\| 31.6 \|\|

*āśvine kartike vāpi māghe mṛtatithāvapi \|*  
*caturdaśyāñca dīyeta dīpadānaṃ sukhāya vai \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The gift of lamp is conducive to the pleasure of those who die in the month of Asvina, Karttika or Magha or on the fourteenth day of the lunar fortnight..

## \|\| 31.7 \|\|

*pratyahañca pradātavyaṃ mārge suviṣame naraiḥ \|*  
*yāvat saṃvatsaraṃ vāpi pretasya sukhalipsayā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. lamps shall be gifted daily for a year after the death for the benefit of the dead in the troublesome path.

## \|\| 31.8 \|\|

*kule dyotati śuddhātmā prakāśatvaṃ sa gacchati \|*  
*jyotirmayo 'sau pūjyo 'sau dīpadānaprado naraḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The pure soul who is engaged in the gift of lamps shall brighten his path and his family. He shall be honoured among the luminaries of the sky.

## \|\| 31.9-10 \|\|

*prāṅmukhodaṅmukhaṃ dīpaṃ devāgāre dvijātaye \|*  
*kuryādyāmyamukhaṃ pitre adbhiḥ saṅkalpya susthiram \|\| 9 \|\|*

*sarvopahārayuktāni padānyatra trayodaśa \|*  
*yo dadāti mṛtasyeha jīvannapyātmahetave \|*  
*sa gacchati mahāmārge mahākaṣṭavivarjitaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. lie who makes a gift of lamps either for the dead or for himself facing east or north or places them in temples or in the houses of Brahmins will be able to traverse the Great Path without any distress.

## \|\| 31.11 \|\|

*āsanaṃ bhājanaṃ bhojyaṃ dīyate yaddvijāyate \|*  
*sukhe na bhuñjamānastu dena gacchatyalaṃ pathi \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He who makes gifts of seats, vessels or foodstuffs to Brahmins goes along the path happily and enjoys everything.

## \|\| 31.12-15 \|\|

*kamaṇḍalupradānena tṛṣitaḥ pibate jalam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*bhājanaṃ vastradānañca kusumañcāṅgulīyakam \|*  
*ekādaśā he dātavyaṃ pretoddharaṇahetave \|\| 13 \|\|*

*trayodaśa padānītthaṃ pretasya śubhamicchatā \|*  
*dātavyāni yathāśaktyā preto 'sau prīṇito bhavet \|\| 14 \|\|*

*bhojanā ni tilāṃścaiva udakumbhāṃstrayodaśa \|*  
*mudrikāṃ vastrayugmañca tayā yāti parāṃ gatim \|\| 15 \|\|*

12-15. By making a gift of water-jar the thirsty shall drink water. Vessels, clothes, flowers, rings shall be gifted on the eleventh day for the dead, to attain the highest goal. Thus those who wish the dead well shall make gifts of the thirteen articles according to their ability. The dead shall be pleased thereby. Foodstuffs, gingelly seeds, water-jars thirteen in number, a ring, pair of clothes should be gifted in favour of the dead to help him attain the highest goal,

## \|\| 31.16 \|\|

*yo 'śvaṃ nāvaṃ gajaṃ vāpi brāhmaṇe pratipādayet \|*  
*sa mahimno 'nusāreṇa tattatsukhamupāśnute \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He who makes gifts of a horse, a boat or an elephant to Brahmins will become happy.

## \|\| 31.17 \|\|

*nānālokān vicarati mahiṣīñca dadāti yaḥ \|*  
*yamaputtrasya yā mātā mahiṣī sugatipradā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Не who makes a gift of buffalo sojourns in many worlds. A she-buffalo, the mother of the vehicle of Yama, accords benefit.

## \|\| 31.18 \|\|

*tāmbūlaṃ kusumaṃ deyaṃ yāmyānāṃ harṣavardhanam \|*  
*tena samprīṇitāḥ sarve tasmin kleśaṃ na kurvate \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. A gift of betel leaves together with flowers increases the delight of those who are in Yama's abode. Being delighted with that they do not harass him.

## \|\| 31.19-20 \|\|

*go-bhū-tila-hiraṇyāni dānānyāhuḥ svaśaktitaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*mṛtoddeśena yo yadyājjalapātrañca mṛnmayam \|*  
*udapātrasahasrasya phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. The man who gives according to his ability gifts of cows, plots of lands, gingelly seeds, gold and an earthen jar full of water, with the dead in view attains the fruit of the gift of a thousand water-jars.

## \|\| 31.21 \|\|

*yamadūtā mahāraudrāḥ karālāḥ kṛṣṇapiṅgalāḥ \|*  
*na bhīṣayanti taṃ yāmyā vastradāne kṛte sati \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. If a person makes a gift of clothes, the awe-inspiring terrible emissaries of Yama black and tawny in colour do not threaten him.

## \|\| 31.22 \|\|

*mārge hi gacchamānastu tṛṣṇārtaḥ śramapīḍitaḥ \|*  
*ghaṭānnadānayogena sukhī bhavati niścitam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. By making gifts of potfuls of foodstuffs one shall be happy in those paths which one should otherwise have to traverse exhausted utterly and oppressed by thirst.

## \|\| 31.23 \|\|

*śayyā dakṣiṇayā yuktā āyudhāmbarasaṃyutā \|*  
*haimaśrīpatinā yuktā deyā viprāya śarmaṇe \|*  
*tathā pretatvamukto 'sau modate saha daivataiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. A bed with smooth cotton within and covered by a velvet cloth should be given to gods and Brahmins. By that the dead man will be freed from ghosthood and rejoice with deities.

## \|\| 31.24 \|\|

*etat te kathitaṃ tārkṣya dānamantyeṣṭikarmajam \|*  
*adhunā kathayiṣye 'hamanyadehapraveśanam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Thus, О Tarksya, I have explained to you the gifts which should be offered when performing obsequies. I shall now tell you how death enters the body,

## \|\| 31.25-26 \|\|

*jātasya mṛtyuloke vai prāṇino maraṇaṃ dhruvam \|*  
*mṛtiḥ kuryāt svadharmeṇa yāsyataśca parantapa \|\| 25 \|\|*

*pūrvakāle mṛtānāñca prāṇināñca khageśvara \|*  
*sūkṣmobhūtvā tvasau vāyurnirgacchatyāsyamaṇḍalāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. Death is inevitable to all living beings in this mortal world.

О lord of birds, when the living beings die, the vital air shrinking to ifs subtle size comes out of the throat.

## \|\| 31.27 \|\|

*navadvārai romabhiśca janānāṃ tālurandhrake \|*  
*pāpiṣṭhānāmapānena jīvo niṣkrāmati dhruvam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. It may escape through any of the nine apertures or through the pores of hair or through the palate. The subtle soul escapes through Apana in the case of sinners.

## \|\| 31.28 \|\|

*śarīrañca patet paścānnirgate marutīśvare \|*  
*vātāhataḥ patatyeva nirādhāro yathā drumaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. When the vital air escapes, the corpse falls down. When struck down by Death it falls like a tree bereft of its support.

## \|\| 31.29 \|\|

*pṛthivyāṃ līyate pṛthvī āpaścaiva tathāpsu ca \|*  
*tejastejasi līyate samīraṇaḥ samīraṇe \|*  
*ākāśe ca tathā kāśaḥ sarvavyāpī ca śaṅkare \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The earthly matter becomes merged in the principle of fire. The gaseous matter becomes assimilated in the principle of wind. The ethereal matter gets dissolved in the principle of ether and the all-pervasive soul gets merged in Sankara,

## \|\| 31.30 \|\|

*tatra kāmastathā krodhaḥ kāye pañcendriyāṇi ca \|*  
*ete tārkṣya samākhyātā dehe tiṣṭhanti taskarāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The five vices-passion, etc. and the five sense-organs, О bird, are the villains stationed in the body.

## \|\| 31.31 \|\|

*kāmaḥ krodho hyahaṅkāro manastatraiva nāyakaḥ \|*  
*saṃhārakaśca kālo 'yaṃ puṇyapāpasamanvitaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Lust, anger and egotism are led by the mind which is the annihilating agent in collusion with merit and demerit.

## \|\| 31.32 \|\|

*jagataśca svarūpantu nirmitaṃ svena karmaṇā \|*  
*punardehāntaraṃ yāti sukṛtairduṣkṛtairnaraḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The form of the universe is evolved out of one's own activities. The next body it transmigrates to is also the result of its merits and demerits.

## \|\| 31.33 \|\|

*pañcendriyasamāyuktaṃ sakalairviṣyaiḥ saha \|*  
*praviśet sa navaṃ dehaṃ gṛhe dagdhe yathā gṛhī \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Just as a householder enters a new furnished house after the former has been burnt and destroyed, so also the soul enters a new body endowed with all objqcts and the five sense- organs.

## \|\| 31.34-36 \|\|

*śarīre ye samāsīnā sambhavet sarvadhātavaḥ \|*  
*ṣāṭkauśiko hyayaṃ kāyo mātā pitrośca dhātavaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sambhaveyustathā tārkṣya sarve vātāśca dehinām \|*  
*mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ tadyogā ye cānye vyādhayastathā \|\| 35 \|\|*

*asthi śukraṃ tathā snāyuḥ dehena saha dahyate \|*  
*eṣa te kathitastārkṣya vināśaḥ sarvadehinām \|\| 36 \|\|*

34-36. Many are the excretions and discharges from the body including faeces, urine, bile, phlegm, marrow, flesh, fat, and semen. These together with the bones and sinews are burnt along with the body. О Tarksya, thus the position of these in all embodied beings has been clarified.

## \|\| 31.37-39 \|\|

*kathayāmi punasteṣāṃ śarīrañca yathā bhavet \|*  
*ekastambhaṃ snāyubaddhaṃ sthūṇādvayasamuddhṛtam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*indriyaiśca samāyuktaṃ navadvāraṃ śarīrakam \|*  
*viṣayaiśca samākrāntaṃ kāma-krodhasamākulam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*rāga-dveṣasamākīrṇaṃ tṛṣṇādurgasudustaram \|*  
*lobhajālasamāyuktaṃ puraṃ puruṣasaṃjñitam \|\| 39 \|\|*

37-39. Now, I shall tell you how the body looks like. Human body is a single column of sinews embellished with two pegs and endowed with the sense-organs in nine opertures. It is constantly attacked by the objects, agitated by lust and anger, tossed by love and hatred, victimised by an unquenchable thirst, entrapped in the network of covetousness, enshrouded in the cloth of greed, bound by illusion but presided over by a conscious being. It is, as it were, like a city with six vestibules with the Purusa in charge thereof.

## \|\| 31.40 \|\|

*etadguṇasamāyuktaṃ śarīraṃ sarvadehinām \|*  
*tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ sarvā bhuvanāni caturdaśa \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. All the bodies are invested with these attributes and features. All the deities and the fourteen worlds are present therein.

## \|\| 31.41 \|\|

*ātmānaṃ ye na jānanti te narāḥ paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*evametanmayākhyātaṃ śarīraṃ te caturvidham \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Those who do not understand themselves ate no better than brutes. The four kinds of living beings have their bodies of the same nature and same content.

## \|\| 31.42 \|\|

*caturaśītilakṣāṇi nirmitā yonayaḥ purā \|*  
*udbhijjāḥ svedajāścaiva aṇḍajāśca jarāyujāḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Eight million four hundred thousand varieflos of creatures were created by me formerly.

## \|\| 31.43 \|\|

*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭohaṃ tvayānagha \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. They are swedajas, (insects), Udbhijas, (plants), andajas (birds) and jarayujas, born from the womb, О sinless one, I have thus explained what you asked me.

## Chapter 32

## \|\| 32.1-4 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*kathamutpadyate janturbhūtagrāme caturvidhe \|*  
*tvacā raktaṃ tathā māṃsaṃ medo majjāsthi jīvitam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*pādau pāṇī tathā guhyaṃ jihvā-keśa-nakhāḥ śiraḥ \|*  
*sandhimārgāśca bahuśo rekhā naikavidhāstathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*kāmaḥ krodho bhayaṃ lajjā mano harṣaḥ sukhāsukham \|*  
*citritaṃ chidritañcāpi nānājālena veṣṭitam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*indrajālamidaṃ manye saṃsāre 'sārasāgare \|*  
*kartā ko 'tra hṛṣīkeśa saṃsāre duḥ khasaṃkule \|\| 4 \|\|*

1-4. How does life come into' being in the four species of living beings? How are the following formed: skin, blood, flesh, fat, marrow, bone, the living nucleus, hands and feet, tongue, generative and excretory organs, hair, nails, joints, ducts and the different lines drawn over the body, lust and anger, fear and shame, mind, joy, skin of various colours punctured with holes and shrouded with a network of fatty matter. I consider it a wonderful piece of jugglery in this world of human existence. О mighty one, who is their guiding hand here? О lord, let me know all about this matter.

## \|\| 32.5 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*kathayāmi paraṃ gopyaṃ kośasyāsya vinirṇayam \|*  
*yasya vijñānamātreṇa sarvajñatvaṃ prajyate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The Lord said: — I shall tell you a great secret with the decisive method of unravelling the same, by knowing which one becomes omniscient.

## \|\| 32.6 \|\|

*sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā loke sadayaṃ jīvakāraṇam \|*  
*vainateya śṛṇuṣva tvamekāgrakṛtamānasaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O Garuda, you have put a pertinent question about the origin of life. Hence, listen attentively.

## \|\| 32.7 \|\|

*ṛtukāle ca nārīṇāṃ varjyaṃ dinacatuṣṭayam \|*  
*yatastasmin brahmahatyāṃ purā vṛtrasamutthitām \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The first four days of menstrual flow of blood shall be avoided when the sin of a Brahmin's slaughter resides in her.

## \|\| 32.8 \|\|

*brahmā śakrāt samuttārya caturthāṃśena dattavān \|*  
*tāvannālokyate vaktraṃ pāpaṃ yāvadvapuḥ sthitam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Brahma had removed this sin from Indra and apportioned a fourth in women. Hence, even the glancing at her face during these days shall be eschewed.

## \|\| 32.9 \|\|

*prathame 'hani cāṇḍālī dvitīye brahmaghātinī \|*  
*tṛtīye rajakī jñeyā caturthe 'hani śudhyati \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. A woman in her menses is an outcaste on the first day, a slayer of Brahmin on the second day and a washerwoman on the third. She becomes pure on the fourth day.

## \|\| 32.10 \|\|

*saptāhāt pitṛdevānāṃ bhavedyogyā kṛtārcane \|*  
*saptāhamadhye yo garbhastatsaṃmbhūtirmalimlucā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After seven days, she becomes pure enough to take part in the worship of manes and deities- If conception takes place within seven days the progeny is impure.

## \|\| 32.11 \|\|

*niṣakasamaye pitroryādṛk cittavikalpanā \|*  
*tādṛggarbhasamutpattirjāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Whatever be the thoughts of parents during the intercourse, like that is the progeny. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 32.12 \|\|

*yugmāsu puttrā jāyante striyo 'yugmāsu rātriṣu \|*  
*pūrvasaptamamutsṛjya tasmādyugmāsu saṃviśet \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If the sexual intercourse takes place on even nights, boys are conceived and if on odd nights girls are conceived. Hence, it is better to have sexual intercourse on even nights, leaving the first seven days after the menstrual discharge.

## \|\| 32.13-14 \|\|

*ṣoḍaśarturniśāḥ strīṇāṃ sāmānyāt samudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*yā caturdaśamī rātrirgarbhastiṣṭhati tatra cet \|\| 13 \|\|*

*guṇabhāgyanidhiḥ putrastatra jāyeta dhārmikaḥ \|*  
*sā niśā tatra sāmānyairna labhyeta khagādhipa \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. The sixteen days from the appearance of blood are the days of heat for women in a general way. If conception takes place after the intercourse on the fourteenth night, the son born will be virtuous and endowed with good qualifies and good fortune. Ordinary people will never get such an auspicious chance, О lord of birds.

## \|\| 32.15-16 \|\|

*prāyaśaḥ sambhavatyatra garbhastvaṣṭāhamadhyataḥ \|*  
*pañcame 'hani nārīṇāṃ kāryaṃ mādhuryabhojanam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*kaṭukṣārañca tīkṣṇañca tyājyamuṣṇañca dūrataḥ \|*  
*tatkṣetramoṣadhīpātraṃ bījañcāpyamṛtāyitam \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Conception, usually, takes place within eight days. On the fifth day the woman is given sweet dishes which is a good tonic for the embryo. Astringent and pungent things should not be taken at all. The woman's parts can be likened to a medicinal vessel. The seed of a man is like an ambrosial food.

## \|\| 32.17 \|\|

*tasminnuptvā naraḥ svāmī samyak phalamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*tasyāścaivātapo varjya śītalaṃ kevalaṃ caret \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. A man depositing his semen in her vagina is actually sowing a seed. For the proper growth of the child she should avoid excessive sunshine. Cooling articles should be resorted to.

## \|\| 32.18-19 \|\|

*tāmbūlapuṣpaśrīkhaṇḍaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ śucivastrabhṛt \|*  
*dharmamādāya manasi sutalpaṃ saṃviśet pumān \|\| 18 \|\|*

*niṣekasamaye yādṛṅnaracittavikalpanā \|*  
*tādṛksvabhāvasambhūtirjanturviśati kukṣigaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. On the night auspiciously selected for the intercourse, the pair should chew betel leaves and apply scents and sandal paste over their bodies. The ideas and thoughts that hover in the mind of the man at the lime of intercourse have a lasting influence in moulding the character of the child conceived.

## \|\| 32.20 \|\|

*śukrasoṇitasaṃyoge piṇḍotpattiḥ prajāyate \|*  
*vardhate jaṭhare jantustārāpatirivāmbare \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The semen mixingwiththebloodcauses the foetus.. The foetus develops in the womb like the moon in the sky.

## \|\| 32.21 \|\|

*caitanyaṃ bījarūpaṃ hi śukre nityaṃ vyavasthitam \|*  
*kāmaścittañca śukrañca yadā hyekatvamāpnuyuḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The consciousness is present in the semen m the-form of a seed. When love, mind and semen act in unison, a secretion takes place in the womb.

## \|\| 32.22 \|\|

*tadā drāvamavāpnoti yoṣāgarbhāśaye naraḥ \|*  
*raktādhikye bhavennārī śukrādhikye bhavet pumān \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. If the blood is predominant at intercourse, the child will be a girl. If the semen is predominant, the child will be a boy.

## \|\| 32.23 \|\|

*śukrasoṇitayo sāmye garbhāḥ ṣaṇḍatvamāpnuyuḥ \|*  
*ahorātreṇa kalilaṃ budvadaṃ pañcabhidinaiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. If the semen and the blood are of equal potency, the child will be a eunuch. The nucleus of the foetus is formed within a day. It becomes frothy bubble in five days.

## \|\| 32.24 \|\|

*caturdaśe bhavenmāṃsaṃ miśradhātusamanvitam \|*  
*ghanaṃ māṃsañca viṃśāhe garbhastho vardhate kramāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Within fourteen days it becomes a mixture of flesh, and lymphatic substances.

## \|\| 32.25 \|\|

*pañcaviṃśatime cāhni balaṃ puṣṭiśca jāyate \|*  
*tathā māse tu sampūrṇe pañcatattvaṃ nidhārayet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. It becomes a solid flesh in twenty days, sufficiently strong (in twenty five days) and (after that) it begins to-develop gradually. The foetus would have assimilated completely the five elements within a month.

## \|\| 32.26 \|\|

*māsadvaye tu sañjāte tvacā medaśca jāyate \|*  
*majjāsthīni tribhirmāsaiḥ keśāṅgulyaścaturthake \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. By the time the two months are over, the fat and the skin are evolved. The marrow and the bones are formed in three months time. The hair and the heels are formed in the fourth month,

## \|\| 32.27 \|\|

*karṇau ca nāsike vakṣo jāyeran māsi pañcame \|*  
*kaṇṭharandhrodaraṃ ṣaṣṭhe guhyādirmāsi saptame \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The ears, nose and the belly are formed in the fifth month. The throat, back and the reproductive organs are developed in the seventh month.

## \|\| 32.28 \|\|

*aṅgapratyaṅgasampūrṇo garbho māsairathāṣṭabhiḥ \|*  
*aṣṭame calate jīvo dhātrīgarbhe punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*navamemāsi samprāpte garbhasthaujau dṛḍhaṃ bhavet \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. By the eighth month, all the limbs are completed. In the beginning of the ninth month the child in the womb is fully developed.

## \|\| 32.29 \|\|

*cikitsā jāyate tasya garbhavāsaparikṣaye \|*  
*nārī vātha naro vātha napuṃstvaṃ vābhijāyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Whether the child be a girl or a boy or a eunuch dt comes out in the ninth or tenth month.

## \|\| 32.30-32 \|\|

*śaktitrayaṃ viśālākṣaṃ ṣāṭkauśikasamāyutam \|*  
*pañcendriyasamopetaṃ daśanāḍīvibhūṣitam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*daśaprāṇaguṇopetaṃ yo jānāti sa yogavit \|*  
*majjāsthiśukramāṃsāni roma raktaṃ balaṃ tathā \|\| 31 \|\|*

*ṣāṭkauśikamidaṃ piṇḍaṃ syājjantoḥ pāñcabhautikam \|*  
*navame daśame māsi jāyate pāñcabhautikaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

30-32. Equipped with three energies, big-eyed, endowed "with six kosas, five indriyas, ten nanis, ten pranas, the child as such is known by the Yogins. Marrow, bones, semen, flesh, hair, blood are the six kosas of the panca- bhautika body of a creature. This panca- bhautika body develops in the ninth or tenth month.

## \|\| 32.33 \|\|

*sūtivātaiḥ samākṛṣṭaḥ pīḍayā vihvalīkṛtaḥ \|*  
*puṣṭo nāḍyāḥ suṣumṇāyā yoṣidgarbhasthitastvaran \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Then being dragged by the wind in the womb, agitated with pain, nourished by the su- umna nerve in the woman's womb, the same comes out.

## \|\| 32.34-36 \|\|

*kṣitirvāri havirbhoktā pavanākāśameva ca \|*  
*ebhirbhūtaiḥ pīḍitastu nibaddhaḥ snāyubandhanaiḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*mūlabhūtā ime proktāḥ sapta nāḍyantare sthitāḥ \|*  
*tvacāsthināḍyo romāṇi māṃsañcaivātra pañcamam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ete pañca guṇāḥ proktā mayā bhūmeḥ khageśvara \|*  
*yathā pañca guṇāścāpastathā tacchṛṇu kāśyapa \|\| 36 \|\|*

34-36. The principles of earth, water, fire, wind and ether envelop it through the coils of sinews. О lord of birds, the parts predominant in the principle of earth are skin, bones, nerves hair and flesh. О son of Kasyapa lien to those predominant in the principle о water.

## \|\| 32.37 \|\|

*lālā mūtraṃ tathā śukraṃ majjāra raktañca pañcamam \|*  
*āpaḥ pañcaguṇāḥ proktā jñātavyāste prayatnataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The saliva, urine, semen, marrow and blood constitute the parts predominant in the principle of water. They should be realized carefully.

## \|\| 32.38 \|\|

*kṣudhā tṛṣā tathā nidrā ālasyaṃ kāntireva ca \|*  
*tejaḥ pañcaguṇaṃ proktaṃ tārkṣya sarvatrayogibhiḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Hunger, somnolence, thirst, lethargy and brilliance, О lord of birds, are considered by yogins as constituting the qualities of fire in the human constitution.

## \|\| 32.39 \|\|

*rāgadveṣau tathā lajjā bhayaṃ mohastathaiva ca \|*  
*ityetat kathitaṃ tārkṣya vāyujaṃ guṇapañcakam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Love, hatred, bashfulness, fear and delusion are the qualities, О bird, derived from wind.

## \|\| 32.40 \|\|

*ākuñcanaṃ dhāvanañca laṅghanañca prasāraṇam \|*  
*nirodhaḥ pañcamaḥ prokto vāyoḥ pañca guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Punning, breathing, contracting, stretching and thwarting are the qualities of wind in the physical body.

## \|\| 32.41 \|\|

*ghoṣaścintā ca gāmbhīryaṃ śravaṇaṃ satyasaṃkramaḥ \|*  
*ākāśasya guṇāḥ pañca jñāta vyāstārkṣya yatnataḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. О bird, resonant sound, cavity, gravity, hearing and supporting everything are the qualities of Ether.

## \|\| 32.42 \|\|

*śrotraṃ tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā nāsā buddhīndriyāṇi ca \|*  
*pāṇī pādau gudaṃ prāk ca guhyaṃ karmendriyāṇi ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Ears, skin, eyes, tongue and nose are the five sense-organs. Hands, feet, rectum, phallus and speech are the five organs of activity.

## \|\| 32.43-44 \|\|

*iḍāca piṅgalā caiva suṣumṇā ca tṛtīyakā \|*  
*gāndhārī gajajihvā ca pūṣā caiva yasā tathā \|\| 43 \|\|*

*alamvuśā kuhūścaiva śaṅkhinī daśamī smṛtā \|*  
*piṇḍa madhye sthitā hyetāḥ pradhānā daśa nāḍayaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. There are ten principal nerves in the body Ida Pingala, Susumna, Gandhari, Gajajihva, Pusa, Yasa, Alambusa, Kuhu and Sankhinl.

## \|\| 32.45 \|\|

*prāṇāpānau samānaśca udāno vyāna eva ca \|*  
*nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The ten vital airs stationed in the body are Prana, Apana, Samana, Udana, Vyana, Naga, Kurma, Krkara, Devadatta and Dhananjaya.

## \|\| 32.46 \|\|

*ityete vāyavaḥ proktā daśa deheṣu susthitāḥ \|*  
*kevalaṃ bhuktamannañca puṣṭidaṃ sarvadehinām \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The wind accords vitality and takes the assimilated food.to all the joints of the body.

## \|\| 32.47 \|\|

*nayate prāṇado vāyuḥ śarīre sarvasandhiṣu \|*  
*āhāro bhuktamātrastu vāyunā kriyate dvidhā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The food, as soon as it is taken, is split into two by the wind.

## \|\| 32.48-49 \|\|

*sa praviśya guhe samyak pṛthagannaṃ pṛthagjalam \|*  
*ūrdhvamagnerjalaṃ kṛtvā tadannañca jalopari \|\| 48 \|\|*

*agneścādhaḥ svayaṃ prāṇastamagniñca dhamecchanaiḥ \|*  
*vāyunā dhamyamāno 'gniḥ pṛthakkiṭṭaṃ pṛthagrasam \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. The digested food enters the rectum and the solid and liquid waste matter goes out separately. The prana blows on the gastric fire from below, over which food and water are brought by the wind. The fire thus blown separates the waste matter and the nutritive element.

## \|\| 32.50-51 \|\|

*malairdvādaśabhiḥ kiṭṭaṃ bhinnaṃ dehāt pṛthagbhavet \|*  
*karṇākṣināsikā jihvā dantanābhivapurgudam \|\| 50 \|\|*

*nakhā malāśrayā hyete viṇmūtrañcetyanantakam \|*  
*śukraśoṇitasaṃyogādetat ṣāṭkauśikaṃ smṛtam \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. This waste matter goes out of the bodyin the form of twelve types of waste matter.

Ears, eyes, nose, tongue, teeth, navel, rectum, pores in the body, nails, kidneys are the passages through which the waste matter gets out. The six-chambered body is evolved out of the ymixfure of semen and blood.

## \|\| 32.52 \|\|

*romṇāṃ koṭyastathā tisro 'pyardhakoṭi samanvitāḥ \|*  
*dvātriṃśaddaśanāḥ proktāḥ sāmānyādvinatāsuta \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. О son of Vinata, the roots of hair in the body are thirty five millions and the teeth are thirty two in number usually.

## \|\| 32.53 \|\|

*sapta lakṣāṇi keśāḥ syurnakhāḥ proktāstu viṃśatiḥ \|*  
*māṃsaṃ palasahasraikaṃ sāmānyāddehasaṃsthitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The ails are twenty and the hair growing on the face and head are seven hundred thousand. Generally, the fleshy matter in the body weighs about a thousand

## \|\| 32.54 \|\|

*raktaṃ palaśataṃ tārkṣyaṃ buddhameva purātanaiḥ \|*  
*palāni daśa medaśca tvacā caiva tu tatsamā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. О bird, the blood is a hundred palas in weight as mentioned by elderly persons. The fat is about ten palas and the skin is about that much.

## \|\| 32.55 \|\|

*paladvādaśakaṃ majjā mahāraktaṃ palatrayam \|*  
*śukraṃ dvikuḍavaṃ jñeyaṃ śoṇitaṃ kuḍavaṃ smṛtam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The marrow is about twelve galas in weight and the blood plasma is three palas. The semen is two Kuidavas ui volume and the manstrual blood is one Kudava.

## \|\| 32.56 \|\|

*śleṣmāṇaśca ṣaḍūrdhvañca viṇmūtraṃ tatpramāṇataḥ \|*  
*asthnāṃ hi hyadhikaṃ proktaṃ ṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatrayāt \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. The weight of the phlegmatic matter is six and a halt palas. Faeces and urine are in accordance with indigested food and drink. This is the physical body and I,shall mention its allpervasive character.

## \|\| 32.57 \|\|

*evaṃ piṇḍaḥ samākhyāto vaibhavaṃ sampracakṣmahe \|*  
*sukhaṃ duḥ khaṃ bhayaṃ kṣemaṃ karmaṇaiva hi prāpyate \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Pleasure, pain, fear and welfare-these come to a person by actions alone.

## \|\| 32.58-59 \|\|

*adhomukhaṃ cordhvapādaṃ garbhādvāyuḥ prakarṣati \|*  
*tale tu karayornyasya vardhate jānupārśvayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*aṅguṣṭhau copari nyastau jānvāretha karāṅgulī \|*  
*jānupṛṣṭhe tathā netre jānumadhye ca nāsikā \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. Out of the embryo the wind drags the living being whose head is downwards and feet upwards, the palms of the hands on the flanks of knees, the thumb and forgers of the hand on the knees, the eyes and nose on the back and the centre of the knee.

## \|\| 32.60 \|\|

*evaṃ vṛddhiṃ kramādyāti jantuḥ strīgarbhasaṃsthitaḥ \|*  
*kāṭhinyamasthīnyāyānti bhuktapītena jīvati \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Thus the living being in the embryo of the mother develops gradually. The bones attain hardness. Life is sustained by food and drink.

## \|\| 32.61 \|\|

*nāḍī vāpyāyanī nāma nābhyāṃ tatra nibadhyate \|*  
*strīṇāṃ tathāntrasuṣire sa nibaddhaḥ prajāyate \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The tubular organ VapyayanI is set close to the navel part. The child in the embryonic state is fastened with a cord to the aperture therein. He comes out of confinement (along with the cord) in the scheduled lime.

## \|\| 32.62 \|\|

*krāmanti bhuktapītāni strīṇāṃ garbhodare tathā \|*  
*tairāpyāyitadeho 'sau janturvṛddhimupaiti ca \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. In the womb he eats and drinks whatever is eaten and drunk by the mother. Thereby he receives nourishment and develops in form.

## \|\| 32.63 \|\|

*smṛtyastatra prayāntyasya bahvyaḥ saṃsārabhūtayaḥ \|*  
*tato nirvedamāyāti pīḍyamāna itastataḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. In the womb he recollects the past events and happenings of worldly affairs. Tormented from all sides he feels depressed.

## \|\| 32.64 \|\|

*punarnaivaṃ kariṣyāmi bhuktamātra ihodarāt \|*  
*tathātathā yatiṣyāmi garbhaṃ nāpnomyahaṃ yathā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. As soon as I leave the womb I shay be very cautious. I shall never repeat mistakes. I shall so act that I may not be born again."

## \|\| 32.65 \|\|

*iti sañcintayañjīvo smṛtvā janmaśatāni vai \|*  
*yāni pūrvānubhūtāni devabhūtātmajāni vai \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The living being thinks thus and further recollects events of hundreds of past birthsevents which accrued to him. from causes known and unknown.

## \|\| 32.66 \|\|

*tataḥ kālakramājjantuḥ parivartyatvadhomukhaḥ \|*  
*navame daśame vāpi māsi saṃjāyate tataḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Then at the scheduled time the living being emerges from the embryo with the head downward in the ninth or tenth month.

## \|\| 32.67 \|\|

*niṣkramyamāṇo vātena prājāpatyena pīḍyate \|*  
*niṣkramate ca vilapaṃstadā duḥkhanipīḍitaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. While leaving the womb he suffers and faints. When he comes out of the womb he cries in anguish,

## \|\| 32.68-69 \|\|

*niṣkrāmaṃścodarānmūrchāmasahyāṃ pratipadyate \|*  
*prāpnoti cetanāṃ cāsau vāyusparśasukhānvitaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

*tatastaṃ vaiṣṇavī māyā samāskandati mohinī \|*  
*tayā vimohitātmāsau jñānabhraṃśamavāpnute \|\| 69 \|\|*

68-69. Then with the touch of air he obtains a little relief and comes to consciousness. Then the hypnotising power of Visnu overpowers him. Deluded by that power he loses power of discernment.

## \|\| 32.70 \|\|

*bhraṣṭajñānaṃ bālabhāve tato jantuḥ prapadyate \|*  
*tataḥ kaumārakāvasthāṃ yauvanaṃ vṛddhatāmapi \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. In childhood he remains in that state; passing through childhood he attains youth and old age.

## \|\| 32.71 \|\|

*punaśca tadvanmaraṇaṃ janma prāpnoti mānavaḥ \|*  
*tataḥ saṃsāracakre 'smin bhrāmyate ghaṭayantravat \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Then he succumbs to death, then he of vicious desire jealousy He is attains birth. Thus on the wheel of existence he is made to rotate like the potter's wheel.

## \|\| 32.72 \|\|

*kadācitsvargamāpnoti kadācinnirayaṃ naraḥ \|*  
*svargaṃ ca nirayaṃ caiva svakarmaphalamaśnute \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Sometimes he goes to heaven, sometimes to hell. In heaven or hell he reaps the fruits of his activities.

## \|\| 32.73 \|\|

*kadācidbhuktakarmā ca bhuvaṃ svalpena gacchati \|*  
*svarloke narake caiva bhuktaprāye dvijottamāḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. Sometimes, when he has exhausted the merit of his actions he descends to the earth by the residue of his merits. Heaven or hell is not a permanent abode, this you should know, О best of birds.

## \|\| 32.74 \|\|

*narakeṣu mahadduḥkhametadyatsvargavāsinaḥ \|*  
*dṛśyate nātra modante pātyamānāstu nārakaiḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. People going to hell suffer from extreme tortures which they derive from their hellish activities. Their tortures increase when they see their companions enjoying in heaven.

## \|\| 32.75 \|\|

*svarge 'pi duḥ khamatulaṃ yadārohaṇakālataḥ \|*  
*prabhṛtyahaṃ patiṣyāmītyetanmanasi vartate \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Extreme distress awaits the inmates even in the celestial region inasmuch as the thought of fear haunts their mind from the very start they enter heaven that after their merits are exhausted they would be thrown into hell.

## \|\| 32.76 \|\|

*nāra kāṃścaiva samprekṣya mahadduḥ khamavāpyate \|*  
*evaṃ gatimahaṃ gantetyaharniśamanirvṛtaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Looking at the tortures suffered by the people in hell, the residents of heaven feel that after the expiry of their merit the same fate awaits them in hell. Thus all the while they remain discomfortable in heaven.

## \|\| 32.77 \|\|

*garbhavāse mahadduḥ khaṃ jāyamānasya yonijam \|*  
*jātasya bālabhāve 'pi vṛddhatve duḥkhameva ca \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. A Jiva in the embryo is put to great suffering. He suffers in childhood and old age as well.

## \|\| 32.78 \|\|

*kāmerṣyākrodhasambandhādyauvane 'pi ca duḥ saham \|*  
*duḥsvapnaṃ yā vṛddhatā ca maraṇe duḥ khamutkaṭam \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. In youth he suffers from the evil effects cowdung, sacred clay, water from sacred jealousy rage. He is afflicted by bad dreams. Old age ends in death which is extremely painful.

## \|\| 32.79 \|\|

*kṛṣyamāṇaśca yāmyaiḥ sa narake 'pi ca yātyadhaḥ \|*  
*punaśca garbhājjanma syānmaraṇaṃ duṣkaraṃ tathā \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Being dragged by the emissaries ofYama he is thrust into hell. From hell he descends to the earth to suffer again the utmost pangs of birth and death.

## \|\| 32.80 \|\|

*evaṃ saṃsāracakre 'smijjantavo ghaṭayantravat \|*  
*bhrāmyante prāktanairbadhairbaddhā vidhyanti cāsakṛt \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. Thus, on the wheel of this universe, people are made to rotate like the potter's wheel and are tortured incessantly, tangled by the noose of their actions.

## \|\| 32.81 \|\|

*nāsti pakṣinsukhaṃ kiñcitkṣetre duḥ khaśatākule \|*  
*vinatāsuta mokṣāya yatitavyaṃ tato naraiḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. bird, there is no pleasure whatsoever in this world abounding in a multitude of sorrows. О son of Vinata, people should endeavour for salvation.

## \|\| 32.82 \|\|

*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ yathā garbhasya saṃsthitiḥ \|*  
*kathayāmi kramapraśraṃ pṛṣṭaṃ vā vartate spṛhā \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. Thus, I have told you all about the child in the womb. I now answer your further query in the serial order, if you desire cc hear.

## \|\| 32.83 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*madhye kṛtamahāpraśradvayasyāptaṃ mayottaram \|*  
*praśrasyāpi tṛtīyasya uttaraṃ ca vidhīyatām \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. I am satiated with the answer to both of my queries. I implore you, now, to answer my third query too.

## \|\| 32.84 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*mriyamāṇasya kiṃ kṛtyamiti tvaṃ pṛṣṭavānasi \|*  
*śṛṇu tatrottaraṃ tūktaṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. You have asked me what should be done for a dying person. Now listen, I shall tell you my answer in brief to your query.

## \|\| 32.85 \|\|

*āsannamaraṇaṃ jñātvā puruṣaṃ snāpayettataḥ \|*  
*gomūtragomayasumṛttīrthodakakuśodakaiḥ \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Knowing that the person is going to die, he should, be bathed with cow-urine, cowdung, sacred clay, water from sacred rivers and other waters sanctified by Kusa grass.

## \|\| 32.86-87 \|\|

*vāsasī paridhāryātha dhaute tu śuci nī śubhe \|*  
*darbhāṇyādau samāstīrya dakṣiṇāgrānvikīrya ca \|\| 86 \|\|*

*tilān gomayaliptāyāṃ bhūmau tatra niveśayet \|\| 87 \|\|*

86-87. Fie should be dressed in pure, auspicious, clean clothes. He should be laid on earth besmeared with cowdung, bestrewn with darbha grass, with tips pointed to the south and overspread with gingelly seeds.

## \|\| 32.88 \|\|

*prāgudakśirasaṃ vāpi mukhe svarṇaṃ viniḥ kṣepet \|*  
*śālagrāmaśilā tatra tulasī ca khageśvara \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. His head should be put in the east or in the north with gold in the mouth.

## \|\| 32.89 \|\|

*vidheyā sannidhau sarpirdīpaṃ prajvālayetpunaḥ \|*  
*namo bhagavate vāsudevāyeti japastathā \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. О lord of birds, Shalagram stone and Tulasi leaves should be kept nearby. An earthen lamp should be kindled. He should utter: Om namo bhagavate Vasudevaya.

## \|\| 32.90 \|\|

*ādau tu praṇavaṃ kṛtvā pūjādāne tataḥ smṛte \|*  
*samabhyarcya hṛṣīkeśaṃ puṣpadhūpādibhistataḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. He should draw Om at first, worship the same and offer charity.

## \|\| 32.91 \|\|

*praṇipātaiḥ stavaiḥ puṇyairdhyā nayogena pūjayet \|*  
*dattvā dānaṃ ca viprebhyo dīnānāthebhya eva ca \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. He should worship lord Visnu with flowers, incense etc, and pay homage glorifying his deeds and concentrating on him with sincere devotion. He should offer charity to the Brahmins, poor and the indigent.

## \|\| 32.92 \|\|

*puttre mitre kalatre ca kṣetradhānyadhanādiṣu \|*  
*nivartayenmamatvaṃ ca viṣṇoḥ pādau hṛdi smaran \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. He should worship lord Visnu with flowers, incense etc, and pay homage glorifyinghis deeds and concentrating on him with sincere devotion. He should offer charity to the Brahmins, poor and the indigent.

## \|\| 32.93 \|\|

*uccaiḥ puruṣasūktaṃ ca yadi śreṣṭhāpadastadā \|*  
*puttrādyāḥ prapaṭheyuste mriyamāṇe nije jane \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. He should recite Purusa sukta if he is in distress, О good one. If he is dying and is unable to recite, the sons and other relations should do the same.

## \|\| 32.94 \|\|

*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ kṛtyaṃ mṛtyāvupasthite \|*  
*phalamapyasya kṛtsnasya samāsātte vadāmyaham \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. I have told you all that can be done when death is imminent. Now, 1 shall tell you, in brief, the fruits of all these activities.

## \|\| 32.95 \|\|

*snānena śucitāprāptirapāvitryahṛtistataḥ \|*  
*tato viṣṇoḥ smṛtistasya jñānātsarvaphalapradā \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. Bath purifies a man, removes impurity. Meditation on Visnu yields all sorts of fruits.

## \|\| 32.96 \|\|

*darbhatūlī nayetsvargamāturaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tilairdarbhaiśca niḥ kṣiptaiḥ snānaṃ kratumayaṃ bhavet \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. A blade of darbha grass takes him to heaven. There is no doubt in this. Bath with water mixed with gingelly seeds and darbha grass is as much sanctifying as the bath at the end of sacrifice.

## \|\| 32.97 \|\|

*brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca śrīrhutāśastathaiva ca \|*  
*maṇḍale copatiṣṭhanti tasmātkurvīta maṇḍalam \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. A circle should be drawn around the dying person, for, it is said, Brahma, Visnu, Rudra, Laksmi and fire reside within the circle.

## \|\| 32.98 \|\|

*prāgudagvā kṛteneha śirasā lokamuttamam \|*  
*vrajate yadi pāpasyālpatvaṃ puṃso bhavetkhaga \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. If he is laid on earth, with his head eastward or north-ward, О lord of birds, he reaches the sublime regions if his sins are minimum in life.

## \|\| 32.99-100 \|\|

*pañcaratne mukhe mukte jive jñānaṃ prarohati \|*  
*tulasī brāhmaṇā gāvo viṣṇurekādaśī khaga \|\| 99 \|\|*

*pañca pravahaṇānyeva bhavābdhau majjatāṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*viṣṇurekādaśī gītā tulasī vipradhenavaḥ \|\| 100 \|\|*

99-100. If five gems are put into his mouth, knowledge dawns upon him. In this shadowy world of unsurmountable difficulties, the following six incite devotion: meditation on Visnu, observance of vow on the eleventh day of the month (ekadasi) listening to the Bhagavadglta, worship of the sacred plant Tulasi, Brahmins and cows.

## \|\| 32.101 \|\|

*asāre durgasaṃsāre ṣaṭpadī bhaktidāyinī \|*  
*namo bhagavate vāsudevāyeti japannaraḥ \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. By muttering the mantra Om namo bhagavate Vasudevaya, one is completely absorbed in Brahma. Even by worshipping me alone, one can reach my region direct.

## \|\| 32.102 \|\|

*oṅkārapūrvaṃ sāyujyaṃ prāpnuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ \|*
