*pūjayāpi ca mallokaprāptirārāddivaṃ vrajet \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. In the obsence of entanglement, attachment vanishes by the knowledge as revealed in the Purusa Sukta.

## \|\| 32.103-104 \|\|

*bandhābhāve mamatvetu jñānaṃ puruṣasūktataḥ \|*  
*yasyayasyādhikatvaṃ tu sādhaneṣveṣu kāśyapa \|\| 103 \|\|*

*tattatphalasyāpyādhikyaṃ bhavatītyavadhāraya \|*  
*dātavyāni yathāśaktyā prīto 'sau sarvadā bhavet \|\| 104 \|\|*

103-104. But one who has other sources as well obtains reward in proportion to the additional sources. All those sources he should utilize for the pleasure of lord Visnu. I have thus told you in detail the benefits derivable from ablution, etc.

## \|\| 32.105-106 \|\|

*etatte sarvamākhyātaṃ snānādiṣu phalaṃ mayā \|*  
*brahmāṇḍe ye guṇāḥ santi śarīre te vyavasthitāḥ \|\| 105 \|\|*

*pātālabhūdharā lokāstathānye dvīpasāgarāḥ \|*  
*ādityādigrahāḥ sarve piṇḍamadhye vyavasthitāḥ \|\| 106 \|\|*

105-106. The various features of the cosmos are present in the body as well. The various worlds, mountains, continents, oceans and the planets like sun, etc, are all present in the body.

## \|\| 32.107-108 \|\|

*pādādhastu talaṃ jñeyaṃ pādordhvaṃ vitalaṃ tathā \|*  
*jānubhyāṃ sutalaṃ viddhi sakthideśe mahātalam \|\| 107 \|\|*

*tathā talātalañcorau guhyadeśe rasātalam \|*  
*pātālaṃ kaṭisaṃsthantu pādādau lakṣayedbudhaḥ \|\| 108 \|\|*

107-108. The sole of the foot is atala (the nether world of that name); the upper surface of the foot is vitala; the knee is sutala; the calf is talatala; the thigh is rasa tala; the private parts are mahatala.

The patala is in the hips. Thus, the lower worlds are explained and must be noted by the learned men.

## \|\| 32.109-110 \|\|

*bhūrlokaṃ nābhimadhye tu bhuvarlokaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ \|*  
*svargalokaṃ hṛdaye vidyāt kaṇṭhadeśe mahastathā \|\| 109 \|\|*

*janalokaṃ vaktradeśe tapolokaṃ lalāṭake \|*  
*satyalokaṃ mahārandhre bhuvanāni caturdaśa \|\| 110 \|\|*

109-110. The Bhur is stationed in the centre of the navel; the Bhuvar is above it. The Svar is in the heart. The Mahar is in the throat. The Manas is in the mouth; the The lanas is in the mounth; the Tapas is on the forehead and the Satya or Brahma is in the aperture of the head. Thus the fourteen worlds are explained.

## \|\| 32.111-112 \|\|

*trikoṇe saṃsthito meruradhaḥ koṇe ca mandaraḥ \|*  
*dakṣiṇe ceva kailāso vāmabhāge himācalaḥ \|\| 111 \|\|*

*niṣadhaścordhvabhāge ca dakṣiṇe gandhamādanaḥ \|*  
*malayo (ramaṇo) vāmarekhāyāṃ saptaite kulaparvatāḥ \|\| 112 \|\|*

111-112. The mount Meru is in the triangular shoulder blade. The mount Mandara is in the lower angle i.e. hips. The mount Kailasa is in the right angle. The Himalaya is in the left angle. The mount Nisadha is in the upper surface. The Gandha-madana is to the right and the Ramana is in the linear section in the left side. Thus, the seven great mountains are present in the body.

## \|\| 32.113-114 \|\|

*asthisthāne sthito jambūḥ śāko majjāsu saṃsthitaḥ \|*  
*kuśadvīpaḥ sthito māṃse krauñcadvīpaḥ śirāsthitaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|*

*tvacāyāṃ śālmalidvāpo plakṣaḥ romṇāṃ ca sañcaye \|*  
*nakhasthaḥ puṣkaradvīpaḥ sāgarāstadanantaram \|\| 114 \|\|*

113-114. The Jambu dvipa is stationed in the bones; the Saka dvlpa in the marrow; the kusa dvlpa in the flesh, the Kraunca dvlpa in the head; the 6. almali dvlpa in the skin: the Gomeda in the hair; the Puskara dvlpa in the nail and oceans thereafter.

## \|\| 32.115-116 \|\|

*kṣārodaśca tathā mūtre kṣāre kṣīrodasāgaraḥ \|*  
*surodadhiśca śleṣmasthaḥ majjāyāṃ ghṛtasāgaraḥ \|\| 115 \|\|*

*rasodadhiṃ rase vidyācchoṇite dadhisagaram \|*  
*svādulaṃ lambikāsthāne garbhodaṃ śukrasaṃsthitam \|\| 116 \|\|*

115-116. The salt ocean is stationed in the urine; the milk ocean in the milky exudations of the body.

The wine ocean is stationed in the phlegm; the butter ocean in the marrow; the rasa ocean in the lymphatic secretion; the curd ocean in the blood; the water ocean in the region of excreta and the ocean of sugar cane juice in semen.

## \|\| 32.117-119 \|\|

*nādacakre sthitaḥ sūryo binducakre ca candramāḥ \|*  
*locanasthaḥ kujo jñeyo hṛdaye ca budhaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 117 \|\|*

*viṣṇusthāne guruṃ vidyācchrukre śukro vyavasthitaḥ \|*  
*nābhisthāne sthito mando mukhe rāhuḥ sthitaḥ sadā \|\| 118 \|\|*

*pāyu (da) sthāne sthitaḥ ketuḥ śarīre grahamaṇḍalam \|*  
*vibhaktañca samākhyātamāpādatalamastakam \|\| 119 \|\|*

117-119. The Sun is stationed in the sound-circle; the Moon in the spot-circle; the Mars in the eyes; Mercury in the heart; the Jupiter in the navel; the Venus in the semen; the Saturn in the umbilical region; Rahu in the mouth and Ketu in the feet. The planets are stationed in the body. Thus, the body is divided from head to foot.

## \|\| 32.120-123 \|\|

*utpannā ye hi saṃsāre mriyante te na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*bubhukṣā ca tṛṣā raudrā dāhodbhūtā ca mūrchanā \|\| 120 \|\|*

*yatra pīḍāstvimā raudrāstā vai vṛścikadaṃśajāḥ \|*  
*vināśaḥ pūrṇakāle ca jāyate sarvadehinām \|\| 121 \|\|*

*agre agre hi dhāvanti yamalokagatasyavai \|*  
*taptavālukamadhyena prajvaladvahnimadhyataḥ \|\| 122 \|\|*

*keśagrāhaiḥ samākrāntā nīyante yamakiṅkaraiḥ \|*  
*pāpiṣṭhāstvadhamāstārkṣya dayādharmavivirjitāḥ \|\| 123 \|\|*

120-123. Those who are born must die too. Hunger, thirst, unconsciousness and the affliction due to the bites and stings of serpents, scorpions and flies have all their origin in Rudra, О bird, the base wretches who sin and who are devoid of mercy and virtue are dragged through scorching sand and blazing fire, seized by their hair by the servants of Yama.

## \|\| 32.124 \|\|

*yamaloke vasantyete kuṭyāṃ janma na vidyate \|*  
*evaṃ sañjāyate tārkṣya martye jantuḥ svakarmabhiḥ \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. They live in the region of Yama and when they are reborn they will be born in cottages. О Tarksya, thus, man is born in the mortal world as a result of hi own actions.

## \|\| 32.125-126 \|\|

*utpannā ye hi saṃsāre mriyante te na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*āyuḥ karma ca vittañca vidyā nidhanameva ca \|\| 125 \|\|*

*pañcaitāni hi sṛjyante garbhasthasyaiva dehinaḥ \|*  
*karmaṇā jāyate jantuḥ karmaṇaiva pralīyate \|\| 126 \|\|*

125-126. The five characteristics, viz. the span of life, action, wealthy learning and death are created in men even as they are in the womb. The life is born as a result of action; it is dissolved as a result of action.

## \|\| 32.127 \|\|

*sukhaṃ duḥ khaṃ bhayaṃ kṣemaṃ karmaṇaivābhipadyate \|*  
*adhomukhaṃ cordhvapādaṃ garbhādvāyuḥ prakarṣati \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. Happiness or misery, fear of welfare are the results of actions. The foetus ties in the womb with the feet up and the face downwards.

## \|\| 32.128 \|\|

*janmato vaiṣṇavī māyā saṃmohayati satvaram \|*  
*svakarmakṛtasambandho janturjanma prapadyate \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. From the moment of birth the Maya of Visnu enamours and deludes the life. The life takes its birth with its association brought about by its own actions.

## \|\| 32.129 \|\|

*sukṛtāduttamo bhogabhogyavān sukule bhavet \|*  
*yathāyathā duṣkṛtaṃ tat kule hīne prajāyate \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. Due to merit, man enjoys pleasures in heaven and is fortunalety born in a noble family. If he is sinful in actions he is born in a meanminded family.

## \|\| 32.130 \|\|

*daridro vyādhito mūrkhaḥ pāpakṛdduḥ khabhājanam \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ kimarthaṃ te kathayāmi khageśvara \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. He becomes poor, sick, foolish, sinful and miserable. О son of a sage, I have thus told you the various features of the birth of a life.

## Chapter 33

## \|\| 33.1 \|\|

*garuḍa uvāca \|*  
*utpattilakṣaṇaṃ jantoḥ kathitaṃ mayi puttrake \|*  
*yamalokaḥ kiyanmātrastrailokye sacarācare \|*  
*vistaraṃ tasya me brūhi adhvā caiva kiyān smṛtaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. What is the extent of the region of Yama? What is its expanse in proportion to that of the three worlds habitated by the mobile and immobile beings? Please tell me the length of the pathway as recorded by tradition.

## \|\| 33.2 \|\|

*kaiśca pāpaiḥ kṛtairdeva kena vā śubhakarmaṇā \|*  
*gacchanti mānavāstatra kathayasva viśeṣataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О lord, what are the merits or demerits, sins or virtues, as a result of which men go there. Please tell me all concerning this.

## \|\| 33.3 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*ṣaṭaśītisahasrāṇi yojanānāṃ pramāṇataḥ \|*  
*yamalokasya cādhvānamantarā mānuṣasya ca \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Thedistance between the mortal world and the region of Yama is eighty six thousand yojanas (one million, thirtytwo thotisand kilometres).

## \|\| 33.4 \|\|

*dhmātatāmramivātapto jvaladdurgo mahāpathaḥ \|*  
*tatra gacchanti pāpiṣṭhā mānavā mūḍhacetasaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The pathway is so hot as the red hot copper. It is impassable. It blazes. Sinners among men, of confused thoughts, go there.

## \|\| 33.5 \|\|

*kaṇṭakāśca sutīkṣṇā vai vividhā ghoradarśanāḥ \|*  
*taistuvālukṣitirvyāptā hutāśaśca tatholbaṇaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. There are rftany sharp thorns of awful appearance. The area is entirely covered by the burning fire.

## \|\| 33.6 \|\|

*vṛkṣacchāyā na tatrāsti yatra viśramate naraḥ \|*  
*gṛhītaḥ kālapāśaiśca kṛtaiḥ karmabhirulbaṇaiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. There is no shady tree where the people can take rest. Their own actions in the form of the nooses of Yama drag them on.

## \|\| 33.7 \|\|

*tasmin mārge na cānnādyaṃ yena prāṇān prapoṣayet \|*  
*na jalaṃ dṛśyate tatra tṛṣā yena vilīyate \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On the way, there is no provision to sustain life. No-water is available whereby thirst can be quenched.

## \|\| 33.8 \|\|

*kṣudhayā pīḍito yāti tṛṣṇayā ca mahāpathe \|*  
*śītena kampate kvāpi yamamārge 'tidurgame \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Many are oppressed by hunger and many by tiftrst on that High path. On that impassable path of Yama many shiver with chillness.

## \|\| 33.9 \|\|

*yadyasya yādṛśaṃ pāpaṃ sa panthāstasya tādṛśaḥ \|*  
*sudīnāḥ kṛpaṇā mūḍhā duḥ khairvyāptāstaranti tam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The wayfarers find the path difficult in accordance-with the gravity of their sins. They are helpless, miserable and confounded as they plod on.

## \|\| 33.10 \|\|

*rudanti dāruṇaṃ kecit keciddrohaṃ vadanti ca \|*  
*ātmakarmakṛtairdeṣaiḥ pacyamānā muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Some bemoan piteously, some speak harshly. Agitated by the effects of their actions they undergo endless distress.

## \|\| 33.11 \|\|

*īdṛgvidhaḥ sa vai panthā vijñeyo dāruṇaḥ khaga \|*  
*vitṛṣṇā ye narā loke sukhaṃ tasmin vrajanti te \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О bird, that pathway has some special characteristics. Those who have been devoid of convetousness in the world procceed along that path peacefully.

## \|\| 33.12 \|\|

*yāniyāni ca dānāni dattāni bhuvi mānavaiḥ \|*  
*tānitānyupatiṣṭhanti yamaloke puraḥ pathi \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The various gifts that people make in the world; stand them in good stead in the region of Yama. They go-ahead (and make everything comfortable).

## \|\| 33.13 \|\|

*pāpino nopatiṣṭhanti dāhaśrāddhajalāñjali \|*  
*bhramanti vāyubhūtāste ye kṣudrāḥ pāpakarmiṇaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Shraddhas and water libations are of no avail to the-sinner. These wretches hover here and there in their aerial form.

## \|\| 33.14 \|\|

*īdṛśaṃ vartma tadraudraṃ kathitaṃ tava suvrata \|*  
*punaśca kathayiṣyāmi yamamārgasya yā sthitiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. О good one who are devoted to virtue, the pathway has been narrated to you thus. Now I shay tell you the hardships one has to undergo on th-e' way to the region of Yams.

## \|\| 33.15 \|\|

*yāmyanairṛtayormadhye puraṃ vaivasvatasya tu \|*  
*sarvaṃ vajramayaṃ divyamabhedyaṃ tat surāsuraiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The city of Yama is in the southwest direction. It is wholly adamantine, divine and impenetrable to deities as well as demons.

## \|\| 33.16 \|\|

*caturaśraṃ caturdvāraṃ saptaprākāratoraṇam \|*  
*svayaṃ tiṣṭhati vai yasyāṃ yamo dūtaiḥ samanvitaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. It is square in shape with four entrances and seven outer fort walls. Yama is seated inside together with his messengers.

## \|\| 33.17 \|\|

*yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ vai pramāṇena taducyate \|*  
*sarvaratnamayaṃ divyaṃ vidyujjvālārkataijasam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The city extends over a thousand yojanas (1200 km.) and is studded with gems resplendent like lightning, blazing fire and the sunshine.

## \|\| 33.18 \|\|

*tadguhaṃ dharmarājasya vistīrṇaṃ kāñcanaprabham \|*  
*yojanānāṃ pañcaśatapramāṇena samucchritam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The special palace of Yama with its golden splendour is very extensive and twentyfive yojanas sin height.

## \|\| 33.19 \|\|

*vṛtaṃ stambhasahasraistu vaidūryamaṇimaṇḍitam \|*  
*muktājālagavākṣaṃ ca patākāśatabhūṣitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. It is decorated with jewels of lapis lazuli and then& are thousands of pillars all round. There are many pearls over the windows from where hundreds of flags and banners hang: downwards.

## \|\| 33.20 \|\|

*ghaṇṭāśataninādāḍhyaṃ toraṇānāṃ śatairvṛtam \|*  
*evamādibhiranyaiśca bhūṣaṇairbhūṣitaṃ sadā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. It is resonant with the sound of hells, hundred in, number. Hundreds of festoons cover them. It is embellished in various ways as mentioned here and in many other ways as well.

## \|\| 33.21 \|\|

*tatrastho bhagavān dharma āsane tu same śubhe \|*  
*daśayo janavistīrṇe nīlajīmūtasannibhe \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The lord of justice is seated therein his auspicious judgement seat which is ten yojanas in width and which resembles the blue cloud.

## \|\| 33.22 \|\|

*dharmajño dharmaśīlaśca dharmayukto hito yamaḥ \|*  
*bhayadaḥ pāpayuktānāṃ dhārmikāṇāṃ sukhapradaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Yama knows virtue, practices virtue and is benevolent to those who are virtuous. He is terrifying to the sinner and sweet to the virtuous.

## \|\| 33.23 \|\|

*mandamāru tasaṃyogairutsavairvividhaistathā \|*  
*vyākhyānairvividhairyuktaḥ śaṅkhavāditraniḥ svanaiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The wind blows there very gently. Many festivals are celebrated there. Many sages conduct discourses there. Different musical instruments are played.

## \|\| 33.24-25 \|\|

*puramadhyapraveśe tu citraguptasya vai gṛham \|*  
*pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyānāṃ yojanānāṃ suvistaram \|\| 24 \|\|*

*daśocchritaṃ mahādivyaṃ lohaprākāravoṣṭitam \|*  
*pratolīśatasaṃcāraṃ patatākāśataśobhitam \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. In the centre of the city, just near the entrance (to Yama's palace) Citragupta's palace is situated. It is twenty-five yojanas in length and ten yojanas in height. Highly divine, it is surrounded by an iron-wall. There are hundreds of streets where people move about and the whole city shines with hundreds of banners.

## \|\| 33.26 \|\|

*dīpikāśatasaṅkīrṇaṃ gītadhvanisamākulam \|*  
*vicitracitrakuśalaiścitraguptasya vai gṛham \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Citragupta's palace is gay with hundreds of lamps burning and hundreds of musical notes reverberating. It is well-painted by those who are experts in the art of drawing and painting.

## \|\| 33.27 \|\|

*maṇimuktāmaye divye āsane paramādbhute \|*  
*tatrastho gaṇayatyāyurmānuṣeṣvitareṣu ca \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. There, seated in his wonderful driving seat he calculates the age of human beings and others.

## \|\| 33.28-29 \|\|

*na muhyati kadācit sa sukṛte duṣkṛte 'pi vā \|*  
*yadyenopārjitaṃ yāvat tāvadvai vetti tasya tat \|\| 28 \|\|*

*daśāṣṭadoṣarahitaṃ kṛta karma likhatyasau \|*  
*citraguptālayāt ācyāṃ jvarasyāsti mahāgṛham \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. He never commits any mistake in the matter of merit or demerit. Whatever the man acquired in his various births good or bad a long with his eighteen defects is written down by Citragupta. To the east of Citragupta's palace is the building of the Fever.

## \|\| 33.30 \|\|

*dakṣiṇe cāpi śūlasya latāvisphoṭakasya ca \|*  
*paścime kāla pāśasya ajīrṇasyārucestathā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. To the south is the abode of Colic pain and the Spider Tumour. To the West are the abodes of the Noose df Kala, Indigestion and Norftlish for food.

## \|\| 33.31 \|\|

*madhyapīṭhottare jñeyo tathā cānyā viṣacikā \|*  
*aiśanyāṃ vai śiro 'rtiśca āgneyyāñcaiva mṛkatā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. To the north of the central seat, the abode of Cholera can be seen. To the northeast the abode of Head-ache and to the southeast the abode of Unconsciousness.

## \|\| 33.32 \|\|

*atisāraśca nairṛtyāṃ vāyavyāṃ dāhasaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*ebhiḥ parivṛto nityaṃ citraguptaḥ sa tiṣṭhati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Dysentery resides in the south-west and the Burning sensation in the north-west. Citragupta is ever surrounded by these. Whatever job is executed by them is immediately written down by Citragupta.

## \|\| 33.33 \|\|

*yat karma kurute kaścittat sarvaṃ vilakhatyasau \|*  
*dharmarājagṛhadvāri dūtāstārkṣya tathā niśi \|*  
*tiṣṭhanti pāpakarmāṇaḥ pacyamānā narādhamāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О Tarksya, the messengers stay in different places at the threshhold of the palace of Dharmaraja torturing the sinful wretches of evil human beings.

## \|\| 33.34 \|\|

*yamadūtairmahāpāśairhanyamānāśca mudgaraiḥ \|*  
*vadhyante vividhaiḥ pāpaiḥ pūrvakarmakṛtairnarāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Men are bound with nooses by the messengers of Yama and thrashed with heavy iron clubs. All that is in accordance with their own evil actions.

## \|\| 33.35 \|\|

*nānāprahāraṇāgraiśca nānāyantraistathā pare \|*  
*chidyante pāpakarmāṇaḥ krakacaiḥ kāṣṭhavaddvidhā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. They are hit and beaten with various weapons and different mechanical devices. They are split with saws and scissors as though they were pieces of lumber.

## \|\| 33.36 \|\|

*anye jvaladbhiraṅgārairveṣṭitāḥ parito bhṛśam \|*  
*pūrvakarmavipākena dhmāyante lohapiṇḍavat \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Others are burned and baked with blazing coal as though they were balls of iron.

## \|\| 33.37 \|\|

*kṣiptvānye ca dharāpṛṣṭhe kuṭhāreṇāvakartitāḥ \|*  
*krandamānāśca dṛśyante pūrvakarmavipākataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Others are seen being dragged here and there on the ground and chopped with sharp knives. They are seen walling and shouting. All this is due to their own evil actions.

## \|\| 33.38 \|\|

*kecidguḍamayaiḥ pākaistailapākaistathā pare \|*  
*pīḍyante yamadūtaiśca pāpiṣṭhāḥ subhṛśaṃ narāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Some are fettered. Some are fried in oil. Thus, the sinners are tortured by the messengers of Yama.

## \|\| 33.39-40 \|\|

*kṣaṇāhni prārthayantyanye dehidehīti koṭiśaḥ \|*  
*yamaloke mayā dṛṣṭā mamasvaṃ bhakṣitaṃ tvayā \|\| 39 \|\|*

*ityevaṃ bahuśastārkṣya narakāḥ pāpināṃ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*karmabhirbahubhiḥ proktaiḥ sarvaśāstreṣu bhāṣitaiḥ \|*  
*dānopakāraṃ vakṣyāmi yathā tatra sukhaṃ bhavet \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Others in millions are heard shouting: 'Give, Give' and demand repayment of debts. Thus, О Tarksya, are hells for the sinner. Should I describe them in detail? They have already been described in the sacred codes. I shall now explain the benefit accruing from gifts whereby the person feels pleasure-even in hell.

## Chapter 34

## \|\| 34.1 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tārkṣya yathānyāyaṃ dharmādharmsya lakṣaṇam \|*  
*sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ nṝṇāmagre dhāvati dhāvatām \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О Tarksya, listore to the precise traits of virtue and sin. The merit and demerit go ahead of men.

## \|\| 34.2 \|\|

*kṛte tapaḥ praśaṃsanti tretāyāṃ jñānasādhanam \|*  
*dvāpare yajñadāne ca dānamekaṃ kalau yuge \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In the Krta age they extol penance; in the Treta they extol knowledge, in the Dvapara sacrifices and charities, and in the Kali only gifts are extolled.

## \|\| 34.3 \|\|

*gṛhasthānāṃ smṛto dharma uttamānāṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ \|*  
*iṣṭāpūrte svaśaktyā hi kurvatāṃ nāsti pātakam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Those who recite the mantras mentioned in the smrtis and perform istapurta and other

## \|\| 34.4 \|\|

*vṛkṣastu ropito yena khanikūpajalāśayāḥ \|*  
*yamamārge sukhaṃ tasya vrajato nitarāṃ bhavet \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Men who grow trees by the roadside and who dig tanks and lakes, go along this highway very happily.

## \|\| 34.5 \|\|

*agnitāpapradātāre yaiḥ śītapīḍite dvije \|*  
*tapyamānāḥ sukhaṃ yānti sarva kāmaiḥ prapūritāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He who gives fuels (here on the earth) is never tortured by snowfall, ice or dullness in the abode of Yama. He finds his way warm and pleasant.

## \|\| 34.6 \|\|

*suvarṇamaṇimuktādi vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca \|*  
*tena sarvamidaṃ dattaṃ yena dattā vasundharā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. By giving gifts of plots of land men go along that road fully satisfied, decorated and richly adorned by fragrant flowers. All their wishes are fulfilled. He who has gifted plots of land is deemed to have gifted gold, gems, pearls, jewels and clothes.

## \|\| 34.7 \|\|

*yāniyāni ca bhūtāni dattāni bhuvi mānavaiḥ \|*  
*yamalokapathe tāni tiṣṭhantyeṣāṃ samīpataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The gifts that men make in the world stand in good stead in their pathway leading to Yama's palace.

## \|\| 34.8 \|\|

*vyañjanāni vicitrāṇi bhakṣyabhojyāni yāni ca \|*  
*dadāti vidhinā putra prete tadu patiṣṭhati \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Different sort's of dishes and foodstuff's which the sons offer serve the purpose of the pitrs.

## \|\| 34.9 \|\|

*ātmā vai putranāmāsti putrastrātā yamālaye \|*  
*tārayet pitaraṃ ghorāt tena puttraḥ pravakṣyate \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. It is the same atman that is known as son. It is the son who comes to the succour of his father in the abode of Yama. It is he who saves father from hell. He is therefore called yutra (son).

## \|\| 34.10 \|\|

*ato deyañca putreṇa śrāddhamājī vitāvadhi \|*  
*ativāhastadā preto bhogān vai labhate hi saḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Hence, a son should perform Sraddha till his death. The dead man, moving about in his subtle body, will derive enjoyments thereby.

## \|\| 34.11 \|\|

*dahyamānasya pretasya svajanairyo jalāñjaliḥ \|*  
*dīyate pretarūpo 'sau prīto yāti yamālaye \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. At the time of cremation in the funeral pyre if water libations are offered by the descendent, the dead man goes to Yama's abode in all pleasure.

## \|\| 34.12 \|\|

*apakve mṛnmaye pātre dugdhaṃ dattaṃ dinatrayam \|*  
*kāṣṭhatrayaṃ guṇairbaddhvā prītyai rātrau catuṣpathe \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. In an earthen vessel which is not baked, milk should be given for three days for propitiating the dead. Three pieces of things should be tied with a string and dropped on the cross-way.

## \|\| 34.13 \|\|

*prathame 'hni dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca tathā khaga \|*  
*ākāśasthaṃ pibeddugdhaṃ preto vāyuvapurdharaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. О bird, the dead man in his aerial form in the sky will be drinking the milk on the first, second and third day.

## \|\| 34.14 \|\|

*caturthe sañcayaḥ kāryaḥ caturthe?vāpi sāgnike \|*  
*asthisañcayanaṃ kāryaṃ dadyādāpāñjaliṃ tataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Oh the fourth day, the bones should be collected by the son along with his nearest relatives. After that, he should give water- libation to the deceased.

## \|\| 34.15 \|\|

*na pūrvāhne na madhyāhne nāparāhne na sandhiṣu \|*  
*yāte prathamayāme tu dadyādāpajalāñjaṃlīn \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The water-libation should be made in the first three-hour period, not later in the forenoon nor at midday nor in the afternoon nor during dawn or dusk.

## \|\| 34.16 \|\|

*puttreṇa datte te sarve gotriṇo hitabāndhavāḥ \|*  
*svajātyaiḥ parajātyaiśca deyo nadyāṃ jalāñjaliḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The first water-libation should be performed by the son along with the members of the clan and kinsmen. It can be performed by the members of the same caste or even by those of other castes.

## \|\| 34.17 \|\|

*gantavyaṃ naiva vipreṇa dātuṃ śīghraṃ jalāñjalim \|*  
*nivṛttāśca yadā nāryo lokācāraḥ sadābhavet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. No Brahmin should offer water libation to a Sodra. In that case, he will be violating the sacred code of conduct.

## \|\| 34.18 \|\|

*pañcatvañca gate śūdre yaḥ kāṣṭhaṃ nayate citām \|*  
*anuvrajedyadā viprastrirātramaśucirbhavet \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. At the death of a Sudra if a Brahmin takes firewood to the funeral pyre or follows the corpse he becomes impure for three days.

## \|\| 34.19 \|\|

*trirātre ca tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gacchet samudragām \|*  
*prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After three days he should take bath in the river that joins the sea, perform hundred pranayamas and drink ghee in order to regain purity.

## \|\| 34.20 \|\|

*śūdro gacchati sarvatra vaiśyastriṣu dvayoḥ paraḥ \|*  
*gacchati svīyavarṇeṣu dātuṃ prete jalāñjalim \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. A Sudra can offer water-libation to the member of any caste; a Vaisya to the member of three castes. A Ksatriya to the member of two castes; but a Brahmin to the member of his own caste.

## \|\| 34.21 \|\|

*datte jalāñjalau paścādvidadhyāddantadhāvanam \|*  
*tyajanti gotriṇaḥ sarve dināni nava kāśyapa \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. If they go to offer water-libation, the members of the clan should eschew the use of tooth-pick twigs for nine days, О son of Kasyapa.

## \|\| 34.22-23 \|\|

*jalāñjaliṃ tathā dātuṃ gacchanti dvijasattamāḥ \|*  
*yatra sthāne mṛto yastu adhvanyapi gṛhe 'pi vā \|*  
*viśleṣastu tataḥ sthānānna kvacidvihito budhaiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*strījanaścāgrato gacchet pṛṣṭhato navasañcayaḥ \|*  
*ācamanaṃ vidhātavyaṃ pāṣāṇopari saṃsthitaiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. О foremost among birds, if a personjoins the funeral either on the way to the cemetery or in the house of mourning he should leave the party after cremation only at the same place or in the house itself. This is what is laid down by the learned. The women go ahead and the men follow. They should sit on a rock and perform acamana.

## \|\| 34.24 \|\|

*yavāṃśca sarṣapān dūrvāḥ pūrṇapātre vilokayet \|*  
*prāśayennimbapatrāṇi snehasnānaṃ samācaret \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. There must be sufficient water in the vessel to cover up the mustard seeds and the Durva grass should be strewn on the top. Those who took part in the cremation rites should chew mrgosa leaves and take oil bath.

## \|\| 34.25 \|\|

*gotribhirna ca kartavyaṃ gṛhānnañca na bhojayet \|*  
*bhuñjīta mṛnmaye pātre uttānañca vivarjayet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Members should cook food for the party, outside the house. They should not eat meals prepared inside the house. They should take food in earthen vessels, avoiding shallow vessels.

## \|\| 34.26 \|\|

*mṛtakasya guṇā grāhyā yamagāthāṃ samudgiret \|*  
*śubhāśubhe ca dhyātavye pūrvakarmopasañcite \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. They should remember the virtue of the dead and recite Yama-Gatha. They should realize that the auspicious and inauspicious is brought about by the actions alone.

## \|\| 34.27 \|\|

*labdhenaiva ca dehena bhuṅkte sukṛtaduṣkṛte \|*  
*vāyurūpo bhramatyeva vāyurūrdhvaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Even when he enters the new body the dead man is associated with the residual merits and demerits.

## \|\| 34.28 \|\|

*daśāhakarmakriyayā kuṭī niṣpādyate dhruvam \|*  
*navakaiḥ ṣoḍaśaśrāddhaiḥ prayāti hi kuṭīṃ naraḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. After the ten days' rites are completed, the dead man's soul enters the new body. When the nava sraddhas together with the sixteen others are performed, a new body is created for the soul.

## \|\| 34.29 \|\|

*tilairdarbhaiśca bhūmyāṃ vai kuṭī dhātumayī bhavet \|*  
*pañca ratnāni vaktre tu yena jīvaḥ prarohati \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The descendants help to shape a body for the soul, either with the gingelly seeds orthe holy grass and put up the five jewels in the mouth for the body to grow.

## \|\| 34.30 \|\|

*yadā puṣpaṃ pranaṣṭaṃ hi tadā garbhaṃ na dhārayet \|*  
*ādarācca tato bhūmau tiladarbhānviniḥ kṣipet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. If there is no menstruation, no conception takes place. Therefore the descendant should be careful not to eschew the rite of strewing the gingelly seeds and the darbha grass on. the earth.

## \|\| 34.31 \|\|

*paśutve sthāvaratve ca yatra kvāpi sa jāyate \|*  
*tatraiva janturutpannaḥ śrāddhaṃ tatropatiṣṭhati \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He may be born among the cattle or among the stable. Wherever he is born the sraddha reaches him.

## \|\| 34.32 \|\|

*dhanvinā lakṣyamuddiśya mukto bāṇastadāpnuyāt \|*  
*yathā śrāddhaṃ yamuddiśya kṛtaṃ tasyopatiṣṭhati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Just as the arrow discharged by the archer reaches the goal, so the sraddha reaches the person for whom it is performed,

## \|\| 34.33 \|\|

*yāvannotpādito dehastāvacchrāddhairna prīṇanam \|*  
*kṣudhāvibhramamāpanno daśāhena ca tarpitaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. No sraddha can propitiate the soul so long it does not enter the body. If the soul is not propitiated duly during the ten days (after it leaves the body) it will have to wander in the ether without food, agitated by hunger.

## \|\| 34.34 \|\|

*piṇḍadānaṃ na yasyābhūdākāśe bhramate tu saḥ \|*  
*dinatrayaṃ vasaṃstoye agnāvapi dinatrayam \|*  
*ākāśe vasate trīṇi dinamekantu vāsake \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The soul (of the deceased) stays in the water for three days, in the fire for three days and in the ether for three days. For one day, it stays in Indra's heaven.

## \|\| 34.35 \|\|

*dagdhe dehe ca vahnau ca jalenaiva tu tarpitaḥ \|*  
*snehasnānaṃ jalenaiva pūpakaiḥ kṛśarairgṛhe \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. When the body has been burnt in fire, the descen-dent should offer water libation to the departed soul. The mourners should take bath and feed upon the cake or the ricemeal.

## \|\| 34.36 \|\|

*prathame 'hni tṛtīye ca pañcame saptame 'pi vā \|*  
*navamaikādaśe caiva śrāddhaṃ navakamucyate \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. They should perform the Nauaka sraddha on odd days, viz., the first, third, fifth, seventh, ninth and the eleventh.

## \|\| 34.37 \|\|

*gṛhadvāre śmaśāne vā tīrthe devālaye 'pi vā \|*  
*yatrādyo dīyate piṇḍastatra sarvān samāpayet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Whether at the threshold of the house or at the cremation ground or in a holy place or in a temple, wherever the first day's rites are performed, the concluding rites too should be performed there.

## \|\| 34.38 \|\|

*ekādaśāhe yacchrāddhaṃ tat sāmānyamudāhṛtam \|*  
*caturṇāmekavarṇānāṃ śuddhyarthaṃ snānamucyate \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The Sraddha on the eleventh day is common to all castes. There is a ceremonial ablution for purification.

## \|\| 34.39-40 \|\|

*kṛtvā caikādaśāhañca punaḥ snātvā śucirbhavet \|*  
*dadyādviprāya yaḥ śayyāṃ yathoktaṃ pretamokṣadām \|\| 39 \|\|*

*na bhavet yadā sa gotro paro 'pi vidhimācaret \|*  
*bhāryā vā puruṣaḥ kaścit tuṣṭaśca kurute striyaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. After performing the eleventh day sraddha they become free from impurity accruing from death. After the ceremonial bath, they should gift a bed to the brahmana. This releases the dead from pretahood. If there is no member of the same clan for the performance of the rite, another man can perform the same.

## \|\| 34.41 \|\|

*prathame 'hani yaḥ piṇḍo dīyate vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*annādyena ca tenaiva sarvaśrāddhāni kārayet \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Of whatever eatable the pinda is made on the first day as prescribed, the same should be used for the entire sraddha.

## \|\| 34.42 \|\|

*amantraṃ kārayecchrāddhaṃ daśāhaṃ nāmagotrataḥ \|*  
*śrāddhaṃ kṛtantu yairvastraistāni tyaktvā gṛhaṃ viśet \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The sraddha should be performed for ten days, pronouncing the name and gotra of the dead but without mantras. Whosoever performs the sraddha should enter the house discording the clothes he wore at the sraddha.

## \|\| 34.43 \|\|

*asagotraḥ sagotro vā yadi strī yadi vā pumān \|*  
*prathame 'hani yaḥ kuryāt sa daśāhaṃ samāpayet \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Whether of the same lineage or of a different clan, a man or a woman, whosoever performs the first day's rites shall perform the concluding rites on the tenth day as well.

## \|\| 34.44 \|\|

*jīvasya daśabhiḥ piṇḍairdeho niṣpādyate dhruvam \|*  
*vṛddhiśca daśabhirmāsairgarbhasthasya yathā bhavet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. With the offering of ten pindas by the descendant the soul acquires a new body just as the foetus does in the ten months in the womb.

## \|\| 34.45 \|\|

*āśaucaṃ yāvadetasya tāvat piṇḍodakakriyā \|*  
*caturṇāmapi varṇānāmeṣa eva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The rite of pinda and water-libation shall continue as long as there is impurity accruing from death. This is applicable to the members of all castes.

## \|\| 34.46-47 \|\|

*yatra trirātramāśaucaṃ tatrādau trīn pradāpayet \|*  
*caturastu dvitīye 'hni tṛtīye trīṃśtathaiva ca \|\| 46 \|\|*

*pṛthak śarāvayordadyādekāhaṃ kṣīramambu ca \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭantu vai śrāddhaṃ caturthe 'hani kārayet \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. When impurity is far three nights, three pindas should be given on the first day, four on the second and three on the third day. Milk and water should be given in separate cups. Ekoddista should be done on the fourth day.

## \|\| 34.48 \|\|

*prathame 'hani yaḥ piṇḍastena mūrdhā prajāyate \|*  
*cakṣuḥ śrotrañca nāsā ca dvitīye 'hni prajāyate \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. By the pinda of the first day, the head is formed. Eyes, ears and nose are formed on the second day.

## \|\| 34.49 \|\|

*gaṇḍau vaktraṃ tathā grīvā tṛtīye 'hani jāyate \|*  
*hṛdayaṃ kukṣirudaraṃ caturthe tadvadeva hi \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Cheeks, mouth and neck are formed on the third day. Heart, sides and stomach are formed on the fourth day.

## \|\| 34.50 \|\|

*kaṭipṛṣṭhaṃ gudañcāpi pañcame 'hani jāyate \|*  
*ṣaṣṭhe ūrū ca vijñeye saptame gulphasambhavaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Waist, back and anus are formed on the fifth day. Thighs are formed on the sixth and ankles are formed on the seventh.

## \|\| 34.51 \|\|

*aṣṭame divase prāpte jaṅghe ca bhavato 'ṇḍaja \|*  
*pādau ca navame jñeyau daśame balavatkṣudhā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The calves are formed on the eighth day, О lord. Feet are formed on the ninth and hunger on the tenth.

## \|\| 34.52 \|\|

*ekādaśāhe yaḥ piṇḍastaṃ dadyādāmiṣeṇa tu \|*  
*siddhānnaṃ tasya dātavyaṃ kṛśarāḥ pūpakāḥ payaḥ \|*  
*prakṣālya vipracaraṇāvarghyaṃ dhūpañca dīpakram \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. On the eleventh day, the pinda is offered to the dead man along withmeat. Bread and cooked rice togetherwith milk should be offered to a brahmana after washing his feet.

## \|\| 34.53 \|\|

*dvādaśa pratimāsyāni śrāddhānyaikādaśe tathā \|*  
*tripakṣañcāpi ṣaṇmāse dve śrāddhāni ca ṣoḍaśa \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The sixteen sraddhas to be performed on the eleventh day are in anticipation of the twelve monthly sraddhas the eleventh day sraddha, the tripaksa, the six weekly sraddha and the two six monthly sraddhas, О bird.

## \|\| 34.54 \|\|

*prati māsaṃ pradātavyaṃ mṛtāhe yā tithirbhavet \|*  
*sa māsaḥ prathamo jñeya iti vedavido viduḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The day on which the death has occurred is the basis for all monthly and annual sraddhas That month is the nrst one when the eleven days are completed.

## \|\| 34.55 \|\|

*śavahaste ca yacchrāddhaṃ mṛtisthāne dvijāsane \|*  
*tadeva prathamaṃ śrāddhaṃ tat syādekādaśe 'hani \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. That is the first sraddha which is performed by the hand of the dead at the spot of death, or at the seat of the brahmana. Eleven days should be counted from this date.

## \|\| 34.56 \|\|

*sā tithirmāsike śrāddhe mṛto yasmin dine naraḥ \|*  
*riktayośca tripakṣe ca sā tithirnādriyeta vai \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. For all monthly sraddhas the tithi falling on the day of death is the base. During the rikta days and tripaksa, tithi is not reckoned by scholars.

## \|\| 34.57-58 \|\|

*paurṇamāsyāṃ mṛto yo 'sau caturthī tasya conakā \|*  
*caturthyāntu mṛto yastu navamī tasya conakā \|\| 57 \|\|*

*navamyāñca mṛto yaśca riktā tasya caturdaśī \|*  
*etā riktāśca vijñeyā antyeṣṭau kuśalena ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. If the man dies on the full moon day, the fourth day is deficient for him. If the man dies on the fourth or the ninth day, the fourteenth day is deficient for him. The deficient days are called riktas by the experts.

## \|\| 34.59 \|\|

*ekādaśāhe yacchrāddhaṃ navakaṃ tat prakīrtitam \|*  
*catuṣpathe tyajedannaṃ punaḥ snānaṃ samācaret \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The food that is cooked on the eleventh day with the dead man in view should be discarded on the cross-roads and the performer should bathe again.

## \|\| 34.60 \|\|

*ekādaśāhādārabhya ghaṭasyānnaṃ jalānvitam \|*  
*dinedine ca dātavyamabdaṃ yāvaddvijottame \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Since the eleventh day, a potful of corn with water should be gifted to a worthy brahmana every day for a year.

## \|\| 34.61 \|\|

*mānuṣasya śarīre tu vidyate hyasthisañcayaḥ \|*  
*tatsaṃkhyaḥ sarvadeheṣu ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The bones in human body number three hundred and sixty.

## \|\| 34.62 \|\|

*udakumbhena puṣṭāni tānyasthīni bhavanti hi \|*  
*etasmāddīyate kumbhaḥ prītiḥ pretasya jāyate \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. These bones are nurtured by gifting the water-pots. When the water-pot is gifted, the

## \|\| 34.63 \|\|

*yasmin dine mṛto janturaṭavyāṃ viṣame 'pi vā \|*  
*yadā tadā bhaveddāhaḥ sūtakaṃ mṛtavāsarāt \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. If a person dies in the forest or at an odd place, cremation may take place on any day but impurity will be counted from the day of death.

## \|\| 34.64-65 \|\|

*tilapātraṃ tathānnādyaṃ gandhadhapādikañca yat \|*  
*ekādaśāhe dātavyaṃ tena śūddho dvijo bhavet \|\| 64 \|\|*

*kṣattriyo dvādaśāhe tu vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśe tathā \|*  
*śuddhiḥ śūdrasya māsena mṛtake jātasūtake \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. By gifting a vessel full ofgingelly seeds, incense etc. the Brahmin is purified on the eleventh day, the ksatriya on the twelfth, vaisya on the fifteenth and sudra in one month from the day of death. The same period holds good in case of birth as well.

## \|\| 34.66 \|\|

*māsatraye trirātraṃ syāt ṣaṇmāsena tu pakṣiṇī \|*  
*ahaḥ saṃvatsarādarbvāk pūrṇe dattvodakaṃ śuciḥ \|*  
*anenaivā nusāreṇa śuddhiḥ syāt sārvavarṇikī \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Three nights in three months, two fortnights in six months, the day before the year is complete, water-oblations should be made with the dead in view. By acting in this way the caste men obtain purity.

## \|\| 34.67 \|\|

*ekādaśāhaprabhṛti purataḥ prativatsaram \|*  
*viśvedevāṃstu sampūjya piṇḍamekañca nirvapet \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Since the eleventh day from the date of death, the pinda should be given every day for a year after worshipping Vasudeva.

## \|\| 34.68 \|\|

*yathā tārāgaṇāḥ sarve cchādyante raviraśmibhiḥ \|*  
*evaṃ pracchādyate sarvaṃ na preto bhavati kvacit \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. As the rays of the sun cover the stars, so does the Pinda cover the pitrs. Never one becomes a ghost, if the pinda is given by the descendent.

## \|\| 34.69 \|\|

*śayyādānaṃ praśaṃsanti sarvadaiva dvijottamāḥ \|*  
*anityaṃ jīvanaṃ ysamāt paścāt ko nu pradāsyati \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. О foremost among birds, the deities extol the gift of a bed. Man's existence on the earth is not permanent. There is no guarantee that after death the descendent would gift the bed.

## \|\| 34.70 \|\|

*tāvadvandhuḥ pitā tāvadyāvajjīvati mānavaḥ \|*  
*mṛte mṛta iti jñātvā kṣaṇāt sneho nivartate \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The relationship of ordinary kinsmen or even of father lasts as long as the man lives. After death seeing-'the cult in between, love ceases to operate in a trice.

## \|\| 34.71 \|\|

*ātmaiva hyātmano bandhurevaṃ jñātvā muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*jīvannapīti sañcintya svīyaṃ hitamanustamaret \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. The self alone is the kinsman or the enemy. Even as he lives one shall think of this and perform his duty piously.

## \|\| 34.72 \|\|

*mṛtānāṃ kaḥ suto dadyāddvijeśayyāṃ satūlikām \|*  
*evaṃ jānannidaṃ sarvaṃ svahastenaiva dāpayet \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. After my death who will be there to gift a bed of silk cloth." Thinking thus, he should gift things with his own hand while he is alive

## \|\| 34.73-78 \|\|

*tasmācchayyāṃ samāsādya sāradārumaryo dṛḍhām \|*  
*dantādirucirāṃ ramyāṃ hemapaṭṭai ralaṅkṛtām \|*  
*tasyāṃ saṃsthāpya haimañca hariṃ lakṣmyā samanvitam \|\| 73 \|\|*

*ghṛtapūrṇañca kalaśaṃ parikalpayet \|*  
*vijñeyo garuḍa prītyai sa nidrākalaśo budhaiḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

*tāmbūlakuṅkumakṣodakarpūrāgurucandanam \|*  
*dīpikopānahacchatracāmarāsanabhājanam \|\| 75 \|\|*

*pārśveṣu sthāpayecchaktyā saptadhānyāni caivahi \|*  
*śayanasthasya bhavati yaccānyadupakārakam \|\| 76 \|\|*

*bhṛṅgārakarakādarśaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam \|*  
*śayyāmevaṃvidhāṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇāya nivedayet \|\| 77 \|\|*

*sapatnīkāya sampūjya svarlokasukhadāyinīm \|*  
*vastraiḥ suśobhanaiḥ pūjya cailakaṃ paridhāpayet \|\| 78 \|\|*

73-78. The bed stead should be of good wood, ornamentally inlaid and engraved. The bolts and cross-bars should be golden. It should be covered with red velvet cloth. The pillow should be nice and exquisite. It shall be fumigated with incense and scents. A golden idol of Lord Visnu and Laksmi should be put on the sides. A vessel full of ghee, betel leaves, saffron powder, camphor, sandal paste should be placed near by. A lamp, a pair of sandal?, umbrella chowries, seat, vessel and the seven kinds of grass hould be kept near the bed. Other requisites of man going to sleep, such as drinking pot, mirror, etc., should bekept ready. The cot should be embellished with a covering of five colours. The bed should be gifted to a Brahmin after duly worshipping him. This gift of bed will bestow pleasures of heaven upon the giver. The brahmanas should be honoured duly and provided with five clothes.

## \|\| 34.79 \|\|

*karṇakaṇṭhāṅgulībāhubhūṣaṇaiścitrabhūṣaṇaiḥ \|*  
*gṛhopakaraṇairyuktaṃ gṛhaṃ dhenvā samanvitam \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. They should be given ornaments for ears, neck, fingers and arms and vessels necessary for domestic use.

## \|\| 34.80-81 \|\|

*tator'ghaḥ sampradātavyaḥ pañcaratnaphalākṣataiḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

*yathā na kṛṣṇaśayanaṃ śūnyaṃ sāgarakanyayā \|*  
*śayyā mamāpyaśūnyāstu tathā janmanijanmani \|\| 81 \|\|*

80-81. They should be given water for washing feet or sipping and five gems along with raw rice grains. While making the gift the giver should pronounce: О Krsna, just as your bed in the milk ocean is never without Laksmi, so also let my bed be never empty in all births I take.

## \|\| 34.82 \|\|

*dattvaivaṃ talpamamalaṃ kṣamāpya ca visarjayet \|*  
*tathā caikādaśāhe tu vidhireṣa prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. With this prayer to lord Krsna the bed should be given to the Brahmin. The same procedure is prescribed even when the gift is made on the eleventh day.

## \|\| 34.83 \|\|

*dadāti yo hi dharmārthe bāndhavo bāndhave mṛte \|*  
*viśeṣamatra pakṣe tu kathyamānaṃ mayā śṛṇu \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. Even if a kinsman makes this gift, the dead man will be happy in the other world, nourished by -the articles of gift. 0 lord of birds, a special thing to be noted I mention now.

## \|\| 34.84-86 \|\|

*upayuktañca tasyāsīt yat kiñcit svagṛhe purā \|*  
*tasya yadgātrasaṃ lagnaṃ vastraṃ bhājanavāhanam \|*  
*yadabhīṣṭañca tasyāsīt tat sarvaṃ parikalpayet \|\| 84 \|\|*

*sthāpayet puruṣaṃ haimaṃ śayyopari śubhaṃ budhaḥ \|*  
*pūjayitvā pradātavyā mṛtaśayyā yathoditā \|\| 85 \|\|*

*purandaragṛhe sarvaṃ sūryaputrālaye tathā \|*  
*upatiṣṭhet sukhaṃ jantoḥ śayyādānaprabhāvataḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

84-86. Whatever had been regularly used by the deceased formerly, the garments, vessels or vehicles, whatever had been liked by him before should be made over as gifts. Among the gifts, the gift of bed makes the dead man extremely happy in the city of Indra or Yama.

## \|\| 34.87 \|\|

*pīḍayanti na taṃ yāmyāḥ puruṣā bhīṣaṇānanāḥ \|*  
*na gharmeṇa na śītena bādhyate sa naraḥ kvacit \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. The wry-faced attendants of Yama will not torment him. He will never be oppressed by excessive heat or cold.

## \|\| 34.88-89 \|\|

*śayyādānaprabhāveṇa preto mucyate bandhanāt \|*  
*apiḥ pāpasamāyuktaḥ svargalokaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 88 \|\|*

*vimānavaramārūḍhaḥ sevyamāno 'psarogaṇaiḥ \|*  
*ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ yāvat tiṣṭhet pātakavarjitaḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

88-89. As a sequel to the gift of bed he will be free from the bondage of actions. Even if he had been a sinner he is sure to go to heaven, seated in a splendid aerial chariot and attended by celestial damsels. The dead man shall stay in heaven till the final deluge when all living beings are destroyed.

## \|\| 34.90 \|\|

*navakaṃ ṣoḍaśaśrāddhaṃ śayyā sāṃvatsaraṃ tathā \|*  
*bharturyā kurute nārī tasyāḥ śreyo hyanantakam \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. The woman who performs nine or sixteen sraddhas and the regular monthly, rites for the whole of year and also makes the gift of bed for her husband will have an everlasting welfare.

## \|\| 34.91 \|\|

*upakārāya sā bharturjīvantī na mṛtā tathā \|*  
*uddharejjīvamānā sā satī satyavatī priyam \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. While alive she will be of some benefit to her husband. Adhering strictly to truth, she will lift up her husband from hell..

## \|\| 34.92 \|\|

*striyā dadhyannaśayane hemakuṅkumamañjanam \|*  
*vastrabhūṣā tathā śayyā sarvametaddhi dāpayet \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. For favour of the dead woman, a golden doll, saffron powder, collyrium, clothes, ornaments or bed can be gifted in the following manner.

## \|\| 34.93-94 \|\|

*upakārakaraṃ strīṇāṃ yadbhavodiha kiñcana \|*  
*bhūṣaṇaṃ gātralagnañca vastu bhogyādikañca yat \|\| 93 \|\|*

*tat sarvaṃ melayitvā tu svesve sthāne niyojayet \|*  
*pūjayellokapālāṃśca grahān devīṃ vināyakam \|\| 94 \|\|*

93-94. What is useful to women, ornaments, etc., the garments worn by the dead woman should all be collected and placed properly. Then the guardians of the quarters, the deifies of planets, the images of Vinayaka, should be worshipped.

## \|\| 34.95 \|\|

*tataḥ śuklāmbaradharo gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ \|*  
*imamuccārayenmantraṃ viprasya purato budhaḥ \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. Then the performer of rites wears a white cloth after the ceremonial bath and takes a handful of flowers. He stands in front of the Brahmin repeating the following mantra:

## \|\| 34.96 \|\|

*pretasya pratimā hyeṣā sarvopakaraṇairyutā \|*  
*sarvaratnasamāyuktā tava vipraniveditā \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. "This is the idol of the dead (woman) with all the necessary requisites and gems. О Brahmin, this is gifted to you.

## \|\| 34.97 \|\|

*ātmā śambhuḥ śivā gaurī śakraḥ suragaṇaiḥ saha \|*  
*tasmācchayyāpradānena saiṣa ātmā prasīdatu \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. The Self itself is Sambhu, the auspicious Gaurl and Indra together with all the deifies. Hence, the bed is being gifted. May her soul be gratified."

## \|\| 34.98 \|\|

*ācāryāya pradātavyā brāhmaṇāya kuṭumbine \|*  
*gṛhīte brāhmaṇastatra ko 'dāditi ca kīrtayet \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. The gift of bed should be made to the preceptor or to a Brahmin burdened with a big family. After accepting the bed the Brahmin shall exclaim with pleasure "Ha! who has given me this."

## \|\| 34.99 \|\|

*tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya praṇipatya visarjayet \|*  
*vidhinānena vai pakṣin dānamekasya dāpayet \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Then after bowing to the Brahmin and circumambulating him he should be allowed to go. Thus, О bird, the gifts are made to the Brahmin.

## \|\| 34.100 \|\|

*bahubhyo na pradeyāni gaurgṛhaṃ śayanaṃ striyaḥ \|*  
*vibhaktadakṣiṇā hyete dātāraṃ pātayanti hi \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. Gifts of cow, house, bed or virgin should not be made to many simultaneously. If gifts are divided among Brahmins they bring about the downfall of the giver.

## \|\| 34.101 \|\|

*evaṃ yo vitaret tārkṣya śṛṇu tasya ca yat phalam \|*  
*sāgraṃ varṣaśataṃ divyaṃ svargaloke mahīyate \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. О Tarksya, listen to the benefit that accrues to one who makes a gift thus. For a hundred divine years or more he is honoured in heaven.

## \|\| 34.102-104 \|\|

*yat puṇyantu vyatīpāte kārtikyāmayanadvaye \|*  
*dvārakāyāntu yat puṇyaṃ candrasūryagrahe tathā \|\| 102 \|\|*

*prayāge naimiṣe yacca kurukṣetre tarthābude \|*  
*gaṅgāyāṃ yamunāyāñca sindhusāgarasaṃgame \|\| 103 \|\|*

*teṣu yaddīyate dānaṃ tasmādapyadhikantvidam \|*  
*etacchayyāpradānasya nāpnoti ṣoḍaśīṃ kalām \|\| 104 \|\|*

102-104. As a result of the gift of a bed he acquires more benefit than he would by performing holy rites in vyatipata or Kartika month or at the confluence of two ayanas or eclipses of sun and moon at the holy city of Dvaraka, Prayaga, Naimisa. Kuruksetra or Arbuda or on the Ganges or at the confluence of Sindhu with the ocean.

## \|\| 34.105-107 \|\|

*yatrāsau jāyate prāṇī bhuṅkte tatraiva tat phalam \|*  
*karmakṣaye kṣitau yāti mānuṣaḥ śubhadarśanaḥ \|\| 105 \|\|*

*mahādhanī ca dharmajñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ \|*  
*punaḥ sa yāti vaikuṇṭhaṃ mṛto 'sau narapuṅgavaḥ \|\| 106 \|\|*

*divyaṃ vimānamāruhya apsarobhiḥ samāvṛtaḥ \|*  
*aho 'sau havyakavyeṣu pitṛbhiḥ saha modate \|\| 107 \|\|*

105-107. He will be born in the world of mortals, on the destruction of his karman, as a comely man, very rich, comprehending virtue, and a master of all sacred literature. Again after dying, he will go to Vaikuntha seated in a divine aerial chariot, surrounded by celestial damsels. He then deserves the havya and kavya offerings and rejoices in the company of the piles.

## \|\| 34.108-109 \|\|

*aṣṭakāsu kṛtaṃ śrāddhamamāvāsyādine tathā \|*  
*maghāsu pitṛparvāṇi yāniyāni ca teṣu ca \|\| 108 \|\|*

*śṛṇu tārkṣya yathānyāyaṃ pretatve pitaro yadi \|*  
*nopatiṣṭhanti śrāddhāni sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vinā \|\| 109 \|\|*

108-109. Whether performed on the astakas, on the new-moon day, on Magha and on other pitr- days, hear, О Tarksya, the sraddha, if the dead are turned in ghosts, is rendered ineffective without sapindlkarana.

## \|\| 34.110 \|\|

*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ pūrṇe varṣe na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*ādyantu śavaśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvā caivākṣa ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. Sapindlkarana should be done at the end of the year after death. Sodas! should be performed for the purification of the corpse.

## \|\| 34.111 \|\|

*pitṛpāṅktiviśurdhyaṃ śatārdhena? tu yojayet \|*  
*vṛddhiṃ prāpyāgrataḥ kuryācchūdrasya svacchayaiva hi \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. For the purification of the mane's lineage fifty (?) should be added. Further action may be taken by adding more. For the sudra, the same is done at will.

## \|\| 34.112 \|\|

*sāmprataṃ sāgnike kāryaṃ dvādaśāhe sapiṇḍanam \|*  
*na cāsau kurute yāvat preta eva sa vahnimān \|*  
*dvādaśāhe tataḥ kāryaṃ sāgnikena sapiṇḍanam \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. Presently, sapindana should be done within the period of twelve days accompanished by sacrifice. Until it is doen the ghost hovers round the fire. Hence, sapindana with fire shall be done within twelve days.

## \|\| 34.113 \|\|

*asthipokṣe gayāśrāddhaṃ śrāddhañcāparapakṣikam \|*  
*abdamadhye na kurvīta sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vinā \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. Gaya sraddha or amavasya sraddha should not be done in the middle of the year without sapindlkarana.

## \|\| 34.114 \|\|

*sapatnyo yadi bahvyaḥ syurekā putravatī bhavet \|*  
*sarvāstāḥ putravatyaḥ syustenaikenātmajena hi \|\| 114 \|\|*

114\. If there be many co-wives of and if only one has a son, all of them shall be deemed to have a son, by that son alone.

## \|\| 34.115 \|\|

*nāsapiṇḍognimān putraḥ pitṛyajñaṃ samācaret \|*  
*samācārādbhavet pāpī pitṛhā cāpi jāyate \|\| 115 \|\|*

115\. A sapinda son should not perform mane's rites in fire. If he does so, he will incur the sin of slaying his father.

## \|\| 34.116 \|\|

*mṛte bhartari yā nārī prāṇāṃścaiva parityajet \|*  
*bhartraiva hi samaṃ tasyāḥ prakurvīta sapiṇḍanam \|\| 116 \|\|*

116\. If a woman discards her life, after the death of her husband, her sapindana should be done along with that of her husband.

## \|\| 34.117 \|\|

*asthānikāpi yā vyūḍhā vaiśyā vā kṣattriyāpi vā \|*  
*yāḥ patnyo vai pituḥ kaścit kuryāt putraḥ sapiṇḍanam \|\| 117 \|\|*

117\. Whosoever may be the father's wife-vaisya or ksatriya, the son is entitled to perform sapindana for her.

## \|\| 34.118 \|\|

*vipreṇaiva yadā śūdrā pariṇītā pramādataḥ \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭantu tacchrāddha sā tu tenaiva yujyate \|\| 118 \|\|*

118\. If a brahmana has married a sudra, then the son should perform ekoddista sraddha for her (but not sapindana).

## \|\| 34.119 \|\|

*anye tu daśa ye putrā jātā varṇa catuṣṭaye \|*  
*te tāsutāsu yoktvayāḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇe sadā \|\| 119 \|\|*

119\. The sons born in four castese should be united with their repective mothers by the sapindana rite.

## \|\| 34.120 \|\|

*anvaṣṭakāsu yacchrāddha yacchrāddhaṃ vṛddhihetukam \|*  
*pituḥ pṛthak pradāvyaṃ striyāḥ piṇḍaṃ sapiṇḍane \|\| 120 \|\|*

120\. When a sraddha is done in anvastakya, when a sraddha is meant for prosperity, then during sapindana seperate pinda shoud be doen for the father as well as mother.

## \|\| 34.121 \|\|

*pitāmahyā samaṃ mātuḥ pituḥ sahapitāmahaiḥ \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryamiti tārkṣya mataṃ mama \|\| 121 \|\|*

121\. The sapindana of the mother should be done with the grandmother and of father with the grandfather. This is what I think, to be proper, О Tarksya.

## \|\| 34.122 \|\|

*aputrāyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tu patiḥ kuryāt sapiṇḍanam \|*  
*mātrāditisṛbhiḥ sārdhamevaṃ dharmeṇa yojayet \|\| 122 \|\|*

122\. If a woman dies sonless, the husband should perform sapindana and the should be duty united with three mothers.

## \|\| 34.123 \|\|

*putro nāsti na bhartā ca strīṇāṃ tārkṣya sapiṇḍanam \|*  
*kārayedvṛddhisamaye bhrātṛdāyādadevaraiḥ \|\| 123 \|\|*

123\. If a woman has neither son, nor husband, then her sapindana should be done by her brother or husband's brother or in their absence by a successor.

## \|\| 34.124 \|\|

*patiputravihīnānāṃ gotrī nāsti na devaraḥ \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭena dātavyaṃ pareṇa saha bhrātṛbhiḥ \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. If the woman has neither husband nor son nor a clansman nor the husband's brother, then her brother should perform ekoddista.

## \|\| 34.125 \|\|

*ajñānādvighnato vāpi na kṛtañcet sapiṇḍanam \|*  
*navakaṃ ṣoḍaśaśrāddhamābdikaṃ kārayet tataḥ \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. If sapindana is left out by negligence or due to some other obstruction then nevaka, sodasa and annual sraddha should be performed.

## \|\| 34.126 \|\|

*adāhe na ca kartavyaṃ sadāhe kārayedbudhaḥ \|*  
*darbhaputtalakaṃ kṛtvā vahninā dāhayecchavam \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. A wise man will do all this after the cremation has taken place but not otherwise. (If the man has died unknown and hence not cremated) the descendents should make an effigy of grass and bum the same in fire.

## \|\| 34.127 \|\|

*pituḥ putreṇa kartavyaṃ na kurbvīta pitā sute \|*  
*atisnehānna kartavyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ sate \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. It is the son who should do the sapindana of his father not the father of the son. Due to affection the sapindana of the son, should not be done ybyp the father.

## \|\| 34.128 \|\|

*bahavo 'pi yadā putrā vidhimekaḥ samācaret \|*  
*navaśrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍatvaṃ śrāddhānyanyāni ṣoḍaśa \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. If there be many sons, only the elder should perform the obsequial rite. Navaka sraddha, sapindana, sodasa sraddhas should be done by one only.

## \|\| 34.129 \|\|

*ekenaiva tu kāryāṃṇi avibhaktadhaneṣvapi \|*  
*antyeṣṭiṃ kurute hyeko munibhaiḥ samudāhṛtam \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. Even when the property is undivided, the sages have stated that only one son can perform the funeral rites.

## \|\| 34.130 \|\|

*vibhaktaiśca pṛthak kāryā kriyā sāṃvatsarādikā \|*  
*ekaikena ca kartavyā putreṇa ca svayaṃsvayam \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. If divided the others too can perform the rites separately, each of the sons doing the same rite himself.

## \|\| 34.131 \|\|

*yasyaitāni na dattāni pretaśrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*piśācatvaṃsthiraṃ tasya kṛtaiḥ śrāddhaśatairapi \|\| 131 \|\|*

131\. If the sixteen sraddhas are not performed for the deceased his ghosthood becomes permanent, even if hundreds of sraddhas are performed in his favour

## \|\| 34.132 \|\|

*bhrātā vā bhrātṛputro vā sapiṇḍaḥ śiṣya eva vā \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt putrahīne khageśvara \|\| 132 \|\|*

132\. For a sonless person, his brother, brother's son sapinda, or disciple may do sapindana.

## \|\| 34.133 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ putrahīnānāṃ patrī kuryāt sapiṇḍanam \|*  
*ṛtvijaṃ karāyedvātha purohitamathāpi vā \|\| 133 \|\|*

133\. For all sonless persons, the daughter can perform sapindana through rtvij or a purohita.

## \|\| 34.134 \|\|

*mṛte pitaryabdamadhye hyuparāgo yadā bhavet \|*  
*pārvaṇaṃ na sutaiḥ kāryaṃ śrāddhaṃ nāndīmukhaṃ na ca \|\| 134 \|\|*

134\. If after the death of father, an eclipse takes place within a year, the sons should neither do parvana nor nandlmukha.

## \|\| 34.135 \|\|

*tīrthaśrāddhaṃ gayāśrāddhaṃ śrāddhamanyacca paitṛkam \|*  
*abdamadhye na kurvīta mahāguruvipattiṣu \|\| 135 \|\|*

135\. Similarly, during great calamities within the year, TIrtha sraddha, or Gaya sraddha or any other sraddha for the manes should not be done at all.

## \|\| 34.136 \|\|

*yamake ca gajacchāyāmanvādiṣu yugādiṣu \|*  
*pitṛpiṇḍo na dātavyaḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vinā \|\| 136 \|\|*

136\. No pinda shouldbeofferedtothemanes if their sapindana has not taken place.

## \|\| 34.137 \|\|

*yajñapuruṣasya yaddānaṃ devādīnāñca yat tathā \|*  
*apūrṇe 'pyabdamadhyepi kartavyamiti ke ca na \|\| 137 \|\|*

137\. According to some, gifts for the Yajnapurusa and for the deities can be made even if the year is not complete.

## \|\| 34.138 \|\|

*pitṛbhyopi hi yaddattamarghapiṇḍavivarjitam \|*  
*kartavyaṃ tacca vai sarvameṣa eva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 138 \|\|*

138\. Whatever is given to the manes except arghya and pinda all that should be done according to the prescribed method.

## \|\| 34.139 \|\|

*devānāṃ pitaro devā pitṝṇāmṛṣayastathā \|*  
*ṛṣīṇāṃ pitaro devāḥ pitā jayati tena vai \|\| 139 \|\|*

139\. The manes of the deities are deities; the manes of the manes are sages; the manes of the sages are deities; father wins through them.

## \|\| 34.140 \|\|

*pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ yajñanatho vibhurbhavet \|*  
*yajñanāthasya yaddattaṃ taddattaṃ sarvadehinām \|\| 140 \|\|*

140\. The lord cf sacrifice is the lord of all manes, deities and men. Whatever is given to him, is given to all.

## \|\| 34.141 \|\|

*mṛte pitaryabdamadhye yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kārayet sutaḥ \|*  
*saptajanmakṛtā dharmāt hīyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 141 \|\|*

141\. If the son performs the sraddha of his father within the year of his (father's) death he is absolved of the fruits of his sinful acts performed in several birtlis previously.

## \|\| 34.142 \|\|

*pretībhūtāstu pitaro luptapiṇḍodakakriyāḥ \|*  
*bhramanti vāyunā sarve kṣuttṛḍbhyāṃ paripīḍitāḥ \|\| 142 \|\|*

142\. If no pinda is gifted and no water libation is made the deceased obtains ghosthood and moves in air oppressed by hunger and thirst.

## \|\| 34.143 \|\|

*pitari pretatāpanne lupyate paitṛkī kriyā \|*  
*atha māturvipattiḥ syāt pitṛkāryaṃ na lupyate \|\| 143 \|\|*

143\. If father obtains ghosthood, rites due to themanesane of no avail. If mother obtains ghosthood, the manes rites for the father do not cease to be.

## \|\| 34.144 \|\|

*mṛtā mātā pitā tiṣṭhejjīvantī ca pitāmahī \|*  
*sapiṇḍanantu kartavyaṃ pitāmahyā sahaiva tu \|\| 144 \|\|*

144\. If mother is dead, father is alive, the sapindana should be done with the grandmother if she is already dead (but

## \|\| 34.145 \|\|

*satyaṃsatyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ śrūyatāṃ vacanaṃ mama \|*  
*na piṇḍo milito yeṣāṃ mṛtānāntu nṛṇāṃ bhuvi \|\| 145 \|\|*

145\. О bird, hear. I tell you the truth: For those dead persons whose pinch has not been united, no sraddha and no water-libation is fruitful even if offered a thousand times.

## Chapter 35

## \|\| 35.1-3 \|\|

*tarkṣya uvāca \|*  
*aparaṃ mama sandehaṃ kathayasva janārdana \|*  
*puruṣasya ca kasyāpi matā pañcatvamāgatā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*pitāmahī jīvati ca tathā ca prapitāmahī \|*  
*vṛddhaprapitāmahī tadvanmātṛsaktaḥ pitā tathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*pramātāmahaśca tathā vṛddhapramātāmahastathā \|*  
*kena sā melyate mātā etat kathaya me prabho \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. О lord, I have another doubt, please clear it. Suppose the mother of a man dies but all other relatives such as father, grandfather, great grandfather and great great grand father and their wives are alive (except of course the mother) with whom shall the lady be joined in Sapindikarana?

## \|\| 35.4 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*punaruktaṃ pravakṣyāmi sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ khaga \|*  
*umā lakṣmīśca sāvitrītyatābhirmelayeddhruvam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О bird, I shall repeat the mode of sapndlkarana already mentioned to you. The lady in question shall be joined to Uma, Laksmi and Savitrl.

## \|\| 35.5 \|\|

*trayaḥ piṇḍabhujo jñeyāstyajākāśca trayaḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*trayaḥ piṇḍānulepāśca daśamaḥ paṅktisaṃnnidhaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Three generations from one are those who share the pinda. next three generations are those who do not share the pinda. The next three generations are those who receive the wipings of the food-offerings sticking to the hand and the tenth is near the line.

## \|\| 35.6 \|\|

*ityete puruṣāḥ khyātāḥ pitṛmātṛkuleṣu ca \|*  
*tārayedyajamānastu daśa pūrvān daśāvarān \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. These are the names assigned to men in one's father's as well as mother's family. The householder who performs the sraddhas saves ten preceding and ten succeeding generations.

## \|\| 35.7 \|\|

*sapiṇḍaḥ sa bhavedādau sapiṇḍīkaraṇe kṛte \|*  
*antyastu tyājako jñeyo yo vṛddhaprapitāmahaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When sapindikarana is done, the householder is considered first (i.e. reckoning is made from him). His fourth grandfather, i.e. the great-great-grandfather is tyajaka for him i.e. neither pinch nor water libation is offered for him.

## \|\| 35.8 \|\|

*antimastyājako jñeyo lepakaḥ prathamo bhavet \|*  
*lepakastvantimo yastu sa bhavet paṅktisannidhaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Grandfather of the fourth degree of the first tyajaka is the first lepaka \[i.e. the 7 grandfather reckoning from the householder\]. Grandfather of the 4 degree of the first lepaka is in the 10 degree since he is the fourth in reckoning from the 7 one.

## \|\| 35.9 \|\|

*yajamāno bhavedeko daśa pṛrtve daśāvare \|*  
*ityete pitaro jñeyā ekaviṃśati saṃkhyakāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The householder is one, ten generations before and ten generations after. These twentyone generations are meant 'whenever the word twentyone generations is used.

## \|\| 35.10 \|\|

*vidhinā kurute yastu saṃsāre śrāddhamuttamam \|*  
*jāyate 'tra na sandehaḥ śṛṇu tasyāpi yat phalam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Now, listen to the benefit accruing from performing the sraddhas and offering gifts according to sastras in favour of the dead.

## \|\| 35.11 \|\|

*pitā dadāti puttrān vai vicchinnasantatiḥ khaga \|*  
*homadātā bhavetsopi yastasya prapitāmahaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The father blesses the performer with the birth of sons, the grandfather with cattlewealth and the great-grandfather with coins of gold.

## \|\| 35.12 \|\|

*kṛte śrāddhe guṇā hyete pitṝṇāṃ tapeṇe smṛtāḥ \|*  
*dadyādvipulamannādyaṃ vṛddhastu prapitāmahaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Such are the benefits derivable from sraddhas and tarpanas. The great grandfather blesses one with plenty of foodstuffs.

## \|\| 35.13 \|\|

*yasya puṃsaśca martye vai vicchinnā santatiḥ khaga \|*  
*sa vasennarake ghore paṅke magnaḥ karī yathā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. О bird, the man whose line is broken shall stay in hell permanently like an elephant stuck in the mud.

## \|\| 35.14 \|\|

*yonyantareṣu jāyate yatra vṛkṣasarīsṛpāḥ \|*  
*na santatiṃ vinā so 'tra mucyate narakāddhruvam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Or he may be born in other species of living beings such as a tree, a bird, a reptile. If a child is born and dies the man cannot be freed from hell, still.

## \|\| 35.15 \|\|

*ācāryastasya śiṣyo vā yo dūre 'pi hi gātrejaḥ \|*  
*nārāyaṇabaliṃ kuryāt tasyāddeśena bhaktitaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. For his sake the Narayana Bali should be performed by his preceptor or disciple or even by a distant relative.

## \|\| 35.16 \|\|

*viśuddhaḥ sarvapāpebhyo muktaḥ sa narakāddhruvam \|*  
*nivasennākaloke ca nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He will be freed from sins and saved from hell. He will stay permanently in heaven. No doubt need be entertained on this account.

## \|\| 35.17-18 \|\|

*ādau kṛtvā dhaniṣṭhāñca etannakṣatrapañcakam \|*  
*revatyantaṃ sadā dūṣyamaśubhaṃ sarvadā bhavet \|\| 17 \|\|*

*dāha (bali) statra na kartavyo vipradisarvajātiṣu \|*  
*dīyate na jalaṃ tatra aśubhaṃ jāyate dhruvam \|*  
*lokayātrā na kartavyā duḥ khārtaḥ svajano yadi \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. If a member of the Brahmin caste dies on days, when the moon is in conjunction with any of the stars. Dhanistha and the four succeeding ones, ending with Revati, it is very inauspicious. Cremation or water libation is not performed during those days.

## \|\| 35.19 \|\|

*pañcakānantaraṃ tasya kartavyaṃ sarvamanyathā \|*  
*putrāṇāṃ gotriṇāṃ tasya santāpo 'pyupajāyate \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Even if the family is excessively miserable no job for livelihood should be taken up during these days. Every job shall be done after the five days are over.

## \|\| 35.20 \|\|

*gṛhe hānirbhavettasya ṛkṣeṣveṣu mṛtaśca yaḥ \|*  
*athavā ṛkṣamadhye 'pi dāhastasya vidhīyate \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Great distress will befall sons and clansmen of the dead who dies on any of these five days. Loss in the house is also inevitable.

## \|\| 35.21 \|\|

*kriyate mānuṣāṇāntu sadya āhutikāraṇāt \|*  
*sadyāhutikaraṃ puṇyaṃ tīrthe taddāha uttamaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Still if the immediate cremation is conducive to the welfare of the people the cremation with, the following extra procedure can be duly performed and an immediate offering in the fire can also be made.

## \|\| 35.22 \|\|

*viprairniyamataḥ kāryaḥ samantro vidhipūrvakaḥ \|*  
*śavasya ca samīpe tu kṣipyante puttalāstataḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The immediate offering is held to be sacred in regard to the body to be cremated. The body is well consecrated with mantras by the Brahmins duly.

## \|\| 35.23 \|\|

*darbhamayāśca catvāro viprā mantrābhimantritāḥ \|*  
*tato dāhaḥ prakartavyaḥ taiśca puttalakaiḥ saha \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Four effigies of Darbha are consecrated with the mantras of the naksatras. These are thrown beside the corpse.

## \|\| 35.24 \|\|

*sūtakānte tataḥ putraḥ kuryācchāntikamuttamam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Cremation is proceeded along with the effigies. On the expiry of the period of impurity the son shall perform rites, for tranquillity and peace.

## \|\| 35.25 \|\|

*pañcakeṣu mṛto yo 'sau na gatiṃ labhate naraḥ \|*  
*tilān gāśca suvarṇaṃ ca tamuddiśya ghṛtaṃ dadet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The man who dies on any of these five days does not attain salvation unless for his sake the gingelly seeds, cow, gold and ghee are gifted away.

## \|\| 35.26 \|\|

*viprāṇāṃ dīyate dānaṃ sarvopadravanāśanam \|*  
*sūtakānte ca satputraiḥ sa preto labhate gatim \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Gifts are made to Brahmins to ward off impending calamity after the expiry of impurity period. Gifts help the deceased attain release from the bonds of his previons actions.

## \|\| 35.27 \|\|

*bhājanopānahau cchatraṃ hemamudrāca vāsasī \|*  
*dakṣiṇā dīyate vipra sarvapātakamocanī \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Food-stuffs, sandals, umbrella, gold coins, clothes and other gifts are given to the Brahmins for redemption from, all worldly sins.

## \|\| 35.28 \|\|

*bālavṛddhāturāṇāñca mṛtānāṃ pañcakeṣu hi \|*  
*vidhānaṃ yo na kurvīta vighnastasya prajāyate \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Whether it is a young man or an old man or a child,, if any one dies in five days and no redemptionary rites are performed it will create obstacles.

## \|\| 35.29-32 \|\|

*aṣṭādaśaiva vastūni pretaśrāddhe vivarjayet \|*  
*āśiṣo dviguṇān darbhān praṇavān naikapiṇḍatām \|\| 29 \|\|*

*agnaukaraṇamucchiṣṭaṃ śrāddhaṃ vai vaiśvadaivikam \|*  
*vikiraṃ ca svadhākāraṃ pitṛśabdaṃ na coccaret \|\| 30 \|\|*

*anuśabdaṃ na kurvīta nāvāhanamatholmukam \|*  
*āsīmāntaṃ na kurvīta pradakṣiṇavisarjanam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*na kuryāt tilahomañca dvijaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ tathā \|*  
*na kuryādvaiśvadevaṃ cetkartā gacchatyadhogatim \|\| 32 \|\|*

29-32. In the sraddha for the deceased the following eighteen are prohibited; benediction, twice-twisted darbhas recital of svasti astu (hall), pranava (Om), Ekoddista, Agnikarana (consigning cooked rice to the fire, Ucchista (leavings of food) Vaisvadeva sraddha, VIkira, (scattering of cooked rice bits), recital of the words svadha, Pitr, and Anu Avahana invocation, lighting of the torch (ulmuka) the circumambulation, following upto the border, offering of gingelly seeds into the fire (Tilahoma) and Purnahuti. If these are included in the rites, the performer will suffer utter destruction. О son of Kasyapa, I have already told you about the sixteen sraddhas.

## \|\| 35.33-34 \|\|

*malinaśrāddhasaṃjñānaṃ pūrvaṣoḍaśakaṃ tathā \|*  
*sthāne dvāre cārdhamārge citāyāṃ śavahastake \|\| 33 \|\|*

*śmaśānavāsibhūtebhyaḥ pañcamaṃ prativeśyakam \|*  
*ṣaṣṭhaṃ sañcayane proktaṃ daśa piṇḍā daśāhikāḥ \|*  
*śrāddhaṣoḍaṣakañcaitat prathamaṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. The sixteen sraddhas already mentioned are:-One at the place of death, then midway to the cremation ground, at the funeral pyre, in the hand of the corpse and the fifth Prativesyaka offered to the spirits living in the cremation ground, the sixth at the collection of ashes and bones, then the pindas. offered during the ten days. Thus sixteen in all.

## \|\| 35.35-36 \|\|

*anyacca ṣoḍaśaṃ madhye dvitīyaṃ tārkṣya me śṛṇu \|*  
*kartavyānīha vidhinā śrāddhānyekādaśaiva tu \|\| 35 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇuśivādyañca tathānyacchrāddhapañcakam \|*  
*evaṃ ṣoḍaśakaṃ prāhuretat tattvavido janāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. О Tarksya, listen to another mode of reckoning six" sraddhas. Ten daily pindas, one extra at the collection of bones, then five for Brahma Visnu, Shiva and others. These sixteen sraddhas are reckoned by persons who know the Ritual.

## \|\| 35.37 \|\|

*dvādaśa pratimāsyāni śrāddhamekādaśe tathā \|*  
*tripakṣasambhavañcaiva dve rikte khaga ṣoḍaśa \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О bird, a third method of reckoning the sixteen sraddhas is as follows:- the twelve monthly sraddhas together with that on the eleventh, that on tripaksa (after the expiry of six weeks) and those on the two rikta days.

## \|\| 35.38 \|\|

*ādyaṃ śavaviśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvānyacca triṣoḍaśam \|*  
*pitṛpāṅktiviśuddhyarthaṃ śatāddhaiṃna tu yojayet \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The first set of sixteen sraddhas are for purifying the corpse. The fifty sraddhas are for purifying the line of Manes (pitrpankti).

## \|\| 35.39 \|\|

*śatārdhena vihīno yo militaḥ paṅktibhāṅna hi \|*  
*catvāriṃśat tathaivāṣṭaśrāddhaṃ pretatvanāśanam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. If enjoining (saplndlkarana) is devoid of fifty sraddhas it shall not reach the pitrs. The redemption from ghosthood is possible only if fifty sraddhas are performed.

## \|\| 35.40 \|\|

*sakṛdūnaśatārdhena sambhavet paṅktisannidhaḥ \|*  
*melanīyaḥ śatārdhena sandhiḥ śrāddhena tattvataḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. If the performance of fifty sraddhas is even slightly deficient, the presence of pitrs cannot be effected. It is corollary to the fifty sraddhas that the descendent should perform sapindana (effectively).

## \|\| 35.41-42 \|\|

*(atha śavāvidhiḥ) \|*  
*śavasya śibikāyāṃ karacaraṇabandhanaṃ tatra kartavyam \|*  
*evaṃ cenna vidhānaṃ vidhīyate tatpiśācaparibhavanam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*saṃjāyate rajanyāñca śavanirgamane rāṣṭraṃ bhayaśūnyam \|*  
*śavaṃ na muñceta mucyate cet duḥ sparśāddurgatirbhavet \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. The hands and feet of the dead together with the covering cloth should be tied to the bamboo bier. If this is not done, there is risk of an attack by the piascas. If the dead bod is taken out during the night there is a fear from spirits roaming in the sky. The dead body should not be left unattended. By touching it mishaps may occur.

## \|\| 35.43-44 \|\|

*grāmamadhye sthite prete śrute bhuṅkte yadṛcchayā \|*  
*tadannaṃ māṃsavajjñeyaṃ tattoyaṃ rudhiropamam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*tāmbūlaṃ dantakāṣṭhañca bhojanaṃ ṛtusevanam \|*  
*grāmamadhye sthite prete varjayet piṇḍapātanam \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. When there is dead body in the middle Г of the village and if any one takes food with i that knowledge, that food is Itnown as flesh, and water as blood. (No one shall take food or water before the dead body is removed). When there is a dead body in the village the following is avoided: Chewing the betel; chewing the tooth-brush twig, 'taking food, sexual intercourse and offering g of pindas

## \|\| 35.45 \|\|

*snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homastarpaṇaṃ surapūjanam \|*  
*grāmamadhye sthite prete śuddhyarthaṃ jñātidharmataḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Ceremonial ablutions, gifts, sacrifices, water-libations and worship of gods are futile when performed with a dead body in the village. The convention holds good for all kiths and kins.

## \|\| 35.46 \|\|

*jñātisambandhināmevaṃ vyavahāraḥ khageśvara \|*  
*vilupya jñātidharmañca pretapāpena lipyate \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О lord of birds, this convention prevail among cousins and kins. If it is violated, the dead man is tarnished by sins.

## Chapter 36

## \|\| 36.1-4 \|\|

*tārkṣyauvāca \|*  
*kasmādanaśanaṃ puṇyamakṣayyagatidāyakam \|*  
*svagṛhantu parityajya tīrthe vai mriyate yadi \|\| 1 \|\|*

*aprāpya tīrthaṃ mriyate gṛhe vā mṛtyu māgataḥ \|*  
*būtvā kuṭīcaro yastu sa kāṃ gatimavāpnuyāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

*saṃnyāsaṃ kurute yastu tīrthe vāpi gṛhe 'pi vā \|*  
*kathaṃ tasya prakartavyamaprāptanidhane 'pi vā \|\| 3 \|\|*

*niyame cet kṛte deva cittabhaṅgo hi jāyate \|*  
*kena tasya bhavet siddhiḥ kṛtenāpyakṛtena vā \|\| 4 \|\|*

1-4. Why is the rite of fasting considered holy and the bestower of salvation? What is the goal attained by a person who had left his house and died in a holy centre? What is it if he died before reaching the holy centre? What is it if he died in the house itself? What is it if he died in a cottage (as. a Vanaprastha) or if he took to samnyasa either in the holy centre or in the house. How shall the rites be performed in such cases? What is the procedure if he does not die a proper death? О lord, what are the rules if the performer wavers and does not stand on his resolve? What are the rites which enable him to achieve realization?

## \|\| 36.5 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*kṛtvā niraśanaṃ yo vai mṛtyumāpnoti ko 'pi cet \|*  
*mānupīṃ tanumutsṛjya mama tulyo virājate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. If any one observes the rite of fasting and dies he will cast off his human form and become equal in lustre to me.

## \|\| 36.6 \|\|

*yāvantyahāni jīvet vrate niraśane kṛte \|*  
*kratubhistāni tulyāni samagravadakṣiṇaiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He will have the benefit of performing as many sacrifices, complete with due fees as the number of days he was able to live observing the rite of fasting.

## \|\| 36.7 \|\|

*tīrthe gṛhe vā saṃnyāsaṃ nītvā cenmriyate yadi \|*  
*pratyahaṃ labhate so 'pi pūrvoktaṃ dviguṇaṃ phalam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. If such a death takes place after taking to Samnyasa whether in the holy centre or elsewhere the benefit accruing from the same is twice that of the previous one.

## \|\| 36.8 \|\|

*mahārogopapattau ca gṛhīte 'naśane kṛte \|*  
*punarna jāyate rogo devavaddhi virājate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. If a person suffering from an incurable disease such as plague etc. observes fast and dies he has no rebirth. He rejoices in heaven like a deity,

## \|\| 36.9 \|\|

*ya āturaḥ san sannyāsaṃ gṛhṇāti yadi mānavaḥ \|*  
*punarna jāyate bhūmau saṃsāre duḥ khasāgare \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. If a sick man takes to Samnyasa he is released from the cycle of rebirth in this world of sorrows and afflictions.

## \|\| 36.10 \|\|

*ahanyahani dātavyaṃ brāhmaṇānāñca bhojanam \|*  
*tilapātraṃ yathāśakti dīpadānaṃ surārcanam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Brahmins should be fed everyday. Gifts of a vessel full ofgingelly seeds or gifts of lamps according to his capacity should be made. Gods should also be worshipped,

## \|\| 36.11 \|\|

*evaṃ vṛttasya dahyante pāpānyuccāvacāni ca \|*  
*mṛto muktimavāpnoti yathā sarve maharṣayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. If gifts are made in favour of the dead, his major and minor sins are washed away. On death, he attains immortality on par with sages.

## \|\| 36.12 \|\|

*tasmādanaśanaṃ nṝṇāṃ vaikuṇṭhapadadāyakam \|*  
*tasmāt svasthe cottare vā sādhayenmokṣalakṣaṇam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Hence, observance of fast enables men to attain heaven. Man should strive for salvation when his body is in perfect health.

## \|\| 36.13 \|\|

*puttradravyādi santyajya tīrthaṃ vrajati yo naraḥ \|*  
*brahmādyā devatāstasya bhaveyustuṣṭipuṣṭidāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The deities Brahma and others bestow contentment and nourishment on the man who forsakes, his sons, wealth and goes to a holy centre.

## \|\| 36.14 \|\|

*yastīrthasaṃmukho bhūtvā vrate hyanaśane kṛte \|*  
*cenmriyetāntarāle 'pi ṛṣīṇāṃ maṇḍale vaset \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. If a person dies after observing the fast on reaching the holy centre or on the way, he goes to the sphere of sages.

## \|\| 36.15 \|\|

*vrataṃ niraśana kṛtvā svagṛhe 'pi mṛto yadi \|*  
*svakulāni parityajya ekākī vicareddivi \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. If a person dies at home after observing the fas the alone will sojourn in heaven leaving the members of his family.

## \|\| 36.16 \|\|

*annañcaiva tathā toyaṃ parityajya naro yadi \|*  
*pītamatpādatoyaśca na punarjāyate kṣitau \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. If a person casts off food and water and drinks only the water from my feet he is not reborn on the earth.

## \|\| 36.17 \|\|

*kṛttyāsīnantattīrthagataṃ rakṣanti vanadevatāḥ \|*  
*yamadūtā viśeṣeṇa na yāmyāstasya pārśvagāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The family deities protect the man who goes to a holy centre and abstains from food. The emissaries of Yama guard him. No torture at the hands of Yama's attendants is in store for him.

## \|\| 36.18 \|\|

*tīrthasevī naro yastu sarvakilbiṣavarjitaḥ \|*  
*tatra mriyate dahyeta tattīrthaphalabhāgbhavet \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. A person who makes frequent pilgrimages to holy centres can destroy sins. The person who cremates him, if he dies, enjoys the benefit of making the pilgrimage.

## \|\| 36.19 \|\|

*sevite 'pi sadā tathi hyanyatra mriyate yadi \|*  
*śubhe deśe kule dhīmān sa bhavedvedaviddvijaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. A frequent visitor to holy centres, even if he dies outside the holy centre, is reborn as an intelligent Brahmin well-versed in the Vedas at a holy place and in a noble family.

## \|\| 36.20 \|\|

*kṛtvā niraśanaṃ tārkṣya punarjīvati mānavaḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇān sa samāhūya sarvasvaṃ yat parityajet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. If a man observing fast, О Tarksya, survives it he should invite brahmmas and gift away his possessions.

## \|\| 36.21 \|\|

*cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret kṛtsnamanujñātaśca tairdvijaiḥ \|*  
*anṛtaṃ na vadet paścāddharmameva samācaret \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Following the instructions of Brahmins he should observe Candrayana and Krcchra. He should never tell a lie thereafter. He shall practise virtuous actions.

## \|\| 36.22 \|\|

*tīrthe gatvā ca yaḥ ko 'pi punarāyāti vai gṛham \|*  
*anujñātaḥ sa vai vipraiḥ prāyaścittamathācaret \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. When a person returns to his own house after making pilgrimage to the holy centre he should take the permission of pious Brahmins and perform expiatory rite

## \|\| 36.23 \|\|

*dattvā vā svarṇadānāni go-mahī-gaja-vājinaḥ \|*  
*tīrthaṃ yadi labhed yastu mṛtyukāle sa bhāgyavān \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. If a person is able to make pilgrimage to holy centres at the stage of death after previously making gifts of gold, cow, land, elephant and horses, he is the most fortunate of all.

## \|\| 36.24 \|\|

*gṛhāt pracalitastīrthaṃ maraṇe samupasthite \|*  
*padepade tu godānaṃ yadi hiṃsā na jāyate \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. A person starting on a pilgrimage when death is imminent should make gifts of cows at every step if the act is not involved in violence.

## \|\| 36.25 \|\|

*gṛhe tu yat kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tīrthasnānena śudhyati \|*  
*kurute tatra pāpañcedvajralepasamaṃ hi tat \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The sin committed at home is washed off by oblutions in holy waters, while the sin committed at the holy centre becomes adamantine and is never washed off at all.

## \|\| 36.26 \|\|

*kliśyet sa nātra sandeho yāvaccandrārkatārakam \|*  
*tatra dattāni dānāni jāyante cākṣayāṇi vai \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. There is no doubt in this that he will be distressed for ever by those sins as long as the sun, the moon and the stars shine. О bird, the gifts made therein are of everlasting benefit.

## \|\| 36.27 \|\|

*āture sati dātavyaṃ nirdhanairapi mānavaiḥ \|*  
*gāvastilā hiraṇyañca saptadhānyaṃ viśeṣataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*
