27\. Even indigent persons when sick should make gifts. Such gifts should consist'of a cow, gingelly seeds, gold and of seven grains.

## \|\| 36.28 \|\|

*dānavantaṃ naraṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭāḥ sarve divaukasaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣibhiḥ saha dharmaṇa citraguptena sarvadā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. On seeing a person making liberal-gifts, the deities, sages and Citragupta are excessively delighted.

## \|\| 36.29 \|\|

*ātmāyattaṃ dhanaṃ yāvat tāvadvipre samarpayet \|*  
*parādhīnaṃ mṛte sarvaṃ kṛpayā kaḥ pradāsyati \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. So long as one is in free possession of one's wealth, one should make it over to the brahmanas. After death, the entire wealth goes to others. Who will not therefore have the grace to give?

## \|\| 36.30 \|\|

*pitruddeśena yaḥ puttrairdhanaṃ viprakare 'rpitam \|*  
*ātmānaṃ sadhanaṃ tena cakre putraprapautrakaiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. By offering gifts of wealth to brahmanas, the sons in fact, prepare for their salvation along with sons, grandsons and great grandsons.

## \|\| 36.31 \|\|

*pituḥ śataguṇaṃ dattaṃ sahasraṃ māturucyate \|*  
*bhaginyā śatasāhasraṃ sodarye dattamakṣayam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. What is given to father will be requited a hundredfold; to mother a thousand-fold, to a sister a hundred thousand fold and to a brother manyfold.

## \|\| 36.32 \|\|

*yadi lobhānna yacchanti pramādānmohato 'pi vā \|*  
*mṛtāḥ śocanti te sarve kadaryāḥ pāpinastviti \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. If, out of covetousness, a person does not give any thing during sickness, that miserly sinner will indeed repent after death.

## \|\| 36.33 \|\|

*atikleśena labdhasya prakṛtyā cañcalasya ca \|*  
*gatirekaiva vittasya dānamanyā vipattayaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Wealth is acquired with strain and stress. It is naturally unsteady. The only solution is in being gifted to others. Otherwise there awaits only disaster.

## \|\| 36.34 \|\|

*mṛtyuḥ śarīgoptāraṃ vasurakṣaṃ vasundharā \|*  
*duścāriṇīva hasati svapatiṃ putravatsalam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Just as an unchaste wife laughs at her husband who fondles the son too much, as also death laughs at a person who makes fuss about protecting himself, and the earth laughs at a person who guards too much his wealth.

## \|\| 36.35-36 \|\|

*udāse dhārmikaḥ saumyaḥ prāpyāpi vipulaṃ dhanam \|*  
*tṛṇavanmanyate tārkṣya ātmānaṃ vittamapyatha \|\| 35 \|\|*

*na caivopadravāstasya mohajālo na caiva hi \|*  
*mṛtyukāle na ca bhayaṃ yamadūtasamudbhavam \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. A virtuous man of liberal temperament and gentleness, even after acquiring a lot of wealth, considers it to be as useless as a blade of grass. He feels no torment, no delusion and no fear of the emissaries of Yama at the hour of death.

## \|\| 36.37 \|\|

*samāḥ sahasrāṇi ca sapta vai jale daśaikamagnau pavane ca ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*mahāhave paṣṭiraśītirgogṛhe anāśake kāśyapa cākṣayā gatiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The aftereffects of death in water last for seven thousand years; those of death in fire for eleven thousand years: those of death in scorching sun for sixteen thousand years; those of death in a battle for sixty thousand yeas; those of death in thwarting the seizure of cow for eighty thousand years, and О Bharata those of death in tasting are everlasting.

## Chapter 37

## \|\| 37.1-2 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*udakumbhapradānaṃ me kathayasva yathātatham \|*  
*vidhinā kena kartavyā kṛtireṣā janārdana \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kiṃlakṣaṇāḥ kena pūrṇāḥ kasya deyā janārdana \|*  
*kasmin kāle pradātavyā pretatṛptiprasādhakāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. О lord, please explain to me the mode of giving water-jar precisely. What are the rules of giving?How many vessels should be given? What are their characteristics? By what stuff are they to be filled? To whom are they to be given? When are they to be given to propitiate the dead?

## \|\| 37.3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*satyaṃ punaḥ pravakṣyāmi udakumbhapradānakam \|*  
*pretoddeśena dātavyā annapānīyasaṃyutāḥ \|*  
*viśeṣeṇa mahāpakṣin pretamuktipradāyakāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. О Tarksya, now I shall explain the mode of gifting a water-jar. It should be offered to the brahman in favour of the dead along with a rice- ball and the libation of water.

## \|\| 37.4 \|\|

*dvādaśāhe ca paṇmāse traipakṣe vāpi vatsare \|*  
*udakumbhāḥ pradātavyā mārge tasya sukhāya vai \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. For the pleasure of the dead on their way to Yama's abode, these vessels should be given on the twelfth day, after six weeks, after six months or at the end of a year.

## \|\| 37.5 \|\|

*ahanyahani dātavyā udakumbhāstilairyutāḥ \|*  
*sulipte bhūmibhāge tu pakkānnajalapūritāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The vessel should be placed on the ground cleaned and smeared with cowdung. They should be filled with cooked food and water.

## \|\| 37.6 \|\|

*pretasya tatra dātavyaṃ bhājanañca yadṛcchayā \|*  
*suprītastena dattena preto yāmyaiḥ sa gacchati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Satiating food should be given to the dead. The dead man is delighted thereby as he goes along with the attendants of Yama.

## \|\| 37.7 \|\|

*dvādaśāhe viśeṣeṇa udakambhān pradāpayet \|*  
*vidhinā tatra saṅkalpaya ghaṭān dvādaśasaṃkhyakān \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On the twelfth day, twelve such jars should be given.

## \|\| 37.8 \|\|

*ekāṣi bardhanī tatra pakvānnaphalapūritā \|*  
*viṣṇumuddiśya dātavyā saṃkalpya brāhmaṇe śubhe \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. A Vardhani \[a vessel\] filled with cooked food and water should be given to a Brahmin with Visnu in view after due sankalpa.

## \|\| 37.9 \|\|

*eko vai dharmarājāya tena tuṣṭena muktibhāk \|*  
*citraguptāya caikaṃ tu gatastatra sukhī bhavet \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. A single Vessel should be given in favour of Yama. Thereby, the dead man attains release. Another vessel should be given in favour of Citragupta. Thereby, the dead man

## \|\| 37.10 \|\|

*ṣoḍaśādyāḥ pradātavyā māṣānnajalapūritāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Sixteen vessels filled with cooked black gram and water should be given to sixteen brahmanas, one to each.

## \|\| 37.11 \|\|

*uktrāntiśrāddhamārabhya śrāddhaṣoḍaṣoḍaśakasya tu \|*  
*ṣoḍaśabrāhmaṇānāntu ekaikaṃ vinivadayet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The sixteen sraddhas are performed beginning with Utkranti-sraddha (the first of the series).

## \|\| 37.12 \|\|

*ekādaśāhātprabhṛti deyo nityaṃ dṛḍhāhvayaḥ \|*  
*pakvānnajalapūrṇo hi yāvat saṃvatsaraṃ dinam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. From the eleventh day a vessel should be given every day for a year.

## \|\| 37.13 \|\|

*jalapātrāṇi vṛddhāni dattānighaṭakāni ca \|*  
*ekā vai vardhanī tatra tasyāṃ pātrantu vaṃśajam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. A Vardhann filled with cooked rice and. water should be given everyday for a year along with a bamboo vessel.

## \|\| 37.14 \|\|

*vastreṇācchādayet tāntu pūjayitvā sugandhibhiḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇebhyo viśeṣeṇa jalapūrṇāni dāpayet \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. A vessel full of water should be given to a Brahmin, after covering it with a cloth and scenting it with fragrant stuffs.

## \|\| 37.15-16 \|\|

*ahanyahani saṅkalpya vidhipūrvaṃ khageśvara \|*  
*brāhmaṇāya kulīnāya vedavṛttayutāya ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

*vidyāvṛttavate deyaṃ mūrkhe tanna kadācana \|*  
*samartho vedavṛttāḍhyastāraṇe taraṇe 'pi ca \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Every day, О bird, with due sankalpa a who is endowed with Vedic lore can cross and vessel should be given to a Brahmin well versed enable others to cross the ocean of worldly in the Vedas and observing all rites. He must existence, be a deserving person, not a fool. Only the man

## Chapter 38

## \|\| 38.1-3 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*dānatīrthārthitaṃ mokṣaṃ svargañca vada me prabho \|*  
*kena mokṣamavāpnoti kena svarge vasecciram \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kena gacchati tejastu svarlokāt satyalokataḥ \|*  
*mānuṣyaṃ kena labhate narakeśu nimajjati \|\| 2 \|\|*

*etanme vadaniścitya bhaktānāṃ mokṣadāyaka \|*  
*brūhi kasmin mṛte svarge punarjanma na vidyate \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. О lord, please explain to me the nature of salvation, and heaven realizable or accessible by gifts or pilgrimages. How does a man attain salvation and how does he reach and stay in heaven for a long time? What are the causes of fall of living beings from heaven and other regions among the upper seven worlds.

## \|\| 38.4 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*mānuṣyaṃ bhārate varṣe trayodaśasu jātiṣu \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. After obtaining human form in any of the thirteen castes in Bharata, if a man dies in a holy centre he is never born again.

## \|\| 38.5 \|\|

*tat prāpya mriyate kṣetre punarjanma na vidyate \|*  
*ayodhyā mathurā māyā kāśī kāñcī avantikā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The seven cities of Ayodhya, Mathura, Maya, KancI,. Avantika, Kasi and Dvaravatl confer salvation.

## \|\| 38.6 \|\|

*purī dvāravatī jñeyā saptaitā mokṣadāyikāḥ \|*  
*sannyastamiti yo bruyāt prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatairapi \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. If at the time when the vital airs have reached the throat (when death is imminent) a man says' I have renounced', he attains the region of Visnu after death and is not born again.

## \|\| 38.7 \|\|

*mṛto viṣṇupuraṃ yāti na punarjāyate kṣitau \|*  
*sakṛduccaritaṃ yena harirityakṣaradvayam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He has already tucked his clothes for his journey to his goal of salvation if he pronoimces the two Tetters Hari even for once.

## \|\| 38.8 \|\|

*baddhaḥ parikarastena mokṣāya gamanaṃ prati \|*  
*kṛṣṇakṛṣṇeti kṛṣṇeti yo māṃ smarati nityaśaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He who remembers me ever and anon saying Krsna, Krsna, Krsna is lifted up by me from hell just as the lotus springs, up breaking through the water.

## \|\| 38.9 \|\|

*jala bhittvā yathā padmaṃ narakāduddharāmyaham \|*  
*śālagrāmajilā yatra yatra dvāravatī śilā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Undoubtedly one attains salvation if one dies near a salagrama stone which is powerful for annihilating all sins and defects.

## \|\| 38.10 \|\|

*ubhayoḥ saṅgamo yatra muktistatra na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*śālagrāmaśilā yatra pāpadoṣakṣayāvahā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. There is no doubt in this that salvation is ever present wherever Shalagrama stone or the stone of Dvaravati or both are present

## \|\| 38.11 \|\|

*tatsannidhānamaraṇānmuktirjantoḥ suniścatā \|*  
*ropaṇāt pālanāt sekāddhyānasparśanakīrtanāt \|*  
*tulasī dahate pāpaṃ nṛṇāṃ janmārjitaṃ khaga \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О bird, by growing, nurturing, sprinkling, saluting and extolling the Tulasi plant Man's sin accumulated in various births is wiped off.

## \|\| 38.12 \|\|

*jñānahṛde satyajale rāgadveṣamalāpahe \|*  
*yaḥ snāto mānase tīrthe na sa lipyeta pātakaiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He who has taken ablution in the holy lake of Manasa where wisdom is the eddy, truth the water and which removes the dirt of love and hatred is never tarnished by sins.

## \|\| 38.13 \|\|

*na kāṣṭhe vidyate devo na śilāyāṃ kadācana \|*  
*bhāve hi vidyate devastasmādbhāvaṃ samācaret \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. God is not present in wood, stone or clods of earth. He is present in our heart. Hence, one should invoke the heart which plays a prominent part in devotion.

## \|\| 38.14 \|\|

*prātaḥ prātaḥ prapaśyanti narmadāṃ matsyaghātinaḥ \|*  
*na te śivapurīṃ yānti cittavṛttirgarīyasī \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Fishermen visit Narmada, the holv river, every morning. But their mind does not become pure.

## \|\| 38.15 \|\|

*yādṛk cittapratītiḥ syāt tādṛk karmaphalaṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*paralokagatistādṛka sūcīsūtravicāravat \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Men reap the fruits of their actions in accordance with their feeling; their goal in the other world is also befitting that. Their activity yields accordant fruits.

## \|\| 38.16 \|\|

*brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe ca strīṇāṃ balavadheṣu ca \|*  
*prāṇatyāgaparo yastu sa vai mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He who lays down his life for the sake of his preceptor, a Brahmin, a woman or a child attains salvations.

## \|\| 38.17 \|\|

*anāśake mṛtau yastu sa vai mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*anāśake mṛto yastu sa muktaḥ sarvabandhanaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. After making various gifts to Brahmins if a person dies during the period of fast he id freed from all bondage and attains i salvation.

## \|\| 38.18-19 \|\|

*dattvā dānāni viprebhyastato mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*ete vai mokṣamārgāśca svargamārgāstathaiva ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

*gograhe deśavidhvaṃse maraṇaṃ raṇatīrthayoḥ \|*  
*uttamādhamamadhyasya bādhyamānasya dehinaḥ \|*  
*ātmānaṃ tatra santyajya svargavāsaṃ labhecciram \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Such are the ways which lead either to salvation or heaven. In thwarting the seizure of cows, civil commotions and national calamities or disasters in holy places and temples, it is equally beneficent whether one succumbs or survives- Life becomes pleasant by making gifts and enjoyments. Death is efficacious in battle and holy places.

## \|\| 38.20 \|\|

*jīvitaṃ maraṇañcaiva dvayaṃ śikṣeta paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*jīvitaṃ dānabhogābhyāṃ maraṇaṃ raṇatīrthayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Casting themselves off, human beings whether of high, middling or low class attain their heavenly abode.

## \|\| 38.21-22 \|\|

*harikṣetre kurukṣetre bhṛgukṣetre tathaiva ca \|*  
*prabhāse śrīsthale caiva arbude ca tripuṣkare \|\| 21 \|\|*

*bhūteśvare mṛto yastu svarge vasati mānavaḥ \|*  
*brahmaṇo divasaṃ yāvat tataḥ patati bhūtale \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. A man dying in Hariksetra, Kuruksetra, Bhrguksetra, Prabhasa, Srisaila, Arbuda, Puskara or Bhutesvara attains heavenly abode for the period of a day of Brahma and thereafter falls to the earth.

## \|\| 38.23 \|\|

*varṣavṛttintu yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe doṣavarjite \|*  
*sarvaṃ kalaṃ sa muddhṛtya svargaloke mahīyate \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He who accords gifts to a Brahmin devoted to the observance sufficient to last for a year raises his family and is honoured

## \|\| 38.24 \|\|

*kanyāṃ vivāhayedyastu brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam \|*  
*indraloke vaset so 'pi svakulaiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He who offers a virgin in marriage to a Brahmin well versed in the Vedas shall stay in Indraloka along with the members of his family.

## \|\| 38.25-26 \|\|

*mahādānāni datvā ca narastatphalamāmuyāt \|\| 25 \|\|*

*vāpī kūpataḍāgānāmārāmasurasadmanām \|*  
*jīrṇoddhāraṃ prakurvāṇaḥ pūrvakartuḥ phalaṃ labhet \|*  
*jīrṇoddhāreṇa vā teṣāṃ tat puṇyaṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavet \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. By giving gifts a man shall reap the fruits thereof. There is no doubt in this that he who resuscitates and repairs tanks, wells, lakes, parks and temples in ruins reaps twice the merit derived by the original builder.

## \|\| 38.27-29 \|\|

*śītavā tātapaharamapi parṇakuṭīrakam \|*  
*kṛtvā viprāya viduṣe pradadāti kuṭumbine \|\| 27 \|\|*

*tisraḥ koṭyordhakoṭī ca naraḥ svarge mahīyate \|\| 28 \|\|*

*yā strī savarṇā saṃśubā mṛtaṃ patimanuvrajet \|*  
*sā mṛtā svargamāpnoti varṣāṇāṃ romasaṃkhyatā \|\| 29 \|\|*

27-29. He who gives to a scholarly Brahmin burdened with a family, ornaments for the ears, neck, fingers and arms, a cosy house fully furnished with utensils and other requisites, a cow and the contrivances to preventchillness, gusts of wind and scorching heat, is honoured in heaven for thirty-five million years. The woman of the same caste who follows her husband in death attains heaven and stays for as many heavenly years as she spent human years in this world with him.

## \|\| 38.30 \|\|

*putrapautrādikaṃ tyaktvā svapatiṃ yānugacchati \|*  
*svargaṃ labhetāṃ tau cobhau divyastrībhiralaṅkṛtau \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. If a woman leaves her sons, grandsons and other relatives and follows her husband in death both of them attain heaven along with three generations.

## \|\| 38.31 \|\|

*kṛtvā pāpānyanekāni bhartṛdrohamatiḥ sadā \|*  
*prakṣālayati sarvāṇi yā svaṃ patimanuvrajet \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Even if a woman has committed sins including evil intention against her own husband she can wash off her sins if she follows her husband in death.

## \|\| 38.32 \|\|

*mahāpāpasamācāro bhartā cedduṣkṛtī bhavet \|*  
*tasyāpyanuvratā nārī nārāyet sarvakilbiṣam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Even if the husband has been a sinner and wicked, his sins will be washed off if the wife follows him after death.

## \|\| 38.33 \|\|

*grāsamātraṃ niyamato nityadānaṃ karoti yaḥ \|*  
*catuścāmarasaṃyuktavimānenādhigacchati \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. If a man offers only a morsel of bread to the needy he will be proceeding to heaven in an aerial chariot endowed with chowries and umbrellas.

## \|\| 38.34 \|\|

*yat kṛtaṃ hi manuṣyeṇa pāpamāmaraṇāntikam \|*  
*tat sarvaṃ nāśamāyāti varṣavṛttipradānataḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. If he offers maintenance sufficient for a year, the sins committed by him till his death are destroyed

## \|\| 38.35 \|\|

*bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ baviṣyañca pāpaṃ janmatrayārjitam \|*  
*pakṣālayati tat sarvaṃ viprakanyopanāyanāt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. If he helps a Brahmin to celebrate the marriage of his daughter, he washes off his sins of the past, future and present births.

## \|\| 38.36 \|\|

*daśakūpasamā vāpi daśavāpīsamaṃ saraḥ \|*  
*saro bhirdaśabhistulyā yā prapā nirjale vane \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The merit accruing from digging ten wells is acquired by digging a tank. The merit accruing from digging ten tanks is acquired by digging a lake. О bird, a drinking water shed erected in a dry place is equal to digging ten lakes.

## \|\| 38.37 \|\|

*yā vāpī nirjale deśe yaddānaṃ nirdhane dvije \|*  
*prāṇināṃ yo dayāṃ dhatte sa bhavennākanāyakaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The drinking water shed erected in a dry place is equal to a gift to an indigent Brahmin. He who shows mercy to living beings becomes a leader of the world.

## \|\| 38.38 \|\|

*evamādibhiranyaiśca sukṛtaiḥ svargabhāgbhavet \|*  
*sa tat sarvaṃ phalaṃ prāpya pratiṣṭhāṃ paramāṃ labhet \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. By means of meritorious deeds like these one can attain heaven. After reaping the fruits of his virtuous deeds one becomes well established.

## \|\| 38.39 \|\|

*phalgu kāryaṃ parityajya satataṃ dharmavān bhavet \|*  
*dānaṃ damo dayā ceti sārametat trayaṃ bhuvi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Leaving off useless insignificant place is so If a man contributes to cremate activities one shall ever be virtuous. Gifts, truth and mercy constitute the essential characteristics of the three worlds.

## \|\| 38.40 \|\|

*dānaṃ sādhordaridrasya śūnyaliṅgasya pūjanam \|*  
*anāthapretasaṃskāraḥ koṭiyajñaphalapradaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. A gift made to an indigent man is excellent; the worship of a Linga in a secluded.

## Chapter 39

## \|\| 39.1 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*sūtakānāṃ vidhiṃ brūhi dayāṃ kṛtvā mayiprabho \|*  
*vivekāya hi cittasya mānavānāṃ hitāya ca \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О lord, out of compassion for me, please tell me the rules of impurity, for my knowledge and for the welfare of human beings.

## \|\| 39.2 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*mṛte janmanī pakṣīndra sūtakaṃ syāccaturvidham \|*  
*caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ sāmānyate vivarjitam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О lord of birds, the impurity accruing from birth and death is fourfold. The rules of impurity are applicable to all the four castes.

## \|\| 39.3 \|\|

*ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ vivarjiyet \|*  
*dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaśca nivartate \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The days of impurity are ten for the relatives on the father's and mother's side. People should avoid taking meals, during this period, with the relatives of the dead. The bereaved family should neither offer nor receive gifts, should neither undertake nor conduct sacrifices. The study of the Vedas and Sastras is strictly prohibited.

## \|\| 39.4 \|\|

*deśaṃ kālaṃ tathātmānaṃ dravyaṃ dravyaprayājanam \|*  
*upapattiṃvmavasthāñca jñātvāṃ karma samācaret \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. One should observe the following while performing obsequial rites: suitability of place and time, sufficiency of wealth, justification of purpose, validity of reason and his capability.

## \|\| 39.5 \|\|

*guhāvahnipravese ca deśāntaramṛteṣu ca \|*  
*snānaṃ sacelaṃ kartavyaṃ sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. If a person is dead in the forest conflagration or in a foreign country, the relatives should take bath along with the dress; the impurity is soon removed thereby.

## \|\| 39.6 \|\|

*āmagarbhāśca ye jīvā ye ca garbhādviniḥ sṛtāḥ \|*  
*na teṣāmagnisaṃskāro nāśaucaṃ nodakakriyā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. If a child is dead in the womb or is born dead, there should be no obsequial rite, no waterlibation and no impurity at all.

## \|\| 39.7 \|\|

*śilpinaḥ kāravo vaidyā dāsīdāsāstathaiva ca \|*  
*rājānaḥ śrotiyāścaiva sadyaḥ śaucāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Artisans, architects, physicians, slaves (male or female), kings and Vedic scholars are purified immediately.

## \|\| 39.8 \|\|

*satrī ca (vratī) mantrapūtaśca āhitāgnirnṛpastathā \|*  
*eteṣāṃ sūtakaṃ nāsti yasya cecchanti pārthivāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He who is at fast or at sacrifice reciting the mantras or he who has set up a sacrificial fire or he who is a reigning ruler-these are exempt from impurity as also those who are exempted by the king.

## \|\| 39.9 \|\|

*prasave ca sapiṇḍānāṃ na kuryāt saṅkaraṃ dvijaḥ \|*  
*daśāhācchrudhyate mātā avagāhya pitā śuciḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In impurity accruing from birth, the rules are less strict. Mother is purified after ten days, father just after taking bath.

## \|\| 39.10 \|\|

*vivāhotsavayajñeṣu antarā mṛtasūtake \|*  
*pūrvasaṅkalpitaṃ vittaṃ bhojyaṃ tanmanurabravīt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. During the days of marriage, festivity, sacrifice, if a person dies, there is no impurity. The food-stuff collected for use can be utilized by the persons concerned. This is what Manu

## \|\| 39.11 \|\|

*sarveṣāmevamāśaucaṃ mātāpitrostu sūtakam \|*  
*sūkataṃ māturevasyādupaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. In birth the relatives incur no impurity. Impurity attaches to parents alone. Primarily, it is the mother who becomes impure. Father is purified by the touch of water alone.

## \|\| 39.12 \|\|

*antardaśāhe syātāñcet punarmaraṇajanmanī \|*  
*tāvat syādaśucirvipro yāvat tat syādanirdaśam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. In birth or death impurity lasts for ten days, О bird.

## \|\| 39.13 \|\|

*udite niyame dāne ārte vipre nivedayet \|*  
*tathaiva ṛṣibhiḥ proktaṃ yathākālaṃ na duṣyati \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. By giving food to the hungry and the indigent, the parents get rid of impuritythe sages have declared.

## \|\| 39.14 \|\|

*mṛnmayena tu pātreṇa tilairmiśrajalaiḥ saha \|*  
*mṛttikayā tathānte ca naraḥ snātvā śucirbhavet \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Man is purified after bathing in water from the earthen jar mixed with gingelly seeds and clay from holy places,

## \|\| 39.15-16 \|\|

*dānaṃ pariṣade dadyāt suvarṇaṃ govṛṣaṃ dvije \|*  
*kṣattriyo dviguṇaṃ caiva vaiśyastu triguṇaṃ tathā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*caturguṇantu śūdreṇa dātavyaṃ brāhmaṇe dhanam \|*  
*eva manukrameṇaiva cāturvarṇyaṃ viśudhyati \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. He should gift some articles to the village assembly, gold, cow and bull to a brahmana. If ksatriya he should do the same twice, if vaisya thrice, if sudra four times. Wealth should be given to a Brahmin.

## \|\| 39.17 \|\|

*saptāṣṭamāntarai śīrṇe gṛhyasaṃskāravarjite \|*  
*ahastu sūtakaṃ tasya tvabdānāṃ saṃkhyayā smṛtam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. A person distanced by seven or eight generation or he who has not undergone a Sacrament ihcurs.no impurity.

## \|\| 39.18 \|\|

*brāhmaṇārthe vipannā ye nārīṇāṃ gograheṣu ca \|*  
*āhaveṣu vipannānāmekarāttramaśaucakam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. For men who have lost life for the sake of a Brahmin, a cow, a woman or in the battlefield, infirmity lasts only for a single night.

## \|\| 39.19-20 \|\|

*na teṣāmaśubhaṃ kiñcidviprāṇāṃ śubhakarmaṇi \|*  
*anāthapretasaṃskāraṃ ye kurvantu narottamaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*na teṣāmaśubhaṅkiñcidvipreṇa sahakāriṇā \|*  
*jalāvagāhanātteṣāṃ sadyaḥ śuddhirudāhṛtā \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Brahmins do not incur impurity if they are engaged in an auspicious rite. Those who arrange cremation, of an orphan child with the Brahmin assisting them in this act become purified as soon as they take bath.

## \|\| 39.21 \|\|

*vinivṛttā yadā śūdrā udakāntamupasthitāḥ /*

*tadāvipreṇadraṣṭavyā iti vedavidoviduḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. When Shudras, following a corpse, reach a pond or a stream or a reservoir, they should return home. While returning they should be glanced at by a Brahmin which is enough for their purification. This is what the Vedic scholars say.

## Chapter 40

## \|\| 40.1-2 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*bhagavanbrāhmaṇāḥ kecidapamṛtyuvaśaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*kathaṃ teṣāṃ bhavenmārgaḥ kiṃ sthānaṃ kā gatirbhavet \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kiñca yuktaṃ bhavetteṣāṃ vidhānaṃ vāpi kīdṛśam \|*  
*tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi brūhi me madhusūdana \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. О lord, I wish to hear this explained. Some brahmin have tragic deaths. How is the passage in regard to those? What is their place? What is their goal? what are the rules of observance in regard to them? Please tell me the aftereffects of such untimely unnatural deaths?

## \|\| 40.3 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*pretībhūtādvijātīnāṃ sambhūte mṛtyuvaikṛte \|*  
*teṣāṃ mārgagatisthānaṃ vidhānaṃ kathayāmyaham \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. О Garuda, I shall tell you in detail about their passage, rules of observance and place. It is a great secret- the rite in case of abnormal death.

## \|\| 40.4-12 \|\|

*śṛṇu tārkṣya paraṃ gopyaṃ jāte durmaraṇe sati \|*  
*laṅghanairye mṛtā viprā daṃṣṭribhiścābhighātitāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*kaṇṭhagrāha vimagnānāṃ kṣīṇānāṃ tuṇḍaghātinām \|*  
*viṣāgnivṛṣaviprebhyo viṣūcyā cātmaghātakāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*patanodvandhanajalairmṛtānāṃ śṛṇu saṃsthitim \|*  
*yānti te narakeghore ye ca mlecchādibhirhatāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*śvaśṛgālādisaṃspṛṣṭā adagdhāḥ kṛmisaṃṅkulāḥ \|*  
*ullaṅghitā mṛtā ye ca mahārogaiśca pīḍitāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*abhisastāstathāvyaṅgā ye ca pāpānnapoṣitāḥ \|*  
*caṇḍālādudakātsarpādbrāhmaṇādvaidyutāgnitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*daṣṭribhyaśca paśubhyaśca vṛkṣādipatanānmṛtāḥ \|*  
*udakyāmūtakīśūdrārajakīsaṅgadūṣitāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*tena pāpena narakānmuktāḥ pretatvabhāginaḥ \|*  
*na teṣāṃ kārayeddāhaṃ sūtakaṃ nodakakriyām \|\| 10 \|\|*

*na vidhānaṃ mṛtādyaṃ ca na kuryādaurdhvadaihikam \|*  
*teṣāṃ tārkṣya prakurvīta nārāyaṇabalikriyām \|\| 11 \|\|*

*sarvalokahitārthāya śṛṇu pāpabhayāpahām \|*  
*ṣaṇmāsaṃ brāhmaṇe dāhastrimāsaṃ kṣattriye mataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

4-12. There are people who are dead by fasts, killed by tanged animals, dead by strangu lation, who are slayers of preceptors killed by wolves, who die of arson or imprecations of Brahmins, who die of cholera, who commit suicide, who fall from a peak and die, who hang themselves to death, who are drowned in tank, river or ocean, — listen to their plight. These go to hell.

Those who are killed by the mlecchas and other infidels, who are defiled by dogs, jackals, etc., who are not cremated, who are full of germs, who die of leaping or great ailments or contact with foul women, or an attack by a low-born person, who die of water, of serpent-bite, who are struck by lightning, killed by fanged beasts, who die of falling from trees, who are defiled by women in menses and impurities who are sudras washermen and others, who are likely to fall into hell bycommitting sin or escaping itbecome ghostsfor such persons there is no rite of cremation, no water-libation, no rite of obsequy and no observance of impurity. For these people, О Garuda, the rite of Narayana ball should be performed. Now, for the benefit of the entire world, I shall narrate the procedure of this rite that dispels the fear of sins.

## \|\| 40.13 \|\|

*sārdha māsaṃ tu vaiśyasya sadyaḥ śūdre vidhīyate \|*  
*gaṅgāyāṃ yamunāyāñca naimiṣe puṣkare 'tha ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. For a Brahmin, the rite should be performed within six months; for a ksatriya within three months; for a vaigya within a month and a half and fora sudra immediately.

## \|\| 40.14-15 \|\|

*taḍāge jalūpūrṇe vā hrade vā vimalodake \|*  
*vāpyāṃ kūpe gavāṃ goṣṭhe gṛhe vā pratimālaye \|\| 14 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇāgre kārayedvipra baliṃ nārāyaṇāhvayam \|*  
*pretāya tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ mantraiḥ paurāṇavaidikaiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. The Narayana ball should be performed through Brahmins in Ganga, Yamuna, Naimisa and Puskara in a tank full of water, in an eddy of pure water, in a cow-shed, in a house or in a temple in front of Krsna's image.

## \|\| 40.16 \|\|

*sarvauṣadhyakṣatairmiśrairviṣṇumuddiśya tarpayet \|*  
*kāryaṃ puruṣasūktena mantrairvā vaiṣṇavairapi \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Water-libation should be performed with mantras from the Vedas and the Puranas. With the articles of worship lord Visnu should be pryopyitiated.

## \|\| 40.17 \|\|

*dakṣiṇābhimukho bhūtvā pretaṃ viṣṇuriti smaret \|*  
*anādinidhano devaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Rites accompanied by reciting the Purusa- sukta and Visnu mantras should be performed. Facing the south, the dead should be identified with lord Visnu.

## \|\| 40.18 \|\|

*avyayaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ pretamokṣaprado bhavet \|*  
*tarpaṇasyāvasāne ca vītarāgo vimatsaraḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The mantra runs thus! "May the lord Pundarikaksa who has no beginning, no end, who bears on his person a conch, a discus and a mace confer salvation to the dead.

## \|\| 40.19-20 \|\|

*jitendriyamanā bhūtvā śucipmāndharmatatparaḥ \|*  
*dānadharmarataḥ śāntaḥ praṇamya vāgyataḥ śuciḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*yajamāno bhabettatra śucirvandhusamanvitaḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā tatra prakurvīta śrāddhatyekādaśaiva tu \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. After offering water-libation, the householder who is not affected by passion and hatred, who is pure, restrained in senses, devoted to virtue and benevolence shall observe silence along with his kinsmen, О Garuda.

## \|\| 40.21 \|\|

*sarvakarmavipākena ekaikāgre samāhitaḥ \|*  
*toyavrīhiyavān ṣaṣṭyā godhūmāṃśca priyaṅgukān \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Then he shall perform eleven sraddhas with full concentration observing all rules.

## \|\| 40.22-23 \|\|

*haviṣyānnaṃ śubhaṃ mudrāṃ chatroṣṇīṣe ca calekam \|*  
*dāpayetsarvasasyāni kṣīrakṣaudrayutāni ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

*vastropānahasaṃyuktaṃ dadyādaṣṭavidhaṃ padam \|*  
*dāpayetsarvaviprebhyo na kuryātpaṅktibandhanam \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. He should make various gifts of water, grain, "wheat, Priyarigu, cooked rice soaked in ghi, silver coin, umbrella, turban, cloth, vegetables, milk, honey and sandals..

## \|\| 40.24 \|\|

*bhūmau sthiteṣu piṇḍeṣu gandhapuṣpākṣatānvitam \|*  
*śaṅkhapātre tathā tāmre tarpaṇañca pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Gifts should be made to all without depriving anyone of the line of those assembled. The rice-balls placed on the ground should be accompanied by scents, flowers and gains.

## \|\| 40.25 \|\|

*dhyānadhāraṇasaṃyukto jānubhyāmavaniṃ gataḥ \|*  
*dātavyaṃ sarvaviprebhyo vedaśāstravidhānataḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Gifts made to Brahmins should be in accordance with the Vedic and sastric injunctions. Water-libation should be offered separately through a conch or a copper vessel.

## \|\| 40.26-27 \|\|

*ṛcā vai dāpayedarghyamekoddiṣṭe pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*āpodevīrmadhumatīrādipīṭhe prakalpitam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*upayāmagṛhīto 'si dvitīyer'ghaṃ nivedayet \|*  
*yenāpāvakacakṣuṣā tṛtīye ca kasalpitam \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The performer of rites should hold breath and kneel on the ground. He should first give the arghya and then perform ekoddista. The first rice-ball should be offered with the mantras Apo daiv, madhumati and the second with the mantra Upayamagrhitosi.

## \|\| 40.28 \|\|

*ye devāsaścaturthe tu samudraṃ gaccha pañcame \|*  
*agnirjyoti stathā ṣaṣṭhe hiraṇyagarbhaḥ saptame \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The third rice-ball is given with the mantra yena Pavaka caksasa the fourth with the mantra ye devasah, and the fifth with the mantra samudram gaccha.

## \|\| 40.29 \|\|

*yamāya tvāṣṭame jñeyaṃ yajjagrannavame tathā \|*  
*taśame yāḥ phalinīti piṇḍe caikādaśe tataḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The mantra agnirjyoti is for the sixth, hiranyagarbhah for the seventh, Yamaya for the eighth and yajjagrat for the ninth.

## \|\| 40.30 \|\|

*bhadraṃ karṇebhiriti ca kuryātpiṇḍavisarjanam \|*  
*kṛtvaikādaśadevatyaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryātpare 'hani \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The mantra yah phalinih is for the tenth and bhadram kanebhih for the eleventh. Thus all the rice-balls should be offered. After this, he shall perform the sraddha for eleven deities on the next day.

## \|\| 40.31 \|\|

*viprānāvāhayetpañca caturvedaviśāradān \|*  
*vidyāśīlaguṇopetānsva kīyāñchīlasattamān \|*  
*abyaṅgānsapraśastāṃśca na t varjyānkadācana \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Five Brahmins should be invited and arghya given to them. They should be learned, of good conduct, most excellent in their family, free from physical decrepitude and noble. Never should they be of the condemnable type,

## \|\| 40.32 \|\|

*viṣṇuḥ svarṇamayaḥ kāryo rudastāmramayastathā \|*  
*brahmā rūpyamayastadvadyamo lohamayo bhavet \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The image of Visnu should be made of gold, that of Rudra of copper, that of Brahma of silver and that of Yama of iron,

## \|\| 40.33 \|\|

*sīsakaṃ tu bhavetpretaṃ tvatha vā darbhakaṃ tathā \|*  
*śannodevīti mantreṇa govindaṃ paścime nyaset \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The effigy of the dead should be made of lead or Darbha grass. Nyasa of Samavedin should be conducted with the mantra Yamayatva.

## \|\| 40.34-35 \|\|

*agna āyāhīti rudramuttaratraiva vinyaset \|*  
*agnimīḷeti mantreṇa pūrveṇaiva prajāpatim \|\| 34 \|\|*

*iṣetvorjoti mantreṇa dakṣiṇe sthāpayedyamam \|*  
*madhye maṇḍalakaṃ kṛtvā sthāpyo darbhamayo naraḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Lord Govinda should be placed in the west with the mantra; agna a yahi, Prajapati in the east with the mantra agnim He; Yama in the south with the mantra ise tva and in the middle a mystic diagram should be drawn on the ground where thebody of darbha grass

## \|\| 40.36 \|\|

*brahmā viṣṇustathā rudro yamaḥ pretaśca pañcamaḥ \|*  
*pṛthakkumbhe tataḥ sthāpyāḥ pañcaratnasamanvitāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. In five vessels containing five gems there be invoked Brahma, Visnu, Rudra, Yama and the dead person.

## \|\| 40.37 \|\|

*vastrayajñopavītāni pṛthaṅmudrāparāṇi ca \|*  
*japaṃ karyātpṛthaktatra brahmādau devatāsu ca \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The cloth, the sacred thread and the coins should be separate for each deity. The mantras too are separate for each deity.

## \|\| 40.38 \|\|

*pañca śrāddhāni kurvīta devatānāṃ yathāvidhi \|*  
*jaladhārāṃ tato dadyatpīṭhepīṭhe pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Five sraddhas are performed separately for the deities with due observance of rules, and water is poured over the pindas separately.

## \|\| 40.39-41 \|\|

*śaṅkhe vā tāmrapātre vā alābhe mṛnmaye 'pi vā \|*  
*tilodakaṃ samādāya sarvauṣadhisamanvitam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*āsanopānahau cchatraṃ mudrikā ca kamaṇḍaluḥ \|*  
*bhājanaṃ bhājanādhāraṃ vastrāṇyaṣṭavidhaṃ padam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*tāmrapātraṃ tilaiḥ pūrṇaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ sadakṣiṇam \|*  
*dadyādbrāhmaṇamukhyāya vidhiyuktaṃ khageśvara \|\| 41 \|\|*

39-41. Water with gingelly seeds shall be taken in a conch or a copper vessel or if it is not available in an earthen vessel along with the articles of worship. О lord of birds, the following articles should be gifted to a pious Brahmin-seat, sandal, umbrella, coins, water pot, vessel, foodstuffs and grains, thus constituting the eight padas as well as a copper vessel with gingelly seeds along with gold and compatible daksinas.

## \|\| 40.42-43 \|\|

*ṛgvedapārage dadyājjātasasyāṃ vasundharām \|*  
*yajurvedamaye vipre gāñca dadyātpayasvinīm \|\| 42 \|\|*

*sāmagāya śivoddeśātpradadyātkaladhautakam \|*  
*yamoddeśāttilāṃllohaṃ tato dadyācca dakṣiṇām \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. Fields with grain plants already grown should be gifted to a Rgvedin, a milch cow to a Yajurvedin, a new cloth to a Samavedin with Lord Siva in view, and similarly gingelly seeds and iron with Yama in view. The sacrificial fee should be paid to the officiating priests.

## \|\| 40.44 \|\|

*paścātputtalakaṃ kāryaṃ sarvauṣadhisamanvitam \|*  
*palāśasya ca vṛntānāṃ vibhāgaṃ śṛṇu kāśyapa \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. An effigy should be made with the medicinal herbs. О Kasyapa, palasa leaves and bunches should be split into several pieces.

## \|\| 40.45 \|\|

*kṛṣṇājinaṃ samāstīrya kuśaiśca puruṣākṛtim \|*  
*śatatrayeṇa ṣaṣṭyā ca vṛntaiḥ prokto 'sthisañcayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The hide of a blackdeer shouldbe spread and the effigy of kusa grass placed over it. Three hundred and sixty blades of kusa grass representing the number of bones in the body should be used

## \|\| 40.46 \|\|

*vinyasya tāni vṛntāni aṅgeṣveṣu pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*catvāriṃśacchirodeśe grīvāyāṃ daśa vinyaset \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The blades of kusa grass should be tied well to constitute the different limbs. Forty blades of kusa grass at the head and ten at the neck.

## \|\| 40.47 \|\|

*viṃśatyuraḥ sthale dadyādviṃśatiṃ jaṭhare 'pi ca \|*  
*bāhuyugme śataṃ dadyātkaṭi deśe ca viṃśatim \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Twenty in the region of the heart, twenty in the belly, hundred in the thighs and twenty at the hips.

## \|\| 40.48 \|\|

*ūrudvaye śatañcāpi triṃśajjaṅghādvaye nyaset \|*  
*dadyāccatuṣṭayaṃ śiśre ṣaḍ dadyād vṛṣaṇadvaye \|*  
*daśa pādāṅgulībhāge evamasthīni vinyaset \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Four for the penis, six for the testicles, ten for the toes. This is the procedure to' represent the bones.

## \|\| 40.49 \|\|

*nārikelaṃ śiraḥ sthāne tumbaṃ dadyācca tāluke \|*  
*pañcaratnaṃ mukhe dadyājjihvāyāṃ kadalīphalam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. A coconut is placed at the head; a silver piece in the palate; five gems in the mouth and a plantain fruit in the tongue.

## \|\| 40.50 \|\|

*antreṣu nālakaṃ dadyādbālakaṃ prāṇa eva ca \|*  
*vasāyīṃ medakaṃ dadyādgomūtreṇa tu mūtrakam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Sands represent the entrails; saffron is placed at the nose; clay represents the fat and cow's urine the urine.

## \|\| 40.51 \|\|

*gandhakaṃ dhātavo deyo haritālaṃ manaḥ śilā \|*  
*retaḥ sthāne pāradañca purīṣe pittalaṃ tathā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Sulphur represents the Dhatus (the principal constituents of the body). Haritala, Manahsila and fried barley flour represent flesh and honey represents the blood.

## \|\| 40.52 \|\|

*manaḥ śilāṃ tathā gātre tilakalkañca sandhiṣu \|*  
*yavapiṣṭaṃ tathā māṃse madhu vai kṣaudrameva ca \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. A clump of twigs and leaves represents the matted hair, hide of deer represents the skin, mercury represents semen and brass pieces the faeces.

## \|\| 40.53 \|\|

*keśeṣu vai vaṭajaṭātvaci dadyānmṛgatvacam \|*  
*karṇayostālapattrañca stanayoścaiva guñjikāḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Manahsila is scattered over the body, gingelly cakes on the joints, palm leaves on the ears and two Gunjas (berries) on the nipples.

## \|\| 40.54 \|\|

*nāsāyāṃ śatapattraṃ ca kamalaṃ nābhimaṇḍale \|*  
*vṛntākaṃ vṛṣaṇadvandve liṅge syādgṛñjanaṃ śubham \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Lotus petals are placed on the nose and the umbilical region; brinjal on the testicles and red garlic on the penis.

## \|\| 40.55 \|\|

*ghṛtaṃ nābhyāṃ pradeyaṃ syātkaupīne ca trapu smṛtam \|*  
*mauktikaṃ stanayormūrdhni kuṅkumena vilepanam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. GhI should be applied over the navel. Lac represents the loin cloth. Pearls are placed at the breast and saffron over the head.

## \|\| 40.56 \|\|

*karpūrāgurudhūpaiśca śubhairmālyaiḥ sugandhibhiḥ \|*  
*paridhānaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃ hṛdaye rukmakaṃ nyaset \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Compher, incense, aguru, garlands are used to decorate the effigy. It is clothed with silken cloth. A gold piece is placed over the chest.

## \|\| 40.57 \|\|

*ṛddhivṛddhī bhujau dvau ca cakṣuṣośca kapardakau \|*  
*sindūraṃ netrakoṇe ca tāmbūlādyupahārakaiḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Riddhi (success) and Vrddhi (prosperity) represent the arms, two chowrie pieces the eyes; red lead is applied to the corners of the eyes. Betal leaves are offered (to the mouth).

## \|\| 40.58 \|\|

*sarvauṣadhiyutaṃ pretaṃ kṛtvā pūjā yathoditā \|*  
*sāgnike (kaiścā) cāpi vidhinā yajñapātraṃ nyasetkramāt \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Thus, the worship of the dead is performed with different medicinal herbs. Due rites are performed in fire and sacrificial vessels are kept around.

## \|\| 40.59 \|\|

*śirome śrīriti ṛcā punantu varuṇeti ca \|*  
*pretasya pāvanaṃ kṛtvā śāliśālaśilodakaiḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The dead body is sanctified by the mantras sanno devl, punantu ma, imam me Varuna and by the water from the salagrama stone.

## \|\| 40.60 \|\|

*viṣṇumuddiśya dātavyā suśīlāgauḥ payasvinī \|*  
*tilā lohaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca kārpāsaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. A good milch cow and a vessel full of gingelly seeds is gifted with Visnu in view.

## \|\| 40.61 \|\|

*saptadhānyaṃ kṣitirgāva ekaikaṃ pānaṃ samṛtam \|*  
*tilapātraṃ tato dattvā padadānaṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Gingelly seeds, iron, gold, cotton, salt, cow- each of them is considered to be pious.

## \|\| 40.62 \|\|

*mahādānāni deyāni tilapātraṃ tatheti ca \|*  
*tato vaitaraṇī deyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Then, VaitaranI decorated with ornaments should be given accompanied by a sraddha.

## \|\| 40.63 \|\|

*kartavyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ śrāddhaṃ pretamuktyartha mātmavān \|*  
*pretaṃmokṣaṃ tataḥ kuryāddhṛdi viṣṇuṃ prakalpya ca \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Then rites for the release of the ghost should be performed with Visnu in view.

## \|\| 40.64 \|\|

*oṃ viṣṇuriti saṃsmṛtya pretaṃ tanmṛtyumeva ca \|*  
*agnidāhaṃ tataḥ kuryātsūtakantu dinatrayam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The Preta is mystically discharged. The corpse or the effigy is cremated while meditating upon Visnu

## \|\| 40.65 \|\|

*daśāhakartrā piṇḍāśca kartavyāḥ pretabhuktaye \|*  
*sarvaṃ varṣavidhiṃ kuryādevaṃ pretaśca muktibhāk \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. (On the cremation of effigy) three days' impurity is observed; Otherwise, impurity lasts for ten days while a rice -ball is offered on each of the ten days. All other rites are performed for a year. Thereby the dead man attains salvation.

## Chapter 41

## \|\| 41.1 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*vṛṣotsargaṃ prakurvīta vidhipūrvaṃ khageśvara \|*  
*kārtikādiṣu māseṣu paurṇamāsyāṃ śubhe dine \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. O lord of birds, the rite of releasing the bull (Vrsotsarga) should be done, as prescribed in the month Karttika or on the full-moon day (purnima) or on other auspicious days.

## \|\| 41.2 \|\|

*vivāhotsarjanaṃ śrāddhaṃ nāndīmukhamupakramet \|*  
*kuryādbhuvaśca saṃskārānagnisthāpanameva ca \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. One should get married, release the bull, perform Nandlmukha and establish fire.

## \|\| 41.3 \|\|

*vāpyāṃ kūpe gavāṃ goṣṭhe sthāpyāgniṃ vidhivattataḥ \|*  
*vivāhavidhinā sarvaṃ kuryādbrāhmaṇavācanam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Fire should be established beside the water reservoir, well, cowshed and everything should be done according to marriage rites such as the recitation of the mantras by the Brahmins.

## \|\| 41.4 \|\|

*pātrāsādanaṃ śrapaṇamupayamanakuśādikam \|*  
*puryukṣaṇānte homaṃ ca karyā dvai brāhmaṇena tu \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He should do patrasadnna, srapana, upanayana, etc. At the end of paryuksana, the Brahmin should do home.

## \|\| 41.5-6 \|\|

*āghārāvājyabhāgau ca cakṣuṣī ca pradā payet \|*  
*prathame 'hariti mantreṇa hotavyāśca ṣaḍāhutīḥ (tayaḥ) \|\| 5 \|\|*

*āghārāvājyabhāgau tu pāyasenāṅgadevatāḥ \|*  
*agnaye rudrāya śarvāya paśupataye ugrāya śivāya \|*  
*bhavāya mahādevāyeśānāya yamāya ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Six offerings should be made with the mantra prathama ahar to Agni, Rudra, Sharva, Pasupati, Shiva, Bhava, Mahadeva, Isana, Yama,

## \|\| 41.7 \|\|

*piṣṭakena sakṛddhomaṃ pūṣāgā iti mantrataḥ \|*  
*ubhayoḥ sviṣṭikūddhomaścaruṇā pāyasena ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Once, homa should be made with pistaka by reciting the mantra Pusa ga. Нота should be made both with care and Payasa.

## \|\| 41.8-9 \|\|

*prathamaṃ vyāhṛtihomaḥ prāyaścittaṃ prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*saṃstravaprāśanaṃ kuryātpraṇītāparimokṣaṇam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*pavitrapratipattiśca brāhmaṇe dakṣaiṇā tataḥ \|*  
*ṣaḍaṅgarudrajāpyena proto mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. First vyahrtl-homa. should be made, then Prayascitta, Samsrava-prasana, Pranita, I parimoksana and Pavitra-pratipatti. Daksina should be given to the Brahmins officiating a the sacrifice. By reciting sadanga mantras dedicated to Audra, the ghost obtains release

## \|\| 41.10 \|\|

*ekavarṇaṃ vṛṣañcaiva sakṛdvatsatarīṃ khaga \|*  
*snāpayitvā tataḥ kuryātsarvālaṅkārabhūṣitam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Bull of one colour and a calf should be bathed and decorated with ornaments.

## \|\| 41.11 \|\|

*pratiṣṭhāpya ca tadyugmaṃ preto mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*puccheca tarpaṇaṃ kāryamucchrite mantrapūrvakam \|*  
*brāhmaṇān bhojayetpaścāddakṣiṇābhiśca toṣayet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. By releasing the twins the ghost obtains release. Then water-libation should be made by reciting the mantras. The Brahmins should be feasted and gratified with the sacrificial fee.

## \|\| 41.12 \|\|

*tataḥ śrāddhaṃ samuddiṣṭamekoddiṣṭaṃ yathāvidhi \|*  
*jalamannaṃ tathā deyaṃ pretoddharaṇahetave \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Then ekoddista should be performed accompanied by water libation and gifts of food.

## \|\| 41.13 \|\|

*dvādaśāhe tataḥ kuryānmāsemāse pṛthakpathak \|*  
*evaṃ vidhiḥ samāyuktaḥ pretamokṣe karoti hi \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. This should be done on the twelfth day and in each month separately. The prescription relates to the release of the ghost.

## Chapter 42

## \|\| 42.1 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*yathā dhenusahasreṣu vatso vindati mātaram \|*  
*tathā pūrvakṛtaṃ karma kartāramanugacchati \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. As a calf can trace its mother cow among a thousand cows so also the actions done in previous births can follow the doer.

## \|\| 42.2 \|\|

*ādityo varuṇo viṣṇurbrahmā somo hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*śūlapāṇiśca bhagavānabhinandati bhūmidam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The sun, Varuna, Visnu, Brahma, moon, fire and lord Shiva appreciate the person who gifts plots of land.

## \|\| 42.3 \|\|

*nāsti bhamisamaṃ dānaṃ nāsti bhamisamo nidhiḥ \|*  
*nāsti satyasamo dharmo nānṛtātpātakaṃ param \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. There is no gift equal to the gift of land, no treasure equal to landed property, no virtue equal to truthfulness and no sin equal to falsehood.

## \|\| 42.4 \|\|

*agnerapatyaṃ prathamaṃ suvarṇaṃ bhūrvaiṣṇavī sūryasutāśca gāvaḥ \|*  
*lokatrayaṃ tena bhavetpradattaṃ yaḥ kāñcanaṃ gāṃ ca mahīṃ ca dadyāt \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Gold is the first offspring of fire, land of Visnu, and cow of the sun. He who gifts gold, cow and land actually makes a gift of three worlds

## \|\| 42.5 \|\|

*trīṇyāhuratidānāni gāvaḥ pṛthvī sarasvatī \|*  
*narakāduddharantyete japapūjanahomataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He who gifts knowledge, land and cow is blessed. Reciting Epics and the Puranas, cultivating seeds in the fertile land and milking the cow save people from the distress of hell.

## \|\| 42.6 \|\|

*kṛtvā bahūni pāpāni raudrāṇi vipulāni ca \|*  
*api gocarmamātreṇa bhūmidānena śudhyati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Even persons committing heinous sins are purified merely by making gift of a cow or a plot of land.

## \|\| 42.7 \|\|

*harantamapi lobhena nirudhyainaṃ nivārayet \|*  
*sa yāti narake ghore yastaṃ na parirakṣati \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. If someone seizes cows or land unlawfully due to greed he should be stopped. He goes to hell who does not protect these.

## \|\| 42.8 \|\|

*akartavyaṃ na kartavyaṃ prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatairapi \|*  
*kartavyameva kartavyamiti dharmavido viduḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Even when the vital airs stick to the throat (when death is imminent) actions unworthy of performance should not be carried out; only worthy actions should be followed. This is known to Vedic scholars who have declared thus

## \|\| 42.9 \|\|

*ākārapravartane pāpaṃ gosahasravadhaiḥsamam \|*  
*vṛtticchede tathā vṛtteḥ karaṇaṃ lakṣadhenukam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. There is as much of sin in seizing cows or land as that which results from slaughtering a thousand cows, in doing an evil action or in depriving someone of his lovelihood. In. helping someone to secure the means of livelihood, the benefit that accrues is equal to that of the gift of a hundred thousand cows.

## \|\| 42.10 \|\|

*varamekāpyapahṛtā na tu dattaṃ gavāṃ śatam \|*  
*ekāṃ hṛtvā śataṃ dattvā na tena samatā bhavet \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It is better to give away a cow once for all rather than give away a hundred cows and seize one. It one seizes a cow he cannot expiate for that sin by even giving a hundred cows.

## \|\| 42.11 \|\|

*svayameva tu yo dattvā svayameva prabādhate \|*  
*sa pāpī narakaṃ yāti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. If a person voluntarily gifts something and wontonly seizes the same he becomes a great sinner. He goes to hell "where he stays till the universe is dissolved in Deluge.

## \|\| 42.12 \|\|

*na cāśvamedhena tathā vidhivaddakṣiṇāvatā \|*  
*avṛttikarśite dīne brāhmaṇe gakṣite yathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The merit that accrues to one in offering protection to a poor Brahmin who is emaciated due to unemployment cannot be surpassed in sacredness even by performing a horse sacrifice accompanied by all sacrificial fees.

## \|\| 42.13 \|\|

*na tadbhavati vedeṣu yajñe subahudakṣiṇe \|*  
*yatpuṇyaṃ durbale traste brāhmaṇe parirakṣite \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The merit one secures by guarding the Vedas and performing sacrifices accompanied by sacrificial gifts cannot equal the merit of protecting a weak Brahmin.

## \|\| 42.14 \|\|

*brahmasvaiścasupuṣṭāni vāhanāni balāni ca \|*  
*yuddhakāle viśīryante saikatāḥ setavo yathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The Vehicles and forces maintained with the wealth misappropriated from the Brahmins break down at the time of battle like bonds made of sand.

## \|\| 42.15 \|\|

*svadattāṃ paradattāṃ vā yo harecca vasundharām \|*  
*ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. If one seizes land gifted by another he is reborn as a worm in faeces where he remains for sixty thousand years.

## \|\| 42.16 \|\|

*brahmasvaṃ praṇayādbhuktaṃ dahatyāsaptamaṃ kulam \|*  
*tadeva cauryarūpeṇa dahatyācandratārakam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The brahmanical asset enjoyed by the deception of affection burns the entire family till the seventh generation. The same if enjoyed stealthily burns the race so long as the moon and the stars shine in the sky.

## \|\| 42.17 \|\|

*lohacūrṇāśmacūrṇāni kadācijjarayetpumān \|*  
*brahmasvantriṣu lokeṣu kaḥ pumāñjarayiṣyati \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Clever persons may be there who can digest powdered iron or stone or even poison. But is there a man in the world who can digest the property of a Brahmin?

## \|\| 42.18 \|\|

*devadravyavināśena brahmasvaharaṇena ca \|*  
*kulānyakulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. By destroying the wealth of gods (temples), by seizing a Brahmin's wealth or by dishonouring and slighting a Brahmin a family falls in esteem.

## \|\| 42.19 \|\|

*brāhmaṇāti kramo nāsti vipre vidyāvivarjite \|*  
*jvalantamagnimutsṛjya na hi bhasmani hūyate \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. There is no question of dishonouring a Brahmin though devoid of learning. No one performs homy in ashes instead of in blazing fire.

## \|\| 42.20 \|\|

*saṃkrāntau yāni dānāni havyakavyāni yāni ca \|*  
*saptakalpakṣayaṃ yāvaddadātyarkaḥ punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Gifts made during the transit of the sim from one sign of Zodiac to the other, Havyas and Kavyas offered at that time enable one to be honoured in heaven for seven Kalpas.

## \|\| 42.21 \|\|

*pratigrahādhyāpanayājaneṣu pratigrahaṃ sveṣṭatamaṃ vadanti \|*  
*pratigrahācchrudhyati jāpyahomaṃ na yājanaṃ karma punanti vedāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Out of the three-acceptance of monetary gifts, imparting knowledge to the I seeker and presiding over another man's sacrifice, acceptance of monetary gifts is the I best. The sin, if any, in the acceptance of monetary gifts is removed by Japan and homas but even the Vedas do not sanctify a person who presides over another man's sacrifice but himself does not perform any sacrifice.

## \|\| 42.22 \|\|

*sadā jāpī sadā homī parapākavivarjitaḥ \|*  
*ratnapūrṇāmapi mahīṃ pratigṛhṇanna lipyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. A person who performs japas and homas and abstains from accepting cooked food from others is not tarnished by any sin even if he accepts the gift of the whole earth, full, of precious jewels.

## Chapter 43

## \|\| 43.1 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*jalāgnibandhanabhraṣṭā pravrajyānāśakacyutāḥ \|*  
*aindavābhyāṃ viśudhyanti dattvā dhenuṃ tathā vṛṣam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Those who voilet rules for the observance of rites in water and fire, those who break wows of renuciation and fast should make gifts of cow or bull for the purity of sense-organs.

## \|\| 43.2 \|\|

*ūnadvādaśavarṣasya caturvarṣādhikasya ca \|*  
*prāyaścittaṃ carenmātā pitā vānyo 'pi bāndhavaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Either the mother or a kinsmen can perform the expiratory rite on behalf of a boy less than twelve but above four.

## \|\| 43.3 \|\|

*ato bālanarasyāpi nāparādho na pātakam \|*  
*rājadaṇḍo na tasyāsti prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Boys of less than four years in age can never be guilty or sinful. Even the king cannot punish them. There is no expiatory rite prescribed for such boys, in the sastras.

## \|\| 43.4 \|\|

*raktasya darśane daṣṭe āturā strī bhaved yadi \|*  
*caturthe 'hni padādīṃśca tyaktvā snātvā viśudhyati \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. If a woman falls sick after the menstrual blood has come out let her discard her cloth on the fourth day and touch the ha vis. She becomes pure thereby.

## \|\| 43.5 \|\|

*āture snāna utpanne daśakṛtvo hyanāturaḥ \|*  
*snātvāsnātvāspṛśedenaṃ tataḥ śudhyet sa āturaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Sometimes, a person is ill and the necessity arises for an ablution. In the circumstances, a healthy man should take ablution ten times. He should touch the sick man after every bath- Thereby, the sickman is purified even without ablution.

## Chapter 44

## \|\| 44.1-3 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*svecchayā tārkṣya maraṇaṃ śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrisarīsṛpaḥ \|*  
*cāṇḍālādyātmaghātaiśca viṣādyaistāḍanaistathā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*jalāgnipātavātaiśca nirāhārādibhistathā \|*  
*yeṣāmeva bhavenmṛtyuḥ proktāste pāpakarmiṇaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*pāṣaḍyanāśramāścaiva mahāpātakinastathā \|*  
*striyaśca vyabhicāriṇya ārūḍhapatitāstathā \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. О bird, now listen. Those who die of their will, or through homed animals, toothed animals, reptiles, low caste people (Candalas), suicide, poison, beating, water, fire, air, hunger are counted among great sinners. So also the women of bad character.

## \|\| 44.4 \|\|

*na teṣāṃ syānnava śrāddhaṃ na saṃskāraḥ sapiṇḍanam \|*  
*śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśoktāni na bhavanti ca tānyapi \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Such sinners do not deserve navas sraddha or cremation or sapindiana or sixteen Sraddha.

## \|\| 44.5 \|\|

*vetanaṃ yat kṣipedapsu gṛhyāgniśca catuṣpathe \|*  
*pātrāṇinirdahedagnau sāgnike pāpakarmaṇi \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Just as money is thrown in water, or sacred fire on the cross-roads, similarly, rites performed for the sinner bear no fruit at all.

## \|\| 44.6-8 \|\|

*pūrṇe saṃvatsare teṣāmitthaṃ kāryaṃ dayālubhiḥ \|*  
*ekādaśīṃ samāsādya śuklapakṣe ca kāśyapa \|\| 6 \|\|*

*viṣṇuṃ yamaṃ ca sampūjya gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ \|*  
*daśa piṇḍān ghṛtāktāṃśca darbheṣu madhusaṃyutān \|\| 7 \|\|*

*yajñopavīti satilān dhyāyan viṣṇuṃ yamaṃ tathā \|*  
*dakṣiṇābhimukhastūṣṇīmekaikaṃ nirvapet tutān \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. However, when the year is complete, the affectionate descendents should do the following: They should worship lord Visnu and Yama on the eleventh day of the bright half of the month with incense, flowers, uncooked rice and offer the rice-balls soaked in ghi and mixed with honey and gingelly seeds. This all the performer should do in silence, facing the south, putting the sacred thread on and meditating on lord Visnu and Yama.

## \|\| 44.9 \|\|

*uddhṛtya miśritān paścāt tīrthe 'bhmaḥ su viniḥ kṣipet \|*  
*kṣipan saṃkīrtayennāma gotraṃ ca mṛtakasya ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Then taking the articles of worship together he should throw them into the water, muttering all the while the personal name and the surname of the dead.

## \|\| 44.10 \|\|

*punarapyarcayedviṣṇuṃ yamaṃ kusumacandanaiḥ \|*  
*dhūpadīpaiḥ sanaivedyairbhakṣyabhojyasamanvitaiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Then again, he should worship lord Visnu pd Yama with sandal paste, flowers, incense, lamp and eatables.

## \|\| 44.11 \|\|

*tasminnupavasedahni viprāṃśceva nimantrayet \|*  
*kulavidyātapoyuktān sādhuśīlasamanvitān \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He should keep fast on that day and invite the brahmanas of noble families, of good character, learned and austere.

## \|\| 44.12 \|\|

*nava saptāthavā pañca svasāmarthyānusārataḥ \|*  
*apare 'hani madhyāhne yamaṃ viṣṇuṃ tathārcayet \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. They may be nine or seven or five, according to his ability. Next day, at noon, Visnu and Yama, should be worshipped.

## \|\| 44.13 \|\|

*udaṅmukhāṃstathā viprāṃstān samyagupaveśayet \|*  
*āvāhanārghadānādau viṣṇuṃ yamasamanvitam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The Brahmins should be seated facing the north. Lord Visnu and Yama should be invoked and worshipped.

## \|\| 44.14 \|\|

*yajñopavītī kurvīta pretanāma prakīrtayet \|*  
*pretaṃ yamaṃ ca viṣṇuṃ ca smaran śrāddhaṃ samāpayet \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The performer of the rite should keep wearing the sacred thread to the right. He should name the dead and think upon him, lord Visnu and Yama and complete the rite.

## \|\| 44.15 \|\|

*anyebhyaścāpi sarvebhyaḥ piṇḍadānārthamuddharet \|*  
*pṛthagvā daśa piṇḍāṃśca pañca dadyāt krameṇa tu \|\| 15 \|\|*

15Не should remember his other ancestors too and offer Finds to each separately or all together. Ten or five pindas, as prescribed, should be offered.

## \|\| 44.16 \|\|

*prathamaṃ viṣṇave dadyādbrahmaṇe ca śivāya ca \|*  
*sabhṛtyāya śivāyātha pretāyāpi ca pañcamam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. First, he should offer a Finds to Visnu, then to Brahma, diva, his attendents and then the fifth to the dead.

## \|\| 44.17 \|\|

*nāma gotraṃ smaret tasya viṣṇuśabdaṃ prakīrtayet \|*  
*namaskāraśiraskantu pañcamaṃ piṇḍamuddharet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. While offering a pinda he should utter the name and surname of the dead and the name of lord Visnu. Bowing with head, he should give the fifth Finds to the departed soul.

## \|\| 44.18 \|\|

*gobhūmipiṇḍadānādyaiḥ śaktyā pretaṃ smaraṃśca tam \|*  
*tilaistilāṃstu viprāṇāṃ darbhayukteṣu pāṇiṣu \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Remembering the dead he should give, according to-his ability, a cow, a plot of land, articles of food, gingelly seeds to the brahmanas with darbha grass in hands

## \|\| 44.19 \|\|

*dadyādannaṃ dvijānāṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ tathā \|*  
*evaṃ śiṣṭatamaṃ vipraṃ hariṇyena prapūjayet \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. So also coins, betel and corn should be given to the brahmanas. The headman among the brahmanas should be honoured with gold.

## \|\| 44.20 \|\|

*nāma gotraṃ smaran dadyādviṣṇuprītostviti bruvan \|*  
*anuvrajya dvijān paścāt tyaktāmbho dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Taking the personal name and the surname of the dead the performer should gift the articles with the formula: May Visnu be pleased. While the brahmanas are leaving he should follow them with his face to the, south and throw water over the earth.

## \|\| 44.21 \|\|

*kīrtayannāmagotre tu bhuvi prītostviti kṣipet \|*  
*mitrabandhujanaiḥ sārdhaṃ śeṣaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ \|*  
*pratisaṃvatsarādi syādekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. White he throws water over the earth, he should mention the name and surname of the dead and pronounce: 'May the departed soul be pleased'. Then lie should eat together with his friends and relatives, in perfect silence. The procedure should be repeated every year on the anniversary of the dead.

## \|\| 44.22 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛte gamiṣyanti svarlokaṃ pāpakarmiṇaḥ \|*  
*sapiṇḍīkaraṇādau tu kṛte caivāpnuvantite \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. When all this has been done, the sinners go to heaven. So also when sapindlkarana has been performed.

## \|\| 44.23 \|\|

*atha kaścit pramādena mriyate hyudakādibhiḥ \|*  
*saṃsārapramukhastasya sarvaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. If some one dies through water or by any other accident caused by inadvertence (and if he has no descendent to perform his obsequies) the king should perform the same as prescribed in the sastras.

## \|\| 44.24 \|\|

*pramādādicchayā martyo na gacchetsarpasaṃmukhaḥ \|*  
*pakṣayorubhayornāgaṃ pañcamīṣu prapūjayet \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. A man should not approach a serpent, willingly or unwillingly. In each fortnight of the month he should worship a naga on the fifth day.

## \|\| 44.25 \|\|

*kuryāt piṣṭamayīṃ lekhāṃ nāgānāmākṛtiṃ bhuvi \|*  
*arcayet tāṃ sitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sugandhaiścandanenaca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. A replica of the naga should be made of clay and worshipped with white flowers and scented sandal.

## \|\| 44.26 \|\|

*pradadyāddhūpadīpantu taṇḍulāṃśca sitān kṣipet \|*  
*āmapiṣṭaṃ tathaivānnaṃ kṣīrañca vinivedayet \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. One should offer incense and a lamp and tlirow white rice-grams. So also corn with mango-juice and milk.

## \|\| 44.27 \|\|

*upasthāya vadedevaṃ muñcan mudrāṃśukāni ca \|*  
*madhuraṃ taddine 'śrīyāddevaśrāddhaṃ samāpayet \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Similarly, money and clothes should be given. One should eat only sweets on that day and perform a deva-sraddha.

## \|\| 44.28 \|\|

*sauvarṇaṃ śaktito nāgaṃ tato dadyāddvijottame \|*  
*dhenuṃ dattvā tato brūyāt prīyatāṃ nāgarāḍiti \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Then according to his ability he should offer an idol of snake made of gold to the best of Brahmins. Then after giving a cow

## \|\| 44.29 \|\|

*yathāvibhavyaṃ kurvīta karmāṇyanyāni pūrvavat \|*  
*svaśākhoktavidhānena itthaṃ kuryādyathātatham \|*  
*pretatvānmocayet tāṃstu svargamārgaṃ nayet ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. According to his means he should perform other rites too. All this should be done as prescribed in his own branch of the Vedas. Thus, he can effect the release of his ancest:\>rs from ghosthood and carry them forward on the path to heaven.

## Chapter 45

## \|\| 45.1 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*pratyabdaṃ śrāddhamevaṃ te kathayāmi khageśvara \|*  
*pratyabdaṃ pārvaṇenaiva kuryātāṃ kṣetrajorasau \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. О Foremost among birds, I shall now tell you the mode of annual Sraddha. Either the ksetraja or the aurasa son should perform the annual sraddha in the manner as he performs the parvana sraddha.

## \|\| 45.2-3 \|\|

*vidhinācetarairevamekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*anagneśca sutau syātāmanagnī kṣetrajorasau \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭaṃ na kuryātāṃ pratyabdaṃ tau tu pārvaṇam \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. The other sons should perform ekoddista and not parvana. If the father or the ksetraja and aurasa sons do not maintain fire, they should not perform ekoddista but should do parvana every year or they can perform ekoddista also.

## \|\| 45.4-7 \|\|

*yadā tvanyataraḥ sāgniḥ putro vāpyathavā pitā \|*  
*pratyabdaṃ pārvaṇaṃ tatra kuryātāṃ kṣetrajaurasau \|\| 4 \|\|*

*anagnayaḥ sāgnayo vā putrā vā pitaro 'pi vā \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭaṃ sutaiḥ kāryaṃ kṣayāha iti kecana \|\| 5 \|\|*

*darśakāle kṣayo yasya pretapakṣe 'tha vā punaḥ \|*  
*pratyabdaṃ pārvaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tasya sarvaiḥ sutairapi \|\| 6 \|\|*

*ekoddiṣṭamaputrāṇāṃ puṃsāṃ syādyopitāmapi \|*  
*ekoddiṣṭe kuśā grāhyāḥ samūlā yajñakarmaṇi \|*  
*bahirlūnāḥ sakṛllanāḥ śrāddhaṃ vṛddhimṛte sadā \|\| 7 \|\|*

4-7. If either or both, the son and the father, maintain sacrificial fire, the annual sraddha should be of parvana type and the ksetraja or aurasa son should perform it. But some say whether the dead man does or does not maintain the sacrificial fire, ekoddista should be performed on the ksaya day. If there is ksaya either at the time of amavasya or in the preta- paksa, the sraddha should be of parvana type and can be performed by any son, while ekoddista should be done for persons without sons or for women.

## \|\| 45.8 \|\|

*kartavye pārvaṇe śrāddhe āśaucaṃ yadi jāyate \|*  
*āśaucāvagame kuryācchrāddhaṃ hi tadanantaram \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. If at the lime of parvana sraddha the performer is defiled by impurity he should perform it on the expiry of impurity.

## \|\| 45.9 \|\|

*ekoddiṣṭe tu samprāpte yadi vighnaḥ prajāyate \|*  
*māsenyasmin tithau tasyāṃ kuryācchrāddhaṃ tadaiva hi \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. If at the lime of ekoddista there is any obstacle, the sraddha should be performed

## \|\| 45.10 \|\|

*tūṣṇīṃ śrāddhāntu śūdrasya bhāryāyāstatsutasya ca \|*  
*kanyāyāśca dvijātīnāmanupetadvijasya ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The sraddha of a sudra should be performed silently (i.e. without reciting mantras) by his wives or sons. The same holds good in the case of sraddha of an unmarried girl in a Brahmin family. So says Marra.

## \|\| 45.11 \|\|

*ekakāle gatā sūnāṃ bahūnāmatha vā dvayoḥ \|*  
*tantreṇa śrapaṇaṃ kuryācchrāddhaṃ kuryāt pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. If two or more die at the same time, the bathing shall be done simultaneously with due mantras but sraddhas should be performed separately.

## \|\| 45.12 \|\|

*dadyāt pūrvaṃ mṛtasyādau dvitīyasya tataḥ punaḥ \|*  
*tṛtīyasya tataḥ kuryāt saṃnipāte tvayaṃ vidhiḥ (kramaḥ) \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The Sraddha of the eldest should be performed first and thereafter according to the age. This is the procedure in simultaneous deaths.

## \|\| 45.13 \|\|

*pratyabdamevaṃ yaḥ kuryādyathātathamatandritaḥ \|*  
*tārayitvā pitṝn sarvān prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. He who does all this every year without fail will obtain the best of state having liberated all the manes.

## \|\| 45.14 \|\|

*na jñāyate mṛtāhaścet prasthānadinameva ca \|*  
*māsaścet syāt parijñātastaddarśe syānmṛtāhikam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. If the day of death is not known nor the starting day, only the month is known then darsa should be the day for sraddha.

## \|\| 45.15 \|\|

*yadā māso na vijñāto vijñātaṃ dinameva ca \|*  
*tadā mārgaśire māsi māghe vā taddinaṃ bhavet \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. If the month is not known but the day is known then that day may be in MargasTrsa or in Magha.

## \|\| 45.16 \|\|

*dinamāsāvavijñātau maraṇasya yadā punaḥ \|*  
*prasthānadinamāsau tu grāhyau śrāddhe mayoditau \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. If both the day and the month of death are not known then the day and the month when he set out on journey should be taken into count for sraddha as stated by me before.

## \|\| 45.17 \|\|

*prasthānasyāpi na jñātau dinamāsau yadā punaḥ \|*  
*mṛtavārtāśrutau grāhyau pūrvaproktakrameṇa tu \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Even if the day and the month of starting are not known then those should be the same when the news of his death is received.

## \|\| 45.18 \|\|

*pravāsamantareṇāpi syātāṃ tau vismṛtau yadā \|*  
*tadānīmapi tau grāhyau pūrvavat tu mṛtāhike \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Even if the month and day are forgotten, when he is not on travel, these should be taken as before.

## \|\| 45.19-20 \|\|

*gṛhasthe proṣite yacca kaścittu mriyate gṛhe \|*  
*āsaucāpagame yatra prārabdhe śrāddhakarmaṇi \|\| 19 \|\|*

*pratyāgataścejjānāti tatra vṛttaṃ gṛhī tathā \|*  
*āśaucaṃ gṛhiṇasteṣāṃ na dravyādestadā bhavet \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. When the householder has gone out of his country and some one dies at home, the period of impurity is over, the sraddha is on and the householder returns to learn about the sad news, in such a state the householder is not effected by impurity.

## \|\| 45.21 \|\|

*putrādinā yadārabdhaṃ śrāddhaṃ tattvena vākhilam \|*  
*samāpanīyaṃ tatrāpi śrāddhaṃ gṛhītu dūrataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The sraddha that has been started by the sons should be finished by them, while the householder shall remain aloof.

## \|\| 45.22 \|\|

*dātrā boktrā ca na jñātaṃ sūtakaṃ mṛtakaṃ tathā \|*  
*ubhayorapi taddoṣaṃ nāropayati karhicit \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. If a donor or a receiver does not know of impurity due. to birth or death of a relative then no fault accrues.

## \|\| 45.23 \|\|

*yadā tvanyatarajñātaṃ sūtakaṃ mṛtakaṃ tathā \|*  
*bhoktureva tadā doṣo nānyo dātā praduṣyati \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. If either of these knows impurity accruing from death or birth of a relative, the fault is of the receiver only, not of the donor of the gift.

## \|\| 45.24 \|\|

*ityuktena prakāreṇa yaḥ kuryānmṛtavāsaram \|*  
*avijñātamṛtāhasya satataṃ tārayatyasau \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Whosoever performs the death anniversary of the dead in the above way, liberates him even if the day of death remains unknown to him.

## \|\| 45.25 \|\|

*nityaśrāddhe 'tha gandhādyairdvijānabhyarcya bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*sarvān pitṛgaṇān samyak sahaivoddiśya yojayet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. In the daily sraddha the Brahmins shall be worshipped, according to one's capacity, In that case, the offering is made first to the father, then to the mother and then to the maternal grandfather.

## \|\| 45.26-27 \|\|

*āvāhanaṃ svadhākāro piṇḍāgnaukaraṇādikam \|*  
*brahmacaryādiniyamā viśvadevāstathaiva ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*nityaśrāddhe tyajedetān bhojyamannaṃ prakalpayet \|*  
*dattvā tu dakṣiṇāṃ śaktyā namaskārairvisarjayet \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Avahana, Svadhakara pinch, Agnlkarana need not be performed. The performer shall observe celibacy during the period. He should worship the Visvedevas, offer the cooked food to the Brahmins along with the fee. He should payhomage to them as they take leave of him.

## \|\| 45.28 \|\|

*devānuddiśya viśvādīn yaddadyāddvijabhojanam \|*  
*tannityaśrāddhavat kāryaṃ devaśrāddhaṃ taducyate \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. With the Visvedevas in view, the Brahmins are fed sumptuously. This rite of feeding the Brahmins is caged Nitya sraddha or Dees, sraddha.

## \|\| 45.29 \|\|

*mātṛśrāddhantu pūrveṇa karmādau paitṛkaṃ tathā \|*  
*uttare 'hani vṛddhau syānmātāmahagaṇasya tu \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The sraddha for the mother is performed first. That for the father on the anniversary day. That for the grandfather on the father's and mother's side on the next day.

## \|\| 45.30 \|\|

*śrāddhatrayaṃ prakurrvīta vaiśvadevantutāntrikam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. If he is unable to perform the same on separate days he should perform all the sraddhas on the same day. The rite of Vaisvadeva should also be performed similarly.

## \|\| 45.31 \|\|

*mātṛbhyaḥ kalpayetpūrvaṃ pitṛbhyastadanantaram \|*  
*mātāmahebhyaśca tathā dadyāditthaṃ krameṇa tu \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. In that case, the offering is made first to the father, then to the mother and then to the maternal grandfather.

## \|\| 45.32 \|\|

*mātṛśrāddhe tu viprāṇāmabhāve sukulodgatāḥ \|*  
*patiputrānvitāḥ sādhvyo yoṣito 'ṣṭau ca bhāvayet \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. In the sraddha to the mother, if brahmins are not available, eight noble and chaste ladies whose husbands and sons are alive should be fed.

## \|\| 45.33 \|\|

*iṣṭāpūrtādike śrāddhaṃ kuryādābhyudayaṃ tathā \|*  
*utpātādinimitteṣu nitya śrāddhavadeva tu \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. When performing istapurta, the procedure is the same, When calamities set in, to ward them off, he should perform a sraddha in the manner of daily sraddha.

## \|\| 45.34 \|\|

*nityaṃ daivañca vṛddhiñca kāmyaṃ naimittikaṃ tathā \|*  
*śrāddhānyuktaprakāreṇa kurvan siddhimavāpnuyāt \|*  
*iti te kathitaṃ tārkṣya kimanyatparipṛcchasi \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The person performing nitya, daiva and vrddha sraddha as well as the kamya and naimittika rites in the manner as mentioned before achieves the desired result. Thus, I have told you all, О Garuda. What else do you want to know?

## Chapter 46

## \|\| 46.1 \|\|

*tārkṣya uvāca \|*  
*sukṛtasya prabhāveṇa svargo nānāvidho naṇām \|*  
*bhogāḥ saukhyāti rūpañca balaṃ buddhaiḥ parākramaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. The different kinds of heavenly enjoyments, worldly pleasures, strength, nourishment and valour men acquire by the power of merit.

## \|\| 46.2 \|\|

*satyaṃ puṇyavatāṃ deva jāyate 'tra paratra ca \|*  
*satyaṃsatyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ vedavākyaṃ na cānyathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. All these happen to meritorious persons here or there, it is true, definitely true. The lord's statement cannot be otherwise.

## \|\| 46.3 \|\|

*dharmo jayati nādharmaḥ satyaṃ jayāte nānṛtam \|*  
*kṣamā jayati na krodho viṣṇurjayati nāsuraḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Virtue triumphs, not evil. Truth triumphs not falsehood. Forgiveness wins, not anger. Visnu conquers not the asuras.

## \|\| 46.4 \|\|

*tadvatsatyaṃ mayā jñātaṃ sukṛtācchobhanaṃ bhavet \|*  
*yatotkṛṣṭatamaṃ puṇyaṃ tathotkṛṣṭataronaraḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. I have understood this truth that everything auspicious results from merit. When our merit is at the peak we are devoted to lord Krsna.

## \|\| 46.5-6 \|\|

*evantu śrotumicchāmi jāyante pāpino yathā \|*  
*yena karmavipākena yathā niyamabhāgbhavet \|\| 5 \|\|*

*yāṃyāṃ yonimavāpnoti yathārūpaśca jāyate \|*  
*tanme vada suraśreṣṭha samāsenāpi kāṅkṣitam \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. There is still a query. What is that action by which one takes sinful births? How does one become a victim of hell? О lord of deities, please tell me briefly what I desire to know; how and what are the forms taken by him?

## \|\| 46.7 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*śubhāśubhaphalaistārkṣya bhuktabhogā narāstviha \|*  
*jāyante lakṣaṇairyaistutāni me śṛṇu kāśyapa \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О Tarkhya, men indulge in activities which result in meritorious or inauspicious fruits. О Kasyapa, now listen how men derive traits from their particular activities.

## \|\| 46.8 \|\|

*gururātmavatāṃ śāstā rājā śāstā durātmanām \|*  
*iha pracchannapāpānāṃ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The preceptor guides the seeker, the king chastens the wicked, Yama regulates and rectifies the person of secret sins.

## \|\| 46.9-10 \|\|

*prāyaścitteṣvacīrṇeṣu yamalokā hyanekadhā \|*  
*yātanābhirvimuktā ye yānti te jīvasantatīm \|\| 9 \|\|*

*gatvā mānuṣabhāve tu pāpacihnā bhavanti te \|*  
*tānyahaṃ tava cihnāni kathayiṣye khagottama \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. When the expiatory and deterrent tortures in hell cease, the living beings are born again in human form with the characteristic traits of their sins. О foremost among birds, I shall tell you what these signs are.

## \|\| 46.11 \|\|

*soḍhvā vai yātanāḥ sarvā gatvā vaivasvatakṣayam \|*  
*nistīrṇayātanāste tu lokamāyānti cihnitāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Having suffered and crossed tortures in hell they return to the world of mortals marked with the characteristic signs.

## \|\| 46.12 \|\|

*gadgado 'natavādī syānmūkaścaiva gavānṛte \|*  
*brahmahā jāyate kuṣṭhī śyāvadantaśca madyapaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If a person has been guilty of falsehood in speech, he becomes a stammerer, a teller of lies or dumb. The Brahmin slayer is born consumptive or leprous; the wine-addict becomes blacktoothed.

## \|\| 46.13 \|\|

*kunakhī svarṇaharaṇādduścarmā gurutalpagaḥ \|*  
*saṃyogī hīnayoniḥ syāddaridro 'dattadānataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The thief of gold becomes bad-nailed; the defiler of the preceptor's bed becomes ugly- skinned, he who associates with sinners is born in a low family.

## \|\| 46.14 \|\|

*ayājyayājako yāti grāhamasūkaratāṃ dvijaḥ \|*  
*kharo vai bahuyājī syātkāko nirmantrabhojanāt \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. He who takes meals at another's house without prior invitation is born a crow. A Brahmin who performs a sacrifice for low castes is born a village-pig. He who performs many such sacrifices is born an ass.

## \|\| 46.15 \|\|

*aparīkṣitabhoktāro vyāghrāḥ syurnirjane vane \|*  
*bahutarjako mārjaraḥ khadyotaḥ kakṣadāhakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He who eats unscrupulously becomes a tiger in wilderness. He who scolds others without a cause becomes a cat; he who bums dry wood is born a glow-worm.

## \|\| 46.16 \|\|

*pātre vidyāpradātā yo balīvardo bhavettasaḥ \|*  
*annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ vipre pradadatkukkaro bhavet \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He who imparts knowledge to the undeserving becomes a bull. He who offers stale food to a Brahmin becomes a hunch-back. He who is malicious to others is born blind. He who steals a book is born similarly.

## \|\| 46.17 \|\|

*mātsaryādapi jātyandho janmāndhaḥ pustakaṃ haran \|*  
*phalānyāharato 'patyaṃ mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He who habitually steals fruits is born a monkey or alternatively suffers from goitre.

## \|\| 46.18-19 \|\|

*mṛto vānaratāṃ yāti tanmukho gaṇḍavān bhavet \|*  
*adattvā bhakṣyamaśrāti anapatyo bhavettu saḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*haranvastraṃ bhavedgodhā garadaḥ pavanāśanaḥ \|*  
*pravajyāgamanādrājan bhavenmarupiśācakaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Не who takes food offered unwillingly becomes impotent. He who is averse to thinking on self is born a stupid trader. He who is ignorant of the truth of virtue falls in a deep ocean. He who steals gold is born an

## \|\| 46.20-21 \|\|

*cātako jalahartā syāddhānyahartā ca mūṣikaḥ \|*  
*aprāptayauvanāṃ sevan bhavetsarpa iti śrutiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*gurudārābhilāṣī ca kṛkalāso bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*jalaprastravaṇaṃ yastu bhindyānmatsyo bhavennaraḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. О bird, he who has sexual intercourse with an ascetic lady assumes a ghostly form. He who steals water becomes a Cataka. He who steals grams becomes a mouse. He who rapes an immature girl becomes a serpent. He who covets his preceptor's wife becomes a chameleon. He who interferes with the flow of water becomes a fish.

## \|\| 46.22 \|\|

*avikreyakrayāccaiva bako gṛdhro bhavennaraḥ \|*  
*ayonigo vṛko hi syādulūkaḥ krayavañcanāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He who sells the forbidden articles becomes deformed in the eye. He who censures others is born of defiled womb. He who deceives a woman becomes an owl.

## \|\| 46.23-24 \|\|

*mṛtasyaikādaśāhe tu bhuñjānaścābhijāyate \|*  
*pratiśrutya dvijebhyor'thamadadajjambuko bhavet \|\| 23 \|\|*

*rājñariṃ gatvā bhaveddaṃṣṭrī taskaro viṅvarāhakaḥ \|*  
*śārivā phalavikretā vṛṣaśca vṛṣalīpatiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. He who takes food on the fast day after a relative's death becomes a dog. He who does not pay the promised sum to a Brahmin becomes a jackal. He who kills a serpent becomes a boar. He who slanders Brahmins becomes a tortoise. He who subsists on the worship of idols becomes a Gandala.

## \|\| 46.25 \|\|

*mārjaro 'gniṃ padā spṛṣṭvā rogavānparamāṃsabhuk \|*  
*udakyāgamanātṣaṇḍo durgandhaśca sugandhahṛt \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The seller of forbidden fruits becomes indigent. He who keeps a sudra woman as concubine becomes a bull. He who kicks the sacred fire becomes a cat. He who eats another's meat becomes a patient.

## \|\| 46.26 \|\|

*yadvā tadvāpi pārakyaṃ svalpaṃ vā harate bahu \|*  
*hṛtvā vai yonimāpnoti tiraścāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Не who indulges in sex at the prohibited time becomes a eunuch. A stealer of scents becomes a foul-smeller. A stealer of other goods becomes a swallow.

## \|\| 46.27 \|\|

*evamādīni cihnāni anyānyapi khageśvara \|*  
*svakarmavitatānyeva? dṛśyante yaistu mānavaiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. О lord of birds, these and other signs brought about by their actions are seen in men and others.

## \|\| 46.28 \|\|

*evaṃ duṣkṛtakarmā hi bhuktvā ca narakānkramāt \|*  
*jāyate karmaśeṣeṇa uktāsvetāsu yoniṣu \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Persons who commit such sins fall into hell and are re-bom in different species of animals.

## \|\| 46.29 \|\|

*tato janmaśataṃ martye sarvajantuṣu kāśyapa \|*  
*jāyate nātra sandehaḥ samībhūte śubhāśubhe \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. After passing through these births men and women are re-bom in human form when their merit and demerit are equalized.

## \|\| 46.30 \|\|

*strīpuṃsayo prasaṅgena niruddhe śukrasoṇite \|*  
*samupetaḥ pañcabhūtairjāyate pāñcabhautikaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. When men and women unite in sexual act and both the semen and the blood are mixed, the child born will be nourished with all the elements in proper proportion,

## \|\| 46.31-32 \|\|

*indriyāṇi manaḥ prāṇā jñānamāyuḥ sukhaṃ dhṛtiḥ \|*  
*dhāraṇā preraṇaṃ duḥ khaṃ mithyāhaṅkāra eva ca \|\| 31 \|\|*

*prayatākṛtivarṇastu rāgadveṣau bhavābhavau \|*  
*tasyedamātmanaḥ sarvamanāderādimicchataḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The receptivity, the inducement, misery, desire, death, effort, feature, complexion, love, hatred, birth, death — these are attributed to the beginningless soul that seeks for its beginning (by entering the womb).

## \|\| 46.33-34 \|\|

*svakarma baddhasya tadā garbhavṛddhirbhavediti \|*  
*purā yathā mayā proktaṃ tava jantorhi lakṣaṇam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*evaṃ pravartitaṃ cakraṃ bhūtagrāme caturvidhe \|*  
*samutpattirvināśaśca jāyate tārkṣya dehinām \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. Bound by its own action the soul takes up body after body and undergoes series of births. This is what (have told you already. This is how the circle whirls in the four species of being.

Thus, О Garuda, do the birth and death of living beings rotate. The rise in the course is due to virtue and the fall is due to evil.
